### [1] [5G NR Sensing of Robotic Arm Motion in Indoor Factory Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28053)
*Neeraj Varshney,Anuraag Bodi,Steve Blandino,Jian Wang,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究工业工厂环境下单站通感一体化系统跟踪机械臂运动学的性能，基于此前从机械臂高分辨率测量中导出的部件特定准确定性信道模型，构建任意关节运动轨迹生成框架，并结合3GPP InF信道模型与5G NR PRS波形进行系统级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中机械臂的精准跟踪对通感一体化系统至关重要，但工业杂波对感知可靠性的影响尚缺乏系统级量化评估，尤其是稀疏与密集杂波环境下的覆盖与检测性能差异。

Method: 利用分层运动学变换生成任意关节运动轨迹，将高保真目标模型与标准化3GPP InF信道模型集成，采用5G NR定位参考信号作为感知波形，在InF-SH与InF-DH场景下结合3GPP合规天线方向图与模拟波束赋形进行仿真。

Result: 视距条件下可实现高估计精度；InF-DH密集杂波导致严重遮挡，即使机械臂位于发射接收点正下方，检测概率也降至约10%；InF-SH中实现高检测概率（如≥90%）需目标紧邻TRP，有效覆盖半径约13米。

Conclusion: 工业杂波显著制约单站通感一体化系统的感知可靠性，密集杂波环境下有效感知覆盖急剧缩减，稀疏杂波下覆盖范围亦受限于TRP邻近区域，需针对工业部署优化感知策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance of monostatic Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems for tracking robotic arm kinematics in industrial factory (InF) environments. Building on a part-specific quasi-deterministic channel model previously derived from high-resolution robotic-arm measurements using our context-aware channel sounder and digital twin, we develop a framework for generating arbitrary articulated motion profiles through hierarchical kinematic transformations. By integrating this high-fidelity target model with the standardized 3GPP InF channel model, we employ 5G New Radio (NR) Positioning Reference Signals (PRS) as the sensing waveform to enable comprehensive system-level evaluation. Simulations are conducted for both sparse clutters (InF-SH) and dense clutters (InF-DH) scenarios using 3GPP-compliant antenna radiation patterns and analog beamforming, allowing us to assess the impact of industrial clutter on sensing performance. The results show that while high estimation accuracy is achievable under Line-of-Sight (LOS) conditions, dense clutter in InF-DH environments introduces severe blockage that significantly limits sensing reliability. In particular, the effective sensing coverage in InF-DH is drastically reduced, with detection probability dropping to approximately \(P_d \approx 10\,\%\) even when the robotic arm is located directly beneath the Transmission Reception Point (TRP). Furthermore, in InF-SH, achieving a high detection probability (e.g., \(P_d \geq 90\,\%\)) requires the target to remain in close proximity to the TRP, with an effective coverage radius of approximately 13 m.

</details>


### [2] [DriveTTO: Understanding Search and Selection in Test-Time Trajectory Optimization for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29205)
*Xinyao Rao,Zecheng Tang,Zheyuan Lai,Liuxuan Sun,Minjun Yu,Xun Cao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveTTO是一种无需训练的方法，通过在原生提案周围搜索并用冻结评分器验证轨迹改进，提升自动驾驶规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在困难驾驶场景中，学习评分器可能从固定池中选出较差轨迹，且周边车辆和道路边界限制高评分机动范围，这些约束随场景变化难以在训练中穷尽覆盖。

Method: DriveTTO围绕原生提案搜索，将每个改进提案与保留轨迹对比验证；冻结评分器在实际到达端点评估完整路径，引导逐步缩小邻域的重采样；一个分支利用规划器提案，另一分支扰动车辆控制，在匹配时间混合位姿，仅当两个冻结评分器均预测增益时接受分支或融合更新。

Result: 在Navtest上，DriveTTO对原生零分案例平均51.35%恢复非零PDMS；考虑新失败后零分率从1.63%降至1.13%；特定配置下Navtest达95.54 PDMS和92.30 EPDMS，Navhard达57.10 EPDMS，HUGSIM在观测命令下达40.1 HD-Score；单一冻结评分器下DriveTTO-S在Navtest和Navhard上改进全部六个评估规划器。

Conclusion: DriveTTO无需训练即可有效恢复零分轨迹并提升多个基准和规划器性能，证明围绕原生提案搜索与冻结评分器验证结合是可行的规划改进方案。

Abstract: In difficult driving scenes, a learned scorer can select a poor trajectory from a fixed pool even when a better candidate is available. Nearby vehicles and road boundaries can also leave only a narrow range of high-scoring maneuvers. These constraints vary with the scene and are difficult to cover exhaustively during training. We introduce DriveTTO, a training-free method that searches around native proposals and checks each proposed refinement against a retained trajectory. A frozen scorer evaluates completed paths at the endpoints they actually reach, guiding resampling in progressively smaller neighborhoods. From a shared reference, one branch draws on planner proposals and another perturbs vehicle controls. DriveTTO blends their poses at matching times and accepts a branch or fusion update only when both frozen scorers predict a gain. On Navtest, DriveTTO recovers nonzero predictive driver model scores (PDMS) in 51.35% of native zero-score cases on average. Accounting for new failures, the zero-score rate falls from 1.63% to 1.13%. Benchmark-specific configurations achieve 95.54 PDMS and 92.30 extended PDMS (EPDMS) on Navtest, 57.10 EPDMS on Navhard, and 40.1 HD-Score on HUGSIM under observed commands. With a single frozen scorer, DriveTTO-S improves all six evaluated planners on both Navtest and Navhard.

</details>


### [3] [FunGrasp-Zero: Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Policy for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Functional Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29195)
*Yanhan Lin,Yuliang Wu,Yihao Guo,Wei-Jin Huang,Jianwen Li,Jiawei Tang,Xiaolong Huang,Yuquan Wang,Ancong Wu,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FunGrasp-Zero强化学习框架，利用物体中心接触图与形态映射实现零样本跨本体功能抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手形态多样导致跨本体功能抓取难以泛化，现有方法依赖特定本体关节姿态重定向，缺乏物体中心功能接触意图的显式建模。

Method: 以物体中心、跨本体一致的接触图表达功能意图，经形态映射适配异构手；构建手-物势场生成吸引/排斥引导向量；用场条件图注意力网络协调多指运动，并以势场奖励约束功能对齐与稳定性；同时建立X-FunBench基准。

Result: 四本体联合训练的单一策略在X-FunBench上对五只未见手抓取新物体平均成功率86%；在Leap、Inspire、Sharpa、Wuji四只真实未见手上零微调平均成功率80%。

Conclusion: FunGrasp-Zero是首个在仿真与真实世界中实现跨未见手零样本功能抓取的强化学习框架。

Abstract: Dexterous functional grasping is critical for robotic tool use, yet current dexterous hands exhibit substantial morphological diversity. This hardware heterogeneity makes cross-embodiment functional grasping a fundamental but largely unexplored problem. Existing functional grasping methods use retargeted hand poses as guidance, tying task representation to a specific embodiment and failing to explicitly capture object-centric functional contact intent, limiting generalization across hands. To address this, we propose FunGrasp-Zero, a reinforcement learning framework for zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping. Instead of relying on embodiment-specific joint configuration, FunGrasp-Zero captures functional intent via an object-centric, cross-embodiment-consistent contact map, adapted to heterogeneous hands through morphological mapping. We construct a hand-object potential field from mapped contact regions, yielding attractive and repulsive guidance vectors. A Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Network (FC-GAT) leverages these vectors to coordinate multi-finger motion across hands, while a potential field reward guides fingers toward target regions and penalizes non-target collisions to enforce functional alignment and stability. We further establish X-FunBench for cross-embodiment functional grasping, evaluating both physical grasp success and functional intent preservation across hands. Evaluated on X-FunBench, our unified policy, co-trained on four embodiments, achieves 86% average grasp success rate across five unseen hands on novel objects. Deployed on four unseen physical hands (Leap, Inspire, Sharpa, Wuji) for novel object grasping, the policy achieves 80% average success rate without fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FunGrasp-Zero is the first RL-based framework to achieve zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping across unseen hands in both simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [4] [Implementation and Software-in-the-Loop Evaluation of a Trans-Domain Digital Twin Architecture for Autonomous Guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV Without Online GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29158)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了一种基于跨域数字孪生（TDDT）的自主导航系统，用于Phoenix VTOL无人机在无卫星定位条件下的自主引导，并通过软件在环仿真验证了系统协调性、决策可追溯性和闭环引导能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在应急响应、基础设施监测和货物运输等场景中需要可靠导航，但卫星定位在封闭空间、自然障碍物和建筑造成的盲区、以及电子战中的无线电干扰和信号欺骗环境下可能不可用或不可靠，因此需要不依赖卫星的导航方案。

Method: 构建基于TDDT的系统，将独立导航、飞行动力学和气象模型在一个“状态估计—预测—决策—命令执行—反馈”循环中协调；利用惯性、磁力、气压和空速传感器数据及环境信息进行估计与预测；通过共享状态、时间同步、信息溯源保留和命令可追溯性确保模型与决策的连续性；在软件在环环境中使用模拟物理孪生进行测试，记录垂直起降、向目标移动、着陆和自主停止，并根据传感器反馈有限调整动力学模型。

Result: 测试记录了垂直起飞、向目的地移动、着陆和自主停止；将评估参考与导航输入分离，实现了对定位误差和计算性能的独立评估；结果确认了测试条件下的运行协调、决策可追溯性和闭环引导。

Conclusion: 该系统为不依赖卫星定位的导航开发与评估提供了一个可复现的平台，并在测试条件下验证了基于TDDT的自主引导的可行性与可追溯性。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV guidance in emergency response, infrastructure monitoring, and cargo transportation requires reliable navigation. However, satellite-based positioning may become unavailable or unreliable in enclosed spaces, blind spots caused by natural obstacles and structures, and environments affected by radio interference and signal spoofing during electronic warfare. This study designs, implements, and evaluates a system based on a Trans-Domain Digital Twin (TDDT) for autonomous guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV. Independent navigation, flight dynamics, and meteorological models are coordinated within a cycle of state estimation, prediction, decision-making, command execution, and feedback. Data from inertial, magnetic, pressure, and airspeed sensors, together with environmental information, form the basis for estimation and prediction. Furthermore, a shared state, temporal synchronization, preservation of information provenance, and command traceability establish continuity between models and decisions. The system was tested with a simulated physical twin in a software-in-the-loop environment. Vertical takeoff, movement toward the destination, landing, and autonomous stopping were recorded, and the dynamic model was adjusted to a limited extent based on sensor-derived feedback. Separating the evaluation reference from navigation inputs enabled independent assessment of positioning error and computational performance. The results confirm operational coordination, decision traceability, and closed-loop guidance under the tested conditions, providing a reproducible platform for the development and evaluation of navigation without satellite-based positioning.

</details>


### [5] [Your Diffusion-Based Driving Planner Only Needs a Nudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29123)
*Zilin Wang,Guozheng Ma,Saeed Rahmani,Jiong Yang,Antyanta Bangunharcana,Yiluan Guo,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Shimon Whiteson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlanNudge通过冻结预训练的扩散规划器并训练轻量级nudge策略（输出速度与曲率的二维调整），用标准离策略强化学习在闭环仿真中微调，解决了扩散规划器的协变量偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散规划器能很好地捕捉人类驾驶行为的多模态性，但在闭环驾驶中协变量偏移会导致其生成碰撞或偏离道路的轨迹；直接对扩散规划器做强化学习微调又因其迭代去噪过程和滚动时域执行而复杂。作者观察到对规划轨迹做小幅调整即可避免许多不安全结果。

Method: 冻结预训练的扩散规划器以保留其规划能力，训练一个多层感知机形式的nudge策略，在每个规划步单次前向输出对规划速度与曲率的二维调整量；这两个维度直接改变车辆运动，因此可用标准离策略强化学习在闭环仿真中训练。

Result: 在nuPlan基准的六个设置中，PlanNudge的最终得分均达到或超过先前最先进的强化学习微调方法，且所用环境步数约为其六分之一、模型计算量不足其五分之一；结果以六次训练运行的均值和标准差报告，并给出了奖励塑形、价值学习和探索的指导原则。

Conclusion: 对冻结扩散规划器的输出施加轻量级nudge调整是一种高效且有效的闭环微调方案，可在显著降低样本与计算成本的同时匹配或超越现有强化学习微调方法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based planners capture the multimodality of human driving behavior well, yet covariate shift in closed-loop driving can lead them to generate trajectories that result in collisions or road departures. Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning offers a promising way to tackle this issue, but applying it to diffusion-based planners is complicated by their iterative denoising process and receding-horizon execution. We find that small adjustments to the planned trajectories can prevent many of these unsafe outcomes. Motivated by this observation, we propose PlanNudge, which preserves the learned planning capabilities of a pretrained diffusion-based planner by freezing it and trains a lightweight policy that we call the nudge policy. At each planning step, the nudge policy outputs a nudge, a two-dimensional adjustment of the speed and curvature of the plan from the frozen planner. The nudge policy is a multilayer perceptron that predicts the nudge in a single forward pass, and both dimensions directly change the motion of the vehicle, so standard off-policy RL can train it in closed-loop simulation. We evaluate PlanNudge on nuPlan, a large-scale autonomous driving benchmark based on real-world driving data. In all six settings, PlanNudge matches or exceeds the final scores of the previous state-of-the-art RL finetuning method while using about one sixth as many environment steps and less than one fifth as much model compute. We also provide guidelines for reward shaping, value learning, and exploration when using off-policy RL to post-train autonomous driving planners. Our results are reported with means and standard deviations over six training runs. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [6] [DUO Policy: Bridging Human Video Coverage and Robot Precision for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29101)
*Mingxi Jia,Patton Jiyang Yin,Haojie Huang,Ivy Xiao He,Xuanru Liu,Gefei Liu,Dian Wang,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUO Policy 将操作技能解耦为在人类视频上预训练的手部策略（负责全局自由空间运动）与在稀疏机器人数据上训练的机器人策略（负责局部精确修正），并由从人类干预中学习的门控机制切换，从而在减少机器人数据采集的同时提升精确任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可扩展地降低机器人数据采集成本，但具身差距使策略局限于粗粒度行为，在插入等精确任务上失败；现有方法将两类数据同等对待，导致策略在精度关键步骤吸收具身误差，或耗费稀缺机器人数据重新学习自由空间运动。作者观察到数据价值随状态变化：人类数据在低精度要求处有用，机器人数据在精度关键状态附近价值更高。

Method: 提出 Dual-Actor Policy（DUO Policy），将操作技能解耦为两个策略：手部策略在人类视频上预训练以完成全局自由空间运动，机器人策略在稀疏的 in-hand 数据上训练以进行局部精确修正；并学习一个由人类干预驱动的门控机制在两者之间切换。

Result: 在 K-cup 咖啡制作、桌面清理和杂乱糖果分拣等任务上，DUO Policy 在四个仿真任务中平均成功率最高超过基线 12.9 个百分点，在三个真实任务中超过 51.7 个百分点，并以 2.75 倍更少的数据采集时间达到与仅机器人训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: 按状态区分数据价值并解耦为双策略的设计对人类数据中的中等具身误差具有鲁棒性，且能泛化到机器人数据覆盖之外的场景布局，在减少机器人数据采集的同时提升精确操作表现。

Abstract: Human videos promise a scalable way to reduce expensive robot data collection, but the embodiment gap limits the resulting policies to coarse behaviors, and they fail at precise tasks such as insertion. Existing methods co-train or finetune a single policy on both data sources, treating each source as equally useful in every state: the policy either absorbs the embodiment error at precision-critical steps or spends scarce robot data relearning free-space motion. We observe that the value of each source depends on the state: human data is useful where precision requirements are low, while robot data is disproportionately valuable near precision-critical states. We therefore propose \textbf{DUO Policy} (Dual-Actor Policy), which decouples a manipulation skill into a hand policy, pretrained on human videos for global free-space motion, and a robot policy, trained on sparse in-hand data for local precise corrections, with a gate learned from human interventions switching between them. This design is robust to moderate embodiment error in human data and generalizes to scene layouts beyond the coverage of the robot data. On tasks such as K-cup coffee making, desk cleanup, and cluttered candy sorting, DUO Policy outperforms the baseline with the highest average success by 12.9 percentage points over four simulated tasks and by 51.7 points over three real-world tasks, and matches robot-only training with 2.75$\times$ less data-collection time. Project Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [7] [JEPA-Tuner: From Predictive Fine-Tuning to Asynchronous Execution of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29096)
*Jingxiang Qu,Tian Xie,Youngjin Hong,Changhyun Choi,Yang Katie Zhao,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-Tuner是一个预测式微调框架，通过在微调中学习动作条件下的未来预测来正则化VLA模型的策略引导，从而提升同步推理的嵌入质量并支持高效的异步动作块执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动作监督微调未能显式捕捉动作条件下观测与机器人状态的时间依赖关系，且异步动作块推理需要衔接下一块的引导信息。

Method: 在微调中引入预测器，给定当前引导和已执行动作去匹配由未来观测编码的引导；联合训练跨模态引导适配器和动作专家；部署时将预训练预测器用于预测下一块边界所需引导，使策略提前生成并直接执行下一块。

Result: 在LIBERO和Meta-World上，同步推理任务成功率提升且多模态引导嵌入更优；在LIBERO仿真和多种尺寸真实机器人演示中，不同延迟设置下异步控制的性能与平滑度均改善。

Conclusion: 动作条件下的未来预测正则化能有效提升VLA策略引导的表征质量与控制性能，并实现无需重叠校正或额外训练的高效异步执行。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generate sequences of actions from visual observations, proprioceptive states, and language instructions. In embodied interaction, executing these actions influences subsequent observations and robot states, providing a natural predictive signal for policy learning. However, standard action-supervised fine-tuning does not explicitly capture these action-conditioned temporal dependencies in the guidance consumed by the action predictor. We introduce JEPA-Tuner, a predictive fine-tuning framework that regularizes the VLA's native policy guidance through action-conditioned future prediction. During fine-tuning, a predictor in JEPA-Tuner learns to match guidance encoded from a future observation given the current guidance and committed actions. This objective encourages a cross-modal guidance adapter to capture how the scene and robot state evolve under those actions, while jointly training the action expert on both observed and predicted guidance keeps the representation informative for control. In addition, when deploying the VLA model with asynchronous action-chunk inference, the pre-trained predictor will anticipate the guidance needed at the next chunk boundary. The policy can thus generate the next chunk in advance and execute it from its first action without overlap correction or additional predictor training, enabling efficient and high-quality asynchronous execution. Experiments on LIBERO and Meta-World show that JEPA-Tuner improves task success under synchronous inference with better multi-modal guidance embeddings. For asynchronous control, JEPA-Tuner improves performance and smoothness across different latency settings on both LIBERO simulator and multi-size real robot demonstrations. The project website with video demos is at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [ViewPACT: Multi-View Geometric Augmentation and Pose-Action Co-Training for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29068)
*Chenglang Tu,Feng Chen,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Binbin Huang,Chen Xu,Yanchao Yang,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR:  ViewPACT通过辅助几何监督学习图像策略，提升相机视角变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像策略可扩展但对相机视角变化脆弱；在LIBERO上发现proprioception是输入捷径，掩码机器人状态会显著降低性能，而仅用图像重训可恢复大部分损失。

Method: 提出ViewPACT：训练时使用特权几何监督，辅助目标恢复相机坐标系下的末端执行器和任务相关物体位姿，部署时仅需RGB观测。

Result: 在未见外部相机视角下，平均仿真成功率从37.4%提升至78.4%；在四个真实机器人任务中，结合仿真和真实图像几何监督达到68.1%平均成功率，对比固定视角行为克隆3.1%和最强替代方案30.6%。

Conclusion: 在评估的固定视角演示设置中，几何监督支持视角鲁棒性。

Abstract: Image policies are scalable but remain brittle to camera viewpoint changes. On LIBERO, we identify proprioception as an input shortcut: masking robot states sharply degrades several policies, while retraining from images alone recovers much of the loss. We introduce ViewPACT, an image policy trained with privileged geometric supervision. Auxiliary objectives recover end-effector and task-relevant object poses in the camera frame, while deployment requires only RGB observations. Joint geometric supervision improves mean simulation success from 37.4% to 78.4% under unseen external-camera viewpoints, with more modest gains under separately evaluated object, scene, and camera-intrinsic shifts. Across four real-robot tasks, combining simulated and real-image geometric supervision achieves 68.1% mean success, versus 3.1% for fixed-view behavior cloning and 30.6% for the strongest evaluated alternative. These results support geometric supervision for viewpoint robustness in the evaluated fixed-view demonstration setting.

</details>


### [9] [BO-COMM: Bidirectional Optical System for Simultaneous Localization and Communication in Swarm Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29044)
*Cheng Hu,Haotian Wu,Qi Lin,Manrui Zheng,Shigang Yue,Tao Zou,Jigen Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BO-COMM是一个结合光学位姿估计、动态状态报告和光学下行交互的群体机器人实验系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人实验需要观测机器人位姿与运行状态，并向机器人回传环境信息；共享信道无线通信在并发报告增加时易出现竞争和拥塞，而静态视觉标记无法传递机器人变化状态。

Method: 在Colias IV机器人上加装CEU-PLUS扩展和圆形LED阵列，用相机观测LED阵列，基于投票估计机器人中心，利用空间LED模式提供朝向和编码状态；投影仪提供空间环境线索，红外模块提供独立命令通道。

Result: 进行了处理时间测量、路径跟随与颜色报告实验以及多信息素演示，在平面场地中验证了该架构的运行。

Conclusion: 在测试的硬件和场地配置下，将光学位姿与状态观测同环境反馈相结合是可行的。

Abstract: Swarm robotics experiments require observations of robot pose and operational state, together with mechanisms for returning environmental information to the robots. Shared channel wireless communication can face contention and congestion as concurrent reporting demands increase, while a static visual marker does not itself convey changing robot states. We present BO-COMM, an optical system that combines pose estimation and dynamic state reporting with optical downstream interaction. A camera observes circular LED arrays mounted on Colias IV robots with CEU-PLUS extension this http URL -based voting estimates robot centers, and the spatial LED pattern provides orientation and an encoded state. A projector supplies spatial environmental cues, while an infrared module provides a separate command channel. Processing-time measurements, path-following and color-reporting experiments, and a multi-pheromone demonstration illustrate the operation of this architecture in a planar arena. The results demonstrate the feasibility of combining optical pose and state observations with environmental feedback in the tested hardware and arena configuration.

</details>


### [10] [Distributed Interactive Conformal Prediction for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29037)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MA-ICP是一种分布式共形预测框架，用于多智能体系统在任务级计算鲁棒裕度，并针对依赖性和分布偏移提供条件覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖学习组件和分布式算法，引入不确定性，现有共形预测方法无法同时处理机器人间依赖、分布式校准以及校准后边界改变闭环系统导致的分布偏移。

Method: MA-ICP将机器人级非一致性分数按机器人类别聚合为任务级分数，通过有限轮邻居通信计算任务级分位数；利用类别相关的分数敏感性上界估计更新鲁棒裕度引起的分数偏移，并建立更新后裕度的校准条件覆盖保证。

Result: 在协同负载控制、带避障的编队控制和分布式目标跟踪任务上验证了MA-ICP的有效性。

Conclusion: MA-ICP能够为异构多智能体系统提供分布无关的高概率任务级鲁棒裕度，并在分布偏移下保持每个机器人类别的校准条件覆盖。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems increasingly rely on learning-enabled components and distributed algorithms for perception, estimation, and control, introducing uncertainty that makes it challenging to satisfy mission requirements. Conformal prediction quantifies uncertainty with distribution-free guarantees, but existing methods do not address the challenging combination of dependence among robots, distributed calibration, and the distribution shift induced when calibrated uncertainty bounds modify the closed-loop system. We introduce Multi-Agent Interactive Conformal Prediction (MA-ICP), a distributed framework for computing robustness margins in heterogeneous systems to satisfy mission requirements with high probability. MA-ICP aggregates robot-level nonconformity scores, which measure errors in modeled or estimated quantities, into one mission-level score per robot class, allowing dependence among robots within each mission. Mission-level scores and quantiles are computed using neighbor-to-neighbor communication in a finite number of communication rounds. Updating the robustness margins changes robot trajectories and distributed algorithm outputs, so we use class-dependent score sensitivities to upper bound the shift in mission-level scores. We then establish calibration-conditional coverage guarantees per robot class for the updated margins. We demonstrate MA-ICP on cooperative payload control, formation control with obstacle avoidance, and distributed target tracking missions.

</details>


### [11] [graspit-py: A Python Interface to GraspIt! for Grasp Planning Under Pose and Friction Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29011)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: graspit-py 提供了原生 GraspIt! 的 Python 接口，支持接触-旋量分析、位姿与摩擦不确定性采样以及 MuJoCo 导出，并通过实验验证了几何精度、任务旋量容量和延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraspIt! 的 C++ API 和 ROS 1 服务在 Python 中使用需原生绑定或服务传输层，且接触几何与不确定性采样需应用特定集成，缺乏统一的 Python 工作流。

Method: 开发 graspit-py Python 接口，提供原生场景访问、接触-旋量分析、可配置接近规划；设计抓取记录关联手掌位姿与闭合坐标到名义接触和采样质量分数；实现 MuJoCo 导出器生成串行铰链链和仿射关节耦合，保留独立碰撞组件，并通过消融实验和线性规划验证。

Result: 在 5 种手模型 75 种配置下，源与导出顶点集最大差异约 1.3 微米（容差 100 微米）；验证了 1,920 次方向任务旋量容量计算；直接 Python 的中位重置-闭合-评分-状态周期延迟比持久 ROS 1 服务低 10.7%。

Conclusion: graspit-py 为抓取中的接触、评分和关节表示提供了可执行的导出-规划-重建工作流，支持数值分析与命令重建，并在精度和延迟上优于现有服务方案。

Abstract: In simulated multifingered grasps, pose error can change contacts formed during hand closure, while friction uncertainty changes the loads supported by those contacts. Existing C++ APIs and ROS 1 services provide programmatic GraspIt! access but require native bindings or a service transport layer for Python use and application-specific integration of contact geometry with uncertainty sampling. A Python workflow for these studies must connect native closure and contacts to caller-defined wrench scores, uncertainty samples, and command reconstruction. We present graspit-py, a Python interface to native GraspIt! operations that supplies this workflow. The interface provides native scene access, contact-wrench analysis, and configurable approach planning under pose and friction uncertainty. A grasp record associates the object-relative palm pose and closed independent coordinates with nominal contacts and quality scores evaluated under sampled pose and friction conditions. Our MuJoCo exporter derives serial hinge chains and affine joint couplings, including nonzero reference angles, and preserves individual collision components. Across 75 configurations of five hand models, maximum discrepancy between source and exported palm-frame vertex sets was approximately 1.3 micrometers against a 100-micrometer geometry tolerance. We isolated reference-angle and collision-component effects through controlled ablations and validated 1,920 directional task-wrench capacity calculations against independent Cartesian-force linear programs. In a common GraspIt! workload, median reset-close-score-state cycle latency through direct Python was 10.7% lower than through persistent ROS 1 services. An executable export-plan-reconstruct workflow and grasp examples on six hand models demonstrate how the contact, score, and joint representations support numerical analysis and command reconstruction.

</details>


### [12] [DMPT-Swarm: A Directional Mass-Preserving Transform for Decentralized Swarm Navigation in Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28999)
*Jacob Lee,Samuel Sutphin,Haroon Malik,Chang Liu,Pingping Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化微观群导航框架，利用GMM表示宏观轨迹，并通过仿射传输与DMPT生成避障参考位置，结合局部APF实现个体导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍环境中实现无需智能体间通信的去中心化群导航，降低通信与计算负担，同时保持避障能力。

Method: 采用Wasserstein-2度量下多元正态分布间的仿射传输生成参考位置，提出方向性保质量变换（DMPT）在保留方向结构的同时修改径向传输以融入障碍影响，再基于变换后的参考位置使用局部人工势场（APF）控制器进行导航，各智能体独立运行。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统基于APF的方法相比，该方法在效率和避障性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无通信条件下实现高效的障碍环境群导航，验证了仿射传输与DMPT结合局部APF的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized microscopic control framework for swarm navigation in obstacle environments, given a precomputed macroscopic trajectory represented by Gaussian mixture models (GMMs). To generate obstacle-aware reference locations without inter-agent communication, we first employ affine transport between multivariate normal (MVN) distributions under the Wasserstein-2 metric. We then propose a directional mass-preserving transform (DMPT) to incorporate obstacle effects by modifying radial transport while preserving directional structure. Based on the transformed references, a local artificial potential field (APF)-based controller is applied for navigation. Each agent operates independently, reducing communication and computational burden. Simulation results demonstrate improved efficiency and obstacle avoidance compared to conventional APF-based methods

</details>


### [13] [World Models for Ocean Engineering: A Critical Review of Maritime Autonomy, Adaptive Latent Prediction, Digital Twins, and Biodiversity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28978)
*Basma Dokkara,Esteban W. G. Cluab,Luiz M. G. Goncalves,Paolo Spagnolo,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述对海洋工程中的世界模型（WMs）进行功能分类，重点分析JEPA与AdaJEPA在模型预测控制中的测试时自适应，并区分已验证证据与前瞻应用，指出基准、不确定性校准与安全自适应等空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋机器人面临部分可观测性、非平稳动力学与稀疏通信，促使学习紧凑预测状态的世界模型（WMs）以支持规划。

Method: 开展功能性综述，构建海洋工程WMs分类体系，聚焦联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）及AdaJEPA在模型预测控制中的测试时自适应，并将其映射到导航、自适应采样、数字孪生与生物多样性监测。

Result: 综合识别出关键空白：基准测试、不确定性校准与安全自适应，并提出通过仿真与现场任务验证的物理信息驱动、部署自适应海洋WMs研究议程。

Conclusion: 需建立物理信息驱动且部署自适应的海洋世界模型，并通过仿真与实地任务验证，以弥补基准、校准与安全自适应方面的不足。

Abstract: Marine robots face partial observability, non-stationary dynamics, and sparse communication, motivating WMs that learn compact predictive states for planning. This review develops a functional taxonomy of WMs for ocean engineering, focusing on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) and AdaJEPA's test-time adaptation within model predictive control, mapping them to navigation, adaptive sampling, digital twins, and biodiversity monitoring, and separating demonstrated evidence from prospective applications. The synthesis identifies key Gaps: benchmarks, uncertainty calibration, safe adaptation and proposes a research agenda for physics-informed, deployment-adaptive marine WMs validated through simulation and field missions.

</details>


### [14] [ACAP: Asynchronous Coding Agents for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31205)
*Zexin He,Rui Li,Yushi Lan,Chengkai Jin,Zhi Hou,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步编码代理框架ACAP，将单机器人组织为多个持久代理团队，通过超代理协调依赖与动态控制分配，在真实世界实验中显著降低任务耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一编码代理控制整个机器人会延迟本可独立进行的工作，而不同机器人部件间存在物理依赖，需要协调。

Method: ACAP将机器人组织为持久代理团队，每个成员负责可配置控制组（如臂+夹爪、躯干+底盘）进行异步推理与执行；引入超代理负责任务分配、依赖协调及动态分配主/成员代理间的控制权。

Result: 在25种任务/平台设置的72次真实世界rollout中，ACAP相比单代理Direct Policy将任务平衡时间成本降低28.2%，在完全并行、并行-顺序和协作任务上均有提升；在所有8种协作设置中平均时间成本低于Fixed Delegation；额外90次rollout研究显示在全部6项任务上30分钟上限平均时间成本低于Direct；并演示了使用相同成员接口进行底盘和躯干控制的移动操作。

Conclusion: ACAP通过异步多代理团队与超代理协调机制，在保持紧耦合操作能力的同时实现独立工作并发推进，有效降低真实机器人任务时间成本，并发布框架与真实世界rollout数据集支持编码代理在物理世界中的推理与行动研究。

Abstract: Coding agents can construct and revise robot behavior during execution, but using one agent for the whole robot can delay otherwise independent work. We introduce Asynchronous Coding Agents as Policy (ACAP), an asynchronous framework that organizes one robot as a team of persistent agents. Each member reasons and acts for a configurable control group, such as an arm with its gripper or the torso and chassis, enabling asynchronous reasoning and execution. However, different robot components often have physical dependencies that require coordination. To address this challenge, ACAP introduces a hyper-agent that assigns tasks, coordinates dependencies, and dynamically allocates control between the lead and member agents. This allows independent work to progress concurrently while supporting tightly coupled manipulation when necessary. Across 72 real-world rollouts covering 25 task/platform settings, ACAP reduces task-balanced time cost by 28.2% compared with a single-agent Direct Policy, with improvements across Fully Parallel, Parallel-Sequential, and Collaborative tasks. Across all eight Collaborative settings, ACAP also achieves lower mean time cost than Fixed Delegation, where only member agents can control their assigned groups. An additional 90-rollout study shows lower mean 30-minute-capped time cost than Direct on all six tasks. We further demonstrate mobile manipulation using the same member interface for chassis and torso control. We present the framework and a real-world rollout dataset containing observations, generated programs, actions, and feedback to support research on how coding agents reason and act in the physical world.

</details>


### [15] [Embo-Harness: Towards Understanding Frontier-Model-as-Policy Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31201)
*Xiao-Ming Wu,Kang Liao,Yuexiao Ma,Taolin Zhang,Mingqian Zhou,Nuo Chen,Ziming Li,Yihang Luo,Rui Li,Zhonghua Wu,Tianyu Xie,Xiawu Zheng,Zhongang Cai,Zhi Hou,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embo-Harness框架，系统研究前沿多模态模型作为机器人策略的闭环系统设计，在RoboLab-120上达到83.3%成功率，并给出实用配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态模型能直接从观测和指令生成机器人动作，但作为策略使用时闭环系统的设计原则及性能来源尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 围绕机器人智能循环的四个组成部分（提示、规划、动作执行、工具使用）进行实验，提炼出六条经验并整合为实用配方，强调利用模型原生视觉推理与工具调用能力，而非手工规划或动作先验。

Result: 在RoboLab-120上成功率达83.3%，比最强基线高26.2个百分点；在四项真实操作任务的20次零样本试验中成功15次；受控干预表明模型更依赖准确的空间目标估计，而非显式低层关节或轨迹生成。

Conclusion: 结果为构建强力的前沿模型即策略闭环系统提供了实用配方，并更清晰地阐明了驱动其性能的关键能力。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models exhibit extraordinary generalization in generating robot actions from observations and instructions, enabling a new paradigm in which they serve directly as policies within closed-loop robotic harnesses. However, the design principles of such harnesses, and the capabilities underlying their strong performance, remain unclear. We present Embo-Harness, a framework for systematically studying frontier-model-as-policy harnesses. We investigate four components of the robotic agentic loop, namely, prompting, planning, action execution, and tool use, and distill six empirical findings into a practical recipe. This recipe prioritizes interfaces that exploit the model's native visual reasoning and tool-use capabilities over handcrafted plans, skills, or action priors, and identifies effective choices for action representation, chunking, reasoning effort, visual observations, short multi-action sequences, and policy refinement. On RoboLab-120, Embo-Harness achieves an 83.3\% success rate, outperforming the strongest reported baseline by 26.2 percentage points. It also succeeds in 15 of 20 zero-shot trials across four real-world manipulation tasks. Beyond performance, controlled interventions indicate that the studied model depends more strongly on accurate spatial target estimation than on explicit low-level joint or trajectory generation. These results provide both a practical recipe for building strong frontier-model-as-policy harnesses and a clearer account of the capabilities that drive their performance.

</details>


### [16] [VITA-WAM: Manifold-Aligned World-Action Modeling for Real-Time Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31195)
*Boqi Zhao,Dechen Gao,Hanchu Zhou,Hang Wang,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VITA-WAM通过流形对齐的流匹配，将潜在观测表示直接映射为潜在动作，实现单步ODE生成动作，推理延迟55.67ms，比FastWAM快2.10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖大生成模型和迭代采样，动作生成延迟高，主要由于将无信息高斯噪声传输为精确动作需多步ODE，以及通过昂贵交叉注意力反复注入任务上下文，限制实时部署。

Method: 提出VITA-WAM架构和VITA流匹配，直接传输包含视觉、语言和本体感受信息的潜在观测表示到潜在动作；采用流潜在对齐（FLA）机制对齐观测与动作流形，实现少步ODE精确生成，并消除重复去噪步骤中的观测条件注意力。

Result: 单步ODE下，VITA-WAM在LIBERO-Plus上比10步FastWAM高21.47个百分点，在LIBERO上高1.65个百分点，无需蒸馏，推理延迟55.67ms，比FastWAM加速2.10倍。

Conclusion: 联合学习并对齐源流形与动作流形可加速动作生成，VITA-WAM在保持高性能的同时显著提升推理效率，适合实时部署。

Abstract: World action model (WAM) is a promising paradigm for visuomotor control by jointly modeling world dynamics and actions. Notably, existing WAMs often rely on large generative models and iterative sampling which incur substantial latency for action generation, limiting real-time deployment. In particular, there are two root causes of inefficiency in conventional flow matching or diffusion based action models in WAMs: 1) transporting uninformative Gaussian noise into precise actions necessitating multiple ODE steps, and 2) repeatedly injecting task context through expensive cross-attention modules. Our key insight is that jointly learning and aligning the source and action manifolds streamlines the transport between them, substantially accelerating action generation. Building on this insight, we introduce VITA-WAM, a WAM architecture with manifold-aligned flow-matching (VITA flow matching) that directly transports latent observation representations containing visual, language, and proprioceptive information into latent actions. More specifically, a flow latent alignment (FLA) mechanism is employed to facilitate manifold alignment between latent observation and action representations, enabling accurate generation with few ODE steps. The resulting architecture also eliminates costly attention for observation conditioning during repeated denoising steps. Notably, with a single ODE step, VITA-WAM outperforms 10-step FastWAM by 21.47 pp on LIBERO-Plus and by 1.65 pp on LIBERO, achieving these gains through the FLA objective without distillation, while inferring at 55.67~ms latency, a $2.10\times$ speedup compared to FastWAM.

</details>


### [17] [EmboEagle: An Efficient Agentic Embodied Framework for Long-horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31173)
*Fan Xu,Zhongtao Wang,Hao Wu,Yitong Zhu,Wenzhuo Ma,Long Li,Zhuohao Li,Zhisheng Chen,Rui Su,Kun Wang,Xian Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmboEagle是一个闭环智能体框架，统一了子任务条件下的WAM控制、潜在空间自适应监督和记忆引导的几何恢复，在长视界机器人操作中显著提升成功率并降低VLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界机器人操作需要跨相互依赖阶段可靠执行，但独立WAM会累积物理误差，而无状态VLM监督存在分块延迟高且缺乏3D空间持久性的问题。

Method: 采用解耦两阶段流匹配适配序列WAM以保留跨阶段视觉历史并校准子任务边界动作监督；将教师干预决策蒸馏为轻量路由门，仅基于WAM动作专家的块前后潜在转移和子任务嵌入；VLM调用时解耦诊断状态与离散路由动作，基于结构化记忆执行参数化恢复原语并进行强制修复后验证。

Result: 在RMBench和LIBERO-Mem上平均成功率分别达91.3%和90.6%，超过最强基线8.0和9.8个百分点；相同执行器权重下真实世界成功率优于VoLoAgent；自适应门控将VLM调用减少74.2%（成功率91.3% vs. 93.7%）。

Conclusion: EmboEagle通过闭环智能体设计有效解决了长视界操作中的误差累积和VLM监督效率问题，在保持高成功率的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation demands reliable execution across interdependent stages, yet standalone World-Action Models (WAMs) suffer from compounding physical errors, while stateless Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervision incurs prohibitive chunkwise latency and lacks 3D spatial persistence. We present EmboEagle, a closed-loop agentic framework that unifies subtask-conditioned WAM control, latent-space adaptive supervision, and memory-guided geometric recovery. First, we adapt sequential WAMs via decoupled two-stage flow matching that preserves cross-stage visual history while calibrating truncated action supervision at subtask boundaries. Second, without an auxiliary vision encoder, we distill teacher intervention decisions into a lightweight routing gate driven solely by the WAM Action Expert's pre- and post-chunk latent transitions and subtask embeddings. Third, upon invocation, the VLM decouples diagnostic status from discrete routing actions and executes parameterized recovery primitives grounded in structured memory (initial references, active grasp bindings, and tool traces), followed by mandatory post-repair verification. Across RMBench and LIBERO-Mem, EmboEagle achieves 91.3\% and 90.6\% average success, surpassing the strongest baselines by 8.0 and 9.8 percentage points, while boosting real-world success over VoLoAgent under identical executor weights. Moreover, adaptive gating cuts VLM calls by 74.2\% versus chunkwise monitoring (91.3\% vs. 93.7\% success).

</details>


### [18] [WeaveVLA: 3D Relational Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31126)
*Zien Wang,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Nan Cheng,Xin Xia,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WeaveVLA通过显式建模任务相关相对3D关系并在观测间维护关系上下文来增强VLA动作生成，在仿真和真实任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型对任务相关3D关系及其在操作过程中的演化理解主要是隐式的，缺乏显式建模与跨观测的空间上下文维护。

Method: 采用任务条件稀疏图神经网络编码物体、夹爪与邻近参考物间的相对3D关系；用成对循环边状态跨观测整合几何证据；用学习查询readout将图表示压缩为关系token，与原始视觉语言上下文共同条件化动作专家；并加入辅助几何重建目标以保持空间结构。在StarVLA-GR00T和π0.5上实例化。

Result: 两个骨干在LIBERO上平均成功率超98%；LIBERO-Plus上分别从74.9%提升至82.0%、从85.7%提升至87.8%；CALVIN D→D亦有提升；三个真实任务中StarVLA-GR00T变体标准条件从55.6%提升至70.0%，OOD扰动下从33.3%提升至53.3%，均超过π0.5基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模相对3D关系并维护跨观测关系上下文能有效提升VLA模型的操作成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models leverage pretrained vision-language representations for instruction-conditioned robot control, yet their understanding of task-relevant 3D relations and how these relations evolve during manipulation remains largely implicit. To address this challenge, we introduce \textbf{WeaveVLA}, a framework that enhances VLA action generation by explicitly modeling task-relevant relative 3D relations and maintaining relational context across observations. WeaveVLA employs a task-conditioned sparse graph neural network to encode relative 3D relations among objects, the gripper, and nearby references. Pair-specific recurrent edge states integrate geometric evidence across observations to maintain evolving spatial context. A learned-query readout summarizes the updated graph representations into compact relational tokens, which condition the action expert alongside the original vision-language context. An auxiliary geometry reconstruction objective further encourages spatial structure preservation. We instantiate WeaveVLA on StarVLA-GR00T and $\pi_{0.5}$, achieving over 98\% average success on LIBERO with both backbones. On LIBERO-Plus, WeaveVLA improves success from 74.9\% to 82.0\% and from 85.7\% to 87.8\%, respectively, with additional improvements on CALVIN D$\rightarrow$D. On three real-world tasks, the StarVLA-GR00T-based variant improves average success from 55.6\% to 70.0\% under standard conditions and from 33.3\% to 53.3\% under OOD perturbations, surpassing the $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline in both settings.

</details>


### [19] [RUIBench: What Makes a Good Robot-Use Interface?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31115)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Zechen Bai,Wenzheng Zeng,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Xiaokang Liu,Ke Sun,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出机器人使用界面(RUI)是影响具身智能体可靠执行的关键因素，并推出RUIBench基准与RUIKit框架系统研究其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型能推理复杂物理任务，但将其智能可靠地转化为机器人执行仍具挑战，作者认为被忽视的关键因素是机器人使用界面(RUI)，它决定智能体如何表达与执行物理动作。

Method: 构建RUIBench基准，涵盖五大套件共30个操作任务（世界知识、上下文学习、发现、动态交互、接触丰富操作）；开发RUIKit统一框架，将六种动作抽象与两种交互形式交叉，在共享感知与控制下形成13种界面。

Result: 仅改变RUI即可使前沿智能体成功率从45%升至81%，微调4B模型从13%升至47%；有效界面减少不必要决策并保留关键反馈，其收益受智能体行为与物理不确定性影响；同时发现验证缺口，智能体常在明显失败时宣称成功。

Conclusion: RUI设计是具身智能体可靠性的关键因素，且需超越单步动作的任务级验证来弥补验证缺口。

Abstract: Foundation models can reason about complex physical tasks, yet translating this intelligence into reliable robot execution remains challenging. We argue that a critical, often overlooked factor is the robot-use interface (RUI), which determines how agents express and execute physical actions. To systematically study its impact, we introduce RUIBench, comprising 30 manipulation tasks across five suites spanning world knowledge, in-context learning, discovery, dynamic interaction, and contact-rich manipulation. We further develop RUIKit, a unified framework that crosses six action abstractions with two interaction forms, yielding 13 interfaces under shared perception and control. In simulation, changing only the RUI shifts a frontier agent's success rate from 45% to 81%, and that of a fine-tuned 4B model from 13% to 47%. Our analysis reveals that effective interfaces reduce unnecessary decisions while preserving essential feedback, with their benefits shaped by agent behavior and physical uncertainty. We further uncover a verification gap, where agents frequently declare success despite observable failures, revealing the need for task-level verification beyond individual actions. Together, these findings establish RUI design as a critical factor in embodied agent reliability.

</details>


### [20] [NoveltyGate: Novelty, Not Error, Decides Which VLA Prefix Tokens to Compute Exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31099)
*Lemeng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NoveltyGate是一种免训练的前缀token路由方法，依据token输入是否超出校准阶段所见的子空间（新颖性检验）而非近似误差，将每层前缀token路由至低秩MLP或原始MLP，在LIBERO上实现最高1.61倍加速并保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA模型（如π0.5）每个控制步都用20亿参数骨干重编码图像和指令，前缀的前馈层占大部分计算。直观思路是仅对近似误差大的token精确计算，但作者发现这一直觉错误：即便验证器知道每个token的精确误差，按误差选择token的动作偏差仍比简单的新颖性检验大1.3–1.7倍。

Method: 提出NoveltyGate：每层、在任何矩阵乘法之前对每个前缀token做一次新颖性检验，判断其输入是否落在校准阶段所见子空间之外，并据此路由到低秩MLP或原始MLP。每层仅决策一次而非每个矩阵乘一次，避免搬运宽中间张量并保持形状静态，使整个前缀可作为单个CUDA图运行。

Result: 在LIBERO上，NoveltyGate将π0.5前缀加速最高1.61倍；在同等计算量下，其与原始策略的偏差比通道剪枝和低秩压缩小2.7–2.9倍，并保持闭环任务成功率。

Conclusion: 误差与依赖之间存在错位（误差–依赖差距）：大误差集中在策略依赖较少的token（如第三人称相机），而新颖性检验偏向动作高度依赖的手腕相机视图。基于新颖性而非误差的路由能更有效节省前缀计算并保持策略行为。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_{0.5}$ re-encode the camera images and the instruction with a 2B-parameter backbone at every control step, and the feed-forward layers of this prefix account for most of its compute. A natural way to save this compute is to send most tokens through a low-rank MLP and compute exactly only the tokens whose approximation error is large. We find that this intuition is wrong. Even a verifier that knows every token's exact error picks the wrong tokens, producing 1.3--1.7$\times$ larger action deviation than a simple test of whether a token's input is novel, that is, whether it falls outside the subspace seen during calibration. Large errors concentrate in tokens the policy relies on little, such as the third-person camera, while the novelty test favors the wrist camera, a view the action depends on heavily. We call this the error--reliance gap. Building on it, we propose NoveltyGate, a training-free method that routes each prefix token, once per layer and before any multiplication, to either a low-rank or the original MLP. Deciding once per layer instead of once per matrix product avoids moving wide intermediate tensors and keeps every shape static, so the whole prefix runs as a single CUDA graph. On LIBERO, NoveltyGate accelerates the $\pi_{0.5}$ prefix by up to 1.61$\times$, stays 2.7--2.9$\times$ closer to the original policy than channel pruning and low-rank compression at equal compute, and preserves closed-loop task success.

</details>


### [21] [Real2Worlds2Real: Scaling Self-Improving Agentic Real2Sim2Real for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31098)
*Jinzhong Gu,Jiakai Chen,Xinyi Cao,Yan Shen,Hangke Sui,Shutian Zheng,Xiang Xiao,Haoran Lu,Fan Xu,Lu Shi,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Wei Zhan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Real2Worlds2Real自改进智能体框架，将可扩展仿真世界构建与策略开发结合，实现从有限真实观测到仿真中策略学习并迁移回真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人操作数据采集成本高，Real2Sim2Real虽具潜力但难以跨多样任务和物理交互扩展。

Method: 智能体利用工具编排从有限视觉观测和任务描述构建数字孪生，并扩展为多样可执行数字表亲；通过视觉与执行反馈自适应选择工具、修正几何与物理；再协调数据生成、策略学习与优化，并将验证流程蒸馏为可复用技能。

Result: 实验表明世界保真度提升、真实世界策略迁移成功，世界多样性和交互数据量增加带来性能增益，技能复用提升建世界效率与长时任务成功率。

Conclusion: 该框架可自我改进并有效扩展Real2Sim2Real范式，实现仿真世界构建与策略开发的协同与技能复用。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation learning remains challenging due to the high cost of collecting real-world interaction data. Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Real2Sim2Real) offers a promising route by enabling policy learning in simulation grounded in real-world observations, yet scaling this paradigm across diverse tasks and physical interactions remains difficult. We introduce Real2Worlds2Real, a self-improving agentic framework that integrates scalable simulation-world construction with policy development. Given limited visual observations and task descriptions, its tool-orchestration harness constructs faithful digital twins and expands them into diverse, physically executable digital cousins. The agent adaptively selects tools and revisits construction decisions, using visual and execution feedback to refine geometry and physical behavior. Building on these worlds, the agent orchestrates policy development by coordinating data generation, policy learning, and refinement based on task requirements and simulation rollout feedback. Across both stages, the agent distills validated procedures into reusable skills that guide subsequent tasks. Experiments demonstrate improved world fidelity and successful real-world policy transfer, with performance gains from increasing world diversity and interaction-data volume. Skill reuse further improves world-building efficiency and long-horizon task success.

</details>


### [22] [Wrist-Camera Video Diffusion as Generalizable Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31079)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yongwei Chen,Yishan Yang,Zexin He,Xingang Pan,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WVP是一个闭环操作框架，通过预测手腕相机视角的视频并利用几何估计恢复相机轨迹，从而得到末端执行器位姿，实现跨任务和跨机器人的泛化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略需要执行新任务、适应陌生场景并跨机器人迁移技能，现有方法难以同时具备广泛任务知识和跨 embodiment 的运动几何复用。

Method: 以手腕相机轨迹表示机器人运动，通过固定手眼变换映射为末端位姿；结合视频生成器预测未来手腕视角，并用从预训练3D模型适配的位姿估计器恢复相机轨迹；通过模型缝合将生成器潜变量直接映射到估计器中间特征空间，跳过RGB解码。

Result: 位姿估计器无需微调即可迁移到Franka Panda、PiPER-X和Galaxea R1 Pro；在桌面和洗衣房场景中，对精确抓取、插入及可变形物体操作等任务，零样本分布外评估成功率超过50%。

Conclusion: 将任务知识扩展（通过广泛人类和机器人视频数据）与几何运动估计复用解耦，使WVP能跨任务和跨机器人泛化，实现闭环操作。

Abstract: A generalist manipulation policy should perform new tasks, operate in unfamiliar scenes, and transfer skills across robot embodiments. We present Wrist Video Policy (WVP), a closed-loop manipulation framework that learns how manipulation unfolds through video prediction and recovers executable motion through geometric estimation. Our key idea is to represent robot motion through wrist-camera trajectories, which determine the corresponding end-effector poses through a fixed hand-eye transformation. We pair a video generator that predicts future wrist views with a pose estimator adapted from a pretrained 3D model to recover the corresponding camera trajectories. Model stitching maps the generator's predicted latents directly into the estimator's intermediate feature space, bypassing RGB decoding. This decomposition allows task knowledge to expand through broad human and robot video data while reusing geometric motion estimation across tasks and embodiments. The resulting pose estimator transfers across Franka Panda, PiPER-X, and Galaxea R1 Pro without further fine-tuning. Across tabletop and laundry-room settings, WVP generalizes to tasks ranging from precise grasping and inserting to handling deformable objects, achieving a success rate of over 50% in zero-shot out-of-distribution evaluations. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [Completing analytical conversion scheme among DH, Hayati, CPC and POE models for manipulator calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31054)
*Oleg Yu. Sumenkov,Danil D. Kulminskiy,Sergei V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在Mooring校准框架下，研究运动学模型解析转换算法对校准性能的影响，并基于李群理论构建了POE-HAY和HAY-CPC之间的直接解析转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中多数运动学模型间的转换关系已建立，但POE与HAY、CPC与HAY之间缺乏直接解析转换，只能通过POE-DH-HAY等顺序组合实现，存在若干局限，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑DH、HAY、CPC和POE四种模型，系统分析现有转换关系；基于李群理论，通过引理和命题形式化建立POE-HAY与HAY-CPC的构造性解析转换算法；用RRRP SCARA机器人和RRHHHH Puma 560机械臂进行说明，并比较直接POE-HAY转换与顺序POE-DH-HAY方法及HAY-CPC方法；最后在ABB IRB 1600机械臂上用API Radian EMSD3激光跟踪仪进行开环运动学校准实验。

Result: 直接POE-HAY转换在多项相关指标上优于顺序POE-DH-HAY方法及HAY-CPC方法；校准实验结果表明，合理的模型选择结合完整的解析转换方案可直接提高校准精度。

Conclusion: 完整的解析转换框架具有实际意义，正确的模型选择与完整的解析转换方案相结合能够直接提升运动学校准精度。

Abstract: Building on Mooring's decomposition of the calibration procedure into four stages - modeling, measurement, identification, and correction - this paper investigates the role of analytical kinematic model conversion algorithms within the calibration framework and their potential to improve calibration performance. Four prominent and widely cited kinematic models: Denavit-Hartenberg (DH), Hayati (HAY), Complete and Parametrically Continuous (CPC), and Product of Exponentials (POE), - are considered. A systematic analysis of the existing conversion relationships reveals that, although most conversions among these models have already been established in the literature, a direct analytical conversion between POE and HAY, and CPC and HAY remains unavailable. Relying instead on a sequential combination of the existing POE-DH and DH-HAY conversions, for example, introduces several limitations. To address this gap, we develop a constructive analytical conversion between POE-HAY and HAY-CPC models based on Lie group theory, formally established through a set of lemmas and propositions. The proposed algorithms are illustrated using an RRRP SCARA robot and an RRHHHH Puma 560 manipulator. The direct POE-HAY conversion is compared with the sequential POE-DH-HAY approach, as well as HAY-CPC in terms of several relevant criteria, demonstrating the advantages of the proposed solution. Finally, we demonstrate the practical significance of the completed analytical conversion framework in an open-loop kinematic calibration experiment on an ABB IRB 1600 manipulator, using an API Radian EMSD3 Laser Tracker for measurements. The experimental results demonstrate that proper model selection combined with completed analytical conversion scheme directly improves calibration accuracy.

</details>


### [24] [ReWeave: Emergent Physical Task Topologies for Embodied Harness Optimization and Cross-Task Meta-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31053)
*Yicheng Xu,Wencan Jiang,Zihao Zhu,Keru Zhou,Chang Zhou,Li Liu,Maoqing Yao,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReWeave框架在不更新策略权重的情况下，联合优化具身智能体的执行框架与涌现的物理任务拓扑，以提升长时程任务的成功率和跨任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务存在只有通过交互才能明确的物理依赖关系，粗略的进度反馈无法识别哪些物理前提条件成立，导致搜索缺乏引导；任务特定的优化容易过拟合单个场景，使跨任务迁移脆弱。

Method: ReWeave保持执行策略和感知冻结，协同优化耦合的上下文、工作流和控制循环；随着新证据修正任务拓扑，将过往试验重新投影到更新后的任务理解上，揭示保留的进展、回退和瓶颈，以指导父代选择和搜索宽度；通过将验证过的局部机制与未满足的前提条件匹配，将72个专家单元整合为44个可复用组。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上将保留成功率从89.00%提升至98.75%，在RoboDojo上从25.50%提升至58.25%；在159个失败的RoboDojo开发试验中89%发现了保留进展；在八个未见过的LIBERO-PRO任务上，共享框架达到51.3%成功率（直接蒸馏为41.3%），持续学习后升至75.0%。

Conclusion: ReWeave通过联合优化执行框架与任务拓扑，实现了无需更新策略权重的持续学习，显著提升长时程具身任务的在线性能和跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks involve physical dependencies that may only become clear through interaction. As task understanding deepens, past trials can reveal new information for adaptation and transfer. However, coarse progress feedback cannot identify which physical preconditions held, leaving search unguided, while task-specific optimization easily overfits to individual scenes, making cross-task transfer brittle. We introduce ReWeave, a framework that jointly optimizes an embodied agent harness and an emergent physical task topology from interaction without updating policy weights. Keeping execution policies and perception frozen, ReWeave co-optimizes coupled context, workflows, and control loops. As new evidence revises the topology, ReWeave reprojects past trials onto the updated task understanding, exposing retained progress, regressions, and bottlenecks that guide parent selection and search width. Across tasks, matching verified local mechanisms to unmet preconditions consolidates 72 specialist units into 44 reusable groups ($0.34\times$ full concatenation) within a compact shared harness capable of continual learning. ReWeave improves held-out success from 89.00% to 98.75% on LIBERO-PRO and from 25.50% to 58.25% on RoboDojo, while uncovering retained progress in 89% of 159 failed RoboDojo development trials and translating stronger offline reasoning into higher online performance. On eight unseen LIBERO-PRO tasks, the shared harness reaches 51.3% success versus 41.3% for direct distillation, rising to 75.0% after continual learning.

</details>


### [25] [SurgeCount: A Protocol-Guided Composition Harness for Bimanual Surgical Sponge Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31037)
*Sung Eun Kim,Xiaoman Zhang,Xucheng Wang,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgeCount 通过将学习到的子技能与书面程序协议结合，实现可审计的机器人手术海绵计数，在真实机器人试验中显著优于端到端策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留海绵会造成可预防伤害，手术团队闭合前需清点所有海绵；需要一种可审计、能处理可变形海绵堆叠并保持步骤顺序的机器人计数系统。

Method: 训练一个子任务条件策略覆盖11个短技能并学习技能结束边界；在每个边界处，有状态视觉语言模型根据操作者编写的步骤顺序、视觉验证与恢复规范选择下一技能，形成对子任务的时间承诺，并通过重组已有技能恢复失败。

Result: 100次真实机器人试验（20个匹配布局）中，SurgeCount 填充60个有序槽位中的47个、完成20个流程中的12个；最强端到端策略为27/60和3/20；将详细协议提示替换为最小提示后降至29/60和5/20。

Conclusion: 长时程性能不仅取决于视觉运动能力，还取决于学习技能如何被承诺、验证和组合；该桌面概念验证支持在临床代表性环境中进一步评估协议引导的机器人海绵计数。

Abstract: We present SurgeCount, a system for auditable robotic surgical sponge counting that composes learned skills under a written procedural protocol. Retained sponges cause preventable harm, and surgical teams count every sponge before closure to reduce that risk. The robot repeatedly isolates one deformable sponge from a tangled pile and places it in the next numbered counting-tray slot, requiring handling of occluded grasp threads while preserving step order. We train one subtask-conditioned policy on eleven short skills and learn when each skill ends. At each learned boundary, a stateful vision-language model selects the next skill from an operator-authored specification of step order, visual verification, and recovery. This creates temporal commitment to each subtask and enables failures to be recovered by recomposing existing skills rather than using a dedicated recovery policy. We evaluate five methods across 100 real-robot trials using 20 matched layouts. SurgeCount fills 47 of 60 ordered slots and completes 12 of 20 procedures, compared with 27 of 60 and 3 of 20 for the strongest of four end-to-end policies trained on the same data. Replacing the detailed protocol prompt with a minimal prompt reduces performance to 29 of 60 slots and 5 of 20 completed procedures. These results show that long-horizon performance depends not only on visuomotor capacity, but also on how learned skills are committed, verified, and composed. This tabletop proof of concept motivates further evaluation of protocol-guided robotic sponge counting in clinically representative settings.

</details>


### [26] [RoboUniverse-10K: Toward the Scaling Era of Agentic Robotics via Real-to-Sim Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31002)
*Jinkun Liu,Yingze Wang,Situ Wang,Deyao Hong,Mingju Gao,Wenyi Li,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Xiangyang Wang,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Boyuan Chen,Yue Chen,Cheng Qian,Tianxing Chen,Huichi Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Hao Tang,Hao Zhou,Ya-Qin Zhang,Calvin Xiao,Tiga Altman,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboUniverse-10K大规模可重置物理环境数据集及零样本基准，揭示开源模型与前沿模型在机器人编程智能体任务上的巨大差距，并通过监督微调训练RoboUniverse-35B-A3B显著提升零样本成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域缺乏像软件工程那样大规模可执行环境支持后训练，现有基准规模小、依赖人工且评估限于分布内，限制了Agentic Robotics发展。

Method: 通过自动化Real2Sim与质量保证流程从真实视频构建10,807个交互式、可重置、可验证的物理环境，并留出500个专家验证的零样本基准RoboUniverse-Verified；评估11个闭源与开源模型；用GPT-6 Astra收集约1.7万多轮、0.79B token轨迹，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B进行全参数监督微调得到RoboUniverse-35B-A3B。

Result: 前沿模型成功率达85.8%，开源基线仅2.6%至10.0%，瓶颈在于低级抓取、可变形物体操作和接触后物理控制；微调后在RoboUniverse-Verified上零样本成功率从0.8%提升至21.2%，并在LIBERO-MAX、RoboCasa、RoboTwin 2.0及真实Franka机械臂上实现零样本任务完成。

Conclusion: 开源RL就绪环境与模型，为Agentic Robotics大规模强化学习奠定基础。

Abstract: Agentic Robotics, in which foundation models act as coding agents to control robots through executable programs, has emerged as a compelling paradigm for embodied intelligence. Yet unlike software engineering, where massive executable environments power post-training, robotics remains constrained by small, manual benchmarks and in-distribution evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboUniverse-10K, 10,807 interactive, resettable, and verifiable physics environments built from real-world videos via an automated Real2Sim and quality assurance pipeline. Each runs in an isolated, Harbor-compatible harness that hides privileged states and grading logic to mirror real-world sensing. From the same pipeline, we hold out 500 unseen tasks to establish RoboUniverse-Verified, an expert-verified zero-shot benchmark. Evaluating 11 closed- and open-source models reveals a huge gap: frontier models reach 85.8\% success, while open-source baselines lag at 2.6\% to 10.0\%, bottlenecked by low-level grasping, deformable-object manipulation, and post-contact physical control. Finally, we collect about 17K multi-turn trajectories totaling 0.79B tokens with GPT-6 Astra and perform full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, yielding RoboUniverse-35B-A3B. Our model achieves substantial zero-shot gains on RoboUniverse-Verified, lifting the success rate from 0.8\% to 21.2\%, while achieving zero-shot task completions across LIBERO-MAX, RoboCasa, RoboTwin 2.0, and a real Franka arm. We open-source the RL-ready environments and model to lay the foundation for large-scale reinforcement learning in Agentic Robotics.

</details>


### [27] [Generalizing Agentic Robotics via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30997)
*Xinxin Liu,Anh Dao,Wenrui Bao,Rafid Ahmed,Bingxin Xu,Ming Li,Chen Chen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Robo-MOPD，用多教师在线策略蒸馏将多个领域专家规划器整合为单一通用智能体机器人规划器，性能超过权重合并、离线SFT和多领域在线RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体机器人规划器要么依赖昂贵的专有API模型，要么需为每个领域单独用RL训练专家且工具不兼容，能否用MOPD构建通用规划器尚不清楚。

Method: 在仿真闭环rollout中，以匹配的冻结领域专家提供token级监督，训练单个9B学生模型，并与权重合并、离线专家SFT、多领域在线RL对比。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和RoboCasa365上单个9B Robo-MOPD分别达72.9%和44.8%成功率（平均58.9%），平均成功率超过所有基线及对应9B专家；LIBERO-10长程任务上比多领域在线RL高13.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 多教师在线策略蒸馏可有效构建通用智能体机器人规划器，在平均性能和长程任务上优于现有整合方法。

Abstract: Building general-purpose robots is a central goal in robotics. Agentic robotics pursues this goal with planners that control robots through tool calls. However, existing methods either rely on proprietary models accessed through APIs, which incur high costs and latency, or post-train a planner with reinforcement learning (RL) in a single domain, requiring a separate specialist for each domain with incompatible tools. In large language model (LLM) post-training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) has been used to consolidate domain specialists into a single generalist. Yet whether MOPD can build a generalist planner for agentic robotics, how well it performs, and how it compares with other consolidation methods remain unexplored. In this paper, we systematically study these questions: we introduce Robo-MOPD, which trains a single student on closed-loop rollouts in simulation under token-level supervision from matching frozen specialists, and compare it with weight merging, off-policy specialist supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and multi-domain online RL. Experiments on LIBERO-PRO and RoboCasa365 show that a single 9B Robo-MOPD planner reaches 72.9% and 44.8% success (58.9% average), surpassing all consolidated baselines in average success and both 9B specialists on their respective benchmarks. Robo-MOPD excels on long-horizon tasks, outperforming multi-domain online RL by 13.0 percentage points on LIBERO-10.

</details>


### [28] [WorldLeague: Benchmarking Physical Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30989)
*Xinyang Zhou,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出WorldLeague基准，用60个模拟机器人任务从轨迹衡量物理目标进展，比较13个AI系统的物理能动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统能操作Blender、玩复杂游戏、用机械臂操作物体，但缺乏统一标准评估其自主实现物理目标的能力范围，需要覆盖感知、记忆、规划与行动的不同任务组合。

Method: 构建WorldLeague基准，包含WorldSeek探索、WorldShape重排、WorldShift适应三类共60个模拟机器人任务；统一控制接口下VLM选择目标与动作，VLA和WAM使用原生控制；按六个能力维度测量交互得分（进展）与物理成功率（达成）。

Result: 13个代表系统中Claude Fable 5.2完成60个目标中的13个，平均交互得分最高（28.58/100），在四个维度领先；GPT-6.1-Sol与GPT-6-Astra在规则遵循任务并列第一；VLM即使最终问题全对，仍有71.8%的回合交互得分为零。

Conclusion: WorldLeague为具身AI、物理AI和智能体AI提供统一比较基础，可衡量从部分进展到完全完成的自主物理目标实现能力。

Abstract: AI systems can now operate Blender, follow instructions in complex video games, and manipulate objects with robot arms. What range of physical goals can today's AI systems achieve autonomously? Answering this question requires testing systems on tasks that demand different combinations of perception, memory, planning, and action. We introduce \textbf{WorldLeague}, a benchmark that compares AI systems on a common set of \textbf{60 simulated robot tasks} by measuring progress toward each task's physical goal from the robot's trajectory. Robots explore scenes to gather evidence in \textbf{WorldSeek}, rearrange objects in \textbf{WorldShape}, and adapt to changes in \textbf{WorldShift}. Tying these goals to a single control interface limits which AI systems can be compared. In WorldLeague, vision-language models (VLMs) choose targets and motions, while vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs) use their native controls. For each of six capability dimensions, we measure how much progress a system makes toward its goals (Interactive Score) and how often it achieves them (physical success rate). Together, these measures form the system's \emph{Physical Agency Profile}. Among 13 representative AI systems, Claude Fable 5.2 achieves 13 of the 60 goals and earns the highest mean Interactive Score (28.58/100). Fable leads in four dimensions, while GPT-6.1-Sol and GPT-6-Astra tie for first on rule-following tasks. Even when VLMs answer every final question correctly, they receive an Interactive Score of zero in 71.8\% of those episodes. WorldLeague gives Embodied AI, Physical AI, and Agentic AI a shared basis for comparing what AI systems achieve autonomously, from partial progress toward a physical goal to full goal completion. Project Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [29] [Foresight Forcing: Learning Spatial Foresight for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30963)
*Mishuo Han,Zhixuan Liang,Zhaoyuan Wan,Wendi Chen,Jiarui Chen,Xinkai Wang,Yue Ma,Yu Zhou,Junting Chen,Yao Mu,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Foresight Forcing（F²），一种利用预训练3D特征空间监督空间转移表示的世界动作模型，提升机器人操作策略的预测与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型预测未来视觉观测，但训练目标未显式建模3D几何转移，导致空间关系变化信息利用不足。

Method: 冻结的3D基础模型从对齐的当前与未来观测中提取块级特征差异，作为密集几何感知监督；将这些目标蒸馏为空间转移查询，查询基于当前观测、语言指令和本体状态逐步精炼，并与当前视觉特征共同生成动作；部署时无需未来观测或3D教师模型。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin2.0上表现强劲，RoboTwin2.0 Full平均成功率95.01%；六个真实操作任务平均得分85.8%。

Conclusion: 显式学习3D空间转移表示可有效增强世界动作模型的操作性能与鲁棒性，且部署时保持轻量。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies can improve action prediction by anticipating how objects move and how their spatial relationships change. World Action Models (WAMs) learn to predict future visual observations, but their training objectives typically leave the underlying 3D geometric transitions implicit. To alleviate this problem, we introduce Foresight Forcing ($\mathcal{F}^{2}$), a WAM that learns a spatial transition representation from changes in a pretrained 3D feature space. During training, a frozen 3D foundation model extracts patch-wise feature differences from aligned current and future observations that provide dense supervision for geometry-aware feature transitions. We distill these targets into spatial transition queries, which are progressively refined from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioceptive state, and use them alongside current visual features to generate actions. At deployment, the policy predicts the queries without future observations or the 3D teacher. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin2.0 demonstrate strong manipulation performance and improved robustness, including 95.01% average success on RoboTwin2.0 Full. On six real-world manipulation tasks, $\mathcal{F}^{2}$ achieves a mean task score of 85.8%.

</details>


### [30] [SayDo: Benchmarking the Safety of Frontier Models from Chat to Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30957)
*Benjamin Alt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SayDo基准，发现前沿视觉语言模型作为机器人控制器时，在具身场景中的危险行为率远高于纯文本聊天（从14%升至47%），文本安全评估无法预测其机器人控制时的安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿VLM越来越多被用作高层机器人控制器，但其安全性几乎只在文本环境中评估，缺乏对具身情境下危害倾向的系统测评。

Method: 构建SayDo基准：包含11项实验室与家庭任务，每项由良性指令搭配场景中的潜在危险；在6种设置上沿具身梯度实现（从关于想象机器人的聊天问答到模拟机械臂闭环控制），并配有良性对照、安全替代变体和脚本化操作者反驳；测试6个开源与闭源权重VLM，共11,480个回合。

Result: 危险动作率从聊天中的14%升至模拟器中的47%，受影响最大的模型从2%升至80%；增幅主要由开源权重模型驱动；73%的不安全回合中模型在行动前已识别危险；控制机器人时对反驳的让步频率约为聊天中的两倍；最安全的模型在良性对照上表现出过度谨慎迹象。

Conclusion: 纯文本环境下的安全评估不能预测同一模型作为机器人控制器时的安全行为，具身情境的安全评测不可或缺。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as high-level robot controllers, yet their safety is evaluated almost entirely in text. We introduce SayDo, a benchmark that assesses the propensity of frontier models to cause harm in embodied contexts. SayDo comprises eleven laboratory and household tasks that pair a benign instruction with a hazard latent in the scene. Tasks are implemented in six settings along an embodiment gradient, from chat questions about an imagined robot to closed-loop control of a simulated robot arm. Benign controls, safe-alternative variants and scripted operator pushback complement each task. Across six open- and closed-weight VLMs and 11,480 episodes, hazardous-action rates rise from 14% in chat to 47% in the simulator, and from 2% to 80% for the most affected model. The increase is largely driven by open-weight models. We find that models flag hazards before acting in 73% of unsafe episodes and give way to pushback about twice as often when controlling robots as in chat. The safest models also show signs of overcaution on benign controls. Safety evaluations run in text therefore do not predict the safety behavior of the same models when used as robot controllers.

</details>


### [31] [Personalization of a Commercial Powered Knee Prosthesis Under Clinically Realistic Conditions via Reinforcement Learning: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30928)
*Xinyi Liu,Atli Örn Sverrisson,David Langlois,Ming Liu,Varun Nalam,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究验证了带约束的策略迭代（PICE）强化学习调参方法在临床现实条件下个性化商用动力膝关节假体的可行性，3名健全参与者在8-18步（1.7-4.2分钟步行）内达到目标，膝关节运动学特征误差平均降低64-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢控制需个性化适配用户需求，但现有方法在空间与设备受限的临床现实条件下难以快速完成调参，因此需验证强化学习调参方法的临床可行性。

Method: 采用带约束的策略迭代（PICE）强化学习调参方法；以一名健全参与者跑步机步行数据离线学习得到初始策略，再应用于另外三名健全参与者，使其通过旁路适配器佩戴商用动力膝关节在5米步道上步行，进行在线个性化调参。

Result: 所有参与者均在8-18个调参步内（相当于1.7-4.2分钟步行）达到目标，膝关节运动学特征误差平均降低64-90%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该强化学习调参方法在具有空间和设备约束的临床现实条件下实现动力膝关节假体个性化是可行的。

Abstract: Personalization of powered prosthesis control to individual user needs remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, policy iteration with constraint embedded (PICE), a reinforcement learning (RL) tuning approach, has been developed and validated. This feasibility study investigated whether the developed approach can personalize a commercial powered knee prosthesis under clinically realistic conditions. To reduce the time for personalization, tuning was initialized with a policy learned offline from one non-disabled participant walking on a treadmill and was then applied to three other non-disabled participants walking with the knee through a bypass adapter on a 5-meter walkway. All participants reached the goal within 8-18 tuning steps (1.7-4.2 minutes of walking), reducing the knee-kinematic feature error by 64-90% on average. These results support the feasibility of implementing the RL-based tuning approach in clinically realistic conditions with space and equipment constraints.

</details>


### [32] [CoSel: Cooperation as Selection for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30918)
*Yiping Li,Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoSel框架，将协同驾驶中的信息利用转化为轨迹选择问题，无需改变规划器或其仅依赖自车输入的设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同端到端方法将共享信息注入规划器并依赖协同驾驶示范学习使用，但共享信息改变驾驶决策的时机在数据中稀少，监督信号有限。

Method: CoSel保持规划器及其自车输入不变，单独训练协同预测器利用自车、路侧单元和网联自动驾驶车辆的观测预测周围智能体及其未来运动，再用这些预测评估规划器候选轨迹：安全时保留原选择，共享信息揭示额外风险时选择最接近的安全替代轨迹。

Result: 闭环评估中，CoSel在V2Xverse上驾驶得分70.7，比最佳对比协同方法高7.5分；在MDrive上平均驾驶得分最高达85.8。自车检测缺失时，72个场景中CoSel碰撞数仅从17增至19，而现有协同端到端模型从19增至32。

Conclusion: 协同无需由规划器学习，通过协同预测与候选轨迹选择即可有效利用共享信息，提升安全性并保持决策可追溯。

Abstract: Cooperative driving can extend a vehicle's awareness beyond its own sensors, but existing cooperative end-to-end methods inject shared information into the planner and rely on cooperative driving demonstrations to learn how to use it. The moments in which shared information should change a driving decision are rare in such data, providing limited supervision for learning this behavior. We argue that cooperation need not be learned by the planner. Instead, reacting to shared information requires predicting how surrounding agents will move and selecting a response from the candidate trajectories of an ego planner. We introduce CoSel, a cooperation-as-selection framework that leaves the planner and its ego-only input unchanged. A separately trained cooperative predictor uses observations from the ego vehicle, roadside units, and connected automated vehicles to predict surrounding agents and their future motion. CoSel evaluates the planner's candidate trajectories against these predictions, preserves the planner's choice when it remains safe, and selects the closest safe alternative when shared information reveals additional risk. Each safety intervention is traceable to the shared evidence that triggers it. In closed-loop evaluation, CoSel achieves a driving score of 70.7 on V2Xverse, outperforming the best compared cooperative method by 7.5 points, and the highest average driving score of 85.8 on MDrive. Under missing ego detections, the collision count of CoSel increases only from 17 to 19 in 72 scenarios, compared with 19 to 32 for an existing cooperative end-to-end model.

</details>


### [33] [DexLatentWorld: 3D Cross-Embodiment Latent Action World Models for Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30907)
*Vinay Udit Mohan,Xuchuan Huang,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于3D点云的隐动作世界模型，从人类操作示教中学习连续动作表示，实现跨本体迁移与零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型依赖昂贵且难扩展的动作标注交互数据，且通常绑定特定机器人本体，难以迁移到未见过的形态。

Method: 直接从重定向人类操作示教的点云观测中学习连续动作表示，用对比目标鼓励跨本体运动表示一致；统一隐动作空间直接作为模型预测控制的搜索空间，采样隐动作条件化粒子式未来预测，再从预测的几何运动恢复可执行机器人命令，无需本体特定动作解码器。

Result: 隐动作具有可控与组合性，可迁移到未见本体并支持未见夹爪几何的零样本操作；任务无关适配后在到达、抓取、放置上分别达90%、92%、60%，优于DexWM的72%、58%、28%；用未见灵巧手在五类真实任务50次试验中零样本成功率达86%。

Conclusion: 几何接地的隐动作可作为统一、可迁移的接口，支撑基于世界模型的机器人操作。

Abstract: World models facilitate planning and control in robotic manipulation by enabling agents to anticipate the consequences of their actions. However, they typically rely on action-labelled interaction data that is costly to acquire and difficult to scale. These models are also often tied to the specific embodiment they were trained on, limiting transferability to previously unseen robot morphologies. We introduce a 3D latent-action world model that learns continuous action representations directly from point-cloud observations of retargeted human manipulation demonstrations. A contrastive objective is used to encourage consistent representations of motion across embodiments. The resulting unified latent action space serves directly as the search space for model-predictive control, where sampled latent actions condition particle-based future predictions. Executable robot commands are then recovered from the predicted geometric motion without requiring an embodiment-specific action decoder. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that the learned latent actions exhibit controllable and compositional motion, transfer to unseen robot embodiments, and support zero-shot manipulation with unseen gripper geometries. Following task-agnostic adaptation, our model achieves 90%, 92%, and 60% success on reaching, grasping, and placing, respectively, compared with DexWM's reported 72%, 58%, and 28%. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot real-world manipulation with an unseen dexterous hand, achieving an 86% success rate across 50 trials on five diverse tasks involving pushing, grasping, and lifting everyday objects. These results highlight the potential of geometry-grounded latent actions as a unified, transferable interface for world-model-based robotic manipulation. Project materials, including demonstration videos and source code, are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [Tabular Foundation Models Are Surprisingly Effective In-Context Robot Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30889)
*Zhen Xu,Hao-Run Cai,Heng-Zhuang Li,Si-Yang Liu,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TabAct，将视觉模仿学习转化为表格数值预测，利用冻结的表格基础模型（TFM）在上下文中学习机器人控制，无需机器人特定训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为通用数值预测预训练的基础模型能否作为上下文机器人学习器，避免机器人特定训练和目标任务微调。

Method: 将视觉模仿转化为数值预测：冻结视觉特征和机器人本体感受作为输入，未来动作块作为目标，每个演示时间步提供一个带标签样本，条件化冻结的TFM进行闭环控制；使用TabICLv2。

Result: 在LIBERO和Meta-World上，仅用一个目标任务演示时，TabAct平均成功率最高，分别超过最强基线20.9和5.8个百分点；五个演示时LIBERO-Object成功率达94.2%，且无需更新模型权重。

Conclusion: 通用数值预训练可为上下文机器人学习提供有效基础。

Abstract: Can foundation models pretrained for generic numerical prediction serve as in-context robot learners? We investigate this question through tabular foundation models (TFMs), which are pretrained to infer input-output relationships from labeled examples in context. We introduce TabAct, which casts visual imitation as numerical prediction: frozen visual features and robot proprioception form the inputs, future action chunks form the targets, and each demonstration time step provides one labeled example that conditions a frozen TFM for closed-loop control. The predictor receives no robot-specific training or target-task fine-tuning. With one target-task demonstration available to each method, TabAct with TabICLv2 achieves the highest average success among all evaluated methods on both LIBERO and Meta-World, exceeding the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 5.8 percentage points, respectively. Additional demonstrations improve control without updating model weights: with five demonstrations, success reaches 94.2% on LIBERO-Object. These results demonstrate that generic numerical pretraining can provide an effective foundation for in-context robot learning.

</details>


### [35] [Bridging Continuous-Time MAPF and Industrial AGV Fleet Coordination with Lazy Priority-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30874)
*Federico Signorile,Raffaele Carli,Alessandro Bertogli,Roberto Olmi,Sven Koenig,Mariagrazia Dotoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对工业AGV路网上的连续时间多智能体路径规划问题，提出AGV-MAPF_R-CTC形式化模型，修正CPBS的边界条件，并引入惰性扩展的Lazy-CPBS算法以降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业AGV基础设施中路网高度连通且冗余，导致安全区间推理和多智能体交互处理成本高，现有连续时间MAPF_R求解器难以直接部署。

Method: 将MAPF_R形式化为带预计算连续时间冲突的配置空间变体AGV-MAPF_R-CTC以复用现有求解器；重新审视CPBS，明确SIPP底层规划器在优先级约束下的边界条件并支持更早解检测；分析优先级搜索空间结构，确定每次优先级细化影响的智能体及可避免重规划的情形；据此提出Lazy-CPBS，仅在高层节点扩展时计算子节点规划，避免未扩展兄弟节点的底层搜索。

Result: 在标准MAPF基准和从专有数字孪生仿真器导入的工业AGV路网上实验，CPBS类求解器在远超最优MAPF_R求解器可处理智能体数量的规模下仍有效；Lazy-CPBS显著降低CPBS开销，并在工业布局中提供最一致的鲁棒性与运行时间权衡。

Conclusion: Lazy-CPBS通过惰性高层节点物化有效缓解CPBS开销，使连续时间MAPF_R在工业AGV车队协调中更具实用性。

Abstract: Continuous-time multi-agent path finding on roadmaps (MAPF$_R$) offers a principled way to coordinate robot fleets with geometric collision avoidance, yet its deployment on industrial automated guided vehicle (AGV) infrastructures remains challenging, as highly connected and redundant roadmaps amplify both safe-interval reasoning and multi-agent interaction handling. To bridge this gap, we formalize AGV-MAPF$_R$-CTC, a configuration-space variant of MAPF$_R$ with precomputed continuous-time conflicts that enables the reuse of MAPF$_R$ solvers for AGV fleet coordination with minimal changes. We then revisit continuous-time priority-based search (CPBS), identifying the boundary conditions required by its safe interval path planning (SIPP) low-level planner under priority-induced constraints and enabling earlier solution detection. As a central contribution, we analyze the structure of the priority search space, clarifying which agents are affected by each priority refinement and when replanning can be avoided. Building on this analysis, we introduce a novel algorithm, Lazy-CPBS, which materializes high-level nodes lazily: a child's plans are computed only when it is expanded, so siblings that are never expanded cost no low-level search. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks and on industrial AGV roadmaps imported from a proprietary digital-twin simulator show that CPBS-based solvers remain effective well beyond the agent counts reachable by optimal MAPF$_R$ solvers, while Lazy-CPBS substantially reduces CPBS overhead and offers the most consistent robustness/runtime trade-off across industrial layouts.

</details>


### [36] [HIVE-Bench: Evaluating Patch-Level Visual Representations for Egocentric Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30813)
*Qiwei Liang,Zhengtu Liang,Minghao Lai,Yikeng Chen,Yiming Wang,Boyang Cai,Yuetong Fang,Taowen Wang,Baiqiao Yin,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Jiaming Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Daolin Ma,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO
