TL;DR: 论文提出HIVE-Bench基准，在24个自我中心双臂任务上比较7个视觉编码器家族的20个预训练编码器，发现保留密集patch token对策略性能至关重要，且从指令运动反推的读数是最强的训练前预测指标，而识别、深度与对应性指标不可靠迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习中的视觉预训练对比多基于第三人称图像池化后的单一向量，而双臂策略实际读取机器人相机上的密集patch token，评估方式与实际使用不符，且缺乏统一基准来系统比较编码器在适应、任务共享与数据/模型规模/层扫描等设定下的表现。

Method: 构建HIVE-Bench：一个共享的流匹配策略在24个自我中心双臂任务上读取来自7个家族共20个编码器的特征；开展适应、任务共享以及数据、模型规模与层数扫描实验；对同一token分别用统计量、状态与物体读数、已发表视觉分数以及指令运动的逆动力学读数进行评分。

Result: 池化每个视图为单一token会大幅损失成功率；适应对每对编码器均有帮助；共享策略以少量更新保留大部分独立策略的成功率。指令运动读数在训练前是最强测量之一；识别、深度与对应性指标不能可靠迁移；微调可改善动作读数，即使其他测量反向变化；没有统计量稳定到足以在部署前替代真实rollout。

Conclusion: 视觉预训练在双臂机器人学习中必须保留密集patch token，且应以指令运动逆动力学读数等任务相关指标评估编码器；现有通用视觉与统计指标不可靠，部署前仍需rollout验证。

Abstract: Pretrained vision is a common start for robot learning, but most comparisons still score one pooled vector from a third-person image. Bimanual policies instead read dense patch tokens from cameras on the robot. We introduce HIVE-Bench: one shared flow-matching policy reads 20 encoders from seven families on 24 egocentric bimanual tasks, across adaptation, task sharing, and sweeps of data, model size, and layer. Keeping the patches matters. Averaging each view to one token removes most of the success, adaptation helps every pair we test, and one shared policy keeps most of the success of separate policies at a fraction of the updates. The same tokens are then scored with statistics, state and object readouts, published vision scores, and an inverse-dynamics readout. That readout of the commanded motion is one of the strongest measurements available before a policy is trained. Recognition, depth, and correspondence do not transfer reliably. Fine-tuning can improve the action readout even when other measurements move the other way, and no statistic is stable enough to replace a rollout before any deployment.

</details>


### [37] [Beyond Domain Randomization: Real-World Policy Adaptation in Learned Dynamics for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30784)
*Rui Dai,Shunpeng Yang,Zhen Fu,Maolin Lei,Jiaqi Song,Hua Chen,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种超越静态域随机化的框架，通过将学习到的动力学模型适应部署目标条件，并采用教师-学生范式同时作用于策略和动力学模型，以提升强化学习策略在未建模动态变化下的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态域随机化训练的强化学习策略难以应对部署环境中超出随机化分布的真实动态变化（如负载变化、摩擦变化），导致性能下降。

Method: 将动力学模型融入在线强化学习流程，采用并行的教师-学生范式对策略和动力学模型进行适应，使策略性能与目标环境的经验动力学对齐。

Result: 在双足运动任务上的实验表明，该方法在极端物理变化下具有竞争力的适应能力，包括负载达到自重的80%以及油润滑低摩擦表面。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够有效提升强化学习策略在超出静态域随机化分布的真实动态变化下的适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies are often trained with static domain randomization for real-world deployment. However, real-world environments often exhibit unmodeled dynamic variations, such as payload changes or friction shifts, that lie outside the randomization distribution, leading to performance degradation upon deployment. We propose a framework that moves beyond static domain randomization by adapting a learned dynamics model to the deployed target conditions, with a concurrent teacher-student paradigm applied to both the policy and dynamics model. This framework integrates the dynamics model into an on-policy reinforcement learning pipeline, letting the performance of the policy align with the empirical target dynamics. Experimental validations on bipedal locomotion tasks demonstrate competitive adaptability under extreme physical variations, including a payload reaching 80% of self-weight and an oil-lubricated low-friction surface.

</details>


### [38] [Object Count Generalization Is an Interface Problem: Warping a Frozen Skill Instead of Retraining It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30765)
*Paul Mattes,Ayman Abdeldayem,Denis Shcherba,Wojciech Samek,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAVE通过选择策略和仿射扭曲策略，将预训练的单物体操作技能扩展到多物体场景，无需更新技能权重，在门架机器人上零样本泛化到30个立方体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习操作策略在场景物体数量N超过训练范围时会因协变量偏移和状态维度变化而性能退化，现有方法最多泛化到6个物体。

Method: WAVE包含三部分：选择策略挑选下一个操作物体；扭曲策略读取以该物体为中心的局部视图（尺寸与N无关），为技能读取的每个量和输出的每个动作生成乘数与偏移；技能在仿射扭曲下作用于单物体场景，扭曲将技能引导绕过未展示物体并编辑动作。选择与扭曲策略用PPO在多物体奖励上训练，将技能视为黑盒，要求技能在同一本体上训练且每个物体有目标位姿。

Result: 在门架机器人和Franka Panda机械臂的立方体排列任务上评估，训练最多9个立方体，零样本泛化到训练数量的数倍（门架达30个）。对比方法（学习多物体操作）泛化上限为6个；微调技能、无技能的同局部视图学习、以及从预训练技能蒸馏的实体变换器在相同训练预算下均在其训练数量内崩溃。

Conclusion: WAVE是一种任务通用、技能无关的框架，通过仿射扭曲和选择策略实现多物体操作的零样本数量泛化，并给出了放置所有N个物体所需尝试次数的界。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies for robot manipulation degrade when the number of objects N in the scene grows beyond the training range. This is a covariate shift that also changes the dimension of the state. We propose WAVE (Warping Actions and Views of an Expert), which extends a pretrained single-object skill to many objects without updating its weights. WAVE has three parts. First, a selection policy picks the one object the robot works on next. Second, a warping policy reads a local view of the scene around that object, whose size does not depend on N. It emits a multiplier and an offset for every quantity the skill reads and every action it outputs. Third, the skill acts under this affine warp. It is shown a scene that holds a single object, while the warp steers it around the objects it is not shown and edits the actions that are sent to the robot. Both the selection and warping policies are trained with PPO on the multi-object reward and use the skill as a black box, so WAVE is task-general and skill-agnostic. It requires only that the skill was trained on the same embodiment and that every object has a goal pose. We also bound the number of attempts needed to place all N objects, assuming positive expected net progress per attempt, and measure this progress for each object count. We evaluate WAVE on cube arrangement tasks with a gantry robot and with a Franka Panda arm. Trained with at most nine cubes, it generalizes zero-shot to several times its training count, on the gantry to scenes of up to thirty cubes. Published methods that learn multi-object manipulation show count generalization up to six objects. Fine-tuning the skill, learning from the same local view without it, and an entity transformer, even when distilled from the pretrained skill, all collapse within their training counts under the same training budget.

</details>


### [39] [Place-Revisit Consistency Evaluation of Reference Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30741)
*Jianzhu Huai,Yujia Zhang,Grzegorz Jozkow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于重访一致性的参考位姿评估方法，用于检测移动传感数据集中基准参考位姿的局部不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里程计与SLAM系统常以参考轨迹作为评估基准，但基准参考位姿中的局部不一致会引入评估偏差。

Method: 利用平台重访先前观测地点的特性，比较参考轨迹隐含的相对位姿与机载传感器（激光雷达扫描配准、立体图像位姿估计）直接估计的相对位姿是否一致，并在KITTI Odometry和SNAIL Radar数据集上实例化验证。

Result: KITTI分析识别出多个重访区域中公开参考位姿与传感器导出相对位姿不一致，平移差异中位数最高达6.60米；代表性点云叠加进一步定位了这些差异。

Conclusion: 重访一致性可作为基准验证工具，用于发现参考位姿的局部不一致并支撑数据集评估的可靠性检验。

Abstract: Reference trajectories are widely used to evaluate odometry and simultaneous localization and mapping systems. However, local inconsistencies in benchmark reference poses can bias such evaluations. This paper presents a place-revisit consistency evaluation method for reference poses in mobile sensing datasets. When a platform revisits a previously observed place after a sufficient temporal gap, the relative pose implied by the reference trajectory should agree with relative poses estimated directly from onboard sensors. We instantiate this idea with lidar scan registration and stereo-image pose estimation, and apply it to the KITTI Odometry and SNAIL Radar. The KITTI analysis identifies several revisit regions where the public reference poses disagree with sensor-derived relative poses, with median translation discrepancies up to 6.60 m. Representative point-cloud overlays further localize these discrepancies and support the use of place revisits as a benchmark-validation tool.

</details>


### [40] [Coordinating Perception and Planning for Test-Time Adaptation in End-to-End Driving under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30739)
*Shiyu Fang,Yiming Cui,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Collister Chua,Wenhui Huang,Peng Hang,Chen Lv,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出即插即用测试时自适应框架P2-TTA，解决端到端自动驾驶在分布偏移下的多任务依赖与持续适应不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶依赖i.i.d.假设，真实分布偏移下性能易退化；现有TTA用于驾驶面临感知更新影响下游规划且感知置信度无法直接评估规划效果，以及长期递归更新导致参数漂移累积两大障碍。

Method: P2-TTA通过联合目标协调自适应：感知熵最小化结合基于自车运动观测的回顾性时序规划一致性目标；采用EMA教师作为移动参数参考，并拒绝增加历史规划损失的候选更新以稳定在线更新。

Result: 在多个模型和不同骨干网络上，平均仅更新0.11‰参数、平均适应时间19.9 ms，在严重分布偏移下L2、碰撞率和PDMS平均恢复率分别为36.4%、30.0%和9.3%；在UniAD上共享感知-规划目标下取得对比TTA基线中最高的L2和碰撞恢复。

Conclusion: P2-TTA是一种高效即插即用的持续自适应框架，能显著缓解端到端驾驶模型在分布偏移下的性能退化，并保持在线更新稳定。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved significant milestones, yet its reliance on the i.i.d. assumption renders it vulnerable to real-world distribution shifts. While Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising paradigm for on-the-fly enhancement, its application to driving is hindered by two barriers: (i) multi-task adaptation dependency, where updates to upstream perception also affect downstream planning, while perception confidence alone does not directly assess these downstream effects; and (ii) continuous adaptation instability, where recursive online updates over long horizons cause compounding parameter drift. To address these challenges, we propose P2-TTA, a plug-and-play TTA framework for the continual adaptation of end-to-end driving models. P2-TTA coordinates adaptation through a joint objective combining perception entropy minimization with a retrospective temporal planning consistency objective derived from observed ego motion. To stabilize online updates, we use an EMA teacher as a moving parameter reference and reject candidate updates that increase historical planning loss. Extensive experiments across multiple models demonstrate that, by updating an average of 0.11 per mille of parameters with an average adaptation time of 19.9 ms on different backbones, P2-TTA achieves average recovery rates of 36.4%, 30.0%, and 9.3% for L2, collision rate, and PDMS, respectively, under severe distribution shifts. Under a shared perception-planning objective, P2-TTA achieves the highest L2 and collision recovery among the compared TTA baselines on UniAD.

</details>


### [41] [LLM-Guided Compositional Continual Learning for Robot Manipulation with Gated Low-Rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30667)
*Nilay Kushawaha,Abdalla Swikir,Fares Abu-Dakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于渐进神经网络与LoRA的持续学习框架，使操作机器人无需遗忘旧技能或从头重训即可掌握新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作机器人需随时间获取新技能，同时避免遗忘旧技能或从头重训。

Method: 将每项技能表示为PNN中专用专家子网络；新任务通过侧连接上的LoRA模块复用先验知识，侧连接由可学习的逐父节点门控控制，可显式衰减单条迁移路径并降低密集PNN侧连接的参数成本；长时部署时用冻结的本地大语言模型将自然语言指令分解为子任务，路由至合适专家，仅在专家库不足时请求新专家；采用共享任务空间动作接口。

Result: 在12个MetaWorld任务及Sawyer-to-Panda-to-UR5e跨本体迁移实验中，相比从头学习，在演示有限时任务成功率提升；真实机器人实验进一步支持组合执行与专家编排。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效支持技能持续获取、跨本体迁移与长时任务执行。

Abstract: General-purpose manipulation robots must acquire new skills over time without forgetting prior ones or retraining from scratch. We present a continual learning framework that represents each skill as a dedicated expert sub- network in a Progressive Neural Network (PNN). New tasks reuse prior knowledge through low-rank adaptation (LoRA) modules on lateral connections controlled by learnable per-parent gates, enabling explicit attenuation of individual transfer paths while reducing the parameter cost of dense PNN laterals. For long-horizon deployment, a frozen local large language model decomposes natural-language instructions into sub-tasks, routes them to suitable experts, and requests a new expert only when the library is insufficient. Using a shared task-space action interface, we evaluate the framework on twelve MetaWorld tasks and in a Sawyer-to-Panda-to-UR5e cross-embodiment transfer experiment, showing improved task success relative to learning from scratch when demonstrations are limited. Real-robot manipulation experiments further demonstrate that the proposed framework supports compositional execution and expert orchestration.

</details>


### [42] [Active Observation for Robust Visual Servoing in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30658)
*Zhilun Hu,Yuqi Cheng,Yuxiang Tan,Wenpan Li,Weiming Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主动观察框架，利用双臂平台中一臂携带RGB-D相机作为主动观察者，另一臂执行抓取，以解决抓取过程中的遮挡问题，实现94%抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取中的视觉伺服常因接近阶段遮挡破坏闭环控制；固定眼在手外相机在目标隐藏后缺乏主动恢复机制，眼在手上相机则易自遮挡且对自标定敏感。

Method: 在双臂平台上让一臂持RGB-D相机作为观察者，另一臂抓取；观察者重定位以维持或恢复目标-夹爪共享视野；采用两阶段主动视点策略对齐高精度RGB-D轴与任务相关误差分量；可见性下降时触发遮挡探索模块，暂停工作臂并按观察质量评分候选视点；在单张RGB-D图中同时检测目标与夹爪，计算相对伺服误差以避免两个独立变换估计之差。

Result: 在双6-DoF Piper平台上使用开放词汇检测器与文本提示、无需任务微调或示教，每物体50次随机试验中抓取成功率达94%，对比反应式视点基线76%和固定相机64%；触发严重遮挡时恢复平均耗时1.8±0.6秒。

Conclusion: 主动观察框架能有效应对抓取过程中的遮挡，显著提升抓取成功率并实现快速遮挡恢复，优于固定相机和反应式视点基线。

Abstract: Robust visual servoing during robotic grasping remains challenging, as approach-phase occlusion frequently disrupts closed-loop control. To address this, we present an active observation framework in which one arm of an existing dual-arm platform carries an RGB-D camera as an active observer while the other executes grasping. The observer repositions to maintain or recover a shared target-gripper view within the feasible shared-view workspace. Occlusion during the final approach is particularly acute: fixed eye-to-hand lacks an active recovery mechanism once the target is hidden, while eye-in-hand can suffer from self-occlusion and sensitivity to hand-eye calibration. The observer first uses an active two-phase viewpoint strategy to align high-precision RGB-D axes with task-relevant error components. If visibility later degrades, an occlusion-triggered exploration module pauses the worker arm and scores candidate viewpoints by observation quality to recover a shared target-gripper view. Because both objects are detected in a single RGB-D image, the resulting Relative Servo Error avoids differencing two independently transformed target and gripper estimates. We evaluate the system on a dual 6-DoF Piper platform using an open-vocabulary detector with text prompts, without task-specific fine-tuning or demonstrations. Across 50 randomized trials per object, the system achieves 94% grasp success versus 76% for a reactive viewpoint baseline and 64% for a fixed camera. On triggered severe-occlusion trials, recovery takes $1.8 \pm 0.6$ s on average.

</details>


### [43] [SWEEP-Bench: Do Embodied Policies Act on the Speed They See?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30637)
*Hiroaki Honda,Koya Sakamoto,Shu Morikuni,Yusuke Iwasawa,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWEEP-Bench基准测试揭示机器人策略对传送带速度的适应缺陷，并发现历史帧与速度可解码性可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少跨多样任务与速度测量机器人策略对运动物体速度的适应能力。

Method: 构建含80个传送带操作任务（16类技能、13档速度0.10-0.40 m/s）的仿真基准，每任务有静态对照且各速度复用同一随机场景；微调八个VLA与世界动作模型，对比仅当前帧与含两帧历史的观测，并做线性探针与真实/估计速度输入实验。

Result: 六个模型成功率在中等速度达峰，失败源于输出适配平均演示速度；历史帧使三个模型成功率提升10-40个百分点，且速度从这三者最易线性解码；真实速度输入改善四个模型，图像估计速度保留大部分增益。

Conclusion: SWEEP-Bench暴露速度相关失败，历史帧仅对速度最易解码的策略（如多帧预训练模型）有帮助。

Abstract: Robot policies must adapt to the speed of moving objects, yet existing benchmarks rarely measure this ability across diverse tasks and speeds. We introduce SWEEP-Bench, a simulation benchmark with 80 conveyor-belt manipulation tasks spanning 16 skill categories, each evaluated at 13 belt speeds (0.10-0.40 m/s). Objects are transported through physical contact with a visibly moving belt. Each task has a static counterpart, and each evaluation episode uses the same randomized scene at all 13 speeds. We fine-tune eight vision-language-action and world-action models on demonstrations spanning the full speed range, comparing observations from the current frame alone with those including two past frames. For six models, the success rate peaks at intermediate speeds, and their failures indicate that they tend to produce actions suited to the average demonstration speed rather than to the actual one. Past frames raise the success rate by 10-40 percentage points for three models, and linear probes show that the belt speed is also most linearly decodable from these three. Adding ground-truth speed input to four of the models improves all of them, and a speed estimated from the images keeps most of this gain. These results show that SWEEP-Bench exposes speed-dependent failures and suggest that past frames help only policies from which the speed is most decodable, such as those pretrained on multiple frames. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [44] [ABI: Actor-Free Behavior-Induced Representations for Zero-Shot Test-Time Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30636)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Guanbo Jiao,Jianming Ma,Xing Gao,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABI是一种无演员、无奖励的多步动作块表示方法，通过冻结表示与轻量任务读出构建块评论家，在测试时引导不同模仿策略实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆策略的纯模仿目标限制零样本泛化，而现有离线强化学习需任务特定奖励或绑定特定演员，训练成本高且限制跨策略泛化。

Method: 利用数据收集行为下评估动作可保持跨目标策略的相对价值排序，结合无奖励后继预测，从无标签轨迹学习多水平动作块表示，无需奖励监督或演员训练；测试时与轻量任务读出配对形成块评论家，引导目标策略且无需参数更新。

Result: 在65个运动、导航和操作任务中，ABI引导弱模仿策略匹配或超越零样本RL基线，样本效率高，在相同提议上优于现有零样本评论家，并提升外部训练RL演员而无需重训练；从原始像素输入在视觉操作上取得显著引导增益并超越先前零样本RL基线。

Conclusion: ABI提供了一种无需奖励和演员训练的高效跨策略泛化框架，适用于视觉运动控制，具有引导弱策略和提升现有RL演员的潜力。

Abstract: Behavior-cloning policies show strong ability in sequential control by generating coherent multi-step action chunks, yet their purely imitative objective limits zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks. While offline reinforcement learning offers principled policy improvement, existing methods either require task-specific reward supervision or remain tied to a specific actor during critic training or policy deployment, inflating training cost and restricting cross-policy generalization. We observe that evaluating actions under the data-collecting behavior largely preserves their relative value ordering across target policies, offering a way around actor coupling. Combining this property with reward-free successor prediction, we propose Actor-Free Behavior-Induced Representations (ABI) to learn multi-horizon action-chunk representations from unlabeled trajectories without reward supervision or actor training. At test time, pairing the frozen representation with lightweight task readouts forms a chunk critic that guides target policies without parameter updates and transfers directly across distinct unseen policies. Our analysis establishes when the value ordering is preserved across policies and when selecting among proposed chunks improves closed-loop return. Across 65 locomotion, navigation, and manipulation tasks, ABI's chunk critic guides weak imitation policies to match or surpass zero-shot RL baselines with high sample efficiency, outperforms existing zero-shot critics on identical proposals, and improves externally trained RL actors without retraining. From raw pixels, ABI delivers substantial guidance gains and surpasses prior zero-shot RL baselines on visual manipulation, highlighting its potential for visuomotor control.

</details>


### [45] [GeoMerge-OPD: Balanced Skill Integration in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30632)
*Le Yang,Haoyang Lyu,Honglei Jin,Wenshuo Chen,Jiawei Liang,Wenqiang Wang,Yutao Yue,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoMerge-OPD，通过响应空间正则化、参数合并与在线策略蒸馏，将异构机器人技能整合为单一VLA策略，实现人形机器人全身多技能平衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型擅长单一技能，但联合微调和参数合并等多技能整合策略在闭环执行中易引发意外动作，严重削弱各技能性能，尤其难以应对操作与运动需求各异的人形全身任务。

Method: 任务条件分析发现专家更新响应能量集中于少数主导方向，且专家响应在高能方向相反，与合并后动作偏移相关。据此提出GeoMerge-OPD：独立专家微调时用G-isometry在任务激活子空间约束更新响应增益；再将专家参数静态合并为学生模型获得初步多任务能力；最后以微调专家为教师，在学生访问的环境观测与中间动作流状态上提供速度场监督以校准残余行为偏差。

Result: 在两个VLA骨干网络上，于SIMPLE任务实现强且平衡的人形技能整合，在LIBERO和LIBERO-PRO上保持稳定性能，优于联合微调与多任务强化学习基线，并在扰动下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeoMerge-OPD有效缓解多技能合并中的动作偏移与技能退化，验证了响应空间结构分析与正则化结合策略蒸馏在异构全身技能整合中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models excel at individual robotic skills, yet integrating heterogeneous skills into a single policy remains a key challenge for general-purpose robotic control, especially in whole-body tasks with differing manipulation and locomotion requirements. Common multi-skill integration strategies, such as joint fine-tuning and parameter merging, can induce unintended actions during closed-loop execution, severely degrading individual skills. Task-conditioned analysis of expert updates reveals response energy concentrated in a few dominant directions and opposing expert responses along high-energy directions within a shared, task-relevant response subspace. This structure co-occurs with unintended action shifts after merging. We propose GeoMerge-OPD, combining response-space regularization, parameter merging, and on-policy distillation. During independent expert fine-tuning, G-isometry constrains update-response gains within task-activation subspaces; the expert parameters are then statically merged into a student with preliminary multitask capabilities. Fine-tuned task experts serve as teachers, providing velocity-field supervision at the environment observations and intermediate action-flow states visited by the student to calibrate residual behavioral deviations. Experiments with two VLA backbones show strong, balanced humanoid skill integration on SIMPLE and consistent performance on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO, outperforming joint fine-tuning and multitask reinforcement-learning baselines while maintaining robustness under perturbations.

</details>


### [46] [VeriTouch: Verified Visuo-Tactile World Models that Improve Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30572)
*Shuxun Wang,Xiaonan Jiang,Zhaoyu Wang,Ziyuan Chu,Xihang Yue,Pengfei Liu,Haodong Zhu,Shiqi Liu,Mingzhi Zheng,Liang Su,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VeriTouch框架通过生成并验证合成的视触觉轨迹来扩展策略学习，无需高昂的真实数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展视触觉策略学习受限于真实数据采集成本高和构建逼真仿真困难，生成模型虽可提供可扩展训练数据，但有用示范需具备合理触觉反馈、与视觉接触的时间对齐及准确动作。

Method: 提出VeriTouch框架：VeriGen世界模型基于初始图像和任务指令联合生成高保真双视角视频与真实触觉序列；VeriEval多模态验证器评估视觉质量并聚焦触觉真实性、接触力合理性和视觉接触与触觉信号的时间对齐以过滤不可靠序列；VeriIDM逆动力学模型结合双相机全局场景信息与夹爪细节及相邻帧运动提取动作。

Result: 在五个真实世界任务上，合成数据提升了全部四个评估策略，进一步扩展合成轨迹显著提升策略性能且无需额外真实数据采集。

Conclusion: VeriTouch能够生成并验证高质量的合成视触觉轨迹，有效扩展视触觉策略学习并提升策略性能，减少对真实世界数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: Scaling visuo-tactile policy learning is constrained by the high cost of real-world data collection and the difficulty of building realistic simulations. Generative models offer a scalable source of training data, yet useful demonstrations require plausible tactile feedback, temporal alignment with visual contact, and accurate actions. To this end, we present VeriTouch, a framework for generating and verifying synthetic visuo-tactile trajectories for policy learning. Conditioned on initial images and task instructions, our world model, VeriGen, jointly generates high-fidelity dual-view videos and realistic tactile sequences over complete manipulation trajectories. By integrating a dedicated tactile expert with a pretrained video model, it captures realistic visual details and tactile deformation dynamics through joint visuo-tactile modeling. Our multimodal verifier VeriEval then checks visual quality while focusing on tactile reliability, assessing tactile realism, contact-force plausibility, and temporal alignment between visible contact and tactile signals, thereby directly filtering out unreliable sequences. Finally, our inverse dynamics model, VeriIDM, combines global scene information from both camera views with fine-grained gripper details and motion across neighboring frames to extract actions from verified videos. Experiments on five real-world tasks show that synthetic data improves all four evaluated policies, with further scaling synthetic trajectories substantially boosting policy performance without additional real-world data collection.

</details>


### [47] [RoboInF: A Benchmark for Instruction Fidelity in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30555)
*Jiawei Guo,Yu Huang,Dijiang Dong,Yue Liu,Peiran Ren,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboInF基准，固定初始场景与机器人状态，通过多分支指令变化评估操作策略对指令的遵循程度，并发布4,242条专家演示的RoboInF-4K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将指令遵循与感知、规划及新场景泛化混在一起评估，任务成功率高并不能证明模型更严格地遵循指令，因此需要一个专门度量行为随指令变化而调整的基准。

Method: 为8个任务各设计6至12条经专家验证的指令分支，分别改变目标、流程或操作细节；任务专用检查器验证最终目标，并利用记录的轨迹、接触和事件序列评估执行全程的合规性；同时构建RoboInF-4K数据集，将专家演示与仿真器推导的运动和接触标注配对；评测了π0.5、OpenWAM及仅推理智能体GPT-6 Astra和Qwen3.8-Flash。

Result: 四个模型均表现出任务完成与忠实执行之间的差距，说明细粒度指令遵循仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 任务成功率不足以反映指令遵循质量，需以执行过程合规性为独立指标，RoboInF为细粒度指令遵循提供了可复现的评测手段。

Abstract: Precise language control requires embodied models to follow instructions governing both task outcomes and execution. Although recent methods train policies to follow increasingly detailed commands, existing benchmarks often assess instruction following alongside perception, planning, and generalization to unfamiliar scenes. Higher task success therefore offers limited evidence of closer adherence to instructions. We introduce RoboInF, a benchmark measuring how reliably manipulation policies adapt their behavior to changes in an instruction. For each of eight tasks, six to twelve expert-validated instruction branches vary the requested goal, procedure, or manipulation detail while holding the initial scene and robot state fixed. Task-specific checkers verify the terminal goal and use recorded trajectories, contacts, and event sequences to assess compliance throughout execution. The accompanying RoboInF-4K dataset pairs 4,242 expert demonstrations with simulator-derived annotations of motion and contact. We evaluate leading manipulation policies ($\pi_{0.5}$ and OpenWAM) and inference-only agents (GPT-6 Astra and Qwen3.8-Flash). All four models exhibit a gap between task completion and faithful execution, indicating substantial room for fine-grained instruction following.

</details>


### [48] [Body and Soul: Decoupling Robotic Brain and Policy with RoboHarness-Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30550)
*Yihao Shen,Yuxuan Chen,Xuantong Liu,Jun Gao,Weijian Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需任务特定准备、无需为新策略重新设计的VLM监督框架，包裹冻结VLA，在RoboCasa365上把成功率从57.5%提升到70.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略能执行单一技能但难以完成需组合多技能的任务，审计显示约43%失败源于目标选择错误或任务进度跟踪丢失；现有VLM监督框架依赖任务与策略特定准备，其收益难以迁移到新任务或新策略。

Method: 冻结VLA负责连续接触丰富的运动控制，VLM（如GPT-6）零样本规划并监控执行；当进度停滞或目标漂移时，VLM先改写指令，仅作为后备才在通用运动原语上执行短生成代码，随后将精细控制交还VLA；监督规则跨策略固定，仅需薄接口适配器和每策略自动分析的技能配置。

Result: 在200个RoboCasa365场景中，搭配GPT-6 Astra使冻结的Xiaomi-Robotics-1成功率从57.5%升至70.5%，保留90.4%（104/115）的独立成功并挽救85次失败中的37次；在共享50场景子集上，同一规则为另外四个冻结策略各提升8至10个百分点，而现有基于准备的框架系统常无法迁移到新策略。

Conclusion: 该框架是首个同时满足新任务最少准备、新策略无需重新设计、并保留策略原有独立能力的VLM监督框架，验证了零样本VLM规划与冻结VLA执行相结合的有效性和跨策略可迁移性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can execute individual skills but fail to complete tasks that require combining several of them. In our audit of Xiaomi-Robotics-1, an estimated 43\% of failures involve choosing the wrong target or losing track of task progress. Wrapping a frozen VLA in a vision-language model (VLM) harness is a natural remedy. Existing harnesses can gain substantially from task- and policy-specific preparation, raising the question of how much of that gain transfers to a new task or policy. An ideal harness should (i) need minimal preparation for a new task, (ii) need no redesign for a new policy, and (iii) preserve standalone capabilities the policy already has. To the best of our knowledge, our \ours{} is the first harness built this way. Its premise is that choosing targets and tracking progress are easy for a VLM such as GPT-6 without task preparation, while the frozen VLA already handles continuous, contact-rich motion. The frozen VLA therefore executes continuous control while the VLM plans zero-shot and monitors execution; when progress stalls or targets drift, the VLM first rephrases the instruction and, only as a fallback, executes short generated code over generic motion primitives before returning fine control to the VLA. The supervision rules stay fixed across policies, requiring only a thin interface adapter and an automatically profiled skill configuration per policy. On 200 RoboCasa365 scenes, \ours{} with GPT-6 Astra raises the success rate of frozen Xiaomi-Robotics-1 from \textbf{\color{blue}57.5\%} to \textbf{\color{blue}70.5\%} and preserves \textbf{\color{blue}90.4\%} of its standalone successes (104/115) while rescuing 37 of 85 failures. On a shared 50-scene subset, the same rules add 8 to 10 percentage points to each of four other frozen policies, while existing preparation-based harness systems often fail to transfer to a new policy.

</details>


### [49] [DelayOPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation from a Synchronous Teacher for Real-time Asynchronous Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30534)
*Hyeonjae Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Sunwoo Park,Chanyu Moon,Yoojin Oh,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DelayOPSD通过让异步策略在自身延迟rollout上模仿同步教师，缓解观测滞后带来的性能下降，在LIBERO、Kinetix和真实机器人上均提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的VLA策略因大视觉语言骨干和迭代去噪导致推理延迟高；异步执行可降低延迟，但新动作块基于滞后观测生成，延迟越大性能越差。作者发现该失配在专家演示上预测差异小，但在策略自身延迟rollout（尤其失败回合）上差异显著。

Method: 提出DelayOPSD：同步教师由同一策略在执行时刻观测下生成，延迟学生仅见较早帧和仍在执行的动作；学生在自身去噪全过程中模仿教师。

Result: 10步延迟下，SmolVLA在演示上训练成功率仅70.2%（与朴素异步70.4%相当），在自身rollout上训练提升至78.7%；LIBERO和Kinetix上平均成功率最高；真实机器人成功率从46%提升至62%。

Conclusion: 利用策略自身延迟rollout进行同步-异步蒸馏可有效缓解滞后观测失配，且部署时无需额外模块。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies incur substantial inference latency due to their large vision-language backbones and iterative denoising. Asynchronous execution reduces this latency by generating the next action chunk while executing the current one, but the new chunk is conditioned on a stale observation, degrading performance as the delay increases. We investigate this mismatch by comparing actions predicted from observations at planning and execution times. We find that these predictions differ little on expert demonstrations but diverge substantially on the policy's own delayed rollouts, particularly in failed episodes. Motivated by this observation, we propose DelayOPSD, which trains an asynchronous policy to imitate its synchronous counterpart on the policy's own rollouts. Specifically, a synchronous teacher, obtained from the same policy using the observation at execution time, supervises a delayed student that observes only the earlier frame and the actions still being executed. The student learns to match the teacher throughout its own denoising process. Under a 10-step delay, training SmolVLA on demonstrations achieves only 70.2% success, comparable to naive asynchronous execution (70.4%), whereas training on its own rollouts improves success to 78.7%. Across LIBERO and Kinetix, DelayOPSD achieves the highest average success among the compared methods, without introducing additional modules at deployment. On a real robot, it further improves success from 46% to 62%.

</details>


### [50] [JEPA-ICL: Progress-Aligned Latent Plans for In-Context Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30516)
*Tingjun Dai,Jianxiang He,Mingfei Han,Qihao Yu,Longtao Jiang,Changlin Li,Minghao Jin,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEPA-ICL视觉-语言-动作框架，耦合预测式规划与示范学习，通过JEPA特征空间预测未来状态并检索示范中进度对齐的上下文来指导动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示范能展示任务完成方式，但具身形态、外观和执行速度差异导致难以识别与机器人当前进度相关的指导信息。

Method: 在JEPA特征空间中预测子任务完成前的有序未来状态形成规划，用规划定位示范中的相关阶段，其JEPA监督的内容特征提供进度对齐上下文，该上下文反过来更新共享策略以生成规划与动作。

Result: Imitator Game上C15预训练下达到80.0%已见任务成功率，超Zero-WAM 12.0个百分点；无上下文示范时LIBERO从96.1%提升至97.6%，LIBERO-Plus从62.9%提升至73.2%；结合示范训练与推理时上下文指导，LIBERO-Plus进一步升至75.2%。

Conclusion: 将机器人自身执行预判与示范经验复用相耦合可显著提升任务成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Demonstrations show how a task can be completed, but differences in embodiment, appearance, and execution speed make it difficult to identify the guidance relevant to the robot's current progress. We introduce JEPA-ICL, a vision-language-action framework that couples predictive planning with demonstration learning. The policy learns to plan by predicting ordered future states in JEPA feature space up to subtask completion, capturing what remains to be accomplished. These plans guide action generation and locate relevant stages in demonstrations, whose JEPA-supervised content features provide progress-aligned context. Learning from this context, in turn, updates the shared policy that produces the plans and actions. This coupling connects anticipating the robot's own execution with reusing demonstrated experience. On the Imitator Game, JEPA-ICL achieves 80.0% seen-task success under C15 pretraining, outperforming Zero-WAM by 12.0 percentage points. Without contextual demonstrations, JEPA-ICL outperforms the VLA-JEPA variant without human-video pretraining, improving success rates from 96.1% to 97.6% on LIBERO and from 62.9% to 73.2% on LIBERO-Plus. With demonstration-based training and contextual guidance at inference, LIBERO-Plus success further increases from 73.2% to 75.2%.

</details>


### [51] [AXE: AlpaSim E2E Closed Loop Challenge Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30511)
*Joonhee Lim,Seonmyeong Lee,Sihwan Hwang,Hanbin Jang,Hiwon Shin,Dogun Kim,Hyelin Jo,Hyeonjun Jeong,Yongjae Lee,Junseong Kwak,Junhyeok Park,Satyam Mishra,Juyeb Shin,Uisung Kim,Hyeonseo Kim,Saparkhan Kassymbekov,Hailemichael Molla Gashaw,Abi Rahman Syamil,Mingyu Kim,Hyeongjun Kim,Yeina Lee,Moogeun Park,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告提出了一套端到端自动驾驶模型的开发流程，包括数据整理、高清地图自动标注、模仿学习和基于模拟器的强化学习，并引入基于物体检测和BEV分割的轨迹候选过滤方法以保障闭环安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确保闭环场景下的安全性，需要过滤掉可能导致碰撞或驶出道路的轨迹候选。

Method: 框架包含数据整理、高清地图自动标注、数据驱动的模仿学习和基于模拟器的强化学习；采用基于选择的端到端规划方法，利用物体检测和BEV分割过滤轨迹候选，并在NVIDIA NuRec和NVIDIA PAI-AV数据集上训练，对固定轨迹候选集进行碰撞、可行驶区域合规性和进度评分，选择通过过滤的最佳候选。

Result: 该方法在模拟rollout中学习评分并选择最佳候选，强调了端到端规划与基于感知的轨迹过滤相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合端到端规划与基于感知的轨迹过滤能够实现安全可靠的闭环自动驾驶。

Abstract: This report presents a comprehensive pipeline for developing an end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving model for the Physical AI AV Track. The framework consists of data curation, HD map auto-labelling, data-driven imitation learning, and simulator-based reinforcement learning. To ensure safety in the closed-loop setting, we introduce a selection-based E2E planning approach that leverages object detection and BEV segmentation to filter out trajectory candidates likely to cause collisions or off-road driving. Trained on NVIDIA NuRec and the NVIDIA PAI-AV dataset, our method scores a fixed vocabulary of trajectory candidates for collision, drivable-area compliance, and progress learned from simulated rollouts, and selects the best candidate that passes the filter. This design highlights the effectiveness of combining E2E planning with perception-based trajectory filtering for safe and reliable closed-loop autonomous driving.

</details>


### [52] [DexDuet: Learning Contact-Preserving Bimanual Dexterity from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30495)
*Haoran Zhang,Haokai Ding,Han Zhou,Zuxing Lu,Heng Zhang,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDuet从单次人类演示中学习保持接触的双手机器人灵巧操作，通过两项交互奖励保留人手-物体配置与接触，并将双手策略分解为共享权重且相互条件化的单手策略，配合自适应课程A-RSI提升跟踪与接触保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要优化物体轨迹跟踪，忽视人手与物体的交互方式，导致机器人可能接触错误物体部位或手指滑动，操作不稳定；因此需要从人类演示中迁移接触保持的双手机器灵巧技能。

Method: 提出DexDuet框架：采用两项交互奖励以保留人类手-物体配置和接触；将双手策略分解为两个共享权重、以彼此状态为条件的单手策略以实现协调操作；提出辅助参考状态初始化A-RSI，一种自适应课程，在演示片段间调整物体辅助和训练起点以克服困难操作阶段。

Result: 在覆盖三个模拟器和五种机器人形态的四个基准上，DexDuet相较对应基线将物体跟踪ADD-AUC提升11至41点，并持续获得更好的接触保持；在122次物体跟踪精度相当的配对运行中，有117次DexDuet更好地保留了人类接触。

Conclusion: DexDuet有效实现了从单次人类演示中学习接触保持的双手机器人灵巧操作，在提升跟踪性能的同时显著改善接触保持，验证了保留手-物体交互信息与协调双手策略的重要性。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a rich source of bimanual dexterous skills, yet transferring them to robot hands remains challenging. Existing methods primarily optimize object trajectory tracking, often overlooking how human hands interact with the object. Even with accurate tracking, robots may contact incorrect object parts or slide their fingers, resulting in unstable manipulation. We present DexDuet, a framework that learns contact-preserving bimanual dexterity from a single human demonstration. DexDuet employs two interaction rewards to preserve human hand-object configurations and contacts. To enable coordinated manipulation, we factorize the bimanual policy into two weight-sharing per-hand policies that condition on each other's states. We further propose assisted reference-state initialization (A-RSI), an adaptive curriculum that adjusts object assistance and training starts across demonstration segments to overcome difficult manipulation phases. Across four benchmarks spanning three simulators and five robot embodiments, DexDuet improves object-tracking ADD-AUC by 11 to 41 points over the corresponding baselines while consistently achieving better contact preservation. Notably, among 122 paired runs with comparable object-tracking accuracy, DexDuet preserves the human's contacts better in 117 cases. Code and videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [53] [EgoStudy: How Does Scaling Egocentric Data Affect VLA and WAM Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30490)
*Puzhen Yuan,Yanjiang Guo,Yichen Liu,Xiang Zhu,Qingzhou Lu,Chenxin Liu,Tun Ban,Haodong Yan,Zhide Zhong,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoStudy系统研究了自我中心人类操作数据用于机器人学习的有效性，揭示了数据处理策略、扩展规律及模型差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据可扩展用于训练灵巧机器人策略，但人机具身差距导致其实际学习效果不明，缺乏系统性控制研究。

Method: 保持机器人数据集固定，系统改变自我中心数据的规模与多样性、数据处理策略及协同训练配置，对比视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）与世界-动作模型（WAM）。

Result: 数据多样性、轨迹对齐和详细语言标注是有效迁移的关键；扩展数据对末端执行器平移预测提升大于细粒度手指运动；数据有限时WAM更优，数据增多时VLA增益更大。

Conclusion: 研究为处理和利用自我中心数据训练VLA与WAM提供实践指导，并明确了扩展此类数据的收益与局限。

Abstract: Egocentric human manipulation data offer a scalable resource for training dexterous robot policies. However, how effectively robots can learn and benefit from these data remains unclear due to the embodiment gap between humans and robots. We present EgoStudy, a systematic, controlled study of egocentric data for robot learning. Holding the robot dataset fixed, we vary the scale and diversity of egocentric data, data processing strategies, and co-training configurations. We investigate three questions: (1) Which data processing and pretraining choices most effectively exploit egocentric data? (2) Which components of robot action prediction benefit most from scaling these data? (3) How do scaling effects differ between vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs)? Our experiments yield three main findings. First, data diversity, alignment between trajectories, and detailed language annotations are key factors in effective transfer. Second, scaling egocentric data yields larger gains in predicting end-effector translation than in predicting fine-grained finger movements. Third, WAMs perform better when egocentric data are limited, whereas VLAs exhibit greater gains as the amount of egocentric data increases. Together, these findings provide practical guidance on processing and using egocentric data to train VLAs and WAMs, while clarifying the benefits and limitations of scaling these data.

</details>


### [54] [OVOGO: Offline Value-Guided On-Policy Generative Optimization for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30477)
*Rongzheng Yang,Jiaming Guo,Yuxuan Guo,Zihao Zhang,Yunkai Gao,Siming Lan,Shaohui Peng,Rui Zhang,Tianyun Ma,Xing Hu,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVOGO是一种面向流式VLA的离线强化学习框架，将离线价值学习与生成过程的同策略优化分离，通过Return-Anchored Ranking Critic和冻结评论家为流策略采样的新动作提供组相对价值信号，在四个设置上均优于初始VLA和基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署期收集的机器人经验可复用，但反复获取新物理交互成本高；离线强化学习可复用经验，而流式策略能在回放观测处低成本重采样生成轨迹，因而有必要将离线价值学习与生成过程的同策略优化分离。

Method: 提出OVOGO框架：引入Return-Anchored Ranking Critic，结合样本内分布价值学习与受保护的局部排序，提升对生成动作的区分度而不赋予人为回报目标；冻结该评论家后，为从当前流策略采样的新动作候选提供组相对价值信号，用于选定转移优化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、DexJoCo、LIBERO及真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，OVOGO均提升初始VLA，成功率高于所评估的离线适应与价值引导基线。

Conclusion: 离线机器人经验可被反复用于价值学习，而低成本生成采样支持策略优化，为流式VLA基于经验的后训练提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action models can benefit from robot experience collected during deployment, but repeatedly acquiring new physical interactions for policy improvement is costly. Offline reinforcement learning provides a natural way to reuse collected experience, while flow-based policies offer an additional opportunity because fresh generation trajectories can be cheaply resampled from the current policy at replay observations. This motivates separating offline value learning from on-policy optimization of the generative process. We propose OVOGO, an offline reinforcement learning framework that realizes this separation for flow-based VLAs. A central challenge in this formulation is to learn a critic that remains grounded in logged robot experience while providing useful value signals for newly generated actions. OVOGO introduces a Return-Anchored Ranking Critic that combines in-sample distributional value learning with guarded local ranking, improving discrimination among generated actions without assigning them artificial return targets. The frozen critic then provides group-relative value signals for selected-transition optimization of fresh action candidates sampled from the current flow policy. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, DexJoCo, and LIBERO, as well as a real-robot dexterous manipulation task, show that OVOGO improves the initial VLA across all four settings and achieves higher success rates than the evaluated offline adaptation and value-guided baselines. These results suggest that offline robot experience can be repeatedly reused for value learning while inexpensive generative sampling supports policy optimization, providing an effective approach to experience-based post-training of flow-based VLAs.

</details>


### [55] [CHAMP: Coordinated Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Whole-Body Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30449)
*Lijun Han,Muyuan Ma,Zichen Wang,Siyuan Liu,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Wang,Bohao Zhang,Heng Zhou,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHAMP 是一个统一的人形机器人全身运动操作框架，通过全身运动预训练和自适应特征提取，实现协调的运动与灵巧操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类环境中运行时，需要协调移动与灵巧操作。关键挑战在于从多样化数据中学习运动先验，并将其有效用于协调的机器人控制。

Method: CHAMP 先在大约 2,298 小时的异构人类与机器人数据上预训练视觉语言模型，以预测未来的身体和手部运动；共享运动表示支持跨具身统一全身运动学习。随后引入协调导向的动作专家（CAE），在目标控制器生成的回放数据上训练，通过稀疏路由自适应选择、变换和融合预训练模型多层的特征，联合生成身体和手部动作。

Result: CHAMP 在七个仿真任务上总体成功率为 84.8%，在 Unitree G1 的七个真实任务上为 91.4%，均优于评估基线；在长时程爆米花服务任务中成功率为 70.0%，最佳基线为 40.0%。

Conclusion: CHAMP 通过全身运动预训练与协调导向动作专家的结合，有效实现了人形机器人协调的移动操作，并在仿真和真实环境中显著优于基线。

Abstract: Coordinating locomotion and dexterous manipulation is fundamental to humanoid robots operating in human environments. A key challenge is learning motion priors from diverse data and using them effectively for coordinated robot control. We present CHAMP, a unified framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines whole-body motion pretraining with adaptive feature extraction. CHAMP pretrains a vision-language model to predict future body and hand motion from approximately 2,298 hours of heterogeneous human and robot data. A shared motion representation enables unified whole-body motion learning across embodiments from datasets with body annotations, hand annotations, or both. To ground these motion priors in robot control, we introduce a Coordination-oriented Action Expert (CAE) and train it on replay data generated using the target controller. Through sparse routing, CAE adaptively selects, transforms, and fuses features from multiple layers of the pretrained model to jointly generate body and hand actions. In experiments, CHAMP achieves overall success rates of 84.8% on seven simulated tasks and 91.4% on seven real-world tasks with a Unitree G1, outperforming the evaluated baselines in both settings. In particular, CHAMP achieves 70.0% success on the long-horizon popcorn-serving task, compared with 40.0% for the best-performing baseline.

</details>


### [56] [ROSE-Drive: Runtime Orchestration and System Evolution for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30438)
*Qihang Huang,Zhidong Wang,Zirui Li,YuJie Yan,Jingsong Liang,Shiyu Fang,Collister Chua,Zhan Chen,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSE-Drive是一个模型无关的运行时编排与系统演化框架，协调冻结的驾驶模型、技能、感知工具与反馈控制器，在Bench2Drive和Fail2Drive上实现能力累积与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶模型在复杂交通闭环驾驶中仍具挑战，除改进单个模型外，系统级编排可通过协调驾驶能力与计算提供互补方案。

Method: 提出ROSE-Drive框架，联合编排驾驶执行与计算：协调冻结驾驶模型与驾驶技能、感知工具和反馈控制器；蒸馏VLM提议驾驶任务与场景关系；共享物理状态与持久任务支持能力组合、任务连续性与自适应调度；编排器跨控制通道融合输出、维持任务责任并利用执行反馈选择控制器、复用运动参考和调度推理；离线修订通过稳定任务接口优化技能及其协调，各系统阶段继承修订而模型权重固定。

Result: 在Bench2Drive v0.0.3、v0.0.4和Fail2Drive上评估四个驾驶模型，显示目标基准修订前的能力累积与迁移；在留出集Longest6上，AutoMoT的DS从25升至52，SimLingo从22升至47，且超过71%的控制步跳过新模型推理。

Conclusion: 系统级编排可在不更新模型权重的情况下累积能力并提升闭环驾驶性能，同时显著降低推理计算需求。

Abstract: End-to-end driving models have made substantial progress, yet reliable closed-loop driving in complex traffic remains challenging. Beyond improving individual models, system-level orchestration offers a complementary approach by coordinating driving capabilities and computation. We introduce ROSE-Drive (Runtime Orchestration and System Evolution), a model-agnostic framework that jointly orchestrates driving execution and computation. It coordinates frozen driving models with driving skills, perception tools, and feedback controllers. A distilled vision-language model (VLM) proposes driving tasks and scene relations. Shared physical state and persistent tasks support capability composition, task continuity, and adaptive scheduling. The orchestrator combines model and skill outputs across control channels, maintains task responsibility through handover, and uses execution feedback to select controllers, reuse valid motion references, and schedule model inference. Offline revisions use execution records to refine skills and their coordination through a stable task interface. Successive system stages inherit these revisions while model, perception, and VLM weights remain fixed. Evaluations with four driving models on Bench2Drive v0.0.3, Bench2Drive v0.0.4, and Fail2Drive show accumulated capability and transfer before target-benchmark revision. On held-out Longest6, driving score (DS) rises from 25 to 52 for AutoMoT and from 22 to 47 for SimLingo across system stages, while both systems skip new model inference on over 71% of control steps.

</details>


### [57] [AgentMoT: An Agentic Mixture-of-Transformers for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30389)
*Qihang Huang,Collister Chua,YuJie Yan,Shiyu Fang,Zhan Chen,Haoran Xu,Zirui Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgentMoT 是一个内置智能体推理与系统调度的 Transformer 混合模型，通过智能体、世界、动作专家形成三条联合训练路径，在 Bench2Drive 上取得 SOTA 驾驶表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶模型需要决定何时使用何种能力、何时重新审视决策，现有方法缺乏对计算成本与收益的动态权衡和系统级调度机制。

Method: 提出 AgentMoT（Mixture-of-Transformers），由智能体、世界、动作专家组合成直接动作、视觉-语言-动作、世界-动作三条联合训练通路；Router 选择原生通路，智能体专家提供推理并选择技能，执行反馈触发唤醒请求，自调度器学习是否重新审视任务决策；以相对能力原则权衡额外计算或干预的收益与成本；采用开环初始化后的双环演化，从闭环经验训练 Router、智能体和自调度器，并由失败驱动更新精炼技能与执行反馈。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive v0.0.3 和 v0.0.4 上分别达到 94.48/92.33 驾驶分数和 85.45/82.73% 成功率；禁用技能时，学习到的路由在 v0.0.3 上比最强固定通路提升 1.79 驾驶分数和 4.09 个百分点成功率；消融实验显示闭环智能体学习、双视图查询和事件触发唤醒均带来增益。

Conclusion: AgentMoT 通过混合专家路由与智能体自调度实现能力与计算的动态权衡，验证了闭环学习与事件触发机制对驾驶决策的有效性。

Abstract: Driving models need to choose which capabilities to use and when to reconsider their decisions. We introduce AgentMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers with built-in agent reasoning and system scheduling. Combinations of agent, world, and action experts form three jointly trained pathways for direct-action, vision-language-action, and world-action computation. The Router selects a native pathway, while the agent expert supplies reasoning and selects skills. Execution feedback raises wake-up requests, and the agent self-scheduler learns whether to reconsider the task decision or keep it. Mandatory requests invoke the agent directly. The relative-capability principle guides these choices by relating the benefit of additional computation or intervention to its cost. After open-loop initialization, dual-loop evolution trains the Router, agent, and self-scheduler from closed-loop experience while failure-driven updates refine skills and execution feedback. AgentMoT achieves state-of-the-art results on Bench2Drive v0.0.3 and v0.0.4, with 94.48/92.33 driving score and 85.45/82.73% success rate, respectively. With skills disabled, learned routing improves over the strongest fixed pathway by 1.79 driving-score points and 4.09 percentage points of success rate on v0.0.3. Ablation studies further show gains from closed-loop agent learning, two-view queries, and event-triggered wake-up.

</details>


### [58] [SOAR: temporal Structure-guided One-step Action generation for Robotic manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30363)
*Di Xin,Mingfei Han,Genming Cui,Yuanxiang Chen,Xiaolong Wu,Siyu Ning,Xiaojun Chang,Zhihui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOAR是一种结构引导的单步生成策略，通过预测动作的DCT表示并利用频谱差异校正轨迹，在保持单步推理效率的同时提升动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步扩散/流策略需迭代推理导致延迟高，而现有单步策略直接消除迭代细化，动作质量受限。

Method: 预测以观测为条件的未来动作DCT表示，捕捉多频段时序结构；直接在动作空间生成干净轨迹而非间接恢复速度场；利用预测结构与生成轨迹的频谱差异进行校正，无需额外评估生成骨干；训练时从校正动作推导MeanFlow速度用于一致性学习，并加入端点目标对齐纯噪声条件。

Result: 在Adroit和Meta-World共53个任务上总体成功率83.9%，推理延迟仅8.7 ms；应用于VLA模型π0.5时，LIBERO达97.2%、RoboTwin 2.0达71.7%；真实世界实验与消融验证有效性和效率。

Conclusion: SOAR在保持单步推理低延迟的同时显著提升动作质量，并可有效增强VLA模型性能，兼具高效性与实用性。

Abstract: Efficient robotic manipulation requires temporally coordinated actions with low inference latency. Multi-step diffusion and flow policies progressively refine action trajectories but require costly iterative inference, whereas one-step policies eliminate iterative trajectory refinement to reduce inference cost. We introduce **SOAR**, a structure-guided one-step generative policy that improves action quality while preserving one-step efficiency. SOAR predicts an observation-conditioned discrete cosine transform (DCT) representation of future actions, capturing temporal variations across multiple frequency bands to provide explicit guidance from key temporal structural information. Unlike velocity-parameterized one-step flow policies that recover clean actions indirectly from a predicted velocity field, SOAR directly generates clean action trajectories in action space. This aligns the model output with the executable control target and provides a direct interface for structural calibration. The predicted structure further corrects the generated trajectory according to their spectral discrepancy, without requiring an additional evaluation of the generative backbone. During training, the corresponding MeanFlow velocity is derived from the calibrated clean action and used for consistency learning, while an endpoint objective aligns training with the pure-noise condition used for one-step inference. Across 53 Adroit and Meta-World tasks, SOAR achieves 83.9% overall success with only 8.7 ms inference latency. When applied to the VLA model, {\pi}0.5, SOAR further achieves 97.2% on LIBERO and 71.7% on RoboTwin 2.0. Real-world experiments and extensive ablations further validate its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [59] [One Policy, Many Bodies: Cooperative Human-Object Interaction with Heterogeneous Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30312)
*Zhihao Cao,Tianxu An,Chenhao Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Stelian Coros,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MorphHOI是一个去中心化的物理仿真框架，通过统一的身体接口和以具身为条件的Transformer，实现跨异构具身、团队组成与交互技能的单一策略协同搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同搬运控制器大多假设同构具身和预定义交互任务，无法用单一物理策略覆盖多样具身、团队组合与新交互技能。

Method: 提出规范身体接口统一观测与动作空间，并以具身条件Transformer根据各智能体及其队友的物理能力调整协同决策；通过任务专用token与轻量微调复用已学协同策略以扩展新技能。

Result: 在已见具身上成功率最高96.7%，未见具身95.6%，新型负载91.4%；并成功扩展到搬箱、楼梯运输和路径点跟随等技能，同时保留原有协同能力。

Conclusion: 单一物理策略可在异构具身与多技能间实现有效协同，统一表示加具身条件化是可行的关键设计。

Abstract: Physics-based human-object interaction (HOI) has enabled multiple characters to cooperatively transport objects, but existing cooperative controllers largely assume homogeneous embodiments and predefined interaction tasks. We ask whether a single physics-based policy can enable cooperation across diverse embodiments, team compositions, and interaction skills. We present MorphHOI, a decentralised framework built on a key insight: effective heterogeneous cooperation requires a unified representation across embodiments while accounting for differences in their physical capabilities. To realise this insight, we introduce a canonical body interface that unifies observation and action spaces, together with an embodiment-conditioned transformer that adapts cooperative decisions to the physical capabilities of each agent and its teammates. To extend cooperation to new interaction skills, we reuse the learned cooperative policy through task-specific tokens and lightweight adaptation, preserving its original capabilities. Our framework achieves success rates of up to 96.7\% on seen embodiments, 95.6\% on unseen embodiments, and 91.4\% on novel payloads. Furthermore, the learned cooperation extends to diverse interaction skills with high success rates, including box carrying, transport on stairs, and waypoint following, while preserving previously acquired cooperative capabilities.

</details>


### [60] [Seeing Together, Acting Apart: Shared Environmental Understanding for Multi-Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30310)
*Haihong Hao,Lei Chen,Mingfei Han,Chenglong Yan,Dong An,Yuqiang Yang,Changlin Li,Minghao Guo,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEER框架，通过构建多机器人共享环境理解来促进协作，而非在策略层面协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作多机器人系统依赖动作协调、轨迹共享或联合策略学习，本文探索能否在决策前通过共享环境理解实现协作。

Method: SEER将多机器人部分自我中心观测组织为共享记忆，通过时空相似性检索相关上下文，并用预测性潜在世界模型编码，将表示作为token注入下游导航智能体。

Result: SEER能捕获与动作相关的环境结构，在专用VLN智能体和通用视觉语言模型智能体上均持续提升导航性能，同时保持单智能体能力。

Conclusion: 协作可从策略空间转移到表示空间，共享环境理解能在去中心化决策下为多机器人提供共享环境上下文。

Abstract: Multi-robot embodied systems are typically designed to collaborate by coordinating actions, sharing trajectories, or learning joint policies. We ask a complementary question: can collaboration emerge before decision-making, by first building a shared understanding of the environment? We introduce Shared Environmental Understanding, a policy-agnostic representation that aggregates partial egocentric observations from multiple robots into a shared latent world model. This shifts collaboration from the policy space to the representation space, enabling decentralized decision-making with shared environmental context. To this end, we propose Shared Environment Encoding and Retrieval (SEER), a framework that organizes multi-robot observations into a shared memory, retrieves relevant context via spatio-temporal similarity, and encodes it using a predictive latent world model. The resulting representations are injected into downstream navigation agents as tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that SEER captures action-relevant environmental structure and consistently improves navigation performance across both specialized VLN agents and general vision-language model agents, while preserving single-agent capabilities.

</details>


### [61] [ConsisX: Cross-View Consistency Thinking in 3D for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30308)
*Yunhe Zhang,Long Gao,Yuedong Fu,Gang He,Li Xu,Chang Wu,Pengyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ConsisX视觉-语言-动作框架，通过跨视角关联建模、实体一致性组织和一致性动作条件化实现3D跨视角一致性思考，提升机器人操作的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角观测提供互补场景信息，但现有VLM基于2D特征相似性建模跨视角交互，导致两类物理不一致问题：不同实体间的跨视角错误关联，以及同一实体的跨视角信息碎片化。

Method: ConsisX包含三个模块：跨视角关联建模（CAM）在共享3D空间引入空间邻近约束以减少错误关联；实体一致性组织（ECO）将跨视角局部信息组织为连贯的3D实体证据以解决信息碎片化；一致性动作条件化（CAC）以任务相关的3D实体证据为条件进行动作预测，实现跨视角一致的动作执行。

Result: 在3个真实世界操作任务和4个仿真基准上实验，ConsisX被证明是有效且有竞争力的机器人操作框架。

Conclusion: 通过在3D空间中整合跨视角关联与实体一致性，ConsisX能够实现物理一致的机器人操作，优于基于2D特征相似性的现有方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models integrate visual perception and language understanding from vision-language models (VLMs) with action prediction for robotic manipulation. Although multi-view observations provide complementary scene information, VLMs model cross-view interactions based on 2D feature similarity, which can lead to physical inconsistency in the form of cross-view incorrect association between distinct entities and cross-view information fragmentation about the same entity. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisX, a novel VLA framework that integrates cross-view association and entity coherence to enable cross-view consistency thinking in 3D for physically consistent robotic manipulation. Specifically, Cross-View Association Modeling (CAM) is proposed to introduce cross-view spatial proximity constraints in a shared 3D space to reduce cross-view incorrect association. Entity Coherence Organization (ECO) is designed to organize cross-view local information into coherent 3D entity evidence, thereby addressing cross-view information fragmentation. Consistent Action Conditioning (CAC) is introduced to condition action prediction on task-relevant 3D entity evidence for cross-view consistent action execution. Experiments on 3 real-world manipulation tasks and 4 simulation benchmarks establish ConsisX as an effective and competitive framework for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [62] [Robust Lateral String Stability and Error Attenuation for Heterogeneous Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30307)
*Sixu Li,Swaroop Darbha,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文建立车队横向弦稳定性框架，提出鲁棒L2横向弦稳定性定义及充分条件，并比较前车跟踪（PT）与期望路径跟踪（DT），提出显式增益的学习前车（LFP）策略以下游误差衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车队跟随车辆依赖前车信息进行横向引导会耦合横向动力学并导致误差向下游传播，需在异构车队、纵向运动变化和局部扰动下实现下游误差衰减并保证横向弦稳定性。

Method: 采用基于弧长的反馈线性化使各车具有相同标称横向误差动力学，将参数不确定性和外部扰动表示为局部扰动；定义以车队长度一致误差界刻画的鲁棒L2横向弦稳定性并推导充分条件，进一步证明该条件保证车队长度一致的L∞误差界；提出带显式增益的LFP策略并刻画车间扰动差异对衰减的限制。

Result: 所给条件可保证鲁棒L2与L∞横向弦稳定性；在所考虑的控制形式下，前车跟踪（PT）会累积横向误差，而期望路径跟踪（DT）可防止累积；LFP策略实现下游误差衰减，且衰减能力受车间扰动差异限制；仿真在车辆异构、纵向运动变化和局部扰动下验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构车队横向误差下游传播提供了可证明的稳定性保证，DT与LFP可抑制误差累积，但车间扰动差异构成下游衰减的根本限制。

Abstract: Keeping an entire vehicle platoon close to its planned path is essential for navigating road boundaries and obstacles safely. Followers may rely on predecessor information for lateral guidance, which couples their lateral dynamics and allows errors to propagate downstream. This paper develops a robust lateral string stability framework to study this propagation and achieve downstream error attenuation in heterogeneous platoons under varying longitudinal motion and local perturbations. Arc-length-based feedback linearization gives each vehicle the same nominal lateral error dynamics, with parameter uncertainty and external disturbances represented as local perturbations. We define robust $\mathcal L_2$ lateral string stability in terms of platoon-length-uniform error bounds and derive a sufficient condition for achieving it. Furthermore, we show that the same condition guarantees platoon-length-uniform $\mathcal L_\infty$ error bounds. For the considered control formulations, predecessor tracking (PT) can accumulate lateral errors, whereas desired-path tracking (DT) prevents this accumulation. We propose a learn-from-predecessor (LFP) strategy with explicit gains for downstream error attenuation and characterize how intervehicle perturbation differences limit this attenuation. Simulations validate the theoretical findings under vehicle heterogeneity, varying longitudinal motion, and local perturbations.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Which: Where, How, and When in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30291)
*Yunhe Zhang,Long Gao,Yuedong Fu,Gang He,Li Xu,Chang Wu,Songyuheng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出W³H-VLA框架，在VLM语义感知基础上增加空间距离与几何结构感知，并自适应引导何时引入空间几何信息，以提升视觉-语言-动作模型的精细操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖VLM特征作为感知源，虽能识别与动作相关的实体，但缺乏对实体空间位置、几何结构约束以及空间几何信息贡献时机的理解，难以满足精确操作需求。

Method: 围绕“哪个、哪里、如何、何时”组织感知与动作预测：保留VLM语义实体感知（which），引入空间距离感知（where）和几何结构感知（how），并通过自适应空间几何引导机制决定空间几何信息的贡献时机（when）。

Result: 在真实世界场景和仿真基准上的实验表明该框架有效且性能优越。

Conclusion: W³H-VLA通过融合语义、空间与几何多层级感知并自适应调控其贡献，能够丰富动作预测的感知信息，提升VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically rely on vision-language model (VLM) features as the primary perceptual source for action prediction, showing strong generalization across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. Although VLM features can identify which entities are relevant to action prediction, precise manipulation also requires understanding where task-relevant entities are located within the spatial layout, how their geometric structure constrains physical interaction, and when spatial or geometric information should make a greater contribution to action prediction. To this end, we introduce {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA to organize VLA perception and action prediction around which, where, how, and when. Specifically, {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA retains VLM-based semantic entity perception which and further introduces spatial distance perception where and geometric structure perception how with adaptive spatial-geometric guidance when to enrich the perceptual information available for action prediction. Experiments across real-world settings and simulation benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and superior performance of {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA.

</details>


### [64] [Skill-Adaptive Noise Scheduling for Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30264)
*Woo Kyung Kim,Gwangpyo Yoo,Eunyoung Park,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaND提出一种技能自适应噪声扩散框架，为每个技能和动作维度学习条件化的噪声调度，并基于Legendre系数聚类实现自适应去噪步数分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略在分层技能学习中采用固定且统一的噪声调度，未将扩散过程视为可学习组件，限制了在接触丰富和灵巧操作任务中的性能。

Method: 引入可学习的技能条件噪声调度，逐动作维度调制扩散过程；采用辅助逆目标从噪声调度导数的Legendre系数重建技能嵌入以防退化；在Legendre系数空间聚类噪声调度，根据重建误差突增点确定各技能的去噪步数。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人硬件上，SaND在接触丰富和灵巧操作任务中成功率高于现有技能学习与规划基线，且所需去噪步数更少。

Conclusion: 将噪声调度作为技能自适应可学习组件并配合自适应去噪步数分配，能有效提升技能学习与规划的性能和计算效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion policies have demonstrated strong effectiveness when integrated into hierarchical skill-based learning frameworks, leveraging offline data to capture multimodal and temporally abstracted behaviors. However, prior approaches have rarely considered the diffusion process as a learnable component, adopting fixed noise schedules applied uniformly across skills, regardless of their distinct behavioral contexts. Such a design fundamentally limits the achievable performance, particularly for contact-rich and dexterous manipulation tasks where different skills exhibit distinct couplings between action dimensions. In this paper, we present a skill-adaptive noise diffusion framework (SaND) which introduces a learnable, skill-conditioned noise schedule that modulates the diffusion process per action dimension, adapting the noising dynamics to each skill. To prevent the learned noise schedule from collapsing into trivial solutions, we employ an auxiliary inverse objective that reconstructs the skill embedding from the Legendre coefficients of the derivative of the noise schedule, encouraging the schedules to remain skill-discriminative. This formulation naturally enables adaptive control over the denoising steps for each skill, where noise schedules are clustered in the Legendre coefficient space, and the appropriate number of denoising steps is determined at the point where reconstruction error abruptly increases. At inference, we identify each skill embedding's cluster from its noise schedule and apply the corresponding number of denoising steps, dedicating more compute to skills requiring fine-grained control and less to simpler ones. In simulation and on real robot hardware, SaND outperforms existing skill learning and planning baselines on contact-rich and dexterous manipulation tasks, achieving higher success rates while requiring fewer denoising steps.

</details>


### [65] [Innata-0: A Native Embodied World System with Model-Agent Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30254)
*Innata Team,Yu Bai,Ziyi Bai,Yuxuan Cai,Chenrui He,Wenhao Li,Zhe Lin,Heng Wang,Hai Yuan,Zhiwei Yu,Yuanhang Zhang,Luyou Chen,Tiezhu Gao,Zheng Geng,Jinbang Guo,Dongxu Han,Diqi He,Börje F. Karlsson,Jintao Li,Zhiyu Li,Xiongkun Linghu,Guang Liu,You Liu,Zhen Luo,Mengsi Lyu,Yufeng Lyu,Zheng Lyu,Quanyue Ma,Chunlei Men,Ziqiang Ni,Tengfei Pan,Yijun Shen,Liang Sun,Shaoxuan Xie,Xiang Yang,Guocai Yao,Hongjun Zhang,Yingli Zhao,Yonghua Lin,Zhongyuan Wang,Huan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Innata-0原生具身世界系统，协同设计世界-动作模型与智能体，实现长程执行与物理控制的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界开放、动态、部分可观测，现有基础模型在长程执行上表现不足，模型与智能体简单集成会导致规划与物理控制不一致。

Method: Innata-0-WAM采用三塔Mixture-of-Transformers架构整合理解、预测与动作生成；Innata-0-Agent通过记忆、验证与恢复协调长程执行；并提出协同设计协议对齐模型训练与智能体执行，利用验证经验支持后续适应与模型改进。

Result: 广泛评估表明在理解、世界预测和机器人操作方面具备强具身能力。

Conclusion: Innata-0是迈向物理智能的第一步，也是更长旅程的开端。

Abstract: The physical world is open-ended, dynamic, and partially observable. While foundation models have advanced embodied intelligence, standalone models struggle with long-horizon execution, and simply integrating models with agents may lead to inconsistencies between planning and physical control. To support reliable and adaptive behaviors in the physical world, we introduce Innata-0, a native embodied world system. Innata-0 jointly designs an embodied world-action model and an agent to combine their complementary strengths. The model, Innata-0-WAM, adopts a tri-tower Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that integrates understanding, prediction, and action generation. The agent, Innata-0-Agent, coordinates long-horizon execution through memory, verification, and recovery. A co-design protocol is further proposed to align model training with agent execution and leverage validated experiences to support subsequent adaptation and model improvement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate strong embodied capabilities across understanding, world prediction, and robotic manipulation. Innata-0 represents our first step toward physical intelligence, and we see it as the beginning of a much longer journey.

</details>


### [66] [RoboAda: Adapting Frozen Robot Policies at Test Time to Physics They Cannot See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30209)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Shuaicheng Niu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Jihun Hamm,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAda在测试时通过机器人自身运动推断物理参数变化，不改变策略和规划，仅校正执行命令，在11种物理偏移下将平均成功率从27.8%提升至62.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略从图像决策，但决定计划能否成功的部分物理属性（如质量、关节弱化、控制器延迟）不可见；部署时物理与训练不一致会导致计划正确但执行偏差，而预先训练无法应对未见变化，现有测试时适应方法要么保留错误命令要么盲目梯度更新可能变差。

Method: 保持策略和规划不变，测试时推断机器人如何将命令转化为运动：每个动作块后，用原始机器人的逆动力学模型将观测运动转换为所需命令，获得免费标签；对机器人响应（包括无变化）的竞争假设按预测这些标签的准确度加权，下一动作块用最能实现规划的命令发送；无需奖励、梯度步骤或变化后机器人数据。

Result: 在LIBERO上评估五个冻结策略、两个世界动作模型和三个视觉-语言-动作模型，11种物理偏移下平均成功率从27.8%提升至62.9%；在Cosmos Policy上比七种测试时适应方法中最佳者高17.3个百分点，且无变化时基本保持原成功率；在双臂RoboTwin 2.0关节空间下，关节目标偏移时成功率从24.9%提升至78.8%。

Conclusion: RoboAda通过测试时从自身运动推断物理变化并校正命令，无需额外训练或奖励，显著提升物理偏移下的策略成功率且不损害原性能。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies decide what to do from camera images, yet some of the physics that decides whether their plans succeed is not visible. An object may look light but be 100 times heavier, and a weakened joint or a lagging controller leaves no trace in the image. When the physics at deployment differs from training, the plan is often still right but is executed as if nothing had changed. Training on such variations in advance cannot handle unseen changes, while adapting once deployed either changes the plan, leaving the miscalibrated commands in place, or updates a robot model by gradient steps that do not know how reliable they are and can make the robot worse even when nothing has changed. We propose RoboAda, which keeps the policy and its plan unchanged and corrects the commands that carry it out by inferring at test time how the robot turns commands into motion. The key observation is that the robot's own motion reveals the change: after each action chunk, an inverse-dynamics model of the original robot converts the observed motion into the commands it would have needed, yielding free labels at every step. Competing hypotheses of how the robot responds, including that nothing has changed, are weighted by how well they predict these labels, and the next chunk is sent with the commands that best realize the plan under them. RoboAda needs no reward, no gradient step and no data from the changed robot. We evaluate RoboAda with five frozen policies on LIBERO, two world action models and three vision-language-action models. Under eleven physical shifts, it more than doubles their average success, from 27.8% to 62.9%. It beats the best of seven test-time adaptation methods by 17.3 points on Cosmos Policy, and largely preserves the policies' success when nothing has changed. On bimanual RoboTwin 2.0 in joint space, it raises success under joint-target offsets from 24.9% to 78.8%.

</details>


### [67] [Counterfactual Velocity Steering for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30204)
*Xufeng Zhan,Yuanzhi Liang,Ye Lu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，对基于流的机器人策略做测试时偏置校正时，任务无关条件下的速度与任务条件速度高度共享（余弦约0.94-0.95），直接相减会损害生成；提出几何校准的动作去偏方法，仅去除与任务速度正交的分量并预测小幅任务相关缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对基于流的机器人策略中测试时偏置校正看似简单（用任务无关条件查询速度场并相减），但作者怀疑这一做法常常是错误的，需要验证参考速度中除有害偏置外是否还包含大量共享传输分量。

Method: 在Fast-WAM与RoboTwin 2.0的50个任务上分析任务无关（平均指令）速度与任务条件速度的对齐度及参考平均范数；提出同状态诊断与收缩分析以确定最优校正尺度依赖于参考信噪比；据此提出几何校准的动作去偏，仅去除与任务速度正交的分量并由指令几何预测小幅任务相关尺度；开展受控的指令损坏与动态视觉损坏实验，以及随机1000次试验扫描和角度尺度离线/在线试点。

Result: 平均指令预测与任务条件速度余弦约0.94-0.95；八个完成状态参考平均范数为个体平均范数的98.3%，共享分量难以抵消。指令损坏下仍达25.1%成功率（50任务，20回合），动态视觉损坏几乎使成功率归零；固定中心正交校正在一次随机1000次试验扫描中达92.7%，基线92.1%，但增益非单调且处于运行间波动内；角度尺度试点离线oracle门控上界94.5%，最佳学习在线实现92.5%。

Conclusion: 共享流分量与训练-服务不匹配是条件校正的核心障碍；不宜激进去除任务无关速度，几何校准的正交去偏提供了可复现的基础，但增益有限且需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Flow-based robot policies generate actions by repeatedly evaluating a conditional velocity field, so test-time bias correction seems simple: query the action expert under a task-agnostic condition and subtract that velocity from the task-conditioned prediction. We show this is often wrong. On Fast-WAM across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, mean-instruction predictions align strongly with task-conditioned velocities (cosine approximately 0.94-0.95). Averaging eight completed-state references barely cancels this direction: their mean norm is 98.3% of the mean individual norm. References thus contain a large shared transport component, not only harmful bias, and aggressive subtraction damages generation. We propose a same-state diagnostic and shrinkage analysis showing that the optimal correction scale depends on reference signal-to-noise. This motivates geometry-calibrated action debiasing, which removes only the component orthogonal to the task velocity and predicts a small task-dependent scale from instruction geometry. In controlled RoboTwin experiments, corrupting instructions still permits 25.1% success (50 tasks, 20 episodes), whereas dynamic visual corruption nearly eliminates success. A fixed centered orthogonal correction reaches 92.7% versus a 92.1% baseline in one randomized 1000-trial sweep, but gains are non-monotonic and within run-to-run variation. An angular scale pilot has an offline oracle-gated bound of 94.5%; its best learned online realization reaches 92.5%. These results identify shared flow and train-serve mismatch as central obstacles and provide a reproducible foundation for conditional correction.

</details>


### [68] [Calibrating World Action Models: From Adaptive System 2 Takeover to Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30188)
*Wenhao Li,Yu Han,Xiu Su,Dehao Wu,Keke Huang,Shan You,Fei Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JiL框架，通过校准世界动作模型（WAM）的置信度，按需触发系统2并蒸馏其知识回系统1，兼顾WAM级速度与智能体级可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM虽快但在困难场景下性能骤降且失败前无预警；基础模型智能体虽可靠但逐步查询延迟与成本过高，需兼顾两者优势。

Method: 1) 为冻结WAM添加轻量判断token，基于二值rollout成功/失败信号，用交叉拟合时序差分学习训练，输出校准成功概率；2) 按控制块以置信度调度，低置信时调用系统2、恢复后交还WAM；3) 将系统2接管轨迹蒸馏进WAM以修复能力缺口，并用已掌握技能锚定与动态置信门控防止能力退化。

Result: 可在几乎无额外开销下预测校准成功概率且不影响动作预测；按需调用系统2在保持WAM级速度的同时接近系统2可靠性；蒸馏后系统1可独立运行并修复能力缺口而不退化已有技能。

Conclusion: JiL通过置信度校准与按需系统2干预及知识蒸馏，实现快速且可靠的机器人控制，弥合系统1与系统2之间的差距。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) deliver fast visuomotor control, yet degrade sharply in challenging scenes and act blindly when about to fail; conversely, foundation-model-based agents generalize and recover reliably, but incur prohibitive latency and cost when queried at every step. To achieve both WAM (System 1)-level speed and agent (System 2)-level reliability--- we propose \textbf{\oursfull (\ours)}, a framework that calibrates WAMs' confidence--- adaptively triggering System~2 only when necessary, and distilling System~2's knowledge to form an evolving System~1. First, \ours equips a frozen WAM with \textbf{calibrated self-assessment}: a single lightweight judgment token reading internal video--action features is trained via our proposed \textbf{cross-fitted temporal-difference (TD) learning} using only binary rollout success/failure signals. It can predict a \textbf{calibrated success probability} at negligible overhead without affecting action predictions. Second, this calibrated success probability schedules control chunk by chunk---invoking System~2 to resolve bottlenecks when confidence drops, and handing control back to the WAM once confidence recovers, preserving WAM-like speed while approaching System~2 reliability. Third, as System~2 intervenes only when the WAM lacks confidence, its takeover trajectories directly capture the WAM's capability gaps. JiL distills these takeovers into the WAM to repair those gaps so System~1 can operate standalone without System~2 at test time, while our mastered-skill anchoring and dynamic confidence gating prevent degrading existing capabilities.

</details>


### [69] [OmniSLAM: Evidence-Authorized Revision of Persistent Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30184)
*Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Liang Li,Zhaosong Li,Lifeng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出地图修订模块，分离历史身份变更与新表面准入，在多视图掩码证据和局部身份竞争下修订持久对象地图，在KITTI-360上提升实例AP，并定位了保守决策的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久对象地图需在保留有效身份和修正早期关联的同时纳入新表面，但现有方法未清晰分离身份变更与新表面准入，易产生错误合并或覆盖。

Method: 将历史身份变更与新表面准入分离；提议合并需在多视图共享/独立掩码解释下检验，且剔除任一观测帧后证据裕度仍为正才允许；新测点通过局部身份竞争分配，并加入掩码见证与种子帧稳定性检查。

Result: 在两个预选KITTI-360窗口上，方法将建筑/汽车实例AP50:90均值从17.259提升至18.762；受控输出干预将增益归因于片段合并、防止有害覆盖和新点局部分配。四个额外空间留出集将均值AP从20.263提升至23.171，但无条件修订略高（23.251）；额外种子帧检查在两组上均无AP增益；330帧车库记录展示了与LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计的离线集成。

Conclusion: 观测支持的修订能在特定条件下改善对象地图，但保守决策仍限制恢复能力；额外种子帧稳定性检查未带来AP收益。

Abstract: Persistent object maps must incorporate new surfaces while retaining valid identities and correcting earlier associations. We present a map-revision module that separates historical identity changes from new-surface admission. Proposed merges are tested against shared and separate mask explanations across views; a merge is allowed only if its evidence margin remains positive after removing any one observed frame. New measured points are assigned through local identity competition, with a mask witness and a seed-frame stability check. On two preselected KITTI-360 windows, the fixed method increases mean building/car instance AP50:90 from 17.259 to 18.762 under a local evaluation protocol. Controlled output interventions attribute the gains to fragment consolidation, protection against harmful overwrites, and local assignment of new points. Four additional spatial holdouts increase mean AP from 20.263 to 23.171, although unconditional revision is slightly higher at 23.251. The extra seed-frame check yields no AP gain over nearest-seed assignment in either set. A 330-frame garage recording demonstrates offline integration with LiDAR--inertial--visual odometry. These results identify where observation-supported revision improves object maps and where conservative decisions still limit recovery.

</details>


### [70] [Trustworthy agentic lunar robotics: safety-constrained contingent planning with a foundation-model planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30179)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出非对称知识级重规划（AKR），将规划器视为不可信黑盒，仅在最小成本感知覆盖验证所有前提后才执行不可逆动作，在月球基地运维中实现零危险动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球基地持续运行需要机器人应对部署前未预见的情况；飞行自主系统遇超出预设程序即安全暂停，而基础模型现场生成的计划无法预先验证，存在不可逆动作的安全风险。

Method: 将运维问题建模为带不可逆动作、错误记录、三值物理证据和昂贵截止时间感知的安全约束条件规划；提出AKR，可逆动作按规划器假设执行，不可逆动作仅在最小成本感知覆盖验证每个前提后执行。

Result: 在21000次蒙特卡洛试验和58次全栈运行中AKR零危险动作，FF-Replan规则在7%试验中执行危险动作；AKR中位运行时间为最快运行的1.10倍（K-replanner为1.50倍）；AKR在任务模型不变下完成21次应急与顺序故障运行中的20次，原PLEXIL及EUROPA→PLEXIL程序均未完成。

Conclusion: AKR在不可信规划器下提供可证明的安全性、感知成本下界与执行终止保证，显著优于现有规划执行规则和传统程序化流程。

Abstract: Sustained lunar-base operations require robots that restore equipment and deliver cargo under conditions unforeseen before deployment. Flown space autonomy executes pre-validated procedures and enters a safe hold outside their scope, whereas foundation models synthesize plans on site that cannot be verified before execution. We formulate these operations as safety-constrained contingent planning with irreversible actions, erroneous records, three-valued physical evidence, and costly, deadline-constrained sensing. We propose asymmetric knowledge-level replanning (AKR), which treats the planner as an untrusted black box: reversible actions run on the planner's assumptions, and irreversible actions run only after a minimum-cost sensing cover verifies every precondition. Under sound measurements, no irreversible action executes on a false precondition for any planner or record, the sensing cost before an irreversible action is bounded below for every sound knowledge-based policy, and execution with a complete planner ends in a verified goal or a certified hold. AKR executed no hazardous action in 21,000 Monte Carlo trials with sound observations or in 58 full-stack runs; the FF-Replan acting rule executed one in 7% of those trials. The median AKR run took 1.10 times the fastest run on the same mission, against 1.50 under the K-replanner acting rule. AKR completed 20 of 21 contingency and sequential-fault runs with an unchanged task model; the original PLEXIL and EUROPA$\rightarrow$PLEXIL procedures completed none.

</details>


### [71] [Quantizing in the Loop: Drift-Aware Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30178)
*Yanlong Chen,Thongthornpatch Chamadol,Yichen Guo,Yunfei Gao,Mohan Liu,Addison Lin Wang,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoopQ是一种面向资源受限机器人的在策略超低比特量化框架，通过在用自身rollout状态校准并结合读出对齐微调，将VLA模型线性层压至2比特甚至1比特仍保持接近全精度的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限机器人上部署大型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型需要极端压缩，但现有训练后量化（PTQ）方法在≤2比特的超低比特下经常崩溃；作者将失败归因于标准流水线中的两个错配：静态离线示范校准导致分布偏移使量化扰动进入未见状态并累积误差，以及最小化逐层权重重建误差是有缺陷的代理目标，因为动作生成只依赖动作头所消费的表示。

Method: 提出LoopQ在策略超低比特量化框架：在量化策略自身rollout产生的状态上校准以闭合环路；再加入读出对齐微调，冻结trellis码，仅优化轻量查找表和缩放因子，使骨干读出与全精度教师对齐。

Result: 所有线性层为2比特时，GR00T N1.7在LIBERO-Plus上达到60.4%成功率，接近全精度的61.8%；每权重1比特时π0.5仍完成80.0%的LIBERO任务；8比特激活下2比特π0.5在嵌入式NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor的整数内核上运行，权重缩小4.1倍、内存减少44%；在真实AgileX Piper机械臂上三个任务均与全精度相差不超过一次成功。

Conclusion: LoopQ通过闭环校准与读出对齐微调，实现了VLA模型在超低比特下的可用量化，在仿真与真实机器人上均接近全精度性能，并显著降低权重与内存开销。

Abstract: Deploying large vision-language-action (VLA) models on resource-constrained robots demands extreme compression, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods frequently collapse at ultra-low bit-widths ($\le$ 2 bits). We trace this failure to two mismatches in standard pipelines. First, calibrating on static offline demonstrations induces distribution shift: minor quantization perturbations drive the policy into unseen states where uncalibrated errors compound.
