Second, minimizing the layer-wise weight reconstruction error is a flawed proxy, as action generation depends only on the representations consumed by the action head. We present LoopQ, an on-policy ultra-low-bit quantization framework. LoopQ closes the loop by calibrating on states from the quantized policy's own rollouts. It then adds readout-aligned fine-tuning, which freezes the trellis codes and optimizes only lightweight lookup tables and scaling factors to align the backbone readout with the full-precision teacher. With all linear layers at 2 bits, LoopQ achieves a 60.4% success rate on LIBERO-Plus with GR00T N1.7, close to the 61.8% of full precision. Even at 1 bit per weight, $\pi_{0.5}$ still completes 80.0% of LIBERO tasks. With 8-bit activations, the 2-bit $\pi_{0.5}$ runs on integer kernels on an embedded NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor, with 4.1$\times$ smaller weights and 44% less memory. On a real AgileX Piper arm, it stays within one success of full precision on each of three tasks. Code and checkpoints are available at this https URL

</details>


### [72] [Confidently Lost: Silent Failure and Edge Deployment of Dense Visual SLAM under Photon Starvation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30173)
*Shrikrishna Bhagirath Rajule,Srinath Neelamagam,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 低光下基于学习双视图几何的稠密视觉SLAM会静默失效：网络在无结构输入时虚构一致的几何并产生虚高置信度，导致关键帧饥饿与重定位误接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习双视图几何的稠密视觉SLAM仅在良好光照下评估，低光下会静默失败，作者希望揭示其失效机制。

Method: 用纯量化噪声帧对与真实暗帧对比较网络跨视图几何自洽性；在暗序列上统计帧消耗、关键帧、因子图边拒绝与对极几何一致性；用关键帧对基线上的同一统计量在ETH3D暗序列上无真值分离崩溃与正常；测试低光适配权重与阈值调整；在足式机器人Jetson AGX Orin上复现。

Result: 9级量化噪声帧对自洽性达0.936，真实暗帧对为0.935；暗序列消耗1605帧仅出7个关键帧、不拒绝任何边且报告满帧率，但超95%被接受对应违反真值对极几何；同一统计量在无真值下可分离崩溃；低光权重与调阈值将跟踪轨迹从1/6提升到5/6但均不准确；四个路线中最暗两条复现该特征。

Conclusion: 网络在黑暗中并非失去置信度，而是制造置信度：缺乏结构时对两视图虚构相同几何并信任其一致性，导致SLAM静默失效；适配只撤回错误几何的置信度而非找到正确几何。

Abstract: Dense visual SLAM built on learned two-view geometry is evaluated in good light; in low light it fails silently. Two frames of 9-level quantized noise, no scene at all, give MASt3R-SLAM's network a cross-view geometric self-consistency of 0.936; a real dark frame pair gives 0.935. Starved of structure, the network invents the same geometry for both views and the system trusts the agreement: on a dark sequence it consumes 1,605 frames, emits 7 keyframes, rejects no factor-graph edges and reports full frame rate, while over 95% of the correspondences it accepts violate the ground-truth epipolar geometry. One inflated statistic is read by two subsystems against thresholds in opposite directions, so keyframing starves while relocalization accepts almost every candidate. The same statistic, read at keyframe-pair baselines, separates the confident collapse from working runs on the ETH3D dark sequences without ground truth or extra computation (in-sample). Low-light-adapted weights take tracked trajectories from 1/6 to 5/6, as does retuning two thresholds; neither yields accurate ones, because the adaptation withdraws confidence from wrong geometry rather than finding right geometry. Replayed on a Jetson AGX Orin carried by a legged robot, the mechanism's signature reproduces on the two darkest of four routes, and which of its faces appears varies with route geometry and sensor encoding. The network does not lose confidence in the dark; it manufactures it.

</details>


### [73] [Toward Open Room-Scale Scanning with Consumer LiDAR via Depth-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30166)
*Minsuk Jang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mobile-LIO是一种完全开源的手机深度-惯性里程计管线，不使用ARKit位姿，在七段iPhone ScanNet++录制中均完成且偏差0.2-2.0米，但精度尚不及ARKit位姿地图（仅达USIBD最粗LOA10等级）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前消费者LiDAR扫描依赖ARKit，其跟踪与地图更新为黑盒，应用只能获得深度和位姿而无法检查生成过程，扫描出错时无法追溯原因；本文探究一种每个阶段都可检查的开源替代方案在手机上是否可行。

Method: 提出Mobile-LIO，仅利用手机深度与惯性数据、不使用ARKit位姿的深度-惯性里程计管线；在七段ScanNet++ iPhone录制上评估，与六个按专用扫描仪配置的开源LiDAR-惯性估计器对比，并利用激光扫描网格评估精度；通过噪声无关渲染定位最大误差来源，并在iPhone 15 Pro Max上以修正后手机代码进行15次重放测试。

Result: Mobile-LIO在全部七段场景完成，与ARKit参考偏差0.2-2.0米；六个开源估计器在42次运行中41次偏差至少4.46米（其中两个还未通过无噪声适配检查）；修正后代码在15次重放中14次跟得上传感器；3米内记录深度与网格中位差1.8-2.3厘米（从17厘米改善）；最大误差场景中噪声无关渲染将误差从1.97米降至0.04米，而记录深度在5-7米处误差达20-54厘米。

Conclusion: 开源深度-惯性里程计能在短时手机重放中跟踪手机深度并跟上传感器，但精度尚不及ARKit位姿地图，仅达到USIBD最粗LOA10等级，且未在设备上实测其精度；比较非受控，不能据此判定优劣。

Abstract: Room scanning with consumer LiDAR today depends on ARKit, whose tracking and map update are a black box: an application receives depth and poses but cannot inspect how they were produced, so an error in a scan cannot be traced to its cause. This paper examines whether an open alternative, one whose every stage can be inspected, is feasible on the device. Mobile-LIO is a depth-inertial odometry pipeline that uses the phone's depth and inertial data and no ARKit poses. On seven ScanNet++ iPhone recordings it completed every scene with 0.2-2.0 m deviation from the ARKit-derived reference, where six open-source LiDAR-inertial estimators run as configured for dedicated scanners had at least 4.46 m in 41 of 42 runs (two of the six also failed a noise-free adapter check, and Mobile-LIO has a speed cap they lack; the comparison is not controlled and does not rone 15 Pro Max, a buildwith corrected phone code kept up with the sensor in 14 of 15 replay runs of three 7-13 s recordings; e estimator before thosecorrections. Laser-scanned meshes give the accuracy. Within 3 m, recorded depth differed from the m on three held-out scenes,maps fused with ARKit poses that the dataset scales and aligns to the mesh la a median 1.8-2.3 cm from 17 cm. Because thepipeline is open, the gap could be examined: in the scene with the largest error, noise-free renderefrom 1.97 m to 0.04 m, sorecorded depth, whose error reached 20-54 cm at 5-7 m, contributed alongside the estimator. An open de tracked on phone depth and kept up with the sensor in short phone replays, but it is not yet as accurate as ARKit-pose maps, whicheach only the coarsestUSIBD accuracy level (LOA10); its accuracy on the device was not measured.

</details>


### [74] [LeadVLA: Learning Follower-Aware Route Execution for Robot Leading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30149)
*Tianle Zeng,Hanjing Ye,Bingyi Xia,Yuchen Zhou,Zibo Zhang,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeadVLA是一种结构化多模态策略，将路线作为显式Path参考，同时学习Pace（路线进度）和Residual（局部跟随者与场景适应），实现机器人引导；并构建LeadInfra数据生成基础设施与LeadBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人引导系统通过显式状态、规划器或模块化接口来中介跟随者理解与物理路线执行；直接用端到端轨迹预测学习这种耦合会使路线执行与跟随者响应相互纠缠。

Method: 提出LeadVLA结构化多模态策略：保留路线为显式Path参考，学习Pace控制路线进度，学习Residual进行局部跟随者与场景条件适应；构建基于Unreal Engine的可配置闭环数据生成基础设施LeadInfra，生成约400万样本训练语料；构建含108个完全留出闭环回合的标准基准LeadBench。

Result: LeadVLA在LeadBench上取得最高任务成功率39.6%，同时保持较强路线保真度和跟随者响应性，并在无需真实世界策略微调的情况下迁移到未见真实环境。

Conclusion: LeadVLA通过结构化分解路线执行与跟随者适应，在闭环基准上优于评估的机器人策略，并展现出零微调的真实环境迁移能力。

Abstract: Robot leading requires executing a route while continuously adapting to a designated follower. Existing robot-leading systems often mediate follower understanding and physical route execution through explicit states, planners, or modular interfaces; directly learning this coupling through end-to-end trajectory prediction can instead entangle route execution with follower response. We introduce LeadVLA, a structured multimodal policy that preserves the route as an explicit Path reference while learning a Pace for route progress and a Residual for local follower- and scene-conditioned adaptation. To make this problem trainable and measurable, we develop LeadInfra, a configurable Unreal Engine-based closed-loop data-generation infrastructure from which we instantiate an approximately 4M-sample training corpus, and LeadBench, a standardized benchmark with 108 fully held-out closed-loop episodes. LeadVLA achieves the highest task success (39.6%) among evaluated robot policies on LeadBench while maintaining strong route fidelity and follower responsiveness, and transfers to unseen real-world environments without real-world policy fine-tuning. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [75] [Value function based Reward shaping for safety in quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30133)
*Janani S K,Manan Tayal,Shishir N.Y Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受障碍函数启发的新型安全导向奖励塑形框架，在Unitree Go1四足机器人仿真中将安全违规率从27%降至0.1%且几乎不牺牲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练时间长，现有奖励塑形方法依赖价值函数，在大状态空间下存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 设计基于障碍函数的安全导向奖励塑形框架，强调简单易实现，并在Isaacgym中对Unitree Go1四足机器人进行仿真实验验证。

Result: 所提策略优于现有基线，将安全违规率从27%降低至0.1%，且性能几乎没有下降。

Conclusion: 该障碍函数启发的奖励塑形框架能有效提升强化学习训练中的安全性，且易于跨环境任务实现。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has progressed from simple control tasks to complex real-world challenges with large state spaces. While RL excels in these tasks, training time remains a limitation. Reward shaping is a popular solution, but existing methods often rely on value functions, which face scalability issues. This paper presents a novel safety-oriented reward-shaping framework inspired by barrier functions, offering simplicity and ease of implementation across various environments and tasks. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed reward formulations, we conduct simulation experiments on the Unitree Go1 quadruped robot in Isaacgym. The policy was better than the existing baselines and reduced safety violations from 27% to 0.1% without almost any compromise on the performance.

</details>


### [76] [RoboThink: Let the Agent Think, Let the Policy Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30111)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Janet Wang,Xingchen Zhao,Zhenyu Zhang,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboThink 将推理智能体与机器人策略解耦：智能体通过指令通道告诉策略做什么，通过反馈通道告诉策略怎么动；策略仅在动作模块中读取反馈，无需重新训练即可在运行中纠正漂移和误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理智能体要么直接接管控制导致耗时过长，要么仅发送指令，但策略只有在新回合开始时才会跟随新指令，任务中途状态会偏离训练分布，而且指令无法纠正运动层面的持续漂移。

Method: 提出 RoboThink：一是指令通道，智能体重写指令使机器人在中途撤销自身动作；二是反馈通道，用智能体标注的 DAgger、角色 token 和反事实反馈，仅从智能体学习，并只由策略动作模块读取；智能体作为插件接入，可跨策略、跨机器人复用。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，Claude Opus 5.5 驱动的 RoboThink 在未知恒定漂移下成功率从 0.09 升至 0.87，随机漂移下从 0.42 升至 0.84，对误听和变更请求的完成率为 0.91 至 1.00；相比 GPT-6 Astra 单独行动每成功需 2.1 分钟和超百万 token，RoboThink 仅需 55 秒和 8k token。在 RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个双臂任务上，恒定漂移下成功率从 0.12 升至 0.74，随机漂移下从 0.31 升至 0.56。

Conclusion: 由智能体思考、策略学习执行智能体指导的分离式架构，能显著提升实时纠错与指令跟随能力，兼顾效率与泛化，且一个智能体训练的策略可与其他智能体配合、随智能体增强而提升而无需再训练。

Abstract: Robot policies act in real time but fail without knowing why, whereas a reasoning agent that watches the robot can often tell what went wrong. Existing systems either hand the agent control, which takes minutes per episode, or let it send instructions. Yet a policy follows a new instruction only from a fresh start, since a half-finished task leaves it in states outside its training data. Nor can an instruction fix how the robot moves. If a drift keeps pulling the hand to the left, "push right, slightly" is not a task the policy has learned. We propose RoboThink, in which the agent thinks and the policy learns to act on the agent's guidance. An instruction channel tells the policy what to do. When the user says "the milk, not the butter" after the robot has started, the agent rewrites the instruction and the robot undoes its own motion, with no training. A feedback channel tells it how to move. It is taught from the agent alone with agent-labelled DAgger, role tokens and counterfactual feedback, and is read only by the policy's action module. On LIBERO, RoboThink with Claude Opus 5.5 raises success from 0.09 to 0.87 under an unknown constant drift and from 0.42 to 0.84 under a random one. It also completes misheard and changed requests in 0.91 to 1.00 of the episodes. Acting alone, GPT-6 Astra needs 2.1 min per success and over a million tokens per episode, whereas RoboThink needs 55 s and 8k tokens. The same recipe works across policies, for both vision-language-action models and world action models, and across robots, from one arm to two arms and a mobile manipulator. On all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with two arms, success rises from 0.12 to 0.74 under the constant drift and from 0.31 to 0.56 under the random one. Finally, the agent is a plug-in. A policy taught by one agent works with other agents, including open-weight ones, and grows stronger with a stronger agent, without retraining.

</details>


### [77] [Agent on Guard: Self-Improving Runtime Intervention for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30101)
*Gengming Cui,Mingfei Han,Jiawei Zhang,Mozheng Liao,Yunkai Guo,Minghao Jin,Tingwen Du,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang,Zhihui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Agent on Guard（AoG）分层框架，通过物理经验学习冻结基础策略与编程智能体的协作时机，在RoboDojo-Sim上显著提升成功率并降低延迟和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略提供学习到的执行能力，编程智能体可推理失败并构建恢复行为，但二者有效结合需判断干预何时有益、策略何时可可靠恢复；该问题本质是时序性的——推理期间物理世界持续演化，有用恢复可能在决策生效前失效。

Method: AoG采用分层框架：演化式外层规划器随物理状态分配控制权，使决策与物理时钟对齐，并考虑观测新鲜度、剩余恢复窗口及推理/恢复/后续执行所需时间；耦合的内层循环联合优化干预时机、恢复技能和交接条件；以下游任务进展、失败复发、最终结果和资源消耗评估恢复，而非仅局部纠正。

Result: 在RoboDojo-Sim上，AoG成功率达50.57%、任务得分57.26，分别超过最强基线11.22个百分点和10.68分；相对GPT-6 Astra智能体，端到端回合延迟降低80.4%，在线token消耗降低85.1%。

Conclusion: AoG将临时智能体解决方案转化为可复用的协作能力，与冻结策略互补，有效提升机器人任务执行的成功率与效率。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies provide learned execution capabilities, while programming agents can reason about failures and construct recovery behaviors. Combining them effectively requires learning when intervention helps and when the policy can reliably resume. This challenge is inherently temporal: the physical world continues to evolve during reasoning, and a useful recovery may become infeasible before a decision takes effect. We introduce Agent on Guard (AoG), a hierarchical framework that improves collaboration between a frozen base policy and a programming agent through physical experience. An evolving outer planner allocates control as the physical state evolves, aligning decisions with the physical clock with attention to observation freshness, remaining recovery windows, and the time required for reasoning, recovery, and subsequent execution. Coupled inner loops jointly refine intervention timing, recovery skills, and handoff conditions. Rather than judging recovery by local correction alone, AoG uses downstream task progress, failure recurrence, final outcomes, and resource consumption to improve both recovery behaviors and scheduling. This turns temporary agent solutions into reusable collaboration capabilities that complement the frozen policy. On RoboDojo-Sim, AoG achieves a success rate of 50.57% and a task score of 57.26, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 11.22 percentage points and 10.68 points, respectively. It also reduces end-to-end episode latency by 80.4% and online token consumption by 85.1% relative to the GPT-6 Astra agent.

</details>


### [78] [Policy-intrinsic Demonstration Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30099)
*Sunjae Jeong,Geunhyeok Yu,Dowoon Lee,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于策略内在特性（无需训练）的演示筛选方法PICKER，通过比较预训练策略在候选演示与目标演示上的错误一致性来选择有用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新目标任务的目标演示很少，需借助其他任务的演示；现有检索式筛选仅看相似性而不考虑策略，性能感知式筛选需训练、耗时过长。

Method: 提出policy-intrinsic curation分支，用预训练策略的前向传播估计候选演示对策略的影响；实例化为PICKER，选择那些使策略产生与目标演示上相同错误的候选演示。

Result: PICKER的成功率与性能感知式筛选相当，耗时与检索式筛选相当，且在不同随机种子下选择稳定。

Conclusion: 无需训练的PICKER在效果和效率上取得平衡，是演示筛选的有效方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a robot policy for a new target task requires target demonstrations, but only a few are available. Demonstrations of other tasks can fill this gap, and demonstration curation selects the useful ones among these candidates. Retrieval-based curation selects the candidates that are most similar to the target demonstrations. Since it does not use the policy, any candidate that looks similar receives a high score. Performance-aware curation therefore estimates the effect of each candidate on the policy. However, this requires training on the candidates and takes a long time. We propose a third branch, policy-intrinsic curation, which uses the policy and still takes little time. It estimates this effect with forward passes of the pretrained policy and needs no training. We instantiate this branch with PICKER (Policy-Intrinsic Curation from Known Errors). It selects the candidates on which the policy makes errors that agree with those on the target demonstrations. PICKER reaches success rates comparable to those of performance-aware curation and takes about as long as retrieval-based curation. Its selection is also stable across seeds.

</details>


### [79] [MotionServe: Efficient Multi-Robot Serving by Exploiting Robot Execution Slack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30083)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionServe 是一个面向共享 GPU 的机器人 VLA 策略服务系统，通过协调机器人观测时机与服务器批处理来减少执行停顿并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个任务专用的 VLA 策略需共享 GPU 推理以降低成本；LoRA 虽可共享冻结骨干并支持跨策略批处理，但机器人推理须跟上物理执行节奏，过早观测会导致重规划干扰，延迟调度虽提升 GPU 效率却消耗执行余量并可能造成停顿。

Method: 提出 MotionServe：MotionExec 依据预测动作块中的运动结构选择观测触发时机，使推理与剩余缓冲执行重叠；SlackBatch 结合每机器人执行预算与估计的未完成 GPU 工作量，决定何时等待、批处理哪些请求以及何时派发。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv WidowX 任务上评估 X-VLA，并在 LIBERO 上评估 OpenVLA-OFT，最多 96 个模拟机器人共享一块 NVIDIA H200 GPU；96 机器人时，平均每回合停顿分别降低 43.4% 和 32.6%，任务成功率分别提升 8.98 和 2.30 个百分点；骨干共享使 96 个策略的权重内存分别减少 97.6% 和 91.1%。

Conclusion: 协调机器人观测时机与服务器端批处理可在共享 GPU 上有效平衡执行实时性与 GPU 效率，显著减少停顿、提升任务成功率并大幅降低多策略权重内存占用。

Abstract: As robotic arms undertake diverse manipulation tasks, a deployment may need to serve many task-specific vision-language-action (VLA) policies concurrently. Dedicating a GPU to each robot is costly, motivating shared-GPU inference. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) allows compatible policies to share a frozen backbone with lightweight policy-specific adapters, reducing model-weight memory and enabling cross-policy batching. However, robot inference must keep pace with physical execution. With action chunking, robots continue executing buffered actions while awaiting new predictions, and the remaining buffered time provides each robot's execution slack for serving its next request. Once this slack is exhausted, the robot stalls until new actions arrive, interrupting task progress. Observing earlier preserves more slack but can lead to disruptive replanning and lower task success, whereas delaying dispatch to form larger batches can improve GPU efficiency but consumes slack and risks stalls. To balance these competing demands, we present MotionServe, a shared-GPU serving system that coordinates robot observation timing with server-side batch formation. MotionExec selects observation triggers based on motion structure in predicted action chunks, allowing inference to overlap with remaining buffered execution. SlackBatch combines the resulting per-robot execution budgets with estimated outstanding GPU work to determine when to wait, which requests to batch, and when to dispatch them. We evaluate X-VLA on LIBERO and SimplerEnv's WidowX tasks and OpenVLA-OFT on LIBERO, with up to 96 simulated robots sharing one NVIDIA H200 GPU. At 96 robots, MotionServe reduces mean stall per episode by 43.4% and 32.6% and improves task success by 8.98 and 2.30 percentage points, respectively, over each model's lowest-stall baseline. Backbone sharing also reduces weight memory for 96 policies by 97.6% and 91.1%.

</details>


### [80] [PushMimic: Learning Adaptive Humanoid Box Pushing via Dynamic Motion Augmentation and Reference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30077)
*Yulun Zhuang,Hanyu Zhang,Jingyu Liu,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PushMimic仅需一个平地运动学参考，通过采样轨迹优化和单阶段蒸馏学习自适应人形推箱策略，在未知箱质量和坡度下调整姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推箱任务中箱子质量和斜坡倾角等物理属性在推动前未知，传统方法需要为每种属性组合单独提供参考动作，成本高且泛化差。

Method: 基于单个平地运动学参考，使用采样轨迹优化将其增强为覆盖不同物理属性的动力学可行运动数据集，并在单阶段训练中蒸馏为单一运动跟踪策略。

Result: 仿真中箱质量5-15 kg、坡度至15度时成功率从57.8%提升至88.8%，手臂推动代价降低22%；Unitree G1硬件实验中在倾斜跑步机上连续推动箱质量至19 kg、坡度至12.6度时能自适应躯干俯仰。

Conclusion: PushMimic只需一个参考运动而非为每种箱质量和坡度分别提供参考，即可实现自适应人形推箱。

Abstract: Humanoid box pushing is challenging when the physical properties of the task, such as the box mass and the ramp inclination, are unknown before the push begins. This paper presents PushMimic, a framework that learns adaptive humanoid box pushing from a single level-ground kinematic reference motion. The learned policy adapts its pushing posture to these properties without observing them. PushMimic augments the reference motion into a dynamically feasible motion dataset over these properties with sampling-based trajectory optimization, and distills the dataset into one motion tracking policy in one stage of training. Simulation results over box masses from 5 to 15 kg and ramp inclinations up to 15 degrees show that PushMimic raises the success rate from 57.8% to 88.8% compared with a baseline that tracks the original reference motion. It also reduces the cost of push of the arms by 22%. Hardware experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid show that the policy adapts its torso pitch during continuous pushing on an inclined treadmill, with box masses up to 19 kg and ramp inclinations up to 12.6 degrees. PushMimic therefore needs only one reference motion, instead of one for every box mass and ramp inclination.

</details>


### [81] [HERO: Hierarchical Region-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30072)
*Xulin Xiao,Shengyi Liang,Mingjie Zhang,Chengkai Wu,Chen Chen,Zhigang Li,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HERO提出分层框架，将全局规划与学习型局部决策分离，用区域级规划提供长程引导，并将图策略限制在可达候选内以降低推理成本，双分支图策略平衡前沿效用与全局方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索在环境规模增大时难以同时保持路径效率与低在线计算；现有分层规划器依赖手工局部视点选择规则，图强化学习推理成本随图规模增长且全局方向仅隐式表示。

Method: 提出HERO分层框架：区域级规划负责长程引导并限制学习型目标选择于当前区域可达候选以降低图策略推理成本；设计双分支图策略，平衡前沿效用与向下一个区域的引导。

Result: 在未见2D地图上取得所评估方法中最低的总体平均路径长度和目标决策成本；MARSIM仿真及三个真实站点部署验证了框架在LiDAR建图和底盘动力学下的适用性。

Conclusion: HERO通过明确划分全局规划与学习型局部决策的职责，在控制在线计算的同时提升探索路径效率，并具备仿真与真实环境的可部署性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration is widely used to map unknown environments, yet maintaining both path efficiency and low online computation remains difficult as environment scale increases. Hierarchical planners reduce global search through coarse regional representations but often rely on handcrafted local viewpoint-selection rules. Graph-based reinforcement learning can improve local decisions, but its inference cost grows with the exploration graph and global direction is usually represented only implicitly. To address these limitations, we present HERO, a hierarchical framework that assigns distinct responsibilities to global planning and learned local decision-making, enabling each level to focus on the function it is best suited to perform. To reduce the growing inference cost of graph-based policies, HERO restricts learned target selection to reachable candidates within the current region while using region-level planning to provide long-horizon guidance. To avoid informative targets causing excessive deviation from this global direction, the proposed Dual-Branch Graph Policy balances Frontier Utility with guidance toward the next region, improving both local exploration and global progress. Experiments on unseen 2D maps show the lowest overall mean path length and target-decision cost among the evaluated methods. MARSIM simulations and deployments at three real-world sites further demonstrate the framework's applicability with LiDAR-based mapping and chassis dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [RoboLiveMem: Is Robot Memory Solved When the World Does Not Wait?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30047)
*Yunbei Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Janet Wang,Ming Liu,Yijiang Li,Yikai Wang,Weirui Ye,Ding Zhao,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboLiveMem 基准通过对比“世界暂停”与“世界运行”两种设置，揭示现有机器人记忆基准高估了模型在动态家庭环境中的记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准在模型思考时暂停模拟器，使记忆在行动时仍然有效，无法反映真实家庭中物体持续移动的挑战，因此需要新的动态基准来检验机器人是否真正记得所需信息。

Method: 提出 RoboLiveMem 基准，包含 29 个任务族、三个层级，覆盖 16 个 BEHAVIOR-1K 真实场景和 ManiSkill 受控任务；每个任务混合真实变化与无害相似物，并在无变化条件下运行，多数任务配有保持变化在视野内的孪生任务，以区分遗忘与行动过晚；在 34,176 个回合上测试 16 个模型。

Result: 发现三个意外结果：(1) 暂停结果掩盖问题——8 个 Claude 和 GPT 模型在真实场景物体变化时，暂停世界平均成功率 97%，运行世界仅 50%，且排名变化，Claude Opus 5.5 从从不失败降至 44%；(2) 速度不是解决办法——较慢模型表现不差，每决策仅 2 秒的模型仍比暂停成功率低至少 36 个百分点；(3) 模型看到变化却依据变化结束前的相机帧行动——行动前重新观察可缩小大部分真实场景差距，但相同物体多次交换时九个模型仍因记忆失分。

Conclusion: 现有暂停式记忆基准严重高估机器人记忆能力，动态环境下的记忆与及时感知行动仍是未解难题，RoboLiveMem 为评估和推动该方向提供了更真实的测试平台。

Abstract: Robot agents built on frontier vision-language models now solve most robot memory benchmarks. Does this mean they can remember what they need in a home, where people and machines keep moving things? Existing memory benchmarks cannot answer this question, because they pause the simulator while the model thinks: nothing can change during a decision, so whatever the robot remembers is still true when it acts. We introduce RoboLiveMem, a benchmark that runs the same memory tasks twice, once with the world paused while the model thinks and once with the world running. It contains 29 task families in three tiers, from 16 realistic BEHAVIOR-1K scenes of homes, workshops and a restaurant to controlled ManiSkill tasks. Every task mixes real changes with harmless look-alikes and runs without any change, so that no fixed strategy succeeds, and most tasks have a twin that keeps the change in view, which separates forgetting from acting too late. Testing 16 models on 34,176 episodes, we find three surprises. (1) Paused results hide the problem: when a remembered object changes in a realistic scene, the 8 Claude and GPT models succeed in 97% of episodes on average with the world paused, but in only 50% with the world running, and their ranking changes: Claude Opus 5.5, which never fails when paused, succeeds in 44%. (2) Speed is not the fix: slower models do no worse, and models given only 2 s per decision still fall at least 36 points below their paused success. (3) Models see the change but act on camera frames from before it ended. Looking again just before acting closes most of the realistic gap, yet when identical objects are swapped several times, all nine models we test still lose points to memory.

</details>


### [83] [Spiking World-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30041)
*Mingquan Liu,Peng Zhou,Shanming Yao,Shuai Wang,Simo Wu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikingWAM，首个脉冲世界动作模型，以稀疏任务骨架作为世界状态，在保持毫米级精度的同时大幅降低能耗，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）预测动作对世界的影响，有望实现通用具身智能，但其模型规模大、每次规划需多次运行网络，难以部署到机器人上；脉冲神经网络（SNN）用稀疏二值脉冲和加法代替乘法可省电，但现有WAM以稠密视频为世界状态，脉冲版本会把大部分计算浪费在背景上。

Method: 提出SpikingWAM，采用任务骨架状态（TSS）仅保留机械臂与任务物体的关键点作为世界状态，并用小型脉冲前端从图像估计物体关键点；通过Body Frame Spike Coding（BFSC）与Grid Coincidence Attention（GCA）保持毫米级精度，利用Persistent Single Step Readout（PSR）使每次规划仅运行一次网络。

Result: 在仿真基准LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上，SpikingWAM以远低于ANN方法的能耗取得有竞争力的成功率，并在LIBERO-Plus上超过所有对比方法；真实机器人实验进一步证实其有效性。

Conclusion: 任务骨架使脉冲网络能以极低能耗实现世界动作模型，为低功耗具身智能提供了新方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict how the world responds to actions and offer a promising path toward general embodied intelligence, but they are large and run the network many times per plan, so they are hard to deploy on robots. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) compute with sparse binary spikes and use additions instead of multiplications, which saves power. However, existing WAMs use dense videos as the world state, so a spiking version would spend most of its computation on the background. We therefore introduce SpikingWAM, the first spiking WAM, which uses a sparse task skeleton as the world state. Inspired by biological motor control, which attends only to the key positions of a task, the Task Skeleton State (TSS) keeps the keypoints of the arm and the task objects, and a small spiking front end estimates the object keypoints from images. Body Frame Spike Coding (BFSC) and Grid Coincidence Attention (GCA) keep millimetre precision, and the Persistent Single Step Readout (PSR) runs the network once per plan. On the simulated benchmarks LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0, SpikingWAM achieves competitive success rates with far less energy than ANN methods and surpasses all compared methods on LIBERO-Plus. Real robot experiments further confirm its effectiveness. These results show that a task skeleton lets spiking networks implement WAMs at very low energy, offering a new solution for low power embodied intelligence. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [84] [Design of a Compact Cable-driven 4-DoF Salamander Robotic Limb with Large Range of Motion and High Load Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30026)
*Zhuowen Li,Qiyuan Fu,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种缆绳驱动的四自由度蝾螈机械肢，通过空间走线将执行器移至近端，在保持大运动范围与高负载能力的同时显著减小质量、体积和惯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两栖足式机器人需在脊柱式游泳与足式陆行间切换，蝾螈肢兼具大运动范围、多自由度和高负载能力，但直驱方案因多执行器需间隔布置或远端安装以避免自碰撞，导致肢体尺寸和转动惯量增大。

Method: 设计缆绳驱动的四自由度蝾螈机械肢，采用空间缆绳走线架构将近端执行器布置与远端关节解耦；通过机械补偿多段缆绳路径、基于模型的前馈解耦和输出侧关节角反馈抑制传动耦合。

Result: 相比直驱肢，质量减少20.4%，投影包络面积减少20.5%，估计有效偏航轴惯量减少31.9%；实验实现髋俯仰、偏航、滚转和膝屈伸的独立解耦控制，关节角范围分别为45°、118°、118°和135°；举升测试中支撑至少5.4倍自重的等效垂直负载。

Conclusion: 该机械肢为蝾螈运动与神经控制研究提供了紧凑且具有生物代表性的平台。

Abstract: A compact yet agile and powerful limb is essential for amphibious legged robots that transition between spine-based swimming and leg-based terrestrial walking, as salamanders do. Salamander limbs combine large ranges of motion (ROM), multiple degrees of freedom (DoFs), and high load capacity. These features enable swinging and body-weight support during walking, as well as folding alongside the body during swimming. Their proximal kinematics can be represented by three rotational DoFs at the hip and one at the knee. Replicating these capabilities within a compact robotic limb is challenging with direct-drive architectures, because multiple joint-mounted actuators must be sufficiently spaced or placed distally to avoid self-collision, increasing limb size and rotational inertia. Here we present a cable-driven 4-DoF salamander robotic limb with a spatial cable-routing architecture that relocates actuators proximally while preserving large ROM and load capacity. Transmission-induced coupling is mitigated by mechanically compensating multi-segment cable paths, model-based feedforward decoupling, and output-side joint angle feedback. Compared with a direct-drive limb, the proposed design reduces mass by 20.4%, projected envelope area by 20.5%, and estimated effective yaw-axis inertia by 31.9%. Experiments verify independent, decoupled control over joint-angle ranges of 45 deg, 118 deg, 118 deg, and 135 deg for hip pitch, yaw, roll, and knee flexion/extension, respectively. During lifting tests, the limb supported an equivalent vertical load of at least 5.4 times its own weight. The proposed limb provides a compact, biologically representative platform for future studies of salamander locomotion and neural control.

</details>


### [85] [FlexWAM: Learning Flexible Visual Routing for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30024)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Zibo Dai,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Zhengrui Lv,Yang Liu,Chen Guang,Xingyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlexWAM通过交替视图内/跨视图注意力、基于观测条件路由的视图路由动作注意力以及反事实视图信用训练的critic，实现了动态调整对多相机视图的依赖，在五个真实操作任务上平均成功率比最强基线提升16.4和23.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在操作过程中会动态调整对各感官模态的依赖，而现有World Action Models虽融合多相机观测，却难以根据当前观测自适应地调整对单个视图的依赖，因此需要让动作专家在整合互补视觉证据的同时优先关注与当前动作最相关的视图。

Method: 提出FlexWAM，包含三个模块：交替视图内注意力与跨视图注意力以捕获视图特定动态和互补线索；用于动作预测的View-Routed Action Attention使用观测条件路由器优先选择相关视图；为校准路由，利用反事实视图信用训练critic，该信用通过抑制每个视图对动作注意力的贡献后动作损失的敏感度估计得到。

Result: 在两个机器人平台的五个挑战性真实操作任务上，使用两种视频骨干网络评估FlexWAM，两个变体相比最强基线平均任务成功率分别提升16.4和23.0个百分点。

Conclusion: FlexWAM能够根据当前观测自适应地路由和优先化多视图视觉证据，显著提升真实机器人操作任务的成功率，验证了动态视图依赖调整机制的有效性。

Abstract: Humans dynamically adjust their reliance on sensory modalities across manipulation stages. Although existing World Action Models (WAMs) fuse multi-camera observations, they struggle to adapt their reliance on individual views based on the current observations. This raises a central question: how can the action expert integrate complementary visual evidence while prioritizing the views most relevant to the current action? FlexWAM addresses this challenge through three modules. First, we alternate within-view and cross-view attention to capture view-specific dynamics and complementary cues. For action prediction, View-Routed Action Attention uses an observation-conditioned router to prioritize relevant views. To calibrate routing, we train a critic using counterfactual view credit, estimated from action loss sensitivity upon suppressing each view's contribution to action attention. We evaluate FlexWAM with two video backbones on five challenging real-world manipulation tasks across two robot platforms. The two variants improve mean task success by 16.4 and 23.0 percentage points over the strongest baseline. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [One Latent World for Heterogeneous LiDARs: A Scan Pattern Agnostic 4D World Model via Direct Ray Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30007)
*Minwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,DongGuw Lee,Yeong Sang Park,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种与扫描模式无关的4D LiDAR世界模型，通过解耦场景与传感器观测，用扩散Transformer生成场景潜变量并直接解码每条光线的命中概率与深度，降低配准误差和深度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR世界模型遵循训练传感器的扫描模式或从中间3D表面/场渲染，前者限制向其他扫描模式迁移，后者导致错误深度和虚假回波。

Method: 将场景与传感器观测解耦，使同一空间潜变量可被同一安装位置的不同LiDAR观测而无需重新生成；解码器直接从潜变量预测每条光线的命中概率和深度，结合全局场景上下文与在初步深度处检索的局部特征；扩散Transformer在潜空间中生成场景，并根据给定自车与物体运动warp观测潜变量以预测4D序列。

Result: 在KITTI-360和nuScenes上生成逼真场景，预测帧间配准误差较最佳对比世界模型降低约70%，解码器深度误差比其他世界模型使用的渲染低19%至28%，生成的扫描有益于多个下游任务。

Conclusion: 该扫描模式无关的4D LiDAR世界模型避免了中间几何渲染的错误深度和虚假回波，实现异构LiDAR间一致场景观测，并在配准、深度精度和下游任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: LiDAR world models generate and predict metric 3D observations in the form of LiDAR scans. They typically synthesize scans following the scan pattern of their training sensor or render them from an intermediate 3D surface or field. However, following one scan pattern limits transfer to LiDARs with other patterns, and rendering from intermediate geometry can cause incorrect depths and false returns. In this paper, we propose a scan pattern agnostic 4D LiDAR world model that decouples the scene from sensor observation, so one spatial latent can be observed by heterogeneous LiDARs at the same mounting position without regeneration. Our decoder predicts the hit probability and depth of each ray directly from the latent, combining global scene context with local features retrieved at a preliminary depth. Without an intermediate surface or field, it reduces incorrect depths and false returns, and as each ray is decoded independently from one latent, LiDARs that share a ray receive the same return and observe one consistent scene. Finally, a diffusion transformer generates scenes in this latent and predicts 4D sequences from observed latents warped by the given ego and object motion. On KITTI-360 and nuScenes, our model generates realistic scenes and reduces the registration error between predicted frames by about 70% relative to the best compared world model. Its decoder yields 19 to 28% lower depth error than the rendering used by other world models, and generated scans benefit several downstream tasks. Our code will be open-sourced. More details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [TeleMultiStream: An Integrated Robot Teleoperation System with Low-Latency Multi-Stream Video Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29980)
*Chenchen Lv,Lingchen Hu,Yaosai Zhang,Chunlan Li,Gengkui Wei,Wei Xin,Li Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TeleMultiStream多流WebRTC视频反馈与UDP姿态下行机器人遥操作系统，在局域网测试中玻璃到玻璃延迟中位数60-66毫秒，优于参考系统约20-27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能模型训练需要大规模高质量演示数据，机器人遥操作是关键采集手段；低延迟遥操作还能让操作员及时接管并在DAgger交互学习中提供纠正演示。

Method: 系统结合多流WebRTC视频反馈与UDP姿态下行，支持异构视频源，采用缓冲复用、有界队列和原生XR渲染减少处理与等待；在ZED Mini有线局域网中对比软件与高性能硬件编码，测试多流配置下的延迟、编码时间和CPU占用。

Result: 单流下软件与硬件编码玻璃到玻璃延迟中位数分别为60和66毫秒，比RobotVision-PC的82毫秒低26.8%和19.5%；低分辨率下两模式视觉延迟相当，三流配置1中位数72和66毫秒；高分辨率配置2中硬件编码将平均编码时间从11.85降至8.80毫秒、延迟中位数从94降至88毫秒，CPU占用约67%对265%；100Hz UDP单程校正延迟1.576和1.800毫秒，丢包率约0.0033%。

Conclusion: 结果支持用于机器人演示采集的响应式遥操作。

Abstract: Training embodied-intelligence models requires large-scale, high-quality demonstration data. Robot teleoperation provides an important means of collecting these data. Low-latency teleoperation also helps human operators take control promptly and provide corrective demonstrations in DAgger-based interactive learning. This paper presents TeleMultiStream, a robot teleoperation system that combines multi-stream WebRTC video feedback with UDP pose downlink. The system supports heterogeneous video sources and uses buffer reuse, bounded queues, and native XR rendering to reduce processing and waiting. In wired-LAN ZED Mini tests, TeleMultiStream's software and high-performance hardware encoding modes achieved dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass latency medians of 60 and 66~ms, respectively. These medians were 26.8\% and 19.5\% lower than the 82-ms RobotVision-PC reference, respectively. At the lower tested resolutions, the two TeleMultiStream encoding modes provided comparable visual latency. Their respective medians in three-stream Configuration~1 were 72 and 66~ms. In the higher-resolution Configuration~2, high-performance hardware encoding outperformed software encoding in latency and CPU utilization. It reduced mean encoding time from 11.85 to 8.80~ms and the dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass median from 94 to 88~ms. Its CPU utilization was approximately 67\%, compared with 265\% for software encoding. During three-stream reception with high-performance hardware encoding, the mean corrected one-way UDP latencies at 100~Hz were 1.576 and 1.800~ms in Configurations~1 and~2, respectively. Mean estimated packet loss was 0.0033\% in both configurations. These results support responsive teleoperation for robot demonstration collection.

</details>


### [88] [SAGE-Drive: Benefit-Aware Adaptive Reasoning for Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29947)
*Donglei Rong,Wenbin Yao,Congcong Bai,Xi Gao,Qiaoqiao Ren,Sheng Jin,Cheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAGE-Drive框架，通过学习预测显式推理对轨迹规划的收益，在推理预算约束下仅对高收益场景调用显式推理，从而以较低计算成本提升规划精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式推理能帮助自动驾驶理解复杂交通交互并支持轨迹规划，但会增加计算开销；且其收益随场景变化差异大，需判断何时值得调用显式推理。

Method: 先对比有/无显式推理的配对规划结果，以规划误差降低量作为推理收益监督标签；再利用无推理初始轨迹的几何特征与多模态场景信息学习预测该收益，并在预设推理预算下仅对预测收益最高的场景调用显式推理。

Result: 在1,497个配对驾驶场景中，显式推理使53.5%场景规划更准、46.5%场景变差，平均收益接近零；测试集上20%预算时ADE为1.5191 m（随机选择为1.5252 m），35%预算时ADE为1.5148 m，接近全场景推理的1.5141 m。

Conclusion: 预测的推理收益可作为有效的推理选择准则，使显式推理仅在能提升规划精度处被调用，以部分计算成本获得接近全量推理的效果。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning enables autonomous vehicles (AVs) to interpret complex traffic interactions and support trajectory planning, but introduces additional computational demands. Determining when explicit reasoning can improve planning accuracy remains a key challenge, as its benefits vary across driving scenarios. To address this challenge, this study proposes Scene-Adaptive Gating of Explicit Reasoning for Driving (SAGE-Drive), a benefit-aware adaptive reasoning framework for autonomous driving planning. First, paired planning outcomes with and without explicit reasoning are analyzed to measure the reasoning benefit, i.e., the reduction in planning error achieved by explicit reasoning, which serves as the supervision label. SAGE-Drive then learns to predict these benefits using geometric features of the initial trajectory planned without reasoning and multimodal scene information, and invokes explicit reasoning only for the scenarios with the highest predicted benefits under a predefined reasoning budget. Experiments on 1,497 paired driving scenarios reveal that explicit reasoning improves planning accuracy in 53.5% of cases while degrading it in 46.5%, yielding a near-zero average benefit. On the test set, with a 20% reasoning budget, SAGE-Drive achieves an average displacement error (ADE) of 1.5191 m, compared with 1.5252 m for random selection. At a 35% budget, it achieves an ADE of 1.5148 m, close to the 1.5141 m obtained when reasoning is applied to every scenario. These findings establish the predicted reasoning benefit as an effective criterion for reasoning selection, allowing explicit reasoning to be invoked where it improves planning accuracy at a fraction of its computational cost.

</details>


### [89] [TidyFlow: Geometry-Aware Tabletop Arrangement Generation with Human-Aligned Tidiness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29918)
*Jiahui Qian,Tiandong Wang,Shubao Fan,Chenyu Niu,Erqi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TidyFlow结合条件流匹配与拓扑感知几何细化，生成符合人类偏好且几何有效的桌面布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面布局需兼顾物体功能关系与有限空间几何约束，现有方法难以同时满足整洁性与几何可行性。

Method: 构建GTL和WTL数据集及六维整洁度评分函数；用条件流匹配学习以物体属性和桌面几何为条件的多模态布局分布；基于碰撞与越界模式构造拓扑感知细化数据，用成对注意力网络迭代修正物体位姿。

Result: 经三次细化迭代，几何有效率GTL从23.21%升至88.91%，WTL从10.58%升至57.91%；整洁度评分与人类排序的Spearman相关系数为0.5543。

Conclusion: TidyFlow能有效提升桌面布局的几何有效性与人类偏好一致性，验证了整洁度评分的合理性。

Abstract: Generating tidy tabletop arrangements that align with human organizational preferences requires considering both functional relationships among objects and geometric constraints within limited tabletop space. We present TidyFlow, a tabletop arrangement generation framework that integrates conditional Flow Matching with topology-aware geometric refinement. We construct two tabletop arrangement datasets based on functional grouping and geometric constraints, namely General Tabletop Layouts (GTL) and Workstation Tabletop Layouts (WTL), and introduce a six-dimensional tidiness scoring function. Conditional Flow Matching is employed to learn a multimodal distribution of target arrangements conditioned on object attributes and tabletop geometry. To address geometric errors in generated layouts, we construct topology-aware refinement data based on collision and boundary-violation patterns, and employ a pair-aware attention network to iteratively refine object poses. Experiments demonstrate that, after three refinement iterations, TidyFlow improves geometric validity from 23.21% to 88.91% on GTL and from 10.58% to 57.91% on WTL. Furthermore, a human preference study demonstrates a positive correlation between the proposed tidiness scores and human rankings, achieving a Spearman correlation coefficient of 0.5543.

</details>


### [90] [MoMa-NMP: Scene-Scale Neural Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29913)
*Adam Kan,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Jiahui Yang,Kenneth Shaw,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MoMa-DemoSmith演示生成流水线，通过分离全局路径发现与局部运动细化，生成大量全身轨迹，并据此训练神经运动规划器MoMa-NMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作器在家庭等复杂环境中需协调基座与机械臂进行长时程规划，而神经运动规划器难以扩展到房间级场景，主要瓶颈在于生成高质量训练轨迹本身就是一个困难的运动规划问题。

Method: MoMa-DemoSmith将问题分解为大型复杂场景的全局路径发现和用于避障的局部运动细化，在程序化生成场景中生成数百万条具有挑战性的全身轨迹；随后用行为克隆和特权在线策略蒸馏训练MoMa-NMP。

Result: 在仿真基准上达到87.0%成功率，在80次未见真实场景试验中成功率为77.5%。

Conclusion: 分离全局路径发现与局部运动细化的演示生成策略可有效扩展神经运动规划器至房间级移动操作场景，并在仿真与真实环境中均取得较高成功率。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators operating in homes and other complex environments must coordinate their base and arm over long horizons, navigating around obstacles, through narrow passages, and between constrained workspaces. Neural motion planners can provide fast closed-loop planning from partial observations, but scaling them to room-sized environments is difficult because generating high-quality training trajectories is itself a challenging motion-planning problem. To address this, we introduce MoMa-DemoSmith, a demonstration-generation pipeline that separates the problem into global path discovery for large complex scenes and local motion refinement for collision avoidance. This key separation enables the generation of millions of challenging whole-body trajectories across our procedurally generated scenes. We train MoMa-NMP on these demonstrations through behavior cloning (BC) followed by privileged on-policy distillation, achieving 87.0% success on our simulation benchmark and 77.5% across 80 trials in unseen real-world scenes. See video results at this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [SuperNav-1.5: Structured Spatial Memory For Agentic Navigation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29912)
*Jiarui Guo,Jinkai Zhang,Yuanhong Yu,Yongchen Zai,Futang Zhu,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperNav-1.5提出了一种由智能体构建的结构化空间记忆（区域分割占据地图+地图锚定记忆节点）和智能体引导的重定位方法，实现跨会话、连续目标的高效导航，并在路径效率上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景级记忆设计存在局限：密集场景图给多模态大模型带来大量推理与上下文开销，图像-文本记录又无法直接转换为可执行的几何路径，因此需要一种轻量且可直接用于导航的空间记忆。

Method: 构建区域分割占据地图，并在地图上标注智能体维护的、基于地图的记忆节点，用于引导探索未访问区域并提供到已知位置的可执行路径；多会话导航中引入智能体引导重定位，由智能体根据当前观测和记忆记录推断区域先验，约束几何匹配以在存储地图中定位机器人。

Result: 在单目标、多目标和需求驱动导航基准上优于基线，路径效率提升尤为明显；多会话评估表明构建的记忆可复用；在真实四足机器人上的部署验证了系统适用性。

Conclusion: 结构化、地图锚定的空间记忆结合智能体引导重定位，可有效支持跨会话与连续目标导航，兼具效率、可复用性和真实平台可行性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of scene-level memory design for efficient navigation across sessions and successive object goals. Recent methods commonly represent a scene using scene graphs or image--text records. However, dense scene-graph representations impose substantial reasoning and context overhead on multimodal large language models, while image--text records cannot be directly converted into executable geometric routes. To address these limitations, we propose SuperNav-1.5, an agentic navigation system equipped with a structured spatial memory constructed by the agent. The memory consists of an area-segmented occupancy map annotated with agent-maintained, map-grounded memory nodes. This map guides the agent toward unvisited areas and provides executable routes to known locations. For multi-session navigation, we introduce agent-guided relocalization, whereby the agent infers an area prior from current observations and stored memory records to constrain geometric matching and localize the robot within the stored map. Together, these designs enable efficient navigation across session and successive object goals. Experiments show that our method outperforms baselines on single-object, multi-object, and demand-driven navigation benchmarks, with particularly strong gains in path efficiency. Multi-session evaluation demonstrates that the constructed memory can be reused, while deployment on a real quadruped robot further shows the system's applicability.

</details>


### [92] [UnderGrasp: Envelopment-Oriented Grasp Refinement for an Underactuated Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29907)
*Zhiwen Song,Ruidi Liu,Guanglu Jia,Zuowei Chen,Hao Wen,Qing Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UnderGrasp是一个针对欠驱动夹爪的抓取后处理优化与评估框架，通过联合编码场景几何和自适应参数搜索，提升抓取成功率和包络率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动夹爪的被动顺应性可实现包络抓取，但现有抓取生成器预测的末端位姿和单一宽度无法充分描述接近-闭合过程，且传统碰撞过滤难以区分有害干涉与可接受接触。

Method: 复用冻结生成器的候选抓取和质量先验，联合编码目标、局部环境、支撑面和接近路径几何，为每个候选生成插入深度、预抓取宽度和目标闭合宽度的多样化组合；结合单独预测的包络质量和交互质量与上游先验进行排序，后端仅用夹爪特定仿真生成的配置和交互标签训练。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，与AnyGrasp结合时，成功率和包络率从0.55/0.12提升至0.82/0.55；与GraspNet结合时从0.42/0.08提升至0.65/0.36。固定插入深度40mm时包络率升至0.35但成功率降至0.52。涉及目标或环境接触的试验条件成功率分别从0.36升至0.89和从0.25升至0.86。

Conclusion: 自适应细化优于固定参数，UnderGrasp能平衡抓取可行性、包络和场景干扰，在接触条件下实现更可靠的执行。

Abstract: Underactuated grippers can form enveloping grasps through passive compliance, while the terminal pose and single width predicted by generic grasp generators do not fully capture the approach-closure process. Conventional collision filtering also struggles to distinguish harmful interference from acceptable contact. We present UnderGrasp, a post-hoc grasp refinement and evaluation framework for an underactuated gripper that reuses grasp candidates and quality priors from a frozen generator. The model jointly encodes the target, local environment, support surface, and approach-path geometry to propose diverse combinations of insertion depth, pre-grasp width, and target closing width for each candidate. These actions are ranked by combining separately predicted envelopment and interaction quality with upstream priors, balancing grasp feasibility, envelopment, and scene disturbance rather than rejecting all environmental contact. The back end is trained solely on configuration and interaction labels generated by gripper-specific simulations, without real-robot grasp labels. Real-robot experiments with an underactuated gripper show that UnderGrasp improves the overall success rate (SR) and the envelopment rate (ER) among successful grasps from 0.55/0.12 to 0.82/0.55 with AnyGrasp, and from 0.42/0.08 to 0.65/0.36 with GraspNet. In contrast, increasing the fixed insertion depth to 40 mm improves ER to 0.35 but reduces SR to 0.52, supporting the value of adaptive refinement. For trials involving target or environmental contact during approach, the conditional success rates increase from 0.36 to 0.89 and from 0.25 to 0.86, respectively, demonstrating more reliable execution under contact. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Motion Mamba 2.0: Autoregressive Diffusion Mamba for Long-Horizon Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29897)
*Yue Huang,Tianshan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Jayeon Kang,Akide Liu,Sungjoon Choi,Dacheng Tao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Motion Mamba 2.0 是一种基于 Mamba 的根-身体扩散架构，结合局部注意力、可恢复循环状态与持久内容记忆，实现长时程实时可控的人体运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散方法逐帧生成运动，但受限的注意力缓存无法访问远期历史，且重复去噪带来显著推理开销，难以满足交互式动画与人形机器人对长时程实时可控运动生成的需求。

Method: 提出 Motion Mamba 2.0：以 Mamba 循环状态压缩运动历史，结合状态变化与文本条件特征构建持久内容记忆（PCM），生成可被局部注意力访问的动态内容槽；并设计定制执行方案，融合 CUDA Graph 重放、算子融合与执行流简化，同时保留规范历史更新。

Result: 在重定向 HiPHI 上实现最长 39.52 秒 rollout 的精确终端控制；在 BONES Seed 与重定向 HumanML3D 上保持强空间控制与文本-运动匹配；RTX 4090 上完整窗口延迟中位数由 1685.35 ms 降至 276.14 ms，配对加速中位数 6.138 倍且输出与提交状态一致；Unitree G1 实机验证了顺序文本引导动作组合、表现性运动及键鼠交互行走。

Conclusion: 该架构与执行优化在保证输出一致的前提下显著提升长时程可控运动生成的效率与实时性，并具备实机部署能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon, real-time controllable motion generation is essential for interactive animation and humanoid robotics, where an agent must respond to changing instructions and spatial goals while moving. Existing autoregressive diffusion methods generate motion incrementally, but bounded attention caches limit access to distant history, while repeated denoising introduces substantial inference overhead. We present Motion Mamba 2.0, a Mamba-based root-body diffusion architecture that integrates local attention, resumable recurrent states, and persistent content memory (PCM) for long-horizon controllable motion generation. PCM transforms compressed motion history from Mamba recurrent states, together with state changes and text-conditioned features, into dynamically updated content slots accessible to local attention. To enable efficient inference, we further develop an execution scheme tailored to this architecture, combining CUDA Graph replay, fused kernels, and execution-flow simplification while preserving the canonical history update. Evaluations on retargeted HiPHI demonstrate accurate terminal control over rollouts of up to 39.52 s, while short-horizon comparisons on BONES Seed and retargeted HumanML3D show that the model also retains strong spatial control and text-motion matching. On an RTX 4090, the optimized execution reduces median complete-window latency from 1685.35 to 276.14 ms, achieving a median paired speedup of 6.138$\times$ while preserving identical outputs and committed states in the tested rollouts. Moreover, we conduct extensive real-world evaluation on the Unitree G1, demonstrating sequential text-guided action composition, expressive motion, and interactive locomotion through keyboard and mouse control.

</details>


### [94] [ProMix: Efficient Data Mixture Optimization for Robotic Imitation via Proxy-Reference DRO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29873)
*Qie Sima,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProMix是一种计算高效的代理-参考DRO方法，通过15M参数代理模型学习域采样权重，并可迁移至高达7B参数的VLA骨干网络，在Open-X数据集上将平均成功率从68.4%提升至76.2%，同时将混合优化成本降至11 GPU小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在异构机器人、任务和环境数据朴素混合训练时性能饱和，而Re-Mix等分布鲁棒数据混合优化需在全语料上训练完整策略两次，计算成本过高，多数实验室难以承受。

Method: 提出ProMix，即代理-参考DRO配方在机器人模仿学习中的高效实现：用15M参数代理模型通过最小化对冻结参考模型的最坏情况超额损失来学习各域采样权重，学习到的权重无需重新调参即可迁移到最大7B参数的VLA骨干；并首次对参考模型在机器人DRO中的作用进行受控分析，发现去除参考模型会因噪声低质量域导致过度悲观（平均-3.8%、最差域-14.3%成功率），且需要熵正则化防止权重崩溃。

Result: 在Open-X Embodiment数据集的9500个真实世界回合上评估，ProMix将平均成功率从均匀混合的68.4%提升至76.2%，在评估噪声范围内与Re-Mix相当，最差域绝对提升+17.1%；混合优化成本降至与骨干无关的11 GPU小时，而50M对比规模为16 GPU小时、7B全规模搜索为192 GPU小时。

Conclusion: ProMix以显著降低的计算成本实现了与Re-Mix相当的数据混合优化效果，并具备跨骨干规模的可迁移性，为资源有限的实验室提供了可行的分布鲁棒数据混合方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in open-world manipulation, yet their performance often saturates when training data are naively pooled across heterogeneous robots, tasks, and environments. Distributionally robust data mixture optimization, exemplified by Re-Mix, can recover much of this performance, but its weight search requires training full-scale policies twice on the entire corpus, placing it out of reach for most laboratories. We present ProMix, a compute-efficient instantiation of the proxy-reference DRO recipe for robotic imitation learning: a 15M-parameter proxy learns per-domain sampling weights by minimizing worst-case excess loss against a frozen reference model, and the learned weights transfer, without re-tuning, to VLA backbones up to 7B parameters. We further provide, to our knowledge, the first controlled analysis of the reference model's role in robotic DRO, showing that removing it induces over-pessimism toward noisy, low-quality domains (-3.8% average and -14.3% worst-domain success rate), and that entropy regularization is needed to prevent weight collapse. Evaluated on 9500 real-world episodes from the Open-X Embodiment dataset, ProMix improves the average success rate over uniform mixing from 68.4% to 76.2%, matching Re-Mix within evaluation noise while reducing mixture-optimization cost to a backbone-independent 11 GPU-hours - versus 16 GPU-hours at the 50M comparison scale and 192 GPU-hours at 7B for full-scale searches - with a +17.1% absolute gain on the worst-case domain.

</details>


### [95] [Arcadian: Open-Source Humanoid Ecosystem for Accessible and Customizable Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29868)
*Zelin Shen,Gabriel Ramos Salinas,Qiyu Zhang,Yue Qin,Chen Qian,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Arcadian是一个开源人形机器人生态系统，通过金属嵌入式3D打印实现高性价比、可定制的一米级人形机器人，并公开全部软硬件资源与社区支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向制造资源和工程支持有限的研究与教学团队，降低人形机器人复现与改造的门槛，在成本、结构耐久性和可定制性之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用金属嵌入式3D打印制造一米级人形机器人，避免CNC加工并保留设计灵活性；通过仿真与硬件测试验证设计，包括将仿真训练的强化学习行走策略迁移到硬件，以及用VR遥操作接口驱动双臂完成简单操作任务；公开CAD模型、物料清单、装配文档及训练部署代码，并通过社区论坛和Discord收集反馈。

Result: 机器人高约一米，建造成本约6800美元；强化学习行走策略成功从仿真迁移到硬件，VR遥操作可驱动双臂完成简单操作任务；全部设计、装配与代码资源已公开。

Conclusion: Arcadian以低成本、高强度和完全可定制的开源生态，为有限资源团队提供了可复现、可修改的人形机器人平台，有效降低了研究与教学门槛。

Abstract: Reproducing and modifying a meter-scale humanoid remains challenging for research and teaching groups with limited fabrication resources and engineering support. This paper presents Arcadian, an open-source humanoid ecosystem that offers accessible and customizable humanoid hardware and software with community support. Arcadian aims to strike a balance among cost-effectiveness, structural durability, and full customizability, lowering the barrier to entry for research and education. Specifically, this meter-scale humanoid adopts metal-embedded 3D printing to achieve a high strength-to-cost ratio, eliminating the need for computer numerical control (CNC) machining while retaining design flexibility. Extensive simulation and hardware tests validate the design: a reinforcement-learning (RL) locomotion policy trained in simulation transfers to the hardware, and a virtual reality (VR) teleoperation interface drives both arms for simple manipulation tasks. The robot is around one meter tall and costs approximately $6,800 to build. All computer-aided design (CAD) models, the bill of materials, assembly documentation, and training and deployment code are publicly released, distributed through this http URL and MakerWorld and supported by a Discord server and a project forum through which community feedback informs design updates. The ecosystem resources are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [96] [How Should a Home Robot Update Its Memories When Human Routines Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29818)
*Oliver Wang,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在模拟家庭中比较三种物体记忆方法，发现保留原始观测历史比仅用语言模型摘要更能适应反复变化的生活习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需要记住物体位置，但人的作息会变化后又恢复，现有基于语言模型摘要的记忆评估都假设静态环境。

Method: 构建居民生病、康复、再病倒的模拟家庭，比较三种方法：保留物体所有出现房间的规则、仅存文字摘要的语言模型记忆、可读取原始观测历史的语言模型记忆。

Result: 规则法优于所有仅摘要方法；提供观测历史可缩小差距；在历史上再加摘要无可测增益；疾病复发时保留历史的方法搜索表现优于首次，仅摘要方法无一致提升；删除首次疾病记忆会消除规则法全部增益和部分其他方法增益；摘要很少区分病日与常规模式。

Conclusion: 常见机器人记忆摘要方法难以记住可能回归的作息事实，未来基准需评估长期部署中的这一方面。

Abstract: A home robot that helps with chores needs to know where objects are, but people move objects according to routines that change and later change back. The robot's memory must adapt to the new routine without erasing experience that may become useful again. One approach is to have a language model maintain written summaries of the home from the robot's own observations, but existing evaluations of such memories use static worlds. We explore simulated households where a resident gets sick, recovers, and later gets sick again. We compare a rule that keeps every room an object has been seen in, language-model memories that store only written summaries, and language-model memories that can also read the raw history of sightings. The rule outperforms every summary-only memory, and giving language models the sighting history closes the gap. Surprisingly, writing summaries on top of the history adds nothing we can measure. When the illness returns, methods that keep the history search better than they did the first time, while summary-only methods show no consistent improvement. Deleting the first illness from memory removes all of the rule's gain and part of theirs. The summaries recorded where objects went during the illness but rarely distinguished sick-day patterns from normal routines. Our findings suggest that common approaches to summarizing robot memories struggle to remember facts about a routine that might return, highlighting the need for future benchmarks to evaluate this aspect of long-term robot deployment.

</details>


### [97] [Superellipse Function: A Powerful Geometric Tool for the Design and Deformation Modeling of Tendon-Driven Continuum Manipulators With Flexural Patterns Experiencing Tension Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29801)
*Yang Liu,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出以超椭圆函数作为肌腱驱动连续体机械臂（TD-CM）柔性图案的几何建模工具，并建立考虑分布式与集中摩擦的张力损失模型，提出设计空间与设计曲线以支持直观设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD-CM的柔性图案和截面几何设计缺乏通用且直观的建模工具，且部分约束肌腱引起的摩擦会导致张力损失和变形偏差，需建立相应数学框架支持设计。

Method: 采用超椭圆函数描述不连续截面几何的柔性图案，建立四个关联几何参数、运动学与变形行为的功能映射；提出互补数学框架与求解算法以建模分布式和集中摩擦力对张力损失和变形的影响，并引入设计空间与设计曲线概念。

Result: 通过基于超椭圆函数设计制造的TD-CM进行多种仿真与实验研究，采用不同误差指标分析比较了框架性能。

Conclusion: 超椭圆函数结合所提摩擦建模框架和设计空间/曲线概念，可实现TD-CM柔性图案的通用几何建模与张力损失下的直观设计。

Abstract: In this article, we feature the Superellipse function as a geometric and versatile tool for effective design and modeling of flexural patterns (FPs) in tendon-driven continuum manipulators (TD-CMs) with discontinuous cross section geometries. We also propose a unique complementary mathematical framework and solution algorithm for modeling the effects of distributed and concentrated friction forces- caused by partially-constrained tendons- on tension loss and deformation behavior of a TD-CM designed using a Superellipse function. Particularly, by employing this geometric function, we establish four intuitive functional mappings relating the manipulator's geometric parameters, kinematics, and deformation behavior. Thanks to the features of these intuitive mappings and Superellipse function, we then introduce the concepts of design space and design curve to facilitate intuitive design of a TD-CM with a generic FP geometry experiencing tendon tension loss. To thoroughly evaluate the performance of this novel framework and its aforementioned features, we performed various simulation and experimental studies on TD-CMs designed and fabricated based on a Superellipse function. Results were then analyzed and compared using different error metrics.

</details>


### [98] [Generating Safety-Critical Driving Scenarios from Role-Typed Signal Temporal Logic Specifications Drafted by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29800)
*Zifan Zeng,Qunli Zhang,Peng Wang,Shiming Liu,Feng Liu,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信号时序逻辑规范与类型检查的引导扩散生成安全关键驾驶场景框架，无需为每种场景类型单独调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录驾驶数据中安全关键场景稀少，需生成用于测试和训练自动驾驶运动规划器；现有引导扩散中每种场景需独立加权目标且与被测车辆优化耦合，攻击可被抵消。

Method: 将每个场景表述为带角色类型子句的信号时序逻辑规范，每个子句限定可修改和可读取的智能体，被测车辆仅从攻击开始时刻读取攻击者；用大语言模型起草规范并由类型检查器接受，编译器据此推导优化：对扩散模型样本做满足规范的最小修改，各子句仅移动其所有者，攻击者先针对未反应车辆求解再考虑其响应，且用同一规范的监控器判定接受。

Result: 在nuScenes和CommonRoad上，独立于规范评判，单一参数集产生的有效场景率比按场景类型调优的加权目标高0.14；在公开数据可表达的16种预碰撞类型中，有11种可由描述生成至少一个有效场景；学习规划器在生成场景上失败率高于其种子场景；微调可降低规划器在关键场景上的失败率，增益大小由标签中某子句要求的时间车距而非生成器决定。

Conclusion: 该框架通过类型化信号时序逻辑规范与编译器实现无需逐场景调参的有效安全关键场景生成，并能提升规划器在关键场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are rare in recorded driving and must be generated to test and train motion planners of automated vehicles. In guided diffusion models, each scenario type needs its own weighted objective, separate from the validity check. Optimized together with the attacker, the vehicle under test can cancel the attack or draw it toward itself. We state each scenario as a signal temporal logic specification with clauses typed by role. Every clause names the agent it may change and the agents it may read, and the vehicle under test reads the attacker only from the attack onset. A large language model drafts the specification from a description, and a type checker accepts it. A compiler derives the optimization: it edits a diffusion model's samples by the smallest change that satisfies the specification, each clause moves only its owner, the attacker is solved against the unreacted vehicle under test before its response, and the monitor of the same specification decides acceptance. On nuScenes and CommonRoad, judged independently of the specification, one parameter set produced valid scenarios at a rate 0.14 above a weighted objective tuned per scenario type, and descriptions yielded at least one valid scenario for 11 of the 16 pre-crash types expressible in the public data. Learned planners fail more often on the generated scenarios than on their seeds. Fine-tuning on them lowers a planner's failure rate on critical scenarios, and the time headway one clause of the label requires, not the generator, sets the size of the gain.

</details>


### [99] [Into My Arms: Structured reinforcement learning for autonomous transport of objects and humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29771)
*Elle Miller,Reem Almheiri,Takuma Tsukakoshi,Ben Sanati,Tamon Miyake,Yushi Wang,Namiko Saito,Shigeki Sugano,Tetsuya Ogata,Rika Antonova,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三种结构化引导方法（SIE、IGT、HOP），使强化学习能高效完成高维、长时程、接触密集的人形全身操作任务，并首次实现无干预的人形机器人从床到轮椅的转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形操作受高维状态-动作空间、接触丰富环境和长任务时程三重难题制约，现有强化学习方法难以可靠解决。

Method: 引入稀疏交互边（SIE）用于全身操作、交互门控运输（IGT）用于长时程持续运输、提示物体路径（HOP）用于缓解碰撞与搜索复杂度；策略仍自由探索操作策略，方法从刚性物体泛化到人体转移。

Result: 首次由人形机器人在执行中无人工干预完成医学假人从床到轮椅的转移；单一策略可适应体重、体型显著变化及随机力扰动，单GPU训练不到一小时。

Conclusion: 该方法为完全由强化学习驱动的人形操作提供了可行路径，可推动能帮助人类的辅助机器人发展。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation remains bottlenecked by high-dimensional state-action spaces, contact-rich environments, and long task horizons. This trilemma is a challenge that state-of-the-art reinforcement learning (RL) methods are yet to resolve reliably. We address it with three contributions that add just enough structure to guide exploration: Sparse Interaction Edges (SIE) for whole-body manipulation, Interaction Gated Transport (IGT) for sustained transport over long horizons, and a Hinted Object Path (HOP) that resolves collision and search complexity bottlenecks. Across all three, the policy remains free to discover its own manipulation strategy. The method generalises from rigid object to human transport. As a flagship demonstration, we show, to our knowledge, the first bed-to-wheelchair transfer of a medical mannequin by a humanoid robot completed with no human intervention during execution. In the domain of human transfer, we show that a single policy can adapt across substantial variations in body mass, size, and random force perturbations, all while training on a single GPU in under an hour. Our approach offers a pathway to fully RL-driven humanoid manipulation, enabling assistive robots that can help humans. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [100] [CarlHOI: Counterfactual Predictive Imagination for Generalizable Multi-Humanoid Object Interaction under Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29749)
*Wenjie Lin,Jin Wei-Kocsis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarlHOI提出一种反事实预测想象框架，基于反事实锚定的关系潜世界模型（CarlWM），使预训练多足式人形机器人团队无需重新训练即可适应未见的环境约束，完成协同搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基人形机器人协同控制虽已进展，但学习到的协作行为难以适应未见环境约束，尤其是需要协调绕行与团队构型变化的障碍场景。

Method: 将共享物体、个体人形机器人与环境障碍显式建模为交互实体；离线使用反事实模拟器轨迹训练CarlWM；在不同共享路点指令下想象替代性集体未来，预测团队与物体的耦合响应；通过预测进展、失败、接触风险及长时价值评估，选择自适应引导指令，同时保留预训练协同控制器。

Result: 构建含三个障碍约束搬运任务的基准；仅用四个机器人训练，却无需重训即可泛化至2至10个机器人团队、未见障碍几何与布局、未见物体及新约束组合；在多种配置下协同搬运成功率优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 反事实预测想象可将预训练多机器人协同技能扩展到原始训练条件之外，提升对未见环境约束的适应与泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in physics-based humanoid control enable teams of varying sizes to coordinate their movements and cooperatively transport shared objects. However, adapting learned cooperative behaviors to unseen environmental constraints remains challenging, particularly when obstacles require coordinated detours and changes in team configuration. We propose CarlHOI, a counterfactual predictive imagination framework built upon a counterfactual-anchored relational latent world model (CarlWM) for human-object interaction (HOI), enabling multi-humanoid teams to adapt to unseen environmental constraints without retraining their underlying cooperative control policies. CarlHOI explicitly represents the shared object, individual humanoids, and environmental obstacles as interacting entities. Trained offline using counterfactual simulator rollouts, the model imagines alternative collective futures under different shared waypoint commands, predicting how the coupled humanoid team and object would respond. By evaluating these imagined outcomes through predicted progress, failure, and contact risks, and long-horizon value estimates, CarlHOI selects adaptive guidance commands while preserving the pretrained cooperative controller. We introduce a benchmark comprising three challenging obstacle-constrained transport tasks to evaluate adaptation and generalization. Despite being trained exclusively with four humanoids, our model generalizes without retraining to teams of two to ten humanoids, unseen obstacle geometries and placements, unseen objects, and novel combinations of environmental constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved cooperative transport success over evaluated baselines across diverse configurations. Results highlight the potential of counterfactual predictive imagination to extend pretrained multi-humanoid cooperative skills beyond their original training conditions.

</details>


### [101] [Performance Evaluation of Nonlinear Model Predictive and Sliding Mode Controllers on a 4-Cable Spatial CSPR in Simscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29748)
*Erfun Bayat Sarmadi,Ali Asadi,Moosa Ayati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对四索驱动的空间索悬挂并联机器人进行了非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）与滑模控制（SMC）的全面对比研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在比较NMPC与SMC在高精度、受限的空间索悬挂并联机器人上的控制性能，并验证基于物理的仿真作为先进控制器验证工具的价值。

Method: 机器人采用两种并行建模方式：含索弹性和摩擦损失的解析动态模型，以及MATLAB Simscape Multibody物理仿真模型；两种控制器在相同参考轨迹、系统约束和调参策略下实现。

Result: NMPC相比SMC实现了更优的跟踪精度和更强的抗扰能力，但计算需求更高；解析模型与Simscape模型在两种控制方案下结果高度一致，验证了解析模型的准确性及其与物理仿真的一致性。

Conclusion: NMPC在所研究的高精度、受限空间索悬挂并联机器人上性能更优，基于物理的仿真可作为验证先进控制器在真实条件下性能的有效补充工具。

Abstract: This paper offers a comprehensive comparative study between Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) and Sliding Mode Control (SMC) applied to a spatial Cable-Suspended Parallel Robot (CSPR) actuated by four cables. The robot is modeled in two parallel ways: (i) via an analytical dynamic model with included cable elasticity and frictional losses, and (ii) in MATLAB Simscape Multibody, capturing realistic physical effects. Both controllers are implemented under identical reference trajectories, system constraints, and tuning strategies. Our results show that NMPC achieves superior tracking accuracy and stronger disturbance rejection compared to SMC, but at the cost of higher computational demand. Moreover, both controllers were tested on an analytical model of the robot and a Simscape model. The close agreement between the two, for both control schemes, confirms the accuracy of the analytical formulation and its consistency with the physics-based simulation. The study highlights the value of physics-based simulation as a complementary tool for validating advanced controllers under realistic conditions, and demonstrates that NMPC offers superior performance for the studied high-precision, constrained CSPR.

</details>


### [102] [SparQ4D: Learning to Act through Sparse 4D Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29682)
*Siyuan Xie,Jinxi Xiao,Ran Ji,Yikai Liu,Mingxi Li,Xuebing Li,Ye Shi,Yuxiang Gao,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级查询解码器，将整体4D场景理解注入策略，提升操作成功率且推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作演示中时空结构隐式存在，显式4D监督成本高，需低成本实现稠密4D预测。

Method: 在预训练策略上附加基于查询的解码器，统一处理深度、2D/3D点运动、多视角对应和手眼关系等任务；训练时采样少量查询学习4D结构，推理时可移除解码器。

Result: 在多种骨干、仿真和真实环境中达到最优操作性能，训练速度提升至5倍；探测分析表明策略中可恢复4D结构。

Conclusion: 该方法高效注入整体4D理解，兼顾表达力与零推理开销，显著提升操作性能并加速训练。

Abstract: Successful manipulation requires a policy to understand where things are and how they will move, yet action demonstrations leave this spatiotemporal structure implicit. While explicit 4D supervision has proven effective, reducing the cost of dense 4D prediction remains challenging. This paper introduces \method, a lightweight yet expressive decoder that efficiently injects holistic 4D scene understanding into policies and improves success rates. At its core is a query-based decoder attached to a pretrained policy, which casts many spatialtemporal tasks into one unified interface (e.g. depth, 2D/3D point motion, multi-view correspondence, and hand-eye relations). This design enables efficient training and inference without sacrificing expressivity: the policy learns rich 4D structure from a small number of sampled queries during training, while the decoder can be removed at inference with zero additional overhead. Across multiple backbones, simulated environments, and real-world settings, \method achieves state-of-the-art manipulation performance, outperforming competing methods while training up to $5\times$ faster. Probing analyses further reveal that this 4D structure is recoverable from the policy.

</details>


### [103] [Grounded in Contact: Reducing Floating and Interpenetration Artifacts with Multimodal Skill-Effect Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29662)
*Siyeon Kim,Mohanraj Devendran Shanthi,Joseph C. Liechty,Tucker Hermans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 学习预测接触丰富操作技能的效果需要能表征多种结果模式的多模态概率模型，而非单一期望结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性模型和高斯模型会平均化滑移、倾倒、旋转等不同结果模式，无法区分近似状态和动作下由接触条件或几何微小变化引起的定性不同物体运动，因此需要显式多模态模型。

Method: 研究在给定部分点云和动作下学习物体位姿变化多模态分布的概率技能效果模型，实例化三类模型：混合密度网络、状态令牌化和流匹配，均与潜在动力学配对用于下一步预测，并在多样物体和技能上与确定性及高斯基线对比。

Result: 显式多模态模型比确定性及高斯基线更准确地预测结果模式；规划中流匹配在每个技能上显著提升任务成功率，并在每个多步序列上取得最高成功率；真实机器人上概率模型抓取成功率约为确定性基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 概率技能效果为不确定条件下的推理与规划提供了实用抽象，显式多模态建模优于确定性及单峰高斯方法。

Abstract: Learning to predict the effects of contact-rich manipulation skills requires models that represent distinct outcome modes rather than a single expected result. Even for basic skills such as pushing, picking, and placing, small variations in contact conditions or object geometry can produce qualitatively different object motions under nearly identical states and actions. Deterministic and Gaussian models tend to average over these alternatives or represent them as a single broad distribution, obscuring distinct modes such as sliding, toppling, and rotating. We study probabilistic skill-effect models that learn multimodal distributions over object pose changes given partial point clouds and actions. We instantiate this abstraction with three representative model classes: mixture density networks, state tokenization, and flow matching, each paired with latent dynamics for next-step prediction. Across diverse objects and skills, explicitly multimodal models predict which outcome occurs more accurately than deterministic and Gaussian baselines. In planning, flow matching significantly improves task success over the deterministic baseline on every skill and achieves the highest success on every multi-step sequence. On a real robot, probabilistic models succeed at pick about twice as often as the deterministic baseline, providing evidence that probabilistic skill effects offer a practical abstraction for reasoning and planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [104] [JKO-GRPO: Augmenting GRPO with Wasserstein Proximal Steps for Flow-Matching VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29646)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JKO-GRPO通过引入Wasserstein近端步骤增强Flow-GRPO，将采样动作组视为经验分布并构造JKO步进行策略改进，并借由随机时间分支扩展到序列VLA控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配VLA模型（如π0、π0.5）的在线强化学习方法（Flow-GRPO、π_RL）仅优化去噪转移的似然比，用优势重加权采样动作，但忽略了动作之间的距离信息。

Method: 在每个状态将采样动作组建模为经验分布，用JKO步形式化策略改进；利用软离散c-变换从组相对优势构造传输目标，并对确定性流采样器进行共享噪声回归以匹配这些目标，再与无KL正则的裁剪Flow-GRPO目标结合；通过随机时间分支选择旧策略轨迹上的动作块索引，并从对应状态应用组级JKO更新以扩展到序列VLA控制。

Result: 该方法在Flow-GRPO基础上增加了基于Wasserstein近端的策略改进机制，能够利用动作间距离信息，并支持序列VLA控制中的组级更新。

Conclusion: JKO-GRPO通过Wasserstein近端步骤和随机时间分支，弥补了现有流匹配强化学习忽略动作距离的不足，为流式VLA模型的在线强化学习提供了新框架。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_0,\pi_{0.5}$ generate continuous action chunks by flow matching, and online reinforcement learning (RL) can improve them beyond supervised fine-tuning. Existing methods, such as Flow-GRPO and $\pi_{\mathrm{RL}}$, optimize likelihood ratios of stochastic denoising transitions, which reweight the sampled actions by their advantages but do not use the distances between them. We propose JKO-GRPO, which augments Flow-GRPO with Wasserstein proximal steps, with two contributions: (1) At each state, we treat the group of sampled actions as an empirical distribution and formulate policy improvement as a Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) step. We use a soft discrete $c$-transform to construct transport targets from group-relative advantages and combine shared-noise regression of the deterministic flow sampler onto these targets and combined with the clipped Flow-GRPO objective without Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization. (2) We extend this approach to sequential VLA control through random-time branching: we select one or multiple action-chunk indices along old-policy trajectories and independently apply group-level JKO updates using candidate continuations from the corresponding states.

</details>


### [105] [TASC: Time-to-Collision Anticipation and Selective Crowd Attention for Non-Holonomic Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29637)
*Mohammadhossein Malmir,Mohammadali Rahmati,Mohammad Reza Kolani,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TASC面向非完整约束服务机器人，将注意力人群编码器、预测性碰撞特征与连续控制策略集成，在密集人群中保持持续运动但碰撞率随密度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在人群中导航需避免长时间停滞（冻结机器人问题），而现有基于注意力的学习导航器多假设全向运动或输出离散动作，不适用于差速驱动底盘。

Method: 采用双选择注意力模块适配PPO进行连续速度控制，加入基于接近速度的碰撞时间（TTC）特征作为观测与奖励项；在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中以10000个并行环境、社会力行人模型和随机人群规模训练，使用高保真3D机器人模型。

Result: 3万次完整评估回合中，各测试人群密度下超时率均低于0.5%；但碰撞率从5人时的4.5%升至10人时的17.4%；消融实验（每变体4个种子）显示去除TTC特征后成功率下降4至7个百分点；策略无需重训即可在Gazebo运行，并通过ROS 2包部署于商用差速驱动机器人，推理延迟低于10 ms，经硬件在环测试验证。

Conclusion: TASC在保持非完整服务机器人持续运动方面有效，TTC特征对成功率有实质贡献，但高密度人群下碰撞率上升表明安全性与活性之间仍需权衡。

Abstract: Service robots that share corridors and lobbies with people must keep moving without endangering them. Reactive planners can stop indefinitely in dense crowds (the freezing robot problem), whereas the attention-based learned navigators that dominate the standard crowd benchmark assume holonomic motion or emit discrete actions unsuited to a differential-drive base. This paper presents TASC, a navigation system for a non-holonomic service robot that integrates an attention-based crowd encoder, an anticipatory collision feature, and a continuous-control policy. We adapt the dual-selection attention module, originally developed for discrete-action value learning, to proximal policy optimization for continuous velocity control, and add a closing-speed time-to-collision feature, a conservative estimate of the time to contact that serves as both an observation and a reward term. The policy is trained on a high-fidelity 3D robot model in NVIDIA Isaac Lab with 10,000 parallel environments, social-force-model pedestrians, and randomized crowd sizes. In 30,000 evaluation episodes, each run to its end, the policy timed out in fewer than 0.5% at every tested crowd density. This liveness comes at a cost: pedestrian collisions rise from 4.5% with five pedestrians to 17.4% with ten. In an ablation with four seeds per variant, policies retrained without the time-to-collision feature succeed 4 to 7 percentage points less often. The policy runs in Gazebo without retraining and on a commercial differential-drive service robot through a ROS 2 package with an inference latency below 10 ms, verified in a hardware-in-the-loop test with virtual pedestrians.

</details>


### [106] [Dr. Grasp-RL: Diverse and Robust Dexterous Grasping via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29610)
*Kunqi Xu,Xiaokang Sun,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出时间反转课程与协调重置机制，使简单MLP策略在PPO训练下同时获得多样抓取模式与闭环鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需兼顾行为多样性与闭环鲁棒性，但生成式方法依赖开环执行，强化学习易收敛到单一策略，难以两者兼得。

Method: 设计时间反转课程学习与协调重置机制，用PPO训练简单MLP策略，以捕获抓取位姿分布的多模态并保持闭环控制。

Result: 实验显示策略具备多样抓取行为与鲁棒闭环执行；仅在约400个物体上训练，便在超过20000个未见物体上表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 时间反转课程与协调重置可让简单强化学习策略同时实现多样性与鲁棒性，并具备强泛化性。

Abstract: Human interaction with physical objects relies on a combination of dexterity and robustness. In robotic manipulation, dexterity requires not only flexible end effectors but also a diverse repertoire of manipulation strategies, while robustness requires closed-loop adaptation to disturbances and unexpected situations. Two prominent research directions in dexterous grasping have largely addressed these capabilities separately. Generative approaches model multimodal distributions to produce diverse grasp poses, but their reliance on open-loop execution can limit robustness. Reinforcement learning approaches learn closed-loop grasping policies, but can converge to a narrow range of successful grasping strategies, limiting behavioral diversity. We introduce a time-reversed curriculum with a coordinated reset mechanism that enables a simple MLP policy trained with PPO to capture multiple modes of the grasp-pose distribution while maintaining closed-loop control. Experiments demonstrate both diverse grasping behaviors and robust closed-loop execution. Trained on only approximately 400 objects, our policy further demonstrates strong generalization when evaluated on more than 20,000 unseen objects.

</details>
