### [107] [JEPA-Shield: Safe Diffusion Planning with Latent World Models via Conformal Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29609)
*Taekyung Kim,Ege Yuceel,Steven Hong,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou,Bardh Hoxha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-Shield将冻结的JEPA潜在世界模型与任务空间安全约束执行相结合，通过共形预测构建不确定性管并用备份盾筛选轨迹，在停车任务中实现高成功率且无约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型能基于图像进行规划，但其预测无法直接保证机器人满足物理约束，需将潜在预测落地到任务空间并显式处理预测不确定性。

Method: 连接冻结的动作条件JEPA世界模型；两个下游模型分别从想象潜在 rollout 预测相对运动、从当前图像解码不安全几何；用分裂共形预测将端到端运动预测误差转为多步不确定性管；基于备份的盾将不确定性管与物理约束比对，在扩散启发的采样MPC中筛选候选轨迹和最终提案；并提出VParkBench基准（几何汽车与牵引挂车泊车任务及可编辑高斯泼溅环境）。

Result: 闭环评估中成功率分别为84.4%、82.3%和99.0%，未观察到约束违反；消融显示屏蔽与不确定性校准具有互补收益。

Conclusion: 在给定可交换性与初始备份假设下，可在动作块边界为不重规划执行的返回计划建立概率约束满足性；结果表明将潜在世界模型预测接地到任务空间量并显式考虑预测不确定性对安全关键规划至关重要。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from images, but their predictions do not directly establish whether a robot will satisfy physical constraints. We present JEPA-Shield, which connects a frozen action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world model to task-space safety enforcement. Two downstream models predict relative motion from imagined latent rollouts and decode unsafe geometry from the current image. Split conformal prediction turns end-to-end motion-prediction errors into multi-horizon uncertainty tubes. A backup-based shield checks these tubes against physical constraints and screens both candidate trajectories and final proposals in diffusion-inspired sampling-based model predictive control. Under the stated exchangeability and initial-backup assumptions, we establish probabilistic constraint satisfaction at action-block boundaries for a returned plan executed without replanning. We also introduce VParkBench, with geometric car and tractor-trailer parking tasks and a editable photorealistic Gaussian-splatting environment. In closed-loop evaluation, JEPA-Shield achieves success rates of 84.4%, 82.3%, and 99.0%, respectively, with no observed constraint violations. Ablations show complementary benefits from shielding and uncertainty calibration. The results demonstrate the importance of grounding latent world-model predictions in task-space quantities and explicitly accounting for prediction uncertainty in safety-critical planning.

</details>


### [108] [Action Windows Are Inherently Temporal: A Benchmark for Time-Sensitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29558)
*Haoxiang Zhan,Haoqin Hong,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AWAIT仿真基准，评估视觉语言模型智能体在有限动作窗口内完成任务的能力，并计入决策与执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准仅部分覆盖时间敏感任务，而现实日常任务中物理过程演化形成有限干预窗口，且窗口对决策与执行共享时间预算，VLM智能体在此类任务上表现不佳。

Method: 构建AWAIT仿真基准，要求智能体在动作窗口内完成任务，同时将决策时间和物理执行时间纳入评估，考察智能体识别窗口开启时机及在共享时间预算内协调干预的能力。

Result: 评估显示VLM智能体难以识别动作窗口的开启；即使单次决策耗时短于窗口时长，连续决策与移动仍可能耗尽整个动作窗口。

Conclusion: 现有VLM智能体在时间敏感任务上存在明显不足，AWAIT基准揭示了识别窗口起始和协调决策-执行时间预算的关键挑战。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents have surpassed learned-policy baselines on existing robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, these agents still struggle with time-sensitive everyday tasks that existing benchmarks for VLM agents cover only partially. In these tasks, evolving physical processes create finite windows for successful intervention. These action windows are inherently temporal and impose a shared time budget on decision making and physical execution. Coordinating intervention within this budget requires agents to determine when the window opens. We introduce AWAIT, a simulation benchmark that evaluates task completion within action windows while accounting for decision and execution time. Our evaluation reveals difficulties in recognizing window onset and shows that successive decisions and movements can exhaust an action window even when each decision takes less than the window duration. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [109] [Detecting Is Not Choosing: What a World Model Needs to Keep a VLA Policy Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29553)
*Thanish Bolla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在VLA策略的安全过滤中，世界模型的关键能力是在同一状态下对候选动作块进行排序，而非仅检测危险状态；且状态精度需在尾部与操作物体上准确，而非仅中位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略虽能完成基准任务，却常违反安全约束；利用世界模型采样候选动作块并选择预测安全的动作是一种补救方案，但需明确该模型应具备何种能力。

Method: 在仿真中以模拟器作为精确世界模型构建前瞻安全过滤器，测试其对多种VLA策略（LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboCasa）安全率的提升；同时评估学习型 critic、学习动力学模型与视频模型在危险状态检测与同状态候选排序上的表现，并分析状态扰动对选择性能的影响。

Result: 精确世界模型过滤器将安全率提升11.5至25.7个百分点；学习型critic检测危险状态AUROC最高0.864，但同决策内排序AUROC仅0.551-0.560，接近随机；状态扰动下选择性能远早于检测性能退化，LIBERO-Spatial上3.18 mm中位位置误差即消除70%收益；像素读取器0.56 mm中位误差比相近中位的高斯误差少保留6.5个百分点收益。

Conclusion: 面向安全的世界模型应以同状态内候选排序能力为评判标准，且其状态估计必须在尾部与操作物体上准确，而不能仅关注中位精度。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies complete most benchmark tasks but often violate safety constraints. Since they are stochastic, one remedy is to sample several candidate action chunks and execute one that a world model predicts is safe. We ask what such a model must do. In simulation, a lookahead safety filter with the simulator as an exact world model raises the safety rate of three VLA policies on LIBERO and of pi0.5 on LIBERO-Plus by 11.5 to 25.7 points. It also helps pi0.5 on three RoboCasa tasks. Learned critics trained on one policy's data detect dangerous states (pooled AUROC up to 0.864) but rank candidates at one state close to chance (within-decision AUROC 0.551-0.560), and the learned dynamics and video models we test do no better. When the simulator's state is perturbed, selection degrades long before detection. In the loop, a median position error of 3.18 mm removes 70% of the gain on LIBERO-Spatial, partly because the error also corrupts the displacement check. On LIBERO-10 the loss is smaller, and the intervals overlap. The median does not predict the cost: a pixel reader with a 0.56 mm median error keeps 6.5 points less of the gain than Gaussian error of a similar median. A world model for safety should thus be judged by within-state ranking, and its state must be accurate in the tail and on handled objects, not only in the median.

</details>


### [110] [Real-Time Kinodynamic Multi-Agent Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29548)
*Jiarui Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAKT框架将动力学约束前置，把双积分器机器人的运动规划简化为格点搜索，并证明可连续时间避碰；在2D/3D中显著加速大规模机器人编队规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动动力学多智能体规划器在搜索中或优化后施加速度/加速度限制，导致编队规模和速度受限，难以协调大规模机器人。

Method: TAKT在搜索前考虑动力学，将规划转化为双积分器机器人的格点搜索；解析计算离散规划平滑后损失的间隙并在格点上预留裕度；用闭式卷积将有效规划转化为满足连续时间避碰的动态可行轨迹；针对跨多个格点顶点的机器人提出菱形公式以支持单拍侧向避让，并适配MAPF求解器PIBT和LaCAM。

Result: 50个机器人时，2D大厅中规划器比最强基线快2000倍以上，3D中快100倍以上，且完工时间与成本相当或更低；2D秒级规划约千个机器人，3D数十秒，并能实时重规划多达千个机器人。

Conclusion: TAKT通过将动力学约束前置并解析保证平滑后的避碰裕度，实现了大规模机器人编队的高效、动态可行且无碰撞的轨迹规划。

Abstract: Coordinating large robot fleets, from warehouses to quadrotor swarms, requires collision-free trajectories that satisfy dynamic constraints and stay within speed and acceleration bounds. Existing kinodynamic multi-agent planners enforce these bounds during the search or through trajectory optimization after it, which limits fleet size and speed. We present Tickwise Actions to Kinodynamic Trajectories (TAKT), a framework that accounts for the dynamics before the search and reduces planning to a lattice search for double-integrator robots. TAKT analytically bounds the clearance lost when a discrete plan is smoothed and reserves this margin on the lattice. A closed-form convolution provably converts any valid plan into dynamically feasible trajectories with continuous-time collision avoidance. For robots spanning many lattice vertices, we introduce a diamond formulation that permits one-tick sideways yielding and adapt the Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) solvers PIBT and LaCAM to it. With 50 robots, our planners run over 2,000 times faster than the strongest baseline in 2D halls and over 100 times in 3D, with comparable or lower makespan and cost. They plan about a thousand robots in seconds in 2D, and tens of seconds in 3D, and replan up to a thousand robots in real time.

</details>


### [111] [Feedback Completion Certificates for Repeated Dwell Tasks with Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29525)
*Jiaxun Sun,Yuqi Yang,Senlan Yao,Hengyu Xue,Hongming Fu,Zhimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对巡检机器人同一站点多次测量且各次可停留窗口不同的场景，研究了在扰动下有保证截止时间的反馈控制器需保留的历史信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈控制器需在有限扰动下保证所有剩余期限，但需明确应保留哪些历史访问信息以维持任务可行性。

Method: 在无过期义务的历史上，去除冗余窗口后剩余义务按两端点排序，服务窗口形成后缀；用一边界与一截断运行长度保留未来可满足性。基于分支运动基元库恢复反馈策略，单任务类型用每次运行长度的标量阈值，多类型需联合递归。

Result: 四旋翼巡检库中阈值扫描降低了构建可重用全域策略的成本，稀疏布尔基线对单次初始查询更快；两站MuJoCo巡逻中三种任务感知过滤器各12次配对运行均满足所有窗口；8次初始认证运行中完成过滤器保留全部8次证书，仅安全过滤保留3次。

Conclusion: 所提状态表示可有效压缩历史信息并保证任务未来可满足性，任务感知过滤显著优于仅安全过滤的证书保留能力。

Abstract: An inspection robot may owe several measurements at the same station, each with a different window in which a dwell can begin. We ask what a feedback controller must retain about earlier visits to guarantee all remaining deadlines under bounded disturbances. For a common spatial predicate and dwell length, removing redundant windows orders the remaining obligations by both endpoints. On every history without an expired obligation, the served windows form a suffix. One boundary and one capped run length therefore preserve the future satisfaction of the original task. On a verified library of branching motion primitives we use this state to recover a feedback policy. For one task type a scalar threshold per run length suffices, with work linear in the number of listed branches per time layer for fixed dwell. Multiple types require a joint recursion. On a quadrotor inspection library the threshold sweep reduced the cost of constructing a reusable full-domain policy, while a sparse Boolean baseline answered a single initial query faster. In a two-station MuJoCo patrol, three task-aware filters all met every window in twelve paired runs each. Of eight initially certified runs, the completion filter retained the certificate in all eight, compared with three for safety-only filtering.

</details>


### [112] [Reset Dependence Is Embodied: Training Protocol Changes Which Morphologies Appear Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29518)
*Kevin Qiu,Zhao Yang,Marek Cygan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 重置规则（躯干高度阈值）会显著改变机器人身体形态的协同设计排名，基准应明确并检验该规则，并报告跨协议稳健的身体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计基准在固定回合协议下训练控制器后对身体排名，但协议本身可能改变排名，导致同一语料下不同研究选出不同身体；本文聚焦协议中的重置规则。

Method: 对100个DERL进化的UNIMAL身体，用PPO训练控制器，设置三种重置规则：标准（阈值20%起始躯干高度）、严格（50%）、无重置；用同一评估器打分，并辅以置换检验和SAC验证。

Result: 严格与无重置训练的排名相关性仅0.21（若仅种子噪声约0.89）；移除坠落重置对平地进化身体损失45%每步奖励，对多变地形身体仅16%；按三协议最差奖励选身体，样本内保留各协议最佳至少91%，留出种子平均超80%。

Conclusion: 协同设计基准应声明重置规则、检验排名是否依赖该规则，并报告跨协议表现稳健的身体。

Abstract: Robot co-design benchmarks rank bodies after training their controllers under a fixed episode protocol, but that protocol can change the ranking itself. Consequently, two studies can select different bodies from the same corpus even when they use the same task and evaluator. We study one part of that protocol, the reset rule, which ends a training episode when the robot's torso falls below a height threshold. We train proximal policy optimization (PPO) controllers for 100 DERL-evolved UNIMAL bodies with this threshold at 20% of each body's starting torso height (Standard), at 50% (Strict), or with no threshold (Reset-free), and score every controller with the same evaluator. The threshold changes which bodies look best: the rankings after Strict and Reset-free training correlate at only 0.21, against about 0.89 if seed noise were the only difference. Removing fall resets costs some bodies far more than others. Bodies evolved on flat ground lose 45% of their per-step reward relative to Standard training, and those evolved on variable terrain only 16%. Permutation tests over bodies support this difference, and on 24 bodies, flat-ground bodies gain the most from Strict over Reset-free training under both PPO and soft actor-critic (SAC). Choosing bodies by their worst reward across the three protocols costs little: in sample, they keep at least 91% of the reward of each protocol's best bodies, and over 80% averaged across held-out seeds. Co-design benchmarks should state their reset rule, test whether their rankings depend on it, and report bodies that stay strong across protocols. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [A ROS2 based Human-In-The-Loop Framework for UAV Search and Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29507)
*Anubhav Pandey,Harshit Nagpal,Aditya Agarwal,Tejasvi Alladi,Fei Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ROS2的人在回路框架，利用RGB-D和YOLOv11将手势转为无人机导航指令，在雾天和遮挡下提升鲁棒性与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机自主框架缺乏人类上下文感知，难以在非结构化搜救环境中实时适应；ROS1弃用促使迁移至ROS2。

Method: 构建ROS2 HITL框架，用RGB-D传感与YOLOv11识别左右手势并映射为导航命令；采用深度加权姿态估计、自适应RGB-D融合和时间一致性建模，在Gazebo-PX4仿真及UAV-GESTURE真实数据上评估晴空与Koschmieder雾条件。

Result: 遮挡与变环境下深度估计置信度89%；雾天RGB-D融合较RGB平均提升46.7%；遮挡处理提升15%；端到端延迟低于50 ms；真实人未重训手势准确率80%，10 m能见度下深度保持77%而RGB降至24%；改进YOLOv11仿真达91%、真实数据重训后99%。

Conclusion: 开源ROS2实现便于搜救无人机平台从ROS1迁移，使无人机可直接响应操作员手势而无需复杂路径规划。

Abstract: Existing Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) autonomy frameworks lack human contextual awareness, limiting real-time adaptability in unstructured Search and Rescue (SAR) environments. To address data availability, we propose a Robot Operating System (ROS2)-based human-in-the-loop (HITL) framework that translates human directional gestures (left/right) into UAV navigation commands using RGB-Depth (RGB-D) sensing and YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once). The deprecation of ROS1 necessitates migrating to ROS2, prioritising real-time communication, scalability, and long-term maintainability. Unlike approaches optimised for peak accuracy under ideal conditions, the proposed system prioritises robustness and real-time deployment in degraded SAR environments. Our system achieves 89% confidence in depth estimation under occlusion and varying environmental conditions in Gazebo-PX4 simulations. In foggy conditions, RGB-D fusion consistently provides more informative cues than RGB alone, with an average improvement of 46.7% across all scenarios and distances. The framework demonstrates superior occlusion handling (+15%), adaptive RGB-D fusion, temporal consistency modelling, and real-time deployment via a ROS2 data distribution service (DDS), with end-to-end latency below 50 ms. We evaluate the modified YOLOv11 and the depth-weighted pose estimation on a held-out simulated test set and on real people from the public UAV-GESTURE dataset, in clear air and in Koschmieder fog. Without retraining, the framework reads the gestures of real people with 80% accuracy, depth keeps 77% at a visibility of 10 m where RGB drops to 24%, and the modified YOLOv11 reaches 91% in simulation and 99% on held-out real people when trained on real data. The open-source ROS2 implementation eases the transition of SAR-UAV platforms from ROS1, enabling UAVs to act directly on operator gestures without complex path planning.

</details>


### [114] [MAWAM: Joint Imagination for Collaborative Robot Manipulation with a Multi-Agent World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29501)
*Rongze Tang,Chenyu Zhou,Jianjie Fang,Ziming Liu,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAWAM 是一个多智能体世界动作模型，通过联合预测多个具身智能体的未来观测并解码各自动作，实现异构机器人团队的协作操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型仅限单智能体；多机器人协作需要时空对齐未来交互并感知个体具身与角色身份，而现有分散策略、集中式模型和多机器人VLA无法同时满足。

Method: 采用智能体轴课程将单智能体WAM沿智能体维度扩展，在统一因果序列中用联合自注意力对齐多智能体未来，并引入分层语言、身份编码和具身掩码绑定各流；形式化多智能体必要操作的三轴（why、when、who），构建MA-Task Harness编码智能体流水线生成MA-Bench基准（7个任务）。

Result: 在MA-Bench上平均联合成功率达82.7%，比最强基线高30.7%；在真实异构机器人团队上实现实时闭环协作。

Conclusion: MAWAM有效统一了多具身协作的时空对齐与个体身份感知，显著优于现有方法，并具备真实异构团队实时协作能力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) excel at robot manipulation by imagining future observations and decoding actions from them, yet remain confined to a single embodied agent. In practice, manipulation often requires multiple robots to collaborate across disjoint workspaces, coupled timing, and heterogeneous embodiments. Such coordination demands both spatiotemporal alignment of future interactions and awareness of individual embodiment and role identities, whereas existing decentralized per-robot policies, flat centralized models, and multi-robot VLAs fail to unite both. To bridge this gap, we present MAWAM, a multi-agent world action model that jointly predicts the future observations of multiple embodiments and decodes each agent's actions from them for collaborative manipulation. Through an agent-axis curriculum, MAWAM expands a single-agent WAM along an agent dimension to coordinate teams under shared weights. Within one unified causal sequence, joint self-attention spans all agents to spatiotemporally align their imagined futures, while hierarchical language, identity encodings, and embodiment masks bind each stream to its role, identity, and embodiment. We further formalize multi-agent-necessary manipulation along three axes (why, when, and who) and develop MA-Task Harness, a coding-agent pipeline where mutually constraining generators and evaluators co-evolve customizable simulation-data and evaluation packages. Using this pipeline, we build MA-Bench across all axes with seven tasks, pairing joint success rate with role and collaboration scores for fine-grained analysis. On MA-Bench, MAWAM reaches 82.7% mean joint success, 30.7% above the strongest baseline, and achieves real-time closed-loop collaboration on a real heterogeneous robot team.

</details>


### [115] [Representation Alignment Is Only as Good as the Teacher's Behavioral Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29483)
*Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Haoyuan Chen,Hexin Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Pengfei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文发现，VLA微调中常用的视觉编码器对齐（REPA）在14个教师模型和16个操作任务上均未提升π0.5的干净成功率（最多+1点），且在扰动下成功率下降10-39点；作者提出行为结构指标（BSM）来筛选教师，并据此设计BAVA对齐协议，在SmolVLA上将平均成功率提升4.6点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA工作沿用图像生成中的表征对齐思路，但教师编码器的选择主要依赖启发式（全局语义或空间结构），缺乏针对VLA任务本身的评估标准，可能导致对齐无效甚至有害。

Method: 在匹配训练预算下系统评测14个视觉教师与16个操作任务；提出BSM指标，衡量教师特征空间中相近的状态在同一任务内是否跟随相似的专家动作；基于BSM设计BAVA协议：仅当教师BSM高于学生时进行对齐，并根据学生BSM提前停止对齐。

Result: 所有现成视觉编码器对π0.5干净成功率提升不超过1点，扰动下成功率下降10-39点；全局语义与空间结构均无法预测教师是否有益；BSM与扰动下成功率强相关（r=0.87），且所有现成编码器BSM均低于π0.5自身；在SmolVLA上BAVA以1/3对齐预算达到连续对齐效果，平均成功率较未对齐微调提升4.6点。

Conclusion: VLA的表征对齐只有在教师编码了丰富的、行为相关的结构时才有益；BSM可作为筛选教师和指导对齐过程的有效准则，BAVA提供了通用的VLA对齐协议。

Abstract: Recent works align the intermediate features of vision-language-action (VLA) models with frozen vision encoders during fine-tuning, hoping to inject additional priors such as 3D awareness or affordances. Unlike in image generation, where teachers for representation alignment (REPA) are assessed with encoder-intrinsic metrics, teachers for VLAs are still chosen largely by heuristics. We show that this practice is unreliable: across 14 teachers and 16 manipulation tasks, under matched training budgets, no off-the-shelf vision encoder improves the clean success rate of $\pi_{0.5}$ by more than 1 point, and all of them reduce its success rate under perturbations by 10 to 39 points. Neither global semantics nor spatial structure, the criteria used in image generation, predicts which teachers help. We therefore propose the behavioral structure metric (BSM), which measures whether states that are close in a teacher's feature space are followed by similar expert actions within the same task. BSM correlates strongly with success rate under perturbations ($r=0.87$), and every off-the-shelf vision encoder scores below $\pi_{0.5}$ itself. Based on it, we introduce Behavior-Aware VLA Alignment (BAVA), a general VLA alignment protocol that favors vision encoders whose BSM exceeds the student's and stops alignment early according to the student's BSM during training. On SmolVLA, BAVA matches continuous alignment with one third of the alignment budget, and improves the average success rate by 4.6 points over unaligned fine-tuning. Our results suggest that representation alignment benefits VLAs only when the teacher encodes rich behaviorally relevant structure.

</details>


### [116] [MobileNeTTT: a Lightweight DNN-based Time-To-Transit Estimator for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29454)
*Chiara Boretti,Philippe Bich,Manuel Morteo,Roberto Tron,John Baillieul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级神经网络MobileNeTTT，从单目相机估计时间到穿越（TTT）线索，用于仿生机器人导航，参数量仅1.95M，可在边缘硬件实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT是可从视觉皮层计算的深度代理线索，能估计移动通过环境特征剩余时间；相比大型视觉深度基础模型，TTT作为粗略视觉线索更简单，适合机器人导航。

Method: 设计MobileNeTTT轻量神经网络，利用全视场提供丰富视觉特征TTT估计，对相机旋转不敏感，自动适应路径几何和障碍物存在；导航模型仅1.95M参数，比大型视觉基础模型小两到三个数量级。

Result: 仿真和实际实验室实验在动态和空间复杂环境中验证了该方法；网络可在边缘硬件上实时运行。

Conclusion: 基于TTT的轻量估计与导航方法有效，为资源受限平台提供仿生单目导航方案，代码、数据和视频已公开。

Abstract: Time-to-Transit (TTT) is a perceptual cue, proxy for depth, that can be computed in the visual cortex, allowing animals to estimate the time remaining to move past features in the environment. We show how TTT can be estimated and used for bio-inspired navigation of a mobile robot equipped with a monocular camera. The paper describes a lightweight neural network estimation method, named MobileNeTTT, that (1) uses the full field of view and provides a rich array of visual feature TTT estimates; (2) is relatively insensitive to camera rotation; and (3) automatically adjusts to the geometry of the path and the presence of obstacles. Because TTT is a coarse visual cue, it is far simpler to use for robot navigation than current large visual depth foundation models. Our navigation model uses a 1.95M-parameter network that is two to three orders of magnitude smaller than well-known large vision foundation models, making it able to run in real time on edge hardware. Simulation and actual laboratory experiments validate the proposed approach in dynamic and spatially complex environments. Code, data, and video are available.

</details>


### [117] [What Test-Time Training Corrects in Latent World Models? A Slow-Fast Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29453)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Kevin Z. Shen,Yunbei Zhang,Peilin Zhao,Yinan Zhang,Cyril Leung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文分析潜在世界模型在分布偏移下的规划误差，发现误差可分为跨动作共享偏移和动作相关成分，并提出双时间尺度学习框架sft-JEPA，在多个基准上显著提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过预测候选动作结果进行规划，但分布偏移导致预测误差使规划器偏离目标。测试时训练虽能缓解，但尚不清楚它纠正了什么以及更新是否应跨回合保留。

Method: 在PushObj任务上，从同一状态探测多个动作，将预测误差分解为跨动作共享偏移和动作相关成分；据此提出sft-JEPA，使用两个不同时间尺度的学习器：快速学习器跟踪偏移并在每回合重置，慢速LoRA学习器跨回合累积重复知识。

Result: 共享偏移单独即可恢复AdaJEPA 91%的规划增益；回合特定偏移的修正跨回合无益甚至有害；sft-JEPA在PushObj、PointMaze、RoboCasa零样本规划（DINO-WM）和OGBench（LeWAM）上均领先所有基准平均，规划成功率较AdaJEPA最高提升22个百分点。

Conclusion: 世界模型预测误差中跨动作共享偏移是规划失败主因，且部分偏移具有回合特异性，需快速重置与慢速累积相结合的双时间尺度学习，sft-JEPA验证了该设计的有效性。

Abstract: Latent world models plan toward a goal by predicting the outcomes of candidate actions, but under the distribution shift, prediction errors can lead the planner astray. Test-time training using executed actions helps, yet what it corrects and whether its updates should persist across episodes remains unclear. We address both by analyzing the world model's prediction error. On PushObj, an object-pushing task, we probe multiple actions from the same state and decompose the error into an offset shared across actions and an action-dependent component. The offset effectively shifts the planner's goal, and AdaJEPA mainly tracks it: the common shift alone recovers 91% of its planning gain. Part of the offset is episode-specific, so carrying its correction into the next episode does not help and may even hurt. The remaining offset recurs across episodes and action-dependent error can be or needs to be learned and retained from many episodes. Our sft-JEPA therefore uses two learners on different timescales: a fast learner that tracks the offset and resets each episode, and a slow LoRA learner that accumulates recurring knowledge across episodes. Across PushObj, PointMaze, zero-shot planning on RoboCasa (with DINO-WM), and OGBench (with LeWAM), sft-JEPA leads all benchmark averages, improving planning success over AdaJEPA by up to 22 points.

</details>


### [118] [Phase-Conditioned Action Contracts for Auditable Microsurgical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29426)
*Jiaxi You,Yiwei Shi,Yanlin Li,Rihui Song,Weibing Li,Kai Huang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出相位条件可执行策略，通过动作契约解决分阶段机器人手术中固定维度策略输出的动作语义漂移问题，实现可审计的显微外科控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分阶段机器人手术中，固定维度策略输出的坐标在不同阶段对应不同的可允许运动和物理含义，导致动作语义漂移，需显式处理以保证可执行性与可审计性。

Method: 将学习到的动作提议与确定性命令形成分离：相位特定头生成提议，动作契约移除不可允许分量、施加状态相关边界并将剩余动作解码为相位相关任务空间命令；并引入失败聚焦局部修复与只读溯源追踪以支持命令重放和规则级审计。

Result: 在三个留出视网膜注射评估块中达到94.27±0.64%流程完成率和64.40±2.43%的35微米内成功率，无硬性违规；将契约与解码器应用于同一冻结Correction网络使完成率从12.27%升至90.47%；追踪精确重建96,350条命令，1,996次绑定干预均符合声明效果。

Conclusion: 显式可执行动作语义对可审计的分阶段显微外科控制至关重要。

Abstract: Staged robotic procedures may reuse a fixed-dimensional policy output even though the admissible motions and physical meanings of its coordinates change across phases. We call this action-semantic shift. To address it, we propose a phase-conditioned executable policy that separates learned action proposal from deterministic command formation. Phase-specific heads generate proposals, while an action contract removes inadmissible components, enforces state-dependent bounds, and decodes the remaining action into phase-dependent task-space commands. We further introduce failure-focused local repair and a read-only provenance trace for exact command replay and rule-level auditing. Across three held-out retinal-injection evaluation blocks, our method achieves $94.27\pm0.64\%$ procedural completion and $64.40\pm2.43\%$ success within $35\,\mu\mathrm{m}$, with no hard violations. Applying the contract and decoder to the same frozen Correction network raises completion from $12.27\%$ to $90.47\%$. The trace exactly reconstructs 96,350 commands, and all 1,996 binding interventions follow their declared effects. These results show the importance of explicit executable action semantics for auditable staged microsurgical control.

</details>


### [119] [Self-Refining VLA Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29383)
*Jeff Duan,Arjun Sharma,Jennifer Cruden,Vansh Tibrewal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将Predict-and-Perturb（P&P）自精炼方法适配到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，在LIBERO基准上使SmolVLA提升5.3个百分点，且P&P不确定性可预测任务失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型基于流的生成建模会因随机噪声不同而产生不同动作轨迹，现有测试时扩展方法需生成大量候选或训练外部校正器，成本较高。

Method: 把原本用于视频生成模式寻求的P&P自精炼流程迁移到机器人领域，并针对数据底层结构差异修改细化过程，应用于SmolVLA。

Result: 在LIBERO的400个回合中，P&P较原始基线提升5.3个百分点；回合级P&P不确定性与任务失败强相关（AUC 0.851）。

Conclusion: P&P可有效提升VLA模型动作生成质量，且其不确定性可作为任务失败的有效预测信号。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically use flow-based generative modeling to learn a rich, often multimodal, distribution of robot actions. Generating samples from flow-based methods involves iteratively updating random noise, and distinct noise samples from the same state can result in distinct trajectories and task outcomes. Existing test-time scaling methods improve action chunk generation via selection, aggregation, or correction, but commonly require the generation of large ensembles of candidates or the training of an external corrector. We adapt Predict-and-Perturb (P&P), a self-refinement procedure for flow-based models originally used for mode-seeking in video generation, to the robotics domain. We find that applying P&P to VLA models requires modifications to the refinement process used in video generation due to differences in the underlying structure of the data. Across 400 episodes of LIBERO evaluated using SmolVLA, P&P improves upon the stock baseline by 5.3 percentage points. Further, episode-level P\&P uncertainty correlates strongly with task failure (AUC 0.851).

</details>


### [120] [Contact Harness: Enabling VLM Agents to Perform Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29377)
*Shilin Shan,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Yimeng Dong,Bohan Hou,Jiaqi Bai,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Contact Harness 利用可复用优化技能引导 VLM 智能体解读多模态接触反馈并选择动作，无需微调或示教，在真实和仿真接触丰富任务中显著优于 VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠视觉难以可靠判断接触丰富操作中的接触状态，力/触觉信号语义模糊且难以被智能体直接使用，需要结合物理意义、任务阶段和视觉证据进行解读。

Method: 提出 Contact Harness：用可复用优化技能引导 VLM 智能体从多模态反馈评估接触状态并选择后续动作；采用柔顺执行机制基于实时力反馈调节接触运动；无需模型微调、任务演示或仿真训练。

Result: 在 Rokae 和 Franka 机器人上用 GPT-6 Astra 零样本评估：单提示技能在 15 种连接器上全插入率 90.7%，并在光照、背景、位置变化下于真实 PC 上验证；另外四项接触丰富任务平均成功率 100%；六个仿真任务成功率 96.7%，而微调 VLA 基线最高仅 68.3%。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的操作框架在接触丰富真实部署中具有潜力，可无需额外训练实现高成功率的多模态接触状态评估与动作选择。

Abstract: Agents built on pretrained VLMs have demonstrated zero-shot control of real robots, but vision alone is often insufficient for reliable contact-state assessment in contact-rich manipulation. Force and tactile feedback provide complementary information, but their semantic ambiguity makes them difficult for agents to use directly. These signals must be interpreted according to their physical meaning, together with task phase and visual evidence. We propose Contact Harness, which uses reusable, optimized skills to guide a VLM agent in assessing contact states from multimodal feedback and selecting subsequent actions. A compliant execution mechanism regulates in-contact motions using real-time force feedback. The framework requires no model fine-tuning, task demonstrations, or simulation training. We conduct zero-shot real-robot evaluations using GPT-6 Astra on Rokae and Franka robots. With a single prompt skill, Contact Harness achieves a full-insertion rate of 90.7% across 15 connector types, with further evaluations on a real PC under variations in lighting, background, and position. Across four additional contact-rich tasks, including whiteboard wiping, writing, peeling, and pivoting, Contact Harness achieves an average success rate of 100%. On six simulated tasks, Contact Harness reaches 96.7% success, compared with at most 68.3% for pretrained VLA baselines fine-tuned on task demonstrations, showing the potential of agent-based manipulation for contact-rich real-world deployment.

</details>


### [121] [Specification-Aware Sparse Differentiation: Planning under Signal Temporal Logic with Sparsemax Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29346)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对STL规范下的梯度最优控制，提出从任务规范直接推导稀疏模式的方法，将轨迹依赖的稀疏性暴露给稀疏自动微分，在双臂协调任务中实现最高27倍的求解加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的STL最优控制需在非线性规划中计算完整雅可比与海森矩阵，计算量常导致问题不可解；现有CasADi等工具仅能通过结构检测恢复稀疏模式，无法识别SparseMax鲁棒算子中由数据依赖阈值产生的精确零导数。

Method: 从STL语法、时序算子窗口及谓词读取变量直接规定稀疏模式；推导暴露SparseMax轨迹依赖稀疏性的模式，引入边际测试以复用模式，并将海森矩阵限制在每个约束支撑的变量上。

Result: 在双臂共享交接区（5单元区域与64单元圆形区域）的协调任务中，通过单次求解与刚体仿真闭环重规划验证；基于精确海森矩阵的内点法求解比CasADi与IPOPT中使用log-sum-exp鲁棒性的方法最高快27倍。

Conclusion: 直接从任务规范规定稀疏模式可有效利用SparseMax的可认证稀疏性，显著提升STL约束下梯度最优控制的求解效率。

Abstract: Gradient-based optimal control under a Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specification involves solving a nonlinear program whose constraints are smoothed robustness functions of the trajectory, and automatic differentiation without knowledge of sparsity computes the Jacobian and the Hessian that its solver needs in full. This often renders the problem computationally intractable. The dynamics and the specification prescribe a sparsity pattern through the STL syntax, the windows of the temporal operators and the variables that the predicates read. Standard tools such as CasADi recover this pattern by structural detection. In this work, we propose a method to prescribe such a pattern directly from the task specification. We show that our method allows us to exploit SparseMax, a recently proposed smooth robustness operator that is certifiably sparse and sound. Its derivative is exactly zero at every operand at or below a data-dependent threshold, which structural detection cannot see. We derive the patterns that expose this trajectory-dependent sparsity to sparse automatic differentiation, with a margin test for their reuse and Hessians confined to the variables of each constraint's support. We demonstrate the method on a dual-arm coordination task in which two arms share a hand-off zone divided into cells and one until constraint per scheduled visit keeps the other arm out of a cell until the visitor has left it. The two scenes are a zone of five cells and a circle of 64 cells, with single solves and closed-loop re-planning on a rigid-body simulation. Our exact-Hessian interior-point solves are up to 27 times faster than those with the sound log-sum-exp robustness in CasADi and IPOPT.

</details>


### [122] [More Than Picturing the Future: Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning for Generalizable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29339)
*Xin Zhang,Yushuo Hou,Anqi Zhao,Moyun Liu,Xingwei Zhao,Bo Tao,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出运动导向的潜在推理（Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning），将视觉潜在表示从整帧外观重定向到动作引发的交互过程，显著提升VLA模型在分布内和分布外场景的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉预见式潜在推理继承自视觉语言模型，将每个潜在与孤立未来帧对齐，导致策略绑定训练场景外观，场景变化时性能急剧下降。

Method: 语义潜在先按具身思维链阶段确定目标与方法；以其为条件，运动导向潜在由粗到细预测未来步骤：从全局场景到操作物体与目标构成的交互区域，再到机器人状态。

Result: 相比LaST0，在SimplerEnv-WidowX上平均成功率从37.5%升至55.2%（分布内），从22.4%升至30.2%（分布外）；在AgileX Piper双臂平台上训练场景从60.8%升至90.8%，环境变化下从6.7%升至37.5%。

Conclusion: 面向动作的视觉潜在应捕捉场景如何向目标演变而非未来帧外观，该设计能有效缓解场景外观绑定，提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Latent reasoning lets Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models deliberate at a fraction of the cost of explicit chain-of-thought, increasingly through visual foresight, which we show to be indispensable for predicting actions. Yet this paradigm is inherited from Vision-Language Models, where it serves understanding rather than action: each latent is aligned with an isolated future frame, often enriched with further modalities of that frame. We find that this binds the policy to the appearance of its training scenes, so that it degrades sharply once the scene changes. We argue that visual latents for action should capture not what a future frame looks like, but how the scene evolves toward the goal. We therefore propose Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning, which redirects visual latents from the whole frame to the interaction that the action must bring about. Semantic latents, following the stages of embodied chain-of-thought, first establish what to accomplish and how. Conditioned on them, motion-oriented latents anticipate each future step from coarse to fine: from the global scene, to the interaction region spanned by the manipulated object and its destination, to the robot state. Relative to the state-of-the-art latent VLA LaST$_0$, our method raises the mean success rate on SimplerEnv-WidowX from 37.5% to 55.2% in distribution and from 22.4% to 30.2% under out-of-distribution shifts, and on an AgileX Piper dual-arm platform from 60.8% to 90.8% in training scenes and from 6.7% to 37.5% under environmental changes.

</details>


### [123] [A Glimpse of Training VLMs as Foundation Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29338)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Yi Zhang,Zhe-Han Mo,Han-Zheng Wang,Jia-Jun Wang,Xin-Sheng Chen,Jiaxi Song,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Ning Li,Xuanyu Chu,Hengrui Zhang,Huan-ang Gao,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: nanoAstra通过语义动作建模和保能力监督微调训练VLM实现可泛化机器人操作，零样本成功率17.8%，并将机器人空闲时间从35.0%降至6.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra在机器人控制上泛化性强但训练细节未公开，难以复现，因此需要一种可复现的VLM机器人操作训练方法。

Method: 将机器人行为表示为由可复用参数化动作原语组成的语义步骤，并以显式进度描述引导；采用CP-SFT，用机器人专用系统提示将操作输出约束到结构化动作空间，在5000多小时机器人数据和通用VQA数据上训练nanoAstra。

Result: nanoAstra-9B在RoboTwin 2.0随机基准上零样本成功率达17.8%，显著优于先进VLA、WAM基线及同规模VLM；单张H20 GPU上推理快于对应运动执行，支持类动作块流式执行，将机器人空闲时间从35.0%降至6.9%。

Conclusion: 结合语义动作建模与CP-SFT可在保留预训练通用能力的同时实现机器人操作专精，并获得高效可流式执行的泛化操作模型。

Abstract: Recently, GPT-6 Astra demonstrates strong generalization in robot control, yet limited disclosure of its training leaves open how such capabilities can be reproduced. We introduce nanoAstra, an approach to training vision-language models (VLMs) for generalizable manipulation that combines semantic action modeling with capability-preserving supervised fine-tuning (CP-SFT). nanoAstra represents robot behavior as semantic steps composed of reusable, parameterized action primitives, guided by explicit progress descriptions. Meanwhile, CP-SFT uses an explicit robot-specific system prompt to constrain manipulation outputs to a structured action space, aiming to focus adaptation on robot-specific contexts and promote specialization while minimizing interference with broader pretrained capabilities. With this formulation, we train nanoAstra on more than 5,000 hours of robot data together with general VQA data. On RoboTwin 2.0's randomized benchmark, nanoAstra-9B achieves a 17.8% zero-shot success rate, substantially outperforming advanced VLA and WAM baselines as well as similarly sized VLMs. With inference faster than the corresponding motion execution on a single H20 GPU, our model supports action-chunk-like streaming execution that overlaps inference with motion, reducing the robot's idle time from 35.0% to 6.9%.

</details>


### [124] [RSLaA: Self-Reconfiguring Roadside LiDARs as Agents for 3D Traffic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29294)
*Yang Ma,Yichen Sun,Yubing Zheng,Zhongxiang Feng,Zeyang Cheng,Said M. Easa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出将路侧激光雷达视为可自适应调整位姿的智能体，利用基于历史观测占据栅格的替代指标预测下一时间窗感知性能，并比较MPC与MAPPO两种自适应配置方法，在10个站点、89个六小时场景共890次评估中均优于静态部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路侧激光雷达配置优化仅基于道路几何与聚合交通统计一次性完成并固定，而交通流本质动态，平均最优的布局在任意时刻可能远非最优，因此需要随交通场景动态调整的配置方法。

Method: 提出RSLaA框架，每个RSL在决策时间窗内自适应调整位姿；因决策时无真值，采用仅由历史观测概率占据栅格计算的替代指标预测候选位姿下一窗口的感知性能；构建联合仿真基准，将五种候选替代指标与检测器无关的真值感知标签配对评估，确定多视角覆盖指标J_mv最可靠；基于该指标构建两种自适应配置方法：滚动时域MPC优化器与轻量级MAPPO多智能体强化学习（集中训练、分散执行）。

Result: 在10个站点、89个六小时交通场景（共890次评估运行）中，自适应配置在复合感知得分上持续优于静态部署；J_mv被识别为最可靠的感知性能预测指标。

Conclusion: 结果表明将路侧激光雷达视为智能体可带来收益，自适应位姿配置能有效提升动态交通下的协同感知性能。

Abstract: Cooperative perception with roadside LiDARs (RSLs) extends the sensing range of autonomous vehicles and resolves onboard-sensor occlusions, particularly at curves and intersections. Because infrastructure deployment is costly, existing work optimizes RSL configurations once, against road geometry and aggregated traffic statistics, and treats them as fixed thereafter. Traffic flow, however, is inherently dynamic, and a placement optimal on average can be far from optimal at any given moment. We introduce RSLaA (Roadside LiDARs as Agents), a framework in which each RSL adaptively adjusts its pose to the evolving traffic scene over decision-time windows. Since ground truth is unavailable at decision time, the framework centers on surrogate metrics computed solely from a probabilistic occupancy grid of historical observations, which predicts the next-window perception performance of candidate poses. We construct a co-simulation benchmark pairing five candidate surrogates with detector-agnostic ground-truth perception labels at every decision window, identifying the multi-view coverage metric J_mv as the most reliable predictor. Upon this validated surrogate we build two adaptive configuration methods, a receding-horizon (MPC) optimizer and a lightweight MAPPO-based multi-agent reinforcement learning scheme with centralized training and decentralized execution. Across 10 sites and 89 six-hour traffic scenarios (890 evaluation runs), adaptive configuration consistently improves the composite perception score over static deployment. Our results indicate that RSLs could be benefitted from being treated as agents.

</details>


### [125] [A2I-VLA: A Two-Stage Vision-Language-Action model with Semantic-Physical Alignment and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29279)
*Kun Dai,Tianqi Wang,Haibo Lei,Chaoyang Li,Kai Zhang,Fuyuan Qiu,Lijun Zhao,Ruifeng Li,Chuqing Cao,Tao Xie,Wenming Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段VLA框架A2I-VLA，先学习语义对齐的力触觉表征，再将其集成到VLA模型中，在接触丰富的操作任务上将成功率从70%提升至95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉观察无法可靠提供接触丰富操作中物理交互的关键信息，而将力与触觉信号集成到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型面临视觉-语言语义与物理信号之间的表征鸿沟。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段引入跨模态对齐损失促进语义与物理表征对应，通过层次化多模态融合网络整合触觉与力特征流，并用未来力旋量预测损失和动作预测损失联合监督；第二阶段使用门控交叉注意力桥接模块，将第一阶段学习到的层次化物理特征自适应集成到VLA模型中指导动作生成。

Result: 在三个接触丰富操作任务的真实世界实验中，A2I-VLA显著优于基线PI05，成功率从70%提升至95%。

Conclusion: A2I-VLA通过语义对齐的力触觉表征学习与门控交叉注意力集成，有效弥合了物理信号与视觉-语言语义之间的表征鸿沟，显著提升了接触丰富操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Force and tactile feedback provide essential information about physical interactions in contact-rich manipulation that visual observations alone cannot reliably this http URL , integrating these signals into vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging owing to the representational gap between visual-language semantics and physical this http URL address this challenge, we propose A2I-VLA, a two-stage VLA framework that first learns semantically Aligned force-tactile representations and then Integrates them into a VLA this http URL Stage I, a cross-modal alignment loss is introduced to promote correspondence between semantic and physical representations. The tactile and force feature streams are then integrated through a hierarchical multimodal fusion network, and the resulting representations are jointly supervised by future wrench and action prediction losses. The former encourages the model to anticipate physical responses, while the latter associates force-tactile observations with task-relevant manipulation this http URL Stage II, a gated cross-attention bridge module adaptively integrates the hierarchical physical features learned in Stage I into the VLA model to guide action this http URL -world experiments demonstrate that the proposed A2I-VLA significantly outperforms the baseline PI05 across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, improving the success rate from 70% to 95%.

</details>


### [126] [$ζ_0$: A Framework for World Action Models with Joint Scene and Body Foresight in Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29277)
*Yizhi Chen,Zhanxiang Cao,Yitong Wang,Junyu Liu,Hongchu Zeng,Xinyi Peng,Chuxuan Huang,Yicheng Su,Zihao Zhou,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Liyun Yan,Tianyue Zhan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Keqi Zhu,Xiaxi Si,Jinming Yao,Qiao Sun,Shizeng Zhang,Jiangwei Zhong,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ζ0 将视觉-语言-动作策略转化为世界动作模型，通过联合潜表示预测未来视觉特征与实测关节轨迹，在仿真与真实人形任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型只依赖视觉前瞻来引导机器人动作，但人形交互还取决于机器人自身的身体响应；视觉前瞻会遗漏身体运动，而潜距离损失又不能直接监督动作响应。

Method: 提出 ζ0 框架：在共享潜表示中同时预见未来视觉特征与实测关节轨迹；训练时用基于记录未来的教师模型提供参考动作响应，并通过双响应迁移让因果预测器经由教师与执行策略对齐该响应；所有组件联合训练，部署时仅使用因果预测的未来。

Result: 在 HumanoidArena 七个任务上，基于 Ψ0 与 GR00T N1.7 的 ζ0 平均成功率分别为 63.6% 和 79.5%，较基线提升 18.8 和 9.0 个百分点；在六个真实任务上分别为 78.3% 和 85.0%，提升 16.7 和 10.0 个百分点；在 GR00T 上 79.5% 为七任务联合策略中最高。

Conclusion: 联合预见视觉与本体关节轨迹、并以双响应迁移监督动作响应，可有效提升人形机器人策略的性能与泛化，优于仅依赖视觉前瞻或潜距离损失的方法。

Abstract: World Action Models anticipate visual outcomes to guide robot actions, yet humanoid interaction also depends on how the robot's body responds. Visual foresight alone can miss body motion, while latent-distance losses do not directly supervise action responses. We present $\zeta_0$, a framework that turns vision-language-action policies into World Action Models by foreseeing future visual features and measured joint trajectories in a shared latent representation. During training, a teacher conditioned on recorded futures provides a reference action response, and dual-response transfer trains the causal predictor to match it through the teacher and the executing policy. All components are trained jointly, and deployment uses only causally predicted futures. On seven HumanoidArena tasks, $\zeta_0$ built on $\Psi_0$ and GR00T N1.7 reaches 63.6% and 79.5% mean success, 18.8 and 9.0 points above these bases. On six real-world tasks, it reaches 78.3% and 85.0%, gains of 16.7 and 10.0 points. On GR00T, its 79.5% tops all compared seven-task joint policies. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [127] [Tacinf: A Unified Tactile Infrastructure for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29240)
*Qiwei Wu,Xiangyu Chen,Xin Xiang,Rui Zhang,Yihang Kang,Weiran Chen,Zikang Xie,Yangfan Li,Tao Wang,Xiangzheng Wang,Tianyi Bi,Zhiyan Zhang,Xianpeng Wang,Mingqian Zhou,Yan Yu,Chuanyu Li,Wenjie Zhang,Tao Li,Weihua Zhang,Renjing Xu,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tacinf 是一个低成本模块化基础设施，使策略能从任务语言、视觉和触觉预测机器人动作与指尖力，并实现仿真数据合成、触觉基准、VTLA 策略学习、手持数据采集与任务对齐的仿真到现实评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力感知操作需要机器人从任务上下文和触觉反馈推断合适的交互力，而现有系统缺乏连接多源数据采集、基础模型训练与硬件部署的集成工作流。

Method: 提出 Tacinf 集成框架，包含仿真数据合成与触觉基准 Tabero、VTLA 策略学习、与部署同夹爪的手持数据采集 Tacumi、任务对齐的仿真到现实评估 Taco；验证各组件并开源全栈。

Result: 仅在触觉仿真数据上微调的基础模型可零样本迁移到不同真实机器人任务；八种策略变体的仿真与真实成功率强相关（Pearson r=0.998，MMRV=0.010）；四种报告力测量的策略在三个任务两个域中紧握指令下平均握力均高于轻握指令；混合遥操作、仿真和 Tacumi 数据将真实任务平均成功率从仅遥操作的 20% 提升至 60%。

Conclusion: Tacinf 提供了从多源数据采集到基础模型训练与硬件部署的集成工作流，仿真评估可高保真预测真实性能，多源数据混合显著提升真实机器人任务成功率，且全栈开源。

Abstract: Force-aware manipulation requires robots to infer appropriate interaction forces from task context and tactile feedback. We present Tacinf, a low-cost, modular infrastructure that enables policies to predict robot actions and fingertip forces from task language, vision, and touch. Task instructions can optionally specify the desired force level. Tacinf integrates simulation data synthesis and tactile benchmarking (Tabero), vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policy learning, handheld data collection using the same gripper as robot deployment (Tacumi), and task-aligned sim-to-real evaluation (Taco). Together, these components form an integrated workflow connecting data collection from multiple sources, foundation-model training, and hardware deployment. We validate each component, including the simulation data, benchmark, controller, and Tacumi, and are releasing the full stack as open source. A foundation model fine-tuned solely on tactile simulation data transfers zero-shot to different real-robot tasks. Across eight policy variants, mean task success rates are strongly correlated between simulation and the real robot (Pearson r=0.998, mean maximum rank violation MMRV=0.010). For the four policies with reported force measurements, mean grip force is higher under firm instructions than under gentle instructions on all three tasks in both domains. When both object positions and instances are randomized, a mixture of teleoperation, simulation, and Tacumi data raises mean real-robot task success from 20% with teleoperation alone to 60%.

</details>


### [128] [InfoField3D: Differentiable Information Field for Embodied Task-Oriented View Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29230)
*Huan Lyu,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InfoField3D 将任务相关信息提升为可微解析场，把相机放置转化为带约束的连续优化问题，实现高效且鲁棒的视角选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体执行任务需选择能提供充分任务相关信息的观测位置；现有方法或难以纳入可达性等新约束，或需大量候选评估才能搜索。

Method: 构建可微解析信息场，用低阶球谐函数编码方向性信息响应，用高斯占用函数的解析线积分建模视线遮挡；通过可行域灵活加入约束，将任务驱动的相机放置转化为受约束连续优化以高效搜索视角。

Result: InfoField3D 以高保真度紧凑保持逐点信息景观，在更低搜索成本下推荐更高质量、更鲁棒的视角；冻结 VLA 策略的受控评估表明，在初始视角信息受限时推荐视角可提升下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 将几何绑定的信息提升为可微解析场是进行任务驱动视角选择的有效途径，兼顾约束灵活性与搜索效率，并能改善下游任务性能。

Abstract: Effective task execution requires embodied agents to select observation positions that provide sufficient task-relevant information. Existing approaches typically predict viewpoints with learned models or score discrete candidates, but they either struggle to incorporate new constraints such as reachability or require extensive candidate evaluation to search. We introduce InfoField3D, which lifts geometry-bound information into a differentiable analytic field, whose value at each 3D observation position represents the task-required information accessible there. It uses low-order spherical harmonics to encode directional information responses and analytic line integrals of Gaussian occupancy functions to model occlusion along lines of sight. This formulation casts task-driven camera placement as a well-studied constrained continuous optimization problem, enabling both flexible incorporation of constraints through the feasible domain and efficient viewpoint search even in the presence of occlusion. Experiments show that InfoField3D compactly preserves the pointwise information landscape with high fidelity and generally recommends higher-quality, more robust viewpoints at lower search cost than the evaluated baselines. Controlled evaluations with a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy further suggest that the recommended views can improve downstream task performance under information-limited initial viewpoints.

</details>


### [129] [Force2Act: A Force-Aware Visuomotor Policy for Generalizable Compliant Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29208)
*Keerthi Vasan Murugan,Fangguo Zhao,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Force2Act是一种混合力-位置策略，通过全局力规划与反应性柔顺控制，使机器人能在接触丰富的操作中调节超出示范水平的力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有力感知策略从示范中学习力，只能复现训练时的力水平，当交互需要更大力量时失效。

Method: 提出Force2Act：全局力策略在每个动作块规划运动和交互力，力表示为相对于测量交互的量，使规划力随负载缩放；在规划步之间，反应性柔顺策略（Mixture of Force Experts）在每个控制步识别主导力专家并调节力；同时引入Force2Act-Bench仿真基准，含五个接触丰富任务。

Result: Force2Act总体成功率达93.8%，最强基线为77.0%；反应性调节将块旋转任务失败从150次中的30次降至10次；真实机器人上四个任务平均成功率94.2%，基线最高仅27.5%。

Conclusion: Force2Act通过全局力规划与反应性柔顺调节的互补机制，显著提升了接触丰富操作中的力调节能力和任务成功率，并提供了可重复的评测基准。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires a robot to regulate the force it applies, not only where it moves. Existing force-aware policies learn this force from demonstrations and therefore reproduce the force levels they were trained on, which fails once the interaction demands more force than any demonstration. We present Force2Act, a hybrid force-position policy built on two complementary mechanisms. A global force policy plans the motion and the interaction force once per action chunk, with force represented relative to the measured interaction, so that the planned force scales with the load. Between planning steps, a reactive compliance policy, termed Mixture of Force Experts, identifies at every control step the force expert that governs the interaction and regulates the force through it, so that the contact holds when it changes within a chunk. We further introduce Force2Act-Bench, a simulation benchmark of five contact-rich tasks that gives the community a common and repeatable way to test force-aware policies and measure how well they regulate the interaction force. Force2Act achieves an overall success rate of 93.8%, compared with 77.0% for the strongest baseline, and its reactive regulation reduces the failures on block pivoting from 30 to 10 of 150 trials. On a real robot, Force2Act reaches an average success rate of 94.2% over four tasks, against at most 27.5% for the baselines.

</details>


### [130] [SANA-Robot: Efficient World Action Model with Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28943)
*Yuyang Zhao,Zekai Li,Yicheng Pan,Jiaming Tang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Jiaxu Xing,Moritz Reuss,Quanquan Peng,Junsong Chen,Enze Xie,Zhijian Liu,Song Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANA-Robot 是一个 4.5B 参数的世界动作模型，具备分钟级记忆，可在边缘设备上实时运行，在记忆维度和多个 LIBERO 基准上取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能借助视频生成模型的世界知识提升动作生成，但预测开销大，难以在边缘设备实时部署，且通常缺乏有效的长期记忆。

Method: 采用三项核心设计：(1) 恒定成本的混合记忆：用线性注意力维护固定大小状态的全局记忆，用滑动窗口 softmax 注意力层传播历史信息；(2) 统一模型：对动作和视频 token 动态施加不同噪声水平，使模型既能作策略也能作世界模拟器；(3) 面向记忆训练与推理的全栈基础设施，包括高效数据打包与混洗以及基于记忆阶段的异步执行。

Result: 在中等规模（约 5B）模型中，SANA-Robot 在 RoboDojo 的记忆维度上表现最佳，在 LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro 和 LIBERO-Max 上取得最高成功率；在 RTX 5090 上使用 FP8 GEMM 每个动作块耗时 135 ms，使用 NVFP4 GEMM 为 115 ms，并在边缘设备上以 30 Hz 实现闭环实时异步机器人控制。

Conclusion: SANA-Robot 兼具高效率与有效性，证明了在边缘设备上实时部署具备长期记忆的世界动作模型是可行的。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) leverage the world knowledge of video generation models to improve action generation by aligning visual prediction with action prediction. However, their prediction overhead prevents real-time deployment on edge devices, and they usually lack an effective long-term memory. We introduce SANA-Robot, a 4.5B WAM with minute-level memory that runs in real time on edge devices, built on the following core designs: (1) hybrid memory at constant cost: our WAM relies on linear attention to maintain a global memory with a fixed-size state, and on sliding window softmax attention layers that propagate previous information, enabling high success rates on tasks requiring whole-episode memory while keeping an efficient and constant inference speed; (2) a unified model: by dynamically applying different noise levels to action and video tokens, SANA-Robot can serve not only as a policy but also as a world simulator; (3) a full-stack infrastructure for both memory training and inference, including efficient data packing and shuffling as well as memory-phase-based asynchronous execution. SANA-Robot achieves the best performance on the memory dimension of RoboDojo among models of medium size (about 5B) and the highest success rates on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro and LIBERO-Max. In real-world deployment, SANA-Robot takes 135 ms per action chunk on an RTX 5090 with FP8 GEMMs and 115 ms with NVFP4 GEMMs, and achieves closed-loop, real-time asynchronous robot control at 30 Hz on edge devices, demonstrating both the efficiency and the effectiveness of SANA-Robot. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [131] [T-Tac: A Soft and Thin Active Thermal Tactile Sensor for Robotic In-Grasp Material Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28939)
*Jiankun Li,Yu Feng,Yehui Liu,Meng Chen,Rui Luo,Guanglie Zhang,Jianshu Zhou,Wen Jung Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T-Tac是一种用于机器人操作中材料识别的软薄主动热触觉传感器，通过瞬态热响应识别外观相似的材料，在12种材料上平均分类准确率达92.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的材料识别方法在材料外观相似时面临挑战，因此需要一种不依赖视觉的主动热触觉传感方案来实现准确的材料识别。

Method: T-Tac将聚酰亚胺薄膜加热器与离子导电薄膜温度传感器集成在适合机器人夹爪安装的薄膜组件中；采用单面瞬态平面源建模和有限元仿真关联热响应与材料热性质，并通过标定和重复测量表征加热与传感性能，同时在室温和高温下进行加载实验；分类上采用混合框架，将提取的热描述符与多尺度卷积神经网络特征拼接，再用ExtraTrees分类器进行材料预测。

Result: 在12种材料上的实验表明，使用3秒加热脉冲和10秒观测窗口，平均分类准确率达到92.69%。

Conclusion: T-Tac为将主动热材料传感集成到机器人末端执行器提供了一种紧凑方案，为机器人材料识别开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Accurate material identification is important for robotic manipulation, yet vision-based methods face challenges when materials have similar appearances. Herein, we report T-Tac, a soft, thin, active thermal tactile sensor that identifies materials through their transient thermal responses. T-Tac integrates a polyimide film heater with an ionic conductive film temperature sensor, providing controlled thermal excitation and resistance-based temperature measurement in a film assembly suitable for mounting on robotic grippers. Single-sided transient plane source modeling and finite element simulations relate the thermal response to material thermal properties, while calibration and repeated measurements characterize the heating and sensing performance. Loading experiments at room and elevated temperatures further characterize the sensor response under an applied force. A hybrid classification framework concatenates extracted thermal descriptors with multiscale convolutional neural network features and uses an ExtraTrees classifier for material prediction. Experiments on 12 materials achieve a mean classification accuracy of 92.69\% using a 3 s heating pulse and a 10 s observation window. These results support T-Tac as a compact approach to integrating active thermal material sensing into robotic end effectors. This work shed a light on the new approach for robotic material identification.

</details>


### [132] [NAT: Scaling Causal Neural Action Tokenizers Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28873)
*Ahad Jawaid,Alexander Swerdlow,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经动作分词器NAT，在大规模跨具身动作数据上训练，实现可流式执行的动作自回归分词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人策略训练数据规模与多样性增长，需要一个能编码多种具身和数据源（包括未见具身）的通用动作分词器。

Method: 在来自真实机器人、手持夹爪、仿真和人类的4.9万小时动作数据上训练因果动作分词器NAT，其解码器支持动作随token生成而执行。

Result: 在四个仿真基准上，NAT对未见机器人具身的动作分词优于FAST和现有神经动作分词器；在视觉-语言-动作模型中于四个基准及真实机器人上优于FAST+；部分基准上缩小了与流匹配动作头的差距并降低首个动作推理延迟；仅用人类手部运动训练的NAT接近机器人数据训练的分词器。

Conclusion: NAT可作为大规模跨具身通用动作分词器，且机器人数据可能并非学习有效动作空间的必要条件。

Abstract: Action tokenizers let robot policies treat control as autoregressive next-token prediction. As these policies are trained on larger and more diverse datasets, they will need a universal tokenizer that can encode actions from many embodiments and data sources, including ones it has not seen. We study action tokenization at scale, training on 49k hours of action data from real-world robots, handheld grippers, simulation, and humans. We introduce Neural Action Tokenizer (NAT), a causal tokenizer whose decoder lets actions be executed as their tokens are generated. Across four simulation benchmarks, NAT outperforms FAST and prior neural action tokenizers when tokenizing unseen robot embodiments, and in vision-language-action models it outperforms FAST+ across four benchmarks and on a real robot when acting through its tokens. NAT tokens also close the performance gap to a flow-matching action head on some benchmarks while lowering the inference latency for first actions. Our experiments also find that robot data may not be necessary to learn an effective action space: a NAT trained only on human hand motion performs close to tokenizers trained on robot data.

</details>


### [133] [LIBERO-MOR: A Multimodal Robustness Benchmark Across the Vision-Language-Action Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28867)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LIBERO-MOR多模态鲁棒性基准，覆盖视觉、语言、本体感知、动作执行和环境五个维度共22种扰动，在LIBERO上评估七个VLA模型并揭示其鲁棒性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作基准上表现优异，但缺乏对交互全流程中各类变化响应的系统评估，影响可靠部署。

Method: 基于标准LIBERO构建含22种部署相关扰动、3个严重程度、40个任务、配对干净-扰动协议的基准，评测七种代表性VLA模型，并分析聚合性能与流水线域、单一扰动及干预强度的关系。

Result: 发现高干净任务成功率背后存在显著鲁棒性差距，模型间既有共同脆弱点也有特异性失败模式；严重度分析区分出渐进退化、强干预下崩溃及最轻干预即失败三类情形。

Conclusion: LIBERO-MOR提供系统化诊断VLA鲁棒性的框架，连接总体性能与流水线各环节，指导开发更可靠的机器人策略。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. Translating this progress into reliable deployment calls for systematic evaluation of how policies respond to variations throughout the robot interaction loop. We introduce LIBERO-MOR, a multimodal robustness benchmark across the vision-language-action pipeline. Built on standard LIBERO, it covers five deployment-facing domains: vision, language, proprioception, action execution, and environment. The benchmark comprises 22 deployment-inspired perturbations, each evaluated at three severity levels across 40 tasks under a paired clean-perturbed protocol, enabling controlled and reproducible comparisons. We evaluate seven representative VLA models spanning diverse action-generation mechanisms. Our experiments reveal substantial robustness gaps behind high clean-task success, together with shared vulnerabilities and distinct model-specific failure patterns across domains. Severity analysis further distinguishes progressive degradation, collapse at stronger interventions, and failures triggered at the mildest tested level. By connecting aggregate performance to pipeline domains, individual perturbations, and intervention strength, LIBERO-MOR provides a systematic framework for diagnosing VLA robustness and guiding the development of more reliable robot policies.

</details>


### [134] [DeRail: Escaping Trajectory Priors via Counterfactual Action Steering for Mid-Execution Instruction Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28803)
*Yitong Hong,Xiangdong Feng,Xiaoxi Sun,Senqiao Yang,Boyao Han,Zhejian Yang,Shiyang Zhou,Yongcai Wang,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Li Jiang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的推理期方法CAS，通过反事实动作差分抑制轨迹劫持，提升机器人在执行中切换指令时的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和WAM策略多在固定指令下评估，执行中切换指令时会出现轨迹劫持：策略无法充分转向新目标，行为仍被非目标任务轨迹吸引。

Method: CAS在推理时识别干扰的非目标任务，用对应反事实指令查询同一策略，利用目标与反事实动作之差引导执行偏离干扰轨迹先验；无需训练且模型无关。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal-Switch和CALVIN-Custom-Switch上对五种VLA/WAM策略及真机实验均提升切换成功率；CALVIN-Custom-Switch的[15,21]区间内π0.5从5.8%升至54.6%；CAS成功轨迹用于自训练优于普通成功轨迹，泛化到未见过的后续切换。

Conclusion: 轨迹劫持是执行中指令切换的关键失效模式，CAS可有效缓解并产生更优自训练数据，提升策略对后续指令切换的泛化能力。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world must often adapt when user instructions change mid-execution, yet current instruction-conditioned robot policies, including Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs), are largely evaluated under fixed-instruction episodes. We study mid-execution instruction switching and identify trajectory hijacking as a key failure mode, where the policy fails to redirect sufficiently toward the switched target, with its post-switch behavior remaining fully or partially drawn toward trajectories associated with non-target tasks. We propose Counterfactual Action Steering (CAS), a training-free, model-agnostic inference-time method that identifies interfering non-target tasks, queries the same policy with corresponding counterfactual instructions, and uses target--counterfactual action differences to steer execution away from interfering trajectory priors. Experiments on LIBERO-Goal-Switch and CALVIN-Custom-Switch across five VLA and WAM policies, together with real-robot evaluation, show that CAS consistently improves switching success; for example, on CALVIN-Custom-Switch in the $[15,21]$ switching range, CAS improves $\pi_{0.5}$ from 5.8\% to 54.6\%. Successful CAS-guided rollouts further provide higher-quality self-training supervision than vanilla successful rollouts with the same number of trajectories, yielding stronger policies that generalize to unseen later instruction switches. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [135] [VTLA-RL: Learning Visuotactile Policies for Load-Adaptive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28752)
*Qiwei Wu,Qixuan Li,Xin Xiang,Xiangyu Chen,Xiang Xiao,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTLA-RL 提出共享视觉编码器的视触觉抓取策略，结合混合演示与两阶段多任务强化学习后训练，在变载荷下提升真机抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭视觉难以推断物体质量和局部滑移，固定条件下采集的演示无法充分覆盖变载荷所需的接触驱动抓取调整，因此需研究视触觉策略及强化学习后训练。

Method: 视觉编码器在触觉图像历史与RGB输入间共享，在统一动作空间中联合生成并执行运动与双侧力目标；先用真实与仿真混合演示建立运动与接触先验，再用仅基于仿真交互的两阶段多任务强化学习以任务成功回报优化策略，无需持续真实演示监督，训练后直接部署到真实机器人；在仿真中比较触觉表示与RL后训练路径，并在硬件上分别评估载荷变化与放置扰动。

Result: 完整方法抓取成功率高于监督初始化；不同方法的RL后训练增益存在差异；硬件上评估了载荷变化与放置扰动的影响。

Conclusion: 为该任务的触觉表示与训练方法选择提供了实证依据，表明共享编码器结合仿真RL后训练可有效提升变载荷下的真实抓取表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action foundation models learn general manipulation priors, but object mass and local slip are difficult to infer from vision alone. Changing loads require contact-driven grasp adjustments that demonstrations collected under fixed conditions may not adequately cover. We present VTLA-RL to study visuotactile grasping policies and their reinforcement learning (RL) post-training under varying loads. Our main approach shares a visual encoder between tactile-image histories and RGB inputs to jointly generate and execute motion and bilateral force targets in a unified action space. Mixed real and simulated demonstrations first establish motion and contact priors. Two-stage multi-task RL then uses only simulated interaction to optimize the policy through task-success returns without continued real-demonstration supervision, and the trained policy deploys directly to real robots. We compare tactile representations and RL post-training routes in simulation and separately evaluate load variation and placement perturbations on hardware. The full method achieves higher grasping success than the supervised initialization, while RL post-training gains differ across approaches, providing empirical evidence for choosing representations and training approaches for this task.

</details>


### [136] [Entangle: Scaling Human-Video In-Context World-Action Models across Unseen Tasks and Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28732)
*Chendong Xiang,Chengwen Zhang,Ruowen Zhao,Zeyuan Wang,Shuhe Huang,Rongxu Cui,Min Zhao,Fan Bao,Hengkai Tan,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Entangle是一个上下文世界-动作模型，将单个人类视频和当前机器人观测映射为未来视觉状态与可执行动作，在未见任务上取得83.5/100平均分和69.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型可通过上下文学习（ICL）在无需参数更新下执行未见任务，但将人类视频ICL扩展到世界-动作模型（WAMs）面临两个挑战：任务级人-机器人配对与机器人动作在时间上不对齐；标准动作损失使策略依赖初始场景或任务身份捷径而非跟踪上下文。

Method: 通过视频到视频生成将记录的机器人视频转换为匹配的人类上下文，并辅以真实配对记录以扩展时间对齐训练数据；训练时结合变长视频窗口与任务内对比目标，区分配对动作轨迹与同任务其他执行，防止任务身份捷径。

Result: 无需任务特定微调，Entangle随对齐训练数据规模稳定提升，在Astribot S1机器人六个未见任务上平均得分83.5/100，在七个未见RoboTwin任务上平均成功率69.5%，比Zero-WAM高22.6个百分点；在Context-OOD下虽仅用场景对齐配对训练，仍能将上下文指定行为迁移到重排布局和替换工具。

Conclusion: Entangle通过时间对齐数据生成与任务内对比训练，有效实现人类视频驱动的上下文世界-动作建模，并在未见任务和上下文分布外设置下展现良好泛化能力。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables foundation models to perform unseen tasks from context without parameter updates. In robotic manipulation, a human video provides the context that specifies how a task should be performed. However, scaling human-video ICL on world-action models (WAMs) faces two challenges: task-level human-robot pairs are not temporally aligned with robot actions, and standard action losses allow policies to rely on initial-scene or task-identity shortcuts rather than tracking the context. We present Entangle, an in-context world-action model that maps a single human video and current robot observations to future visual states and executable actions. To scale temporally aligned training data, we convert recorded robot videos into matched human contexts via video-to-video generation, supplemented by real paired recordings. For model training, we combine variable-length video windows with an intra-task contrastive objective that distinguishes the paired action trajectory from other executions of the same task, preventing task-identity shortcuts. Without task-specific fine-tuning, Entangle improves steadily as aligned training data scales, reaching an average score of 83.5/100 across six unseen tasks on an Astribot S1 robot, and achieves a 69.5% average success rate on seven unseen RoboTwin tasks, outperforming Zero-WAM by 22.6 percentage points. Under Context-OOD, although trained only on scene-aligned pairs, Entangle transfers context-specified behaviors across rearranged layouts and substituted tools.

</details>


### [137] [Data-Driven Trajectory Prediction via Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28720)
*Danyang Li,Ana Theodora Balaci,Matthew Cleaveland,Roberto Tron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProMo-CoOp是一种两阶段数据驱动的轨迹预测框架，通过聚类行为模式并利用高斯混合模型分配概率，在预测时求解凸优化问题生成候选轨迹，并可选支持STL行为约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要预测周围智能体的未来轨迹，但智能体意图具有不确定性，因此需要多模态预测来覆盖所有可能的未来行为。

Method: 设计阶段将轨迹聚类为行为模式并用高斯混合模型进行概率模式分配；预测阶段针对每个模式求解凸优化问题生成候选轨迹，约束与观测轨迹一致并利用预计算模式分配引导预测；同时支持STL导出的行为约束，其谓词满足概率通过条件流匹配估计。

Result: 实验表明ProMo-CoOp优于最先进的基线方法，并且当预测的谓词满足概率可靠时，基于STL的约束能进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: ProMo-CoOp通过概率模式与约束优化相结合有效实现了多模态轨迹预测，STL约束在可靠概率估计下可增强预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting the future trajectories of surrounding agents is a fundamental requirement for safe autonomous driving. Since agent intent is inherently uncertain, multimodal prediction is essential to account for the full range of plausible future behaviors. We introduce Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization trajectory prediction (ProMo-CoOp), a two-stage data-driven framework. At design time, trajectories are clustered into behavioral modes and a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) provides probabilistic mode assignments. At prediction time, a candidate trajectory is generated for each mode by solving a convex optimization problem that enforces consistency with the observed trajectory and uses the precomputed mode assignments to guide prediction toward the selected behavioral mode. The framework also supports Signal Temporal Logic (STL)-derived behavioral constraints, with predicate satisfaction probabilities estimated using Conditional Flow Matching (CFM). Experiments show that ProMo-CoOp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and that STL-based constraints can further improve performance when the predicted predicate satisfactions are reliable.

</details>


### [138] [IRIS: Inverse Dynamics from Random Exploration with Eye-in-hand Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28707)
*Xiaoxiong Zhang,Zhiqiang Zhou,Tze Ho Elden Tse,Linfang Zheng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IRIS通过在大规模任务无关随机探索数据上预训练逆动力学模型，并采用两阶段训练引入眼在手感知，提升了基于视频的机器人操作中的动作接地泛化性和执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有想象-执行式视频操作方法受限于动作接地：逆动力学模型在有限任务演示上训练，泛化差；精确执行依赖眼在手观测，但眼在手视频规划难学习且数据稀缺。

Method: 提出IRIS框架：1）在任务无关随机探索数据上预训练IDM，获得多样视觉状态转移与动作配对；2）通过两阶段训练将IDM扩展到眼在手感知，无需眼在手视频规划。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，IRIS提升了动作接地和数据效率，实现了更精确的执行，并在性能上与最先进的操作策略具有竞争力。

Conclusion: IRIS通过可泛化、执行感知的IDM有效缓解了视频操作中的动作接地瓶颈，验证了任务无关预训练与两阶段眼在手扩展的有效性。

Abstract: Imagine-then-execute methods offer a promising paradigm for video-based manipulation, where a video planner predicts future visual subgoals and an inverse dynamics model (IDM) grounds them into robot actions. Despite their promise, these methods remain limited by action grounding. First, the IDM is typically trained on limited task-specific demonstrations and can generalize poorly even when given oracle visual subgoals, indicating that action grounding remains a bottleneck beyond visual planning errors. Second, precise execution benefits from eye-in-hand observations, but eye-in-hand video planning is both hard to learn and hard to scale: the viewpoint moves with the robot, and such videos are scarce at internet scale. To overcome these challenges, we propose IRIS, a video-based manipulation framework that learns a highly generalizable, execution-aware IDM. First, IRIS scales action grounding by pretraining the IDM on task-agnostic random exploration data, which provides diverse visual state transitions paired with robot actions without human task annotation. Second, it extends the IDM with eye-in-hand sensing through a two-stage training scheme, without requiring eye-in-hand video planning. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments show that IRIS improves action grounding and data efficiency, enables more precise execution, and achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art manipulation policies.

</details>


### [139] [Distilling Touch into Vision-Only Policies for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28697)
*Chen Yang,Linhao Wang,Linzhe Shi,Guohui Zhang,Changjie Wu,Hang Zhang,Haoyu Li,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacitTouch将视触觉教师策略的行为直接蒸馏到纯视觉策略中，避免重建触觉观测，部署时无需触觉传感器即可接近教师性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈有利于接触密集的机器人操作，但部署触觉传感器增加硬件与维护成本。现有触觉想象方法（如TacImag）虽去除硬件依赖，却保留触觉重建阶段，其误差会传播到动作生成。

Method: 提出TacitTouch蒸馏框架：冻结的视触觉教师在同一噪声动作与流时间下，监督学生去噪预测和中间动作表示；训练同时使用示范动作监督，使学生直接学习触觉引导的动作模式而非重建触觉观测；部署仅保留视觉策略，不含触觉编码器、专家或生成器。

Result: 在四个仿真UniVTAC任务和四个真实任务上，TacitTouch相对纯视觉控制的平均成功率绝对提升分别为16.0%和22.5%，与使用实测触觉输入的教师差距仅2.0%和3.75%；鲁棒性测试中相对TacImag平均成功率绝对提升最高达20.0%。

Conclusion: 从“学习想象触觉感受”转向“学习表现得如同能感知触觉”，可直接将触觉引导行为蒸馏进纯视觉策略，在去除触觉硬件依赖的同时接近触觉教师性能并优于触觉想象方法。

Abstract: Tactile feedback benefits contact-rich manipulation, yet deploying tactile sensors introduces additional hardware and maintenance requirements. Tactile experience can still provide effective supervision for policies that operate without tactile sensors at deployment. Tactile imagination methods such as TacImag remove this hardware dependency by predicting tactile observations, but retain a reconstruction stage whose errors can propagate into action generation. We present TacitTouch, a distillation framework that transfers tactile-guided behavior directly into a vision-only policy. The core shift is from "learn to imagine what touch would feel like" to "learn to behave as if you could feel touch": the student directly learns tactile-guided action patterns without reconstructing tactile observations. A frozen visuotactile teacher supervises the student's denoising predictions and intermediate action representations at the same noisy action and flow time. Training also uses demonstrated-action supervision. Deployment retains only the visual policy, with no tactile encoder, expert, or generator. Across four simulated UniVTAC tasks and four real-world tasks, TacitTouch achieves absolute improvements of 16.0% and 22.5% in mean success rate over vision-only control, respectively. The absolute gaps to the teacher using measured tactile input are only 2.0% and 3.75%, respectively. In robustness tests, it also outperforms TacImag, with absolute gains of up to 20.0% in mean success rate. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [140] [Doing More with Less: Complex Manipulation with a Contact-Adaptive Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28675)
*Hyung Gon Shin,Sanghoon Jung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具有接触自适应手指运动、细长手指和大抓取范围的拟人机械手，并配合基于四元数摆动-扭转分解的动作手套和 forearm 接口，在有限主动自由度和无触觉传感条件下完成手机拾取充电、手内操作、开箱再包装及剪绑线等复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人机械手需在形态、自由度、力输出、耐久性和传感能力之间权衡，现有设计难以同时满足多项要求。

Method: 设计接触自适应手指运动机构、细长手指、宽抓取范围与持续抓握力；用四元数摆动-扭转分解的动作手套提取人类操作意图并映射为机械手命令；设计前臂接口增强操作者具身感。

Result: 实验表明，有限主动自由度且无触觉传感的机械手可通过所提机构与有效双手协调完成手机拾取与充电、手内操作、开箱再包装、剪断和系绳等任务。

Conclusion: 所提机械手机构与动作映射接口能缓解拟人机械手多项设计权衡，并在有限传感与驱动条件下实现复杂操作。

Abstract: Anthropomorphic robotic hands offer the potential to manipulate objects designed for human hands in environments adapted to human use. However, developing a robotic hand that replicates the human hand in terms of morphology, degrees of freedom, force output, durability, and sensing capabilities remains a formidable challenge, as these requirements inherently involve trade-offs. Here, we propose a robotic hand mechanism designed to address several of these trade-offs by enabling contact-adaptive finger motion, slender fingers, a wide grasping range, and sustained gripping force. We also introduce a motion glove based on quaternion swing-twist decomposition to extract human manipulation intentions and translate them into robotic hand commands, along with a forearm-mounted interface designed to enhance the operator's sense of embodiment. Experimental demonstrations show that a robotic hand with limited active degrees of freedom and no tactile sensing can perform complex tasks, including smartphone pickup and charging, in-hand manipulation, object unboxing and repackaging, and cutting and tying cords, through the combination of the proposed mechanism and effective bimanual coordination.

</details>


### [141] [Fast World Planner: Advantage-Aware Action-Chunk Policy for Online Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28616)
*Jiangtao Kong,Yuchen Wang,Yandong Luo,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang,Yanbing Mao,Lu Gan,Tianyi Zhou,Huajie Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAN是一个基于优势感知MPPI的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过显式表示并优化完整的H步规划器提案来提升策略引导，从而在减少50%MPPI精炼迭代的同时提高控制性能与样本效率，并实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制是MBRL在线规划的核心，但迭代精炼带来规划延迟；减少精炼迭代会降低性能，因此需要从规划器学习更强的策略引导。现有方法通过动作级对齐学习，无法直接捕捉完整的H步规划器提案，且规划器模仿将进一步目标改进留给在线精炼。

Method: SWAN将k-MPPI（多模态MPPI扩展）与混合因子分析器（MFA）动作块策略结合以表示提案，捕捉多个模态及时间与动作维度间的相关性；并引入优势引导提案优化（APO），复用MPPI评估以优势估计构建改进目标，在保持规划器对齐的同时训练策略朝向该目标。

Result: 在DMControl、HumanoidBench和MyoSuite的31个高维连续控制任务上，SWAN显著提升控制性能与样本效率，且比最先进的基于规划的MBRL方法少用50%的MPPI精炼迭代；在物理Unitree Go2上实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，以高频控制实现稳定的速度跟踪运动。

Conclusion: SWAN通过显式表示和优化完整H步规划器提案，有效提升了策略引导与在线规划效率，在多个高维连续控制任务和真实机器人上验证了其性能、样本效率及迁移能力。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is central to online planning for model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL), but its iterative refinement incurs planning latency. Reducing refinement iterations often degrades performance, motivating stronger policy guidance learned from the planner. However, existing methods typically learn from the planner through action-level alignment, which does not directly capture the complete $H$-step planner proposal. Moreover, planner imitation leaves further target improvement to online refinement. To address these limitations, we propose FaSt World PlANner (SWAN), an advantage-aware MPPI-based MBRL framework that explicitly represents and improves the complete $H$-step planner proposal to improve policy guidance for efficient online planning. For proposal representation, SWAN combines k-MPPI, a multimodal MPPI extension, with a mixture of factor analyzers (MFA) action-chunk policy to capture multiple modes and correlations across time and action dimensions. For proposal optimization, we introduce Advantage-Guided Proposal Optimization (APO), which reuses MPPI evaluations to construct an improved target using advantage estimates and trains the policy toward this target while preserving planner alignment. Experiments across 31 high-dimensional continuous-control tasks from DMControl, HumanoidBench, and MyoSuite show that SWAN substantially improves control performance and sample efficiency while using 50% fewer MPPI refinement iterations than state-of-the-art planning-based MBRL methods. Finally, SWAN demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a physical Unitree Go2, achieving stable velocity-tracking locomotion with high-frequency control. (see our demo at this https URL )

</details>


### [142] [AsyncMixVLA: Runtime Asynchronous Robot Policy Switching for Adaptation to Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28585)
*Tamoghno Das,Keyon Jazayeri,Yerin Cho,Nazmul Takbir,Arshia Aravinthan,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsyncMixVLA是一个自适应推理框架，用于在异构VLA策略间进行主动、异步切换，以应对任务执行中的环境扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境扰动（如物体移动）会破坏VLA模型可靠性，且不同策略在扰动下表现出互补性失败，因此需要选择性切换策略进行恢复；但同步切换会暂停机器人，异步切换则会使用过时观测。

Method: 学习一个触发机制，在恢复潜力与干预伤害间权衡，选择性升级到高计算策略；将新策略的推理与已提交的 outgoing 动作重叠，并将上下文对齐到预期的接管状态。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long带扰动任务上，扰动任务成功率从69.5%提升至77.1%；总体成功率84.9%，与同步切换的84.5%相当，中位暴露交接延迟从226.5 ms降至0.1 ms；在三个未见扰动族上无需重训练或重校准达到75.1%成功率，优于更强组成策略的71.3%。

Conclusion: AsyncMixVLA通过主动异步策略切换，在保持成功率的同时显著降低切换延迟，并展现出对未见扰动的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models translate visual observations and language instructions into robot actions, enabling general-purpose robotic control. However, environmental perturbations, such as objects moving during execution, can undermine their reliability. Different policies exhibit complementary failures under mid-rollout perturbations, motivating selective switching between policies for recovery. Such switching can stall the robot during inference of the newly invoked policy or, if performed asynchronously, act on stale observations. We present AsyncMixVLA, an adaptive inference framework for proactive, asynchronous switching across heterogeneous VLA policies. A learned trigger balances recovery potential against intervention harm to selectively escalate to a higher-compute policy. AsyncMixVLA overlaps its inference with committed outgoing actions and aligns its context to the anticipated takeover state. On LIBERO-Long with mid-rollout perturbations, AsyncMixVLA improves perturbed task success from 69.5% to 77.1% over the lightweight policy. It achieves 84.9% overall success, comparable to 84.5% for synchronous switching, while reducing median exposed handoff latency from 226.5 ms to 0.1 ms. Without retraining or recalibration, it achieves 75.1% success across three unseen perturbation families, compared with 71.3% for the stronger constituent policy in aggregate.

</details>


### [143] [Selective ROM Weight Storage for Responsive LLM Control on Edge Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28563)
*Boxiang Song,Zhihong Chen,Xiaoyu Hao,Jinhong Huang,Chengkai Zhu,Jiaqi Yang,Hongyang Pan,Changhao Yan,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合内存架构与异构调度，联合优化边缘机器人LLM推理的内存、带宽与决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘机器人用LLM做复杂任务时，内存容量受限、解码时反复权重传输与激活/KV缓存争抢外部带宽，且顺序生成动作导致决策延迟，并与周期性控制相互干扰。

Method: 设计banked ROM存储部分固定权重并经专用数据通路供给计算阵列，LPDDR保留其余权重与可变状态；协调操作数交付并在输出切片边界预抢占以加速推理并减少对周期控制的干扰；采用结构化决策接口单次前向为候选阶段打分，避免常规选择的顺序token生成。

Result: 基于Qwen3-8B实验，峰值解码加速1.56倍，P99任务响应时间降低约227.27倍，抓取与运输任务完成率100.00%。
