Conclusion: 混合内存与异构调度可同时改善边缘机器人LLM推理的内存服务、计算访问和决策执行效率。

Abstract: Edge robots increasingly use large language models (LLMs) for complex tasks, demanding efficient inference alongside timely control. Limited memory capacity constrains model deployment, while repeated weight transfers during decoding compete with activations and KV cache for external bandwidth. Sequential generation of robot actions further delays decisions and periodic control on shared hardware. We present a hybrid memory architecture with heterogeneous scheduling to address these bottlenecks jointly. Banked ROM stores selected fixed weights and supplies a standard compute array through a dedicated datapath. LPDDR retains the remaining weights and mutable state, preserving model flexibility. Coordinated operand delivery and preemption at output slice boundaries accelerate inference while limiting interference with periodic control. A structured decision interface scores candidate phases in a single forward pass, avoiding sequential token generation for routine choices. Together, these mechanisms improve memory service, compute access, and decision execution for edge robotic workloads. Experiments with Qwen3-8B achieve a peak decode speedup of 1.56$\times$ and reduce P99 task response time by a factor of approximately 227.27$\times$, while completing 100.00\% of the tested grasping and transport tasks.

</details>


### [144] [PhysPPF: Learning Robotic Manipulation with Past, Present, and Future Physical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28551)
*Junjie Li,Dawei Yan,Guoting Wei,Ruike Song,Lu Wang,Qingguo Chen,Zhenbo Luo,Haokui Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysPPF 是一个在紧凑物理 token 空间进行统一时空建模的 VLA 框架，用 WWH 蒸馏 3D 感知 token、用 PPF 做有界记忆与未来 token 推理，显著提升复杂操作任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法将空间感知与时间推理分开增强：空间方法只把几何或可供性先验注入当前帧、不跟踪多步转移；历史感知与世界模型 VLA 多在 2D 特征或像素空间建模，缺乏显式 3D 接触线索或生成开销高。

Method: 空间上，WWH（What-Where-How）仅在训练时把预训练感知教师蒸馏为紧凑 token 组，编码场景中的 3D 结构、交互位置与夹爪接近方式，测试时无需 3D 编码器；时间上，PPF（Past-Present-Future）直接在该 WWH 空间操作、不做像素重建，将已执行状态整合为有界过去物理记忆，并用非对称世界模型联合推断当前与未来物理 token；随后并行门控分支把过去、当前、未来表示注入流匹配动作专家，避免跨时间干扰。

Result: 在 SimplerEnv、CALVIN、LIBERO 上一致优于现有空间、历史感知与世界模型 VLA，且延迟与参数开销适中；相对 StarVLA 基线分别提升 +17.2、+29.3、+3.5 分。

Conclusion: 在紧凑物理 token 空间统一时空建模、并以门控方式融合多时间尺度表示，可在低开销下有效提升 VLA 的精确 3D 接触与长程协调能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation, yet complex tasks requiring both precise 3D contact and long-horizon coordination remain challenging. Most existing studies enhance spatial perception or temporal reasoning in VLAs separately. Spatial methods primarily inject geometric or affordance priors into current-frame perception without tracking multi-step transitions, while history-aware and world-model VLAs typically capture past context or future dynamics in 2D feature or pixel spaces, either lacking explicit 3D contact cues or incurring high generation overhead. To address this, we propose PhysPPF, a VLA framework that performs unified spatiotemporal modeling in a compact physical token space. Spatially, WWH (What-Where-How) distills pretrained perception teachers exclusively during training into compact token groups capturing what 3D structures occupy the scene, where to interact, and how the gripper should approach, without test-time 3D encoders. Temporally, operating directly in this WWH space without pixel reconstruction, PPF (Past-Present-Future) consolidates executed states into a bounded past physical memory and jointly infers present and future physical tokens via an asymmetric world model. Parallel gated branches then inject these past, present, and future representations into a flow-matching action expert to guide manipulation without cross-temporal interference. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv, CALVIN, and LIBERO show that PhysPPF consistently outperforms existing spatial, history-aware, and world-model VLAs with modest latency and parameter overhead, improving over the StarVLA baseline by +17.2 points on SimplerEnv, +29.3 points on CALVIN, and +3.5 points on LIBERO.

</details>


### [145] [SBTenv: A Literature-Validated Split-Belt Treadmill Simulation Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28547)
*Philip Byrne,John Drazan,Nidhi Seethapathi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了基于MuJoCo的开源分带跑步机环境SBTenv，无需经验几何校正即可复现实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏开源的MuJoCo分带跑步机环境，而复杂模型的高通量仿真需求日益增长。

Method: 在MuJoCo中构建SBTenv，通过建模轮辐尖端的柔性接触，并与已发表的被动无轮缘轮行走实验数据进行验证对比。

Result: SBTenv复现了实验平均稳态速度（R²=0.643，RMSE=0.0162 m/s），且无需先前仿真所需的经验几何校正。

Conclusion: SBTenv作为开源工具，有望支持分带跑步机上肌肉骨骼与机器人运动的高通量仿真研究。

Abstract: The split-belt treadmill is an important tool for studying human locomotor adaptation. Physics-based simulations complement such experiments by allowing hypotheses about the mechanisms of adaptation to be tested directly, and high-throughput simulation is increasingly needed as these models grow in complexity. However, no open-source split-belt treadmill environment exists in MuJoCo, a physics engine widely used for high-throughput simulation. Here, we present SBTenv, a split-belt treadmill environment built in MuJoCo and validated against published experimental data from a rimless wheel walking passively on a split-belt treadmill. By modeling compliant contacts at the spoke tips, SBTenv reproduces the experimental average steady velocity ($R^2=0.643$, RMSE $=0.0162$ m/s) without the empirical geometric correction required in prior simulations. We expect SBTenv, openly shared, to enable high-throughput simulation of musculoskeletal and robotic locomotion on a split-belt treadmill.

</details>


### [146] [Environment-Aware Probabilistic State Estimation of Continuum Robots from Planar Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28521)
*Aedan Mangan,Spencer Teetaert,Chengnan Shentu,Michael G. Brandel,Jeremy J. Heit,Alexander Norbash,Timothy D. Barfoot,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种概率状态估计器，利用已知环境的有符号距离场约束连续体机器人形状，融合异步测量与移动相机图像，实现无内置传感器条件下的全状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人全长柔顺，适合受限环境，但接触下建模复杂，需从运行中可得信息估计状态；许多应用中环境可预先已知（如术前影像或设计模型）。

Method: 将已知环境表示为有符号距离场以约束估计形状；估计器在弧长和时间上连续，可从机器人任意位置的非同步测量估计全状态；融合移动相机拍摄的体固标记平面图像与指令驱动，无需嵌入式传感器。

Result: 在仿真主动脉弓和硬件体模中，估计器引导机器人进入目标分支；单视角下利用已知环境将末端误差分别从机器人长度的2.5%降至1.6%、从20.0%降至11.3%。

Conclusion: 连续的全状态及其不确定性估计可支持无传感器连续体机器人的导航与控制。

Abstract: Continuum robots are compliant along their entire length, making them well suited to navigating confined environments where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. However, this compliance makes their modeling more complex compared to rigid robots, especially under contact, so their state must often be estimated from information available during operation. In many applications, the environment is known in advance, for example from preoperative imaging in medicine or from design models in industrial inspection. We present a probabilistic state estimator that represents the known environment as a signed distance field, informing the estimated shape. Continuous in arclength and time, it estimates the full state from asynchronous measurements anywhere along the robot. It also fuses planar images of body-fixed markers from a moving camera with commanded actuation, so the robot needs no embedded sensors. In a simulated aortic arch and a hardware phantom, the estimator tracked the robot into the target branch, and with a single view, using the known environment lowered the tip error from 2.5% to 1.6% and from 20.0% to 11.3% of the robot length, respectively. The continuous estimate of the full state and its uncertainty can support navigation and control of sensorless continuum robots.

</details>


### [147] [Lifted Multiple Shooting for Parallel-in-Time Contact-Rich Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28512)
*Wei-Chen Li,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出提升式多重射击轨迹优化与并行内点求解器IP-PAS，有效解决接触丰富动态规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触动力学非光滑、多模态，导致基于梯度的优化难以处理机器人与环境的耦合动态（惯性、接触力、摩擦）。

Method: 将接触冲量作为优化变量，构造提升式多重射击转录，直接由非线性规划求解器求解；用隐式微分计算灵敏度以支持多种接触本构关系；提出时间并行原始-对偶内点求解器IP-PAS，实现O(log T)并行深度。

Result: 在六个全动态接触丰富规划任务（含非抓取推物与灵巧手内操作）上，无需接触引导代价；相较标准多重射击成功率提升最多81.5个百分点；IP-PAS解质量与串行方法相当，加速最多3.1倍，可扩展到40000时间步。

Conclusion: 提升式多重射击与IP-PAS为长时域、高维接触丰富规划提供了直接可解且高效的方案。

Abstract: Planning through contact requires accounting for the coupled dynamics of a robot and its environment, including inertial effects, contact forces, and friction. However, the non-smoothness and multimodality of contact dynamics and contact geometry makes gradient-based optimization challenging. In this work, we propose lifted multiple shooting, a trajectory optimization transcription that can be solved directly using nonlinear program solvers. By introducing contact impulses as optimization variables, our formulation allows the optimizer to satisfy the state-transition constraints using friction-cone-feasible impulses while separately enforcing their consistency with the contact model. Our formulation supports a broad class of contact constitutive relations, with sensitivities computed through implicit differentiation. To enable efficient planning over long horizons and for high-dimensional systems, we further introduce IP-PAS, a parallel-in-time primal-dual interior-point solver that achieves O(log T) parallel computational depth. We evaluate the proposed methods on six fully dynamic contact-rich planning tasks, including nonprehensile pushing and in-hand manipulation, without contact-guiding costs. Lifted multiple shooting improves success rates by up to 81.5 percentage points over standard multiple shooting, while IP-PAS achieves solution quality comparable to serial methods with up to a 3.1x speedup and scales to horizons of 40,000 timesteps.

</details>


### [148] [AlpaSafe: Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28509)
*Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlpaSafe 是一个轻量级风险感知轨迹选择器，复用已冻结驾驶VLA的场景表示，在无额外rollout或VLA训练的情况下以规则融合选择候选轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的驾驶VLA每步提出多个候选轨迹但仅执行一个；标准评估用oracle选择器打分最佳候选，部署中并不存在，且已执行候选的风险未被考察。现有在线评估器需专门训练场景或世界模型并在推理时逐个rollout，开销大。

Method: 读取冻结VLA提示预填充的最后一个隐藏token；用基于候选闭环仿真结果训练的轻量头将其与每个候选轨迹融合，预测相对分数及碰撞/越界logits；以固定规则剔除在两个风险logits上被支配的候选，并在剩余候选中选最高分者。

Result: 在525个AlpaSim场景上，AlpaSafe在selected regret和top-1一致率上优于Alpamayo默认首样本规则及均匀随机选择，并将碰撞有责或越界率从6.41%和6.10%降至4.93%；选择器仅1.7M参数，每决策耗时0.75 ms。

Conclusion: 复用冻结VLA表征即可实现高效风险感知的轨迹选择，无需rollout或VLA训练，显著降低执行候选的风险且计算开销极小。

Abstract: Reasoning-based driving vision-language-action (VLA) models propose multiple candidate trajectories at each decision, yet only one is executed. Standard evaluation scores the best candidate, assuming an oracle selector that deployment lacks, and leaves the risk of the executed candidate unexamined. Existing online evaluators typically close this gap with a purpose-trained scene or world model, often rolling out each candidate through it at inference time. We introduce AlpaSafe, a risk-aware trajectory selector that reuses the scene representation a frozen driving VLA computes while proposing its candidates. AlpaSafe reads the last hidden token of the VLA's prompt prefill. A lightweight head, trained on the closed-loop simulation outcomes of the candidates, fuses this token with each candidate trajectory and predicts a relative score and collision/offroad logits. A fixed rule removes candidates dominated on the two risk logits and selects the highest-scoring candidate among those remaining, adding neither rollouts nor VLA training. Experiments on 525 AlpaSim scenes show that AlpaSafe outperforms Alpamayo's default first-sample rule and uniform random selection in selected regret and top-1 agreement, and reduces the collision-at-fault or offroad rate from 6.41% and 6.10% to 4.93%. The selector has 1.7M parameters and takes 0.75 ms per decision.

</details>


### [149] [LoKiTO: Local Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization for UAVs based on Memory-Efficient Neural Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28438)
*Guillermo Gil-Garcia,J. A. Cobano,L. Merino,F. Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoKiTO结合Chebyshev多项式参数化与在线神经ESDF，实现严格动力学约束和低内存的3D局部轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度规划器受限于显式体积地图的高内存消耗或无法严格满足动力学限制。

Method: 采用Chebyshev基参数化将动力学约束嵌入模块化代价函数，并用LiDAR在线更新的神经ESDF提供碰撞感知（内存约1.0 MB）。

Result: 在密集模拟环境中对比Fast-Planner和EGO-Planner，动力学可行性合规率超99.4%，中位障碍间距更高。

Conclusion: 良好条件的连续参数化与显式动力学推理相结合，为空中机器人局部重规划提供了实用可扩展的基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces LoKiTO, a 3D local kinodynamic trajectory optimizer that combines a Chebyshev polynomial parameterization with an online neural Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF) representation for aerial robotics. While current gradient-based planners often struggle with the high memory requirements of explicit volumetric maps or fail to strictly enforce dynamic limits, LoKiTO embeds kinodynamic constraints directly into a modular cost function and exploits the numerical conditioning properties of the Chebyshev basis. Collision awareness is provided by a continuous, memory-efficient neural ESDF updated online from LiDAR measurements, which drastically reduces map memory consumption to approximately 1.0 MB. Benchmarking in highly cluttered simulated environments against state-of-the-art baselines (Fast-Planner and EGO-Planner) demonstrates the superiority of our approach. Experimental results show that LoKiTO achieves over 99.4% dynamic feasibility compliance, significantly outperforming the baselines, and maintaining higher median clearance from obstacles. By coupling a well-conditioned continuous parameterization with explicit kinodynamic reasoning, LoKiTO provides a practical and extensible basis for local replanning in aerial robotics.

</details>


### [150] [Belief-Driven Quantum Magnetometer Control for GPS-Denied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28416)
*Murtuza Lemonwala,Laxima Niure Kandel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种闭环磁异常导航方法，利用滤波器位置信念预测磁场分布，自适应调节Ramsey干涉磁力仪的相位与 interrogation time，在真实飞行数据上显著降低定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机搭载的低SWaP量子磁力仪信号弱且固定参数开环运行，长 interrogation time 虽提高灵敏度但在磁场不确定时产生相位模糊，需要根据导航信念动态调整传感参数。

Method: 将滤波器预测的磁场分布通过闭式规则映射为Ramsey相位和 interrogation time，在模糊极限与退相干极限之间切换；使用美国空军真实飞行数据（含轨迹、惯性漂移、实测磁场及18个月后实测地图）评估，仅建模Ramsey传感器，并与固定设置基线对比及消融实验。

Result: 72个独立飞行窗口中63个优于固定基线（p=2e-11）；低SNR传感器误差降低20-27%，中等对比度降低30-44%；相位对齐在平稳飞行中贡献主要增益，丢失起始后 interrogation time 自适应使中位误差降低37-42%；高质量传感器增益小；IBM超导量子比特上 interrogation time 在经验最优的1.3倍内；结合信息感知路由使命误差降低15-40%。

Conclusion: 信念驱动传感通过闭环调节Ramsey磁力仪参数，在低质量传感器和困难场景下显著提升磁异常导航精度，且规则接近经验最优，无需事后调参。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles increasingly need to navigate without satellite positioning. Magnetic anomaly navigation (Mag-Nav) with quantum magnetometers offers a passive, unjammable fix, but the low size-weight-and-power (SWaP) sensors that small drones can carry are weak, and they are operated open loop at a fixed setting. Interferometric (Ramsey) magnetometers face a trade-off: long interrogation times are more sensitive but become ambiguous when the expected field is uncertain. The navigator's own position belief, pushed through the anomaly map, predicts that field. We close this loop. The filter's predictive field distribution sets the Ramsey phase and interrogation time at every epoch, through a closed-form rule that moves between an ambiguity limit and a decoherence limit. We evaluate it on real U.S. Air Force flight data (real tracks, inertial drift, measured fields, and a survey map tested 18 months after it was flown), with only the Ramsey sensor modelled. Against fixed-setting baselines, 63 of 72 independent flight windows favour belief driven sensing ($p = 2 \times 10^{-11}$). Error falls by 20-27% for low-SNR sensors and 30-44% for mid-contrast sensors. An ablation separates the two ingredients. Phase alignment drives the gain in steady flight, and the rule matches the best fixed setting without the hindsight tuning that setting requires. After a lost start, interrogation time adaptation is essential, cutting median error by 37-42% even against hindsight-tuned phase-aligned settings. Gains are small for high-quality sensors with accurate maps. On an IBM superconducting qubit, the rule's interrogation time stays within $1.3\times$ of the empirical optimum. Coupled with information-aware routing, belief-driven sensing lowers mission error by 15-40%.

</details>


### [151] [ScaleEvals: Scaling Generalist Policy Evaluation through Reset-Free Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28411)
*Amitoj Sandhu,Ethan Sick,Omkaar Buddhikot,Nadia Laswi,Wesley Durbano,Nadun Ranawaka,You Liang Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ScaleEvals，一种无需人工重置、基于连续物理玩耍的可扩展机器人策略评估协议，利用VLM自动生成任务与评分，实现高效、低人力成本的策略排名与能力诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人策略评估受限于人工重置、人工评分及固定小任务集，诊断覆盖不足且难以规模化。

Method: ScaleEvals通过机器人集群连续物理玩耍进行无重置评估；VLM AutoPrompter生成带难度、语言提示和技能标签的场景任务，VLM AutoGrader评估任务进度；采用软胜Bradley-Terry模型聚合分数，并用标签分层ANOVA提供能力诊断。

Result: 在DROID和YAM上完成3000+自主物理rollout，每站每小时23.0净rollout，减少98%人工劳动；AutoGrader与人工评分一致率90.7%；对五个SOTA策略排名与DROID人工评估排名相关（r=0.94, rho=0.90, p=0.018），YAM内部一致（r=0.839, rho=0.80）；行为组合性可预测未见提示下的策略顺序，10任务240个前瞻rollout中三策略排序成对一致率96%。

Conclusion: ScaleEvals可规模化、低人工地评估通用机器人策略，不仅能预测领先策略，还能预测排名反转位置，为策略能力诊断提供可解释依据。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of generalist robot policies is bottlenecked by manual resets, human grading, and small fixed task suites with limited diagnostic coverage. We present ScaleEvals, a scalable reset-free protocol that evaluates policies through continuous physical play across a robot fleet. A VLM AutoPrompter generates scene-grounded tasks with difficulty estimates, language prompts, and skill tags, enabling performance analysis across diverse rollouts. A VLM AutoGrader assesses task progress, achieving 90.7% agreement with human graders. Scores are aggregated via a soft-win Bradley-Terry model, while tag-stratified ANOVA provides interpretable capability diagnostics. Across 3,000+ physical autonomous rollouts on DROID and YAM, ScaleEvals achieves 23.0 net rollouts/hr per station while reducing active human labor by 98%. By ranking five SOTA generalist policies, ScaleEvals correlates with held-out manual evaluation ranking on DROID (r=0.94, rho=0.90, p=0.018), and remains internally consistent on a second embodiment, YAM (r=0.839, rho=0.80). Beyond aggregate rankings, we observe behavioral compositionality: recombining verb-, skill-, and object-conditioned evidence predicts policy order on unseen prompts. Across 10 tasks and 240 prospective physical rollouts, we recover three-policy orderings of 96% pairwise concordance, demonstrating ScaleEvals can predict not just which policy leads, but where rankings reverse.

</details>


### [152] [Occupancy Planar Patch: Incremental Probabilistic Bounded-Plane Reconstruction from LiDAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28403)
*Jiahao Wang,Chaehyeon Song,Dongjiao He,Jan Quenzel,Lintong Zhang,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出占用平面片（OPP）增量有界平面片表示，在距离空间直接建模LiDAR射线噪声与自由空间，实现高精度实时表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR扫描沿已知射线具有各向异性距离噪声并隐含自由空间信息，现有基于无序点集的观测模型未充分利用该物理测量几何。

Method: 构建增量有界平面片（OPP），在距离空间评估表面似然与自由空间，显式建模传感器噪声、平面不确定性和射线净空；用单一射线-面片关联权重联合更新平面几何、细化表面边界并融合相机影像。

Result: 在Oxford Spires和Multi-Campus六个序列上，OPP精度最高、重建误差最低，存储地图最紧凑之一；几何重建实时运行，每帧81 ms，纹理投影额外134 ms。

Conclusion: 以保守策略仅在几何证据确定处接纳表面，OPP优先保证结构精度而非无约束完整性，适合建筑建模。

Abstract: Online surface reconstruction from LiDAR scans requires observation models that respect the sensor's physical measurement geometry. Unlike unordered point sets, LiDAR returns carry anisotropic range noise along known rays, which also implicitly identify free space. We introduce the Occupancy Planar Patch (OPP), an incremental bounded planar patch representation that evaluates surface likelihoods and free space directly in range-space, allowing sensor noise, plane uncertainty, and ray clearance to be explicitly modelled during reconstruction. A single ray-to-patch association weight jointly updates plane geometry, refines surface boundaries, and blends camera imagery. The method is evaluated using six sequences from the Oxford Spires and Multi-Campus datasets. OPP achieves the highest precision and lowest reconstruction error among evaluated methods, while producing stored maps among the most compact. By conservatively admitting surfaces only where geometric evidence is certain, OPP prioritises structural precision over unconstrained completeness, which is well suited for architectural modelling. Geometric reconstruction operates in real time, requiring 81 ms per frame on a representative sequence, with texture projection adding 134 ms.

</details>


### [153] [A Hybrid Multi-Goal Path Planning Framework for Powered Wheelchairs in Dynamic Environments: From Simulation to Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28394)
*Damla Leblebicioğlu,Ege Özgül,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TSP-PF-RRT与TSP-PF-A*混合框架，结合全局任务排序与局部避障，在仿真与实机上验证椭圆模型对碰撞行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主电动轮椅在人类中心环境中需同时实现高效多目标路径规划与对静态/动态障碍的连续避障。

Method: 融合TSP全局任务排序、势场（PF）引导以及局部RRT和A*规划器；PF将全局路径信息传递给局部规划器以保持路径对齐；局部规划采用方向感知、扩展邻域的A*或带环境感知采样与两步扩展的RRT；在正常、拥挤和超拥挤仿真环境中用点模型与椭圆模型评估，并用运动捕捉与LiDAR/RGB-D进行硬件测试。

Result: 引入轮椅几何的椭圆模型改变了观测到的碰撞行为，两种混合规划器在椭圆模型下取得可比的避障性能。

Conclusion: 所提混合框架能有效结合全局规划与局部避障，且考虑平台几何对避障行为评估具有重要影响。

Abstract: Autonomous powered wheelchairs (PWs) operating in human-centered environments must combine efficient multi-destination path planning with continuous collision avoidance strategies in the presence of static and dynamic obstacles. This study presents a hybrid framework integrating Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP)-based global task ordering, Potential-Field (PF) guidance, and local Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and A-star (A*) planners. PF guidance transfers global path information to local planners, helping the robot stay aligned with the global path during obstacle avoidance. Local planning is enhanced with direction-aware, extended-neighborhood A* or RRT using environment-aware sampling and two-step extension. The TSP-PF-RRT and TSP-PF-A* methods are evaluated in normal, crowded, and extra-crowded simulation environments using point-based and elliptical robot models. The elliptical model accounts for platform dimensions during collision avoidance phase. Hardware tests on the physical PW use motion capture (MoCap) for ground-truth measurements, while outside-MoCap experiments rely on onboard LiDAR and RGB-D sensing. Results show that incorporating the wheelchair geometry changes the observed collision behavior, yielding comparable collision avoidance performance between the two hybrid planners under the elliptical model.

</details>


### [154] [Knowing What You Carry: Scalable Physics-Anchored World Models for Robotic Planning with Liquid Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28387)
*Yicheng Zhu,Linfeng Tian,Yang Chen,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU并行液晃仿真器PLiMB及物理锚定世界模型，用于携带液体载荷的地面车辆在侧翻极限附近的运动规划，预测精度和安全性优于刚体或低阶液晃模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代规划器需对数千候选运动进行快速预测，但液体载荷在机动后持续运动且状态难以测量，刚体假设或低阶液晃模型在共振附近误差大，耦合流体仿真成本过高。

Method: 构建PLiMB（GPU并行液晃仿真器，首次实现FLIP液体与多体车辆双向耦合），提供训练数据和高保真参考；提出可扩展物理锚定世界模型，结合模态液晃与侧翻先验、从车辆运动历史估计隐藏液体状态（无需罐内传感器）及学习校正，无需流体仿真即可在单GPU 20Hz控制周期内批量评分数千候选方案。

Result: 在侧翻极限附近，刚体假设或公开低阶液晃模型的规划器在存在安全方案时反复选择不安全方案；本文模型在标准罐中从未选错，在液晃模态位于机动频带的宽罐中不安全方案约减半；利用车辆历史估计液体状态使近极限预测误差减半。

Conclusion: 物理锚定世界模型结合历史液体状态估计可在计算高效前提下显著提升液体载荷车辆近极限规划安全性，优于传统低阶或刚体方法。

Abstract: Payload-carrying robots, such as delivery robots and autonomous trucks, must choose motions that keep them and their payload safe, so their planners predict the outcome of each candidate motion. Modern planners compare thousands of candidates per step on a GPU, using world models such as batched simulators or learned predictors. Most of them assume a rigid payload, but a liquid payload keeps moving after a maneuver, and its state is rarely measured. Near a safety limit, small prediction errors can make an unsafe plan look safe. Coupled fluid simulations are accurate but too costly for every candidate, while low-order slosh models are cheap but accurate only away from resonance. We study this trade-off on a ground vehicle with a partly filled tank near its rollover limit. First, we build PLiMB (Parallel Liquid-MultiBody), a GPU-parallel slosh simulator that, to our knowledge, couples FLIP liquid two-way with many multibody vehicles for the first time. It provides training data and a high-fidelity reference. Second, we propose a scalable physics-anchored world model that combines a modal-slosh and roll prior, an estimate of the hidden liquid state from the vehicle's own motion history (no sensor in the tank), and a learned correction. Without simulating the fluid, it scores thousands of candidate plans in one batched pass within a 20 Hz control tick on one GPU. We judge each world model by the plans it leads a planner to choose. Near the rollover limit, planners that treat the liquid as a rigid payload or use published low-order slosh models repeatedly pick unsafe plans when a safe one exists. Ours never does in our standard tank and makes about half as many in a wide tank whose sloshing mode lies in the maneuver band. There, the liquid state left by earlier driving can change the best plan, and reading it from the vehicle's history halves our model's near-limit prediction error.

</details>


### [155] [Defending Vision-Language-Action Models against Transferable Attacks via Visual-Proprioceptive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28346)
*Jiahao Wang,Ming F. Li,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于本体感知的防御框架，利用可信本体感知状态检测并缓解VLA模型中的可迁移对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型易受可迁移对抗攻击威胁，且观察到此类攻击的共同后果是破坏模型对机器人自身的感知。

Method: 利用机器人本体感知状态建立可信空间参考，与从视觉特征推断的感知位置比较，通过两者差异检测对抗攻击。

Result: 实验表明能有效检测可迁移对抗补丁并提升闭环性能，真实机器人实验验证了防御有效性。

Conclusion: 基于本体感知的防御框架可有效检测和缓解VLA模型的可迁移对抗攻击，并在真实任务中有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in a wide range of robotic tasks. However, adversarial attacks in VLA models have shown strong transferability across robotic tasks, posing significant threats to robotic systems. Despite their different attack strategies, we observe that these transferable attacks share a common consequence: they disrupt the VLA model's perception towards the robot itself. Motivated by this observation, we propose a proprioception-grounded defense framework that leverages trusted proprioceptive information to detect and mitigate transferable adversarial attacks. Specifically, we utilize the robot's proprioceptive states to establish a trusted spatial reference, and compare it with the robot's perceived location inferred from visual features. Discrepancies between these two sources provide a reliable signal for detecting adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate effective detection of transferable adversarial patches and improved closed-loop performance. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of our defense on real-world robotic tasks.

</details>


### [156] [Learning a Food World Model That Predicts the Next State from Production Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28319)
*Inkyu Sa,Gaurav Kuppa,Steve Van der Hoeven,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria,Luis Rayas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 从61,773次生产抓取中自监督学习食物抓取的世界模型，预测抓取后高度图和抓取质量及其不确定性，质量预测RMSE为10.93 g，低于指令目标、按食物均值及控制器内部估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食物装配机器人每次抓取需达到目标重量，需要能预测抓取结果（质量与抓取后状态）的世界模型，以支持规划与策略学习。

Method: 利用相机和秤自动标注61,773次生产抓取，基于2.5 mm网格的抓取前高度图、指令动作、食物和器具，预测抓取后高度图与抓取质量及不确定性；在9种食物的12,294次留出抓取上评估。

Result: 质量预测RMSE为10.93 g，比指令目标和按食物均值低9%，比控制器内部估计低24%；基于20个手工特征的梯度提升树RMSE低0.2 g但仅预测质量；利用不确定性拒绝最不确定抓取后，可对89.4%的抓取给出RMSE 10.28 g的预测；对指令体积和深度响应微弱，且在训练未见目标重量上无预警失效。

Conclusion: 世界模型可有效预测抓取质量与后状态，但受闭环生产数据分布限制；未来需动作基元条件化和跨食物与器具的泛化模型，并给出了从生产数据学习世界模型的建议。

Abstract: Food assembly robots pick ingredients from a pan, and every pick must deliver a target weight. We learn a world model of food picking from 61,773 production picks, labeled automatically by a camera and a scale (self-supervised learning). From the pre-pick heightmap of the pan on a 2.5 mm grid, the commanded pick action, the food and the utensil, the model predicts the post-pick heightmap and the picked mass with uncertainty. We evaluate the mass prediction on 12,294 held-out picks of nine foods. The production controller keeps each pick near its commanded target. The model's root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.93 g is 9% lower than that of the commanded target or of a per-food mean. This RMSE is 24% lower than that of the controller's internal mass estimate. Gradient-boosted trees on 20 hand-crafted features have a 0.2 g lower RMSE, but they predict only the mass, not the next state that planning and policy learning need. Using the predicted uncertainty, we decline the most uncertain picks and answer 89.4% of picks with an RMSE of 10.28 g. The prediction barely responds to the commanded volume and depth, because they vary little in closed-loop production. The model also fails without warning at target weights not seen in training. Future directions include action-primitive conditioning and a more general model across foods and utensils. We give recommendations for learning world models from production data.

</details>


### [157] [Probing Invisible Actuation Shifts for Test-time Calibration of Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28308)
*Jie Liu,Hui Xie,Qinfeng Feng,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PEA是一种测试时标定方法，通过主动探测识别执行偏移（增益、延迟、漂移）并补偿后续命令，同时用可移除低秩适配器适应视觉偏移，无需策略训练即可提升冻结视觉运动策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略（如VLA和WAM）部署时相机和控制器与训练数据不同，导致视觉偏移和执行偏移；执行偏移对策略不可见，即使在域随机化下也会严重降低成功率。

Method: 在每回合前执行短探测：逐轴用已知命令移动机械臂，使增益、延迟和漂移留下可分离轨迹；闭式辨识器从响应中估计参数并补偿后续命令；对耦合视觉偏移，用可移除低秩适配器自适应冻结策略。

Result: 在LIBERO上使用WAM和VLA实验，无需策略训练，辨识器恢复大部分因执行偏移损失的成功率，并泛化到训练中未见的偏移（如Cosmos-Policy在LIBERO-Spatial上从61.0%提升到89.7%）。

Conclusion: PEA通过测试时主动探测和补偿执行偏移，结合低秩适配处理视觉偏移，可即插即用于任意冻结视觉运动策略，显著提升部署鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world action models (WAMs) are increasingly deployed on robots whose cameras and controllers differ from those used to collect their training data. Such deployment introduces two kinds of shifts. Visual shifts, such as a moved camera, change the observed images; actuation shifts, such as a retuned controller, latency or an added payload, change only how commanded actions translate into robot motion. Invisible to the policy, actuation shifts sharply degrade its success, even under domain randomization. In this work, we propose PEA, a test-time calibration method that identifies actuation shifts through active probing before task execution. Specifically, before each episode, the robot executes a short probe that moves one arm axis at a time with known commands, so that gain, delay and drift leave separable traces. A closed-form identifier estimates them from the response, and PEA compensates every subsequent command so that the robot executes the motion the policy intends. The identifier requires no policy training and thus applies to any frozen visuomotor policy. Since visual shifts remain visible to the policy, PEA further adapts the frozen policy with a removable low-rank adapter, addressing coupled visual and actuation shifts. Experiments on LIBERO with a WAM and a VLA show that, without any policy training, the identifier of PEA recovers most of the success lost to actuation shifts and generalizes to shifts unseen during training (e.g., from 61.0% to 89.7% on LIBERO-Spatial with Cosmos-Policy).

</details>


### [158] [Intervention-Robust VLA: Diverse Demonstrations for Robot Deployment with Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28289)
*Tzu-Yuan Huang,Shao-Hsuan Chang,Dai-Jie Wu,Ralf Römer,Yung-Hui Li,Stefan Sosnowski,Angela P. Schoellig,Kenneth Marino,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InteR-VLA是一个干预鲁棒的VLA框架，通过对齐训练分布与安全过滤后的部署分布，在保持安全性的同时显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数据中直接学习安全行为需要大量安全关键场景覆盖，现实中不可行；测试时安全过滤器虽能纠正不安全动作，但会把机器人推入训练数据覆盖不足的状态，导致任务性能下降。

Method: 提出InteR-VLA框架，利用多样化的完成任务示范扩大任务相关状态覆盖，并在相同数据预算下使训练分布与安全过滤后的部署分布更好对齐，从而缓解干预引发的分布偏移。

Result: 在分布内和分布外真实世界实验中，InteR-VLA保持安全性，将Safe Success Rate从标称策略的0%提升至最高90%，并在两种评测VLA上持续优于标称数据训练。

Conclusion: 通过分布对齐实现干预鲁棒性，可在不增加数据预算的前提下兼顾安全干预与任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown strong potential for learning complex robotic behaviors from demonstrations. However, learning safe behavior directly from data requires extensive coverage of safety-critical situations, which is often impractical in real-world settings. Test-time safety filters provide an alternative by correcting unsafe actions during deployment, but these interventions can drive the robot into states poorly covered by the training data, thereby degrading task performance. We introduce InteR-VLA, an intervention-robust framework for VLAs that leverages diverse task-completing demonstrations to broaden task-relevant state coverage and maintain competence under safety interventions. By better aligning the training distribution with safety-filtered deployment under the same data budget, our approach mitigates intervention-induced distribution shift while preserving task performance. Real-world experiments across in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings show that InteR-VLA maintains safety, increases Safe Success Rate up to 90% from 0% for nominal policies, and consistently outperforms nominal-data training on both evaluated VLAs.

</details>


### [159] [Neural Fidelity Calibration for Informative Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28274)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经保真度校准（NFC），用条件流匹配在线校准仿真器物理参数与残差保真度域，缩小sim-to-real差距并提升真实机器人策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域随机化依赖专家物理知识，基于真实数据的仿真器校准仅针对少数手工挑选参数，且仿真器遗漏真实效应、感知重建环境含传感误差，限制了策略改进。

Method: NFC采用条件流匹配在潜空间中在线推断仿真器物理参数与残差保真度域的后验，残差域刻画sim-to-real动力学差异与感知不确定性；仅检测到异常时微调预训练策略，序列NFC以仿真预训练估计器为提议种子，并用幻觉策略按乐观不确定性调制后验不确定性。

Result: 在五台机器人（最多39个物理参数及128x128残差地形）上，NFC后验精度优于现有基于仿真的推断方法；在断轴轮式机器人于雪地、泥地和湿草上，崎岖地形成功率从26%提升至70%，朝向抖动降低88%。

Conclusion: NFC能准确在线校准仿真保真度并有效支持real-to-sim策略微调，显著提升真实部署性能与运动平稳性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) can transfer agile robot skills from simulation to the real world, but closing the sim-to-real gap by domain randomization requires expert physics knowledge, and simulator calibration from real data typically targets a few hand-picked parameters. Even then, simulators miss real-world effects, and environments reconstructed from onboard perception inherit sensing errors that limit policy improvement. We propose Neural Fidelity Calibration (NFC), which uses conditional flow matching to calibrate simulator physical parameters and residual fidelity domains online during deployment. Residual fidelity captures sim-to-real dynamics discrepancies and perception-induced environment uncertainty. NFC operates in a latent space so that realistic environments can be sampled from the inferred posterior for real-to-sim policy fine-tuning. The framework is informative: the pretrained policy is fine-tuned only when an anomaly is detected, sequential NFC seeds its proposal from the simulation-pretrained estimator, and a hallucination policy modulates posterior uncertainty following optimism in the face of uncertainty. NFC attains the most accurate posteriors among established simulation-based inference methods on five robots with up to 39 physical parameters and a 128x128 residual terrain. On a wheeled robot with a broken wheel axle deployed on snow, mud, and wet grass, NFC raises success on rough terrain from 26% to 70% and reduces orientation jerk by 88%. Code and videos will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [160] [Neural Fidelity Calibration: Turning Field Anomalies into Simulation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28260)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Neural Fidelity Calibration（NFC）框架，基于条件流匹配从机器人执行数据在线推断仿真器物理参数与残余保真度（未建模动态及感知环境几何偏差），结合异常检测触发重校准和乐观探索策略，在五个平台及户外轮式机器人实验中显著提升后验精度与崎岖地形导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真实实验室机器人的策略迁移常在实际场景中失败；现有仿真器校准仅针对少数手工挑选参数，且即便校准也无法捕捉未建模动态，而通过机载感知重建的环境又继承了传感噪声和伪影。

Method: NFC使用条件流匹配，直接从机器人执行数据在线推断仿真器物理参数与残余保真度（剩余动态差异及感知与真实环境几何之间的差距）；由仿真训练的异常检测器仅凭机载传感信号判断何时需要重校准或微调；探索策略利用不确定性乐观原则主动调节后验不确定性。

Result: 在五个机器人平台（最多39个校准参数和128x128残余地形图）上，NFC相比既有基于仿真的推断基线获得最准确的后验分布；在轮式机器人轴故障条件下跨越雪、泥和湿草的物理户外实验中，NFC将崎岖地形导航成功率从26%提升到70%，并将姿态抖动降低88%。

Conclusion: NFC通过在线联合推断物理参数与残余保真度，并配合异常检测和主动探索，有效缩小仿真到现实的差距，显著提升真实户外环境中的导航鲁棒性与平顺性。

Abstract: A policy that transfers from simulation to a lab robot often fails in the field. Calibrating a simulator from real-world data typically targets only a few hand-picked parameters, and even calibrated simulators fail to capture unmodeled dynamics, while environments reconstructed via onboard perception inherit sensing noise and artifacts. We propose Neural Fidelity Calibration (NFC), a framework based on conditional flow matching that infers online, directly from a robot's execution data, both simulator physical parameters and residual fidelity - the remaining dynamics discrepancy and the gap between perceived and true environment geometry. A simulation-trained anomaly detector determines when recalibration or fine-tuning is required using onboard sensory signals alone, while an exploration policy actively modulates posterior uncertainty using optimism in the face of uncertainty. Across five robot platforms with up to 39 calibrated parameters and a 128x128 residual terrain map, NFC achieves the most accurate posterior distributions compared to established simulation-based inference baselines. In physical outdoor experiments with a wheeled robot suffering an axle failure across snow, mud, and wet grass, NFC increases rough-terrain navigation success from 26% to 70% while reducing orientation jerk by 88%.

</details>


### [161] [FlowPAC: Flow-Guided Visual Pruning and Action Caching for Efficient VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28256)
*Jintao Cheng,Yipu Zhang,Haozhe Wang,Lin Jiang,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPAC 是一个利用任务感知光流作为共享物理信号来联合优化视觉与动作计算的局部性感知框架，实现 VLA 模型在边缘设备上的免训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型推理延迟高、内存占用大，难以边缘部署；现有免训练加速方法（视觉 token 剪枝、动作缓存）采用静态资源分配，与动态机器人交互不匹配，导致控制性能下降及 KV-cache 布局碎片化、非关键区域计算浪费等硬件低效问题。

Method: 提出 FlowPAC 框架，以任务感知光流为共享物理信号联合优化视觉与动作计算：引入方向感知轨迹传播（DATP）与局部性感知连续 token 选择（LACT）协同处理视觉 token 动态并强化空间局部性，降低内存碎片并实现高效 KV-cache 压缩；辅以流条件动作块刷新（FCAR）根据运动动态自适应计算动作。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上的闭环评估显示，FlowPAC 相比稠密基线实现 2.13 倍端到端加速，在仅保留 30% 视觉 token 的情况下基本保持成功率。

Conclusion: 将计算分配与物理动态及硬件约束相对齐，是 VLA 模型在边缘系统高效部署的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied AI, yet their high inference latency and memory footprint remain major barriers to edge deployment. Existing training-free acceleration techniques, such as visual token pruning and action caching, typically rely on static resource allocation. This mismatch with dynamic robotic interactions not only degrades control performance but also causes severe hardware inefficiencies, including fragmented KV-cache layouts and wasted compute on non-critical regions. To address this, we propose FlowPAC, a locality-aware framework that leverages task-aware optical flow as a shared physical signal to jointly optimize visual and action computation. Specifically, we introduce Direction-Aware Trajectory Propagation (DATP) and Locality-Aware Contiguous Token Selection (LACT) to jointly handle visual token dynamics and enforce spatial locality, reducing memory fragmentation and enabling efficient KV-cache compression. This is complemented by Flow-Conditioned Action-Block Refresh (FCAR) for adaptive action computation based on motion dynamics. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on LIBERO demonstrate that FlowPAC achieves a 2.13$\times$ end-to-end speedup over the dense baseline while essentially maintaining the success rate with 30% visual-token retention. These results validate that aligning computation allocation with both physical dynamics and hardware constraints is essential for efficient VLA deployment on edge systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Seeking in Space: Benchmarking Purposeful Active Exploration of Vision-Language Models for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31215)
*Xiwei Liu,Yulong Li,Xuhui Li,Xinlin Zhuang,Zhixiang Lu,Haolin Yang,Jianxu Chen,Yifan Lu,Zhiran Zhang,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ActSpace-Bench基准，评估视觉语言模型在真实室内场景中主动探索并收集证据以回答空间问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长推理已给定的图像视频，但能否在证据未直接提供时主动在空间中搜寻证据尚不清楚。

Method: 构建包含72个真实扫描室内场景、234个探索回合、994个问题的基准；模型从单视角出发导航真实帧收集证据后作答。引入受控评估协议，将模型探索与同预算随机游走及最优路径对比，分离证据搜寻与空间推理；问题经3D标注验证且需探索，设掩蔽证据问题测试弃答能力，超95%抽样问题经人工确认正确。评估23个专有、开源及空间专用VLM。

Result: 最强模型能有目的地搜寻证据，但因记忆有限丢失大量信息；较弱及专用模型探索不优于随机游走，或无法推理所给证据。

Conclusion: 记忆与证据 grounded 推理是主动空间智能的下一个目标。

Abstract: Humans explore unfamiliar spaces with purpose, deciding where to look until they have gathered the evidence a spatial question requires. However, can vision-language models (VLMs), which reason increasingly well over curated images and videos, also seek in space when the evidence is not handed to them? We present ActSpace-Bench, a benchmark of 994 questions across 234 exploration episodes in 72 real-scanned indoor scenes, where a model starts from a single view, navigates real captured frames, and must gather evidence before answering. To assess whether such exploration is purposeful, we introduce a controlled evaluation protocol that fixes each model as the reasoner and compares its own exploration against a same-budget random walk and an oracle route, separating evidence seeking from spatial reasoning. Every question is verified to require exploration and answered from 3D annotations, masked-evidence questions test whether models know when to abstain, and over 95% of sampled questions are confirmed correct by human verification. Evaluations of 23 proprietary, open-weight, and spatially specialized VLMs reveal that the strongest models do seek evidence with purpose but lose much of it to limited memory, whereas weaker and specialized models explore no better than a random walk or fail to reason over the evidence they are given, pointing to memory and evidence-grounded reasoning as the next targets for active spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [163] [Habitats: A Framework for Open-Ended Evolution of Agent Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31160)
*Nicolas Bolouri,Gaya Mehenni,Daniel Toyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Habitats框架通过分离种群级规划与奖励合成，实现可执行智能体目标的开放式进化，在多个环境中提升专家质量与行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的奖励生成方法通常针对预定义任务细化目标，并将探索决策与实现合成耦合，可能缩小行为库。

Method: Habitats维护奖励-策略对种群，在交叉适应度矩阵下评估每个策略于每个奖励，Planner选择父代奖励并发出定向变异指令，独立Mutator将指令转化为验证过的奖励程序；在Card Sandbox及Cart-Pole、Hopper、Cheetah环境中与独立生成、直接变异基线对比。

Result: 在四个环境和三个随机种子下，Habitats获得最高宏平均最终专家质量和行为多样性，最终多样性分别为直接变异的1.8倍和独立生成的2.5倍；在10个未见下游目标上，冻结策略库在10000适应回合的配对胜率高于同规模基线，并超过从头训练20000回合的任务专用策略。

Conclusion: 分离种群级规划与奖励合成能够发现多样、可学习且可复用的行为。

Abstract: Designing what an agent should optimize can be as difficult as optimizing the agent itself. Existing language-model-based reward-generation methods typically refine objectives for predefined tasks and couple the decision of what to explore with the synthesis of how to implement it, potentially narrowing the resulting behavioral repertoire. We introduce Habitats, a framework for open-ended evolution of executable agent objectives. Habitats maintains a population of reward-policy pairs and evaluates every policy under every population reward, producing a cross-fitness matrix that exposes self-mastery, transfer, and functional diversity. A population-level Planner uses this information to select parent rewards and issue targeted mutation directives, while a separate Mutator translates those directives into validated reward programs. We evaluate Habitats in a discrete Card Sandbox and three continuous-control environments (Cart-Pole, Hopper, and Cheetah) against Independent Generation and Direct Mutation baselines. Across four environments and three random seeds, Habitats achieves the highest macro-averaged final Specialist Quality and Behavioral Diversity, with final diversity reaching 1.8x that of Direct Mutation and 2.5x that of Independent Generation. Finally, across ten downstream objectives unseen during evolution, frozen Habitats policy libraries achieve higher paired win rates than size-matched Direct Mutation and Independent Generation libraries at 10,000 adaptation episodes, while also surpassing task-specific policies trained from scratch for 20,000 episodes. These results suggest that separating population-level planning from reward synthesis enables the discovery of diverse, learnable, and reusable behaviors.

</details>


### [164] [Self-Upgrading Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31117)
*Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Qinglong Zheng,Changyi Yang,Tong Wei,Andy Mao,Zetian Li,Chaoran Cheng,Hui Ren,Ruihan Guo,Xianrui Zhong,Xizheng Yu,Jingyuan Li,Xi Zhu,Kaiqu Liang,Chang Xu,Zun Qian,Zidi Xiong,Yuezhu Zhao,Xu Huang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自升级具身智能体，无需训练、梯度或外部模型，通过夜间分析自身记录并改写权重实现持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体终身固定权重，改进依赖额外训练或推理时开销，且任务改变后失效；权重空间编程虽有望解决，但局限于纯推理模型或依赖更大商业模型。

Method: 27B具身智能体白天执行家务任务并记录，夜间阅读记录，定位导致浪费的隐藏状态方向，重写一个权重张量；每次修改前在练习任务上对比未修改自身进行测试，仅保留有效修改。

Result: 在数千个留出回合、每任务48个新种子下，一次保留的修改使智能体显著完成更多任务、步数更少、浪费和token更少；多轮夜间升级稳定持续，无崩溃；同家族四个尺寸中仅27B有效。

Conclusion: 具身智能体可边工作边积累经验并自我升级，无需分离预训练与后训练或人工干预，为机器人终身持续学习、稳定递归自我改进开辟道路。

Abstract: An embodied agent built on a language model goes to work with the weights it was given and keeps them for life. To get better, it needs more training, or reflection, memory, search or prompts that cost every step and are lost when the task changes. Editing weights by reasoning, weight-space programming, could end this, but has stayed with models that only reason or relied on a far larger commercial model. We introduce the self-upgrading embodied agent, to our knowledge the first that learns from its own experience by upgrading its own weights on the job, between its tasks, with no training, no gradient, no reward model, no second model, no test-time scaling and no harness or prompt engineering. By day a 27B agent works in a household environment. At night it reads its record, finds many of its steps wasted on replies that reach the token limit before any action, traces the habit to one direction in its hidden states, and rewrites one tensor of its weights. An agent that rewrites itself must first never break itself, so it is its own examiner: it tests every change against its unchanged self on practice tasks and keeps only what holds. When an edit holds, the next day it is a better agent, at the same cost per step: over thousands of held-out episodes, 48 fresh seeds a task, its one kept edit makes it complete significantly more tasks, in fewer steps, with fewer wasted and fewer tokens. This self-improvement is multi-round, stable and continual: through the nights that follow, the upgrade holds, without collapse. Of four sizes of its family, only the 27B agent does this. From here on, an embodied agent need not keep training and inference apart: as it works, it gathers its own experience and upgrades itself, with no separate pre-training or post-training and no human intervention. We believe this opens lifelong continual learning for robots: stable, continual, recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [165] [Boltz-Flow: Generating High-Quality Robot Action from Boltzmann Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31038)
*Hengzhi Jiang,Haixu Liu,Jindou Jia,Tinglan Wang,Shaobo Chen,Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Rao,Laiyuan Cui,Jiaxin Dong,Yin Ma,Hai Wei,Jiaqi Bai,Tuo An,Xinyu Zhou,Hao Ban,Hongdong Zhu,Yanqi Wang,Jiaqi Dou,Nuo Qian,Qi Liu,Bin Jin,Chang Wu,Qingfeng Li,Hui Yang,Weiyu Guo,Dicong Qiu,Qi Gao,Jianwei Niu,Kai Wen,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Boltz-Flow用观测与指令条件化的玻尔兹曼机替代流匹配策略中的固定高斯源，并通过伊辛机求解源状态以提升策略在任务多样性下的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略的源分布固定、单峰且按时间步与控制维度因子化，既限制任务多样性下的表示能力，又丢弃操作所需的维度协调。

Method: 保持基础VLA不变，用条件玻尔兹曼机为二值源状态赋予能量值，以离线演示监督的listwise目标对齐冻结流积分后动作质量；推理时该能量定义伊辛实例，训练中使用物理相干伊辛机求解并解码为连续噪声后单次标准积分。

Result: 在三个基准上改进π0.5，在LIBERO与RoboTwin 2.0上优于强策略基线和噪声优化方法，在LIBERO-Plus上仅次于LEAP；噪声自适应微调、动作间热启动及SmolVLA骨干上亦有增益。

Conclusion: 条件化玻尔兹曼源可突破高斯源的表示与协调瓶颈，为流匹配VLA提供可扩展的初始化方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies integrate a velocity field from a fixed, observation-independent Gaussian source. However, since the source is unimodal and identical across all tasks, the limited representational capacity of Gaussian distributions constrains how far a policy can scale with growing task diversity. Moreover, the source factorizes over time steps and control dimensions, discarding the coordination that manipulation requires. We introduce Boltz-Flow, which replaces Gaussian initialization with a more powerful, observation- and instruction-conditioned Boltzmann source while leaving the base VLA unchanged. Boltz-Flow parameterizes this source with a conditional Boltzmann machine that assigns an energy value to each binary source state. A listwise objective, supervised entirely by offline demonstrations, aligns this energy with the quality of actions produced after frozen flow integration. At inference, the energy defines an Ising instance whose solution is decoded into continuous noise before a single standard integration. We use a physical coherent Ising machine during training. Experiments show that Boltz-Flow improves $\pi_{0.5}$ across three benchmarks, outperforming the compared strong policy baselines and competing noise optimization methods on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 and ranking second only to LEAP on LIBERO-Plus. Additional experiments show gains under noise-adapted fine-tuning, with action-to-action warm starts, and on the SmolVLA backbone.

</details>


### [166] [ActField: Learning Action Effects as Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30659)
*Yunpeng Qing,Yuheng Huang,Changxu Wei,Yilun Kong,Yichen Shi,Shunyu Liu,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActField提出一种连续时间JEPA世界模型，通过动作条件向量场对物理时间积分显式建模潜在状态演化，将动作无关演化与动作诱导变化分离，并利用参考条件表示和多步动力学损失提升长期预测，在多个数据集和操作任务上改善了视觉预测保真度与动作诱导运动预测精度，并提升下游策略规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件JEPA世界模型仅在离散观测间隔预测未来表示，使动作效应的时间演化隐式化，难以准确预测动作诱发的视觉状态变化，影响具身任务中的预测与决策。

Method: 构建连续时间JEPA世界模型ActField，用动作条件向量场在物理时间上积分潜在状态演化；将动作无关演化与动作诱导变化分离，后者由潜在上下文调制；引入参考条件表示与多步动力学损失以支持准确的长时程预测。

Result: 在LIBERO、DROID和BEHAVIOR上实验表明，视觉预测保真度和动作诱导运动预测精度优于现有世界模型；在仿真和真实操作任务中，通过VLM引导的动作选择提升了下游策略规划效果。

Conclusion: 显式连续时间建模动作效应并结构化分离演化成分，可有效提升动作条件视觉动力学预测和下游策略规划性能。

Abstract: Learning how actions change visual states is essential for prediction and decision-making in embodied tasks. Existing action-conditioned JEPA world models typically predict future representations at discrete observation intervals, leaving the temporal evolution of action effects implicit. We introduce ActField, a continuous-time JEPA world model that explicitly models latent state evolution through an action-conditioned vector field integrated over physical time. ActField separates action-independent evolution from action-induced changes, with the latter modulated by the latent context. A reference-conditioned representation and multi-step dynamics loss further support accurate long-horizon prediction. Together, these components provide a structured and temporally continuous formulation for predicting action-conditioned visual dynamics. Experiments across LIBERO, DROID, and BEHAVIOR demonstrate improved visual prediction fidelity and more accurate action-induced motion prediction over existing world models. Further evaluations on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate the utility of ActField in improving downstream policy planning through VLM-guided action selection.

</details>


### [167] [JaxNaviSim: A GPU-Accelerated, Data-Driven Simulator and Learning Benchmark for Maritime Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30600)
*Quang Anh Pham,Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Hue Tse Leong,Akshat Kumar,Tien Anh Mai,Siu Loon Hoe,Venky Shankararaman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JaxNaviSim，一个结合AIS数据与快速可控交通仿真的数据驱动模拟器和学习基准，用于评估船舶导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口交通增长使协调船舶更具挑战性，现有基于AIS的轨迹模型以开环方式预测轨迹，无法模拟对其他船舶的逐步响应或检测碰撞与到达目标。

Method: 发布新加坡海峡约1.41亿条AIS记录数据集（3万艘船、约670万船时）；为每条轨迹定义导航任务，模型每10秒操控船舶至锚点或出口；实现JAX GPU批量仿真器，纳入多种船舶动力学模型和海面流；基准测试模仿学习、离线/在线强化学习，并引入PAR安全基线。

Result: PAR将碰撞率降至1-3%；低跟踪误差不能保证到达；学习策略虽能到达多数目的地，但在20-45%的回合中发生碰撞。

Conclusion: JaxNaviSim提供了闭环、可控制的船舶交通仿真与学习基准，揭示跟踪精度与导航安全/到达之间的差距，强调需要更可靠的安全策略。

Abstract: Coordinating vessels through the shared waterways of busy ports grows more challenging as vessel traffic increases. Port authorities need realistic simulations to assess how arrival schedules, anchorage assignments, and routes affect vessel throughput and navigation safety. Existing AIS-based trajectory models predict a vessel's track from its past trajectory in an open-loop fashion. They do not simulate step-by-step responses to other ships or test whether a vessel collides or reaches its goal. To address this problem, we introduce JaxNaviSim, a data-driven simulator and learning benchmark coupling Automatic Identification System (AIS) data with fast, controllable traffic simulation, in which learned models steer vessels through recorded traffic. First, we release a dataset of ~141 million AIS records from over 30K vessels across eight high-density regions of the Singapore Strait, among the world's busiest waterways, with ~6.7 million vessel-hours. Unlike several existing AIS datasets which mostly cover open seas, ours captures close-quarters vessel traffic in the narrow strait. Second, we define a navigation task for each recorded vessel trajectory. A model steers that vessel every 10 s from its recorded state to its anchoring point or region exit, while surrounding ships follow their recorded tracks or are steered by the model. We implement a JAX simulator that runs these tasks in large batches on a GPU. We also incorporate multiple vessel dynamics models and sea surface currents. Finally, we benchmark imitation learning (IL), offline and online RL, and include Predictive Action Refinement (PAR) as a safety baseline that cuts collision rates to 1-3%. The comparison shows that low tracking error does not ensure arrival, and learned policies can reach most destinations yet collide in 20-45% of episodes.

</details>


### [168] [VeriWorld: A Verifiable Visual SWE-Bench for Spatial Reasoning in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30226)
*Yan Zheng,ShengYun Peng,Xuyao Liang,Xiaoyan Cong,Nuo Chen,Bangya Liu,Zihan Wang,Wenyan Cong,Zhiwen Fan,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriWorld是一个用于交互式3D环境中空间推理的基准，具有可执行动作和确定性验证，用于分离感知、推理及二者交互的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立测试感知和推理，难以判断失败源于视觉理解、下游推理还是二者之间的衔接。

Method: 构建VeriWorld基准，在交互式3D环境中对同一实例、环境和验证器评估匹配的视觉、结构化及组合输入条件；对无清晰划分的任务族单独报告。

Result: 发现两个诊断性现象：模型在显式提供任务相关信息时表现远好于从渲染场景中读取信息，揭示视觉到结构的差距；动作空间和测试框架设计会改变结果，表明交互协议本身是混杂变量。

Conclusion: VeriWorld开源以支持可复现、细粒度的VLM空间推理评估。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for spatial tasks such as navigation and manipulation, where they must infer geometry and object relationships from visual input in order to act correctly. Existing benchmarks usually test perception and reasoning in isolation, making it hard to tell whether failures come from visual understanding, downstream reasoning, or the bridge between them. We introduce VeriWorld, a benchmark for spatial reasoning in interactive 3D environments with executable actions and deterministic verification. For task families where all three channels are well-defined, we evaluate matched visual, structured, and combined input conditions on the same instance, environment, and verifier; families without a clean visual/structured/combined split are reported separately. This design supports controlled comparisons of perception, reasoning, and their interaction. Using this setup, we uncover two diagnostic findings. First, models often perform much better when task-relevant information, such as object identity, current state, or target location, is given explicitly than when it must be read from the rendered scene, revealing a recurring visual-to-structure gap. Second, action-space and harness design can change outcomes even under the same task and information condition, showing that interaction protocol is itself a confounding variable. VeriWorld is open-sourced to support reproducible and fine-grained evaluation of spatial reasoning in VLMs.

</details>


### [169] [CARE-3D: Reinforcement Learning for Evidence Grounded Agentic Reasoning in Situated 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29969)
*Xiaoqian Hu,Ruihong Qiu,Yanran Tang,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARE-3D是一个强化后训练框架，通过反事实证据归因和分组覆盖采样优化具身智能体的自适应3D接地推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从监督轨迹学习接地交互，但主要模仿预设的证据获取模式，而非优化所返回证据对正确答案的支持；且结果奖励对中间工具选择的信用分配有限，导致不常用工具探索不足。

Method: 先通过监督微调(SFT)建立有效的接地交互，再用分组相对策略优化(GRPO)训练智能体并冻结3D接地模块；结合反事实证据归因实现证据敏感的信用分配，以及分组覆盖采样保持工具覆盖。

Result: 在MSQA和Beacon3D上，CARE-3D相比仅使用答案和格式奖励的SFT与GRPO取得一致提升，尤其在空间推理和接地答案连贯性方面。

Conclusion: CARE-3D能自适应决定接地内容、调用工具并评估证据效用，有效提升具身3D推理中的证据支持质量与工具覆盖。

Abstract: Situated 3D reasoning requires embodied agents to interpret objects, scene geometry, and viewpoint dependent spatial relations. Existing methods learn grounded interactions from supervised traces, but largely imitate prescribed evidence acquisition patterns rather than optimizing the support that the returned evidence provides for the correct answer. Moreover, outcome rewards provide limited credit for intermediate tool choices and may leave infrequent tools underexplored. An effective agent should instead adaptively decide what to ground, which tool to invoke, and how to assess the utility of returned evidence. To this end, this paper presents CARE-3D, a reinforcement post-training framework for adaptive grounded reasoning. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on grounding instructions first establishes valid interactions, after which Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimizes the agent while keeping the 3D grounding module frozen. CARE-3D combines Counterfactual Attribution of Returned Evidence for evidence sensitive credit assignment, and Group Coverage Sampling to preserve tool coverage. Experiments on MSQA and Beacon3D show consistent gains over SFT and GRPO using only answer and format rewards, especially in spatial reasoning and grounding answer coherence. Our code is available at \href{ this https URL }{CARE-3D}.

</details>


### [170] [EMIP: Embodied Multi-Party Interaction Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29333)
*Koichiro Terao,Rikunari Sagara,Moe Nagao,Naoto Iwahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EMIP：基于Minecraft的多方具身交互平台，支持多人与多AI智能体全双工语音与同步物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类与具身AI智能体协作需要能支持多方同时进行语音对话和物理动作的共享实验平台，现有平台缺乏此类能力。

Method: 构建于Minecraft的虚拟环境，采用互联网客户端-服务器架构连接多人与多AI智能体；提供基于MCP的接口用于环境观察、动作执行和音频输入输出；支持通过单个客户端集中控制智能体组、多智能体协作搬运物体，以及记录与检索观察历史等实验支持功能。

Result: 通过使用示例展示了并发语音与物理动作、任务协调和重叠语音等能力；平台源代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: EMIP作为可复用平台，旨在支持不同参与者配置和智能体控制方法下的人-人、人-智能体、智能体-智能体协作研究。

Abstract: Experimental platforms that enable multiple participants to engage in spoken dialogue and perform physical actions concurrently in a shared environment are important for studying cooperation between humans and embodied AI agents. In this paper, we propose EMIP (Embodied Multi-Party Interaction Platform), a virtual environment platform built on Minecraft. Through an Internet-based client-server architecture, EMIP connects multiple humans and multiple AI agents to the same virtual environment and supports full-duplex speech communication and simultaneous physical interaction. It provides interfaces based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for environmental observation, physical action execution, and audio input and output, enabling researchers to develop their own agent control methods using large language models (LLMs). Additional features include centralized control of an agent group through a single EMIP agent client, collaborative object transportation by multiple agents, and experimental support functions such as recording and retrieving observation histories. By integrating these features into a reusable platform, EMIP aims to support research on human-human, human-agent, and agent-agent cooperation under different participant configurations and agent control methods. Usage examples illustrate concurrent speech and physical action, task coordination, and overlapping speech. The source code for the platform is publicly available on GitHub ( this https URL ).

</details>


### [171] [When Do Better Visual World Models Make Better Plans? Decision-Aligned Selection for Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28919)
*Chan Aristella Lu,Arya Fayyazi,Junil Yeo,Jin Lu,Kyu Hyung Lee,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出决策相关残差（DRR）指标，并设计决策对齐选择（DAS）规则，通过多个独立训练检查点的标准化共识来评分候选动作，从而提升视觉世界模型在目标条件规划中的候选排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型通过预测候选动作结果进行规划，但训练目标优化的是预测精度，而规划真正依赖的是候选排序质量，二者存在错位。

Method: 识别出成本误差中能重排候选的DRR部分，并引入DAS插件式选择规则：用多个独立训练检查点的标准化共识对候选打分，以平均掉检查点特有的残差。

Result: DRR降低与选择改善高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.82，预测误差为0.61）；在两种模型家族和五个任务上，DAS在12/18个配置中优于单检查点，在14/18个配置中优于训练三倍时长的单模型；闭环中在6/9个预注册LeWM比较中提升成功率。

Conclusion: 规划性能的关键在于降低决策相关残差而非单纯降低预测误差，DAS作为一种即插即用的选择规则能有效改善候选排序与闭环成功率。

Abstract: Visual world models enable goal-conditioned planning by predicting the outcomes of candidate actions in latent space. However, planning depends on how well a model ranks candidates, whereas training optimizes how accurately it predicts each one. We identify the Decision-Relevant Residual (DRR), the part of the cost error that can reorder candidates, and show that the more training reduces the DRR, the more it improves selection (Spearman $\rho = 0.82$, vs. $0.61$ for prediction error). Building on this insight, we introduce Decision-Aligned Selection (DAS), a plug-in selection rule that scores candidates by the standardized consensus of several independently trained checkpoints, which averages out their checkpoint-specific residuals. Across two model families (LeWorldModel and DINO-WM) and five tasks, DAS selects better actions than single checkpoints in 12 of 18 configurations and outperforms training one model three times longer in 14 of 18. In closed loop, DAS improves success over single checkpoints in 6 of 9 pre-registered LeWM comparisons.

</details>


### [172] [Distilling Visual Agents from Limited Interaction Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28618)
*Siyu Zhou,Jing Jiang,Chengqi Zhang,Guodong Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出利用少量任务及交互历史，通过轨迹选择和在线策略蒸馏（OPD）训练视觉智能体，仅需极少在线交互即可实现高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在少量任务和记录交互历史下，如何蒸馏出有能力视觉智能体所需交互经验量。

Method: 框架采用技能-上下文覆盖及有序视觉决策相似性选择代表性轨迹；OPD通过选定任务上的新交互或固定历史下新学生响应学习；在Navigation、Sokoban和空间建图任务上评估。

Result: 单个在线训练任务即可迁移；四条固定轨迹将4B学生Navigation成功率从49.17%升至82.08%、Sokoban从60.16%升至85.55%，恢复超90%与27B教师差距；独立Navigation面板回放将成功率从45.83%升至76.67%，保留91.36%同任务在线增益且优化期间无新交互；训练重复、匹配SFT及9B扩展进一步支持紧凑经验价值。

Conclusion: 极少交互经验结合OPD即可蒸馏出强视觉智能体，实现高效跨任务迁移并接近大模型教师性能。

Abstract: How much interaction experience is needed to distill a capable visual agent? We study learning from few tasks and recorded interaction histories. Our framework selects representative trajectories using skill-context coverage and ordered visual-decision similarity. On-policy distillation (OPD) learns through fresh interaction on selected tasks or fresh student responses at fixed histories. Across Navigation, Sokoban, and spatial mapping, even one online training task supports transfer to other tasks. Four fixed trajectories raise a 4B student's success from 49.17% to 82.08% on standard Navigation and from 60.16% to 85.55% on Sokoban, recovering over 90% of the measured gap to a 27B teacher on these panels. On an independent Navigation panel, replay improves success from 45.83% to 76.67%, retaining 91.36% of the same-task online gain without new interaction during optimization. Training repetitions, matched supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and a 9B extension further support the value of compact experience.

</details>


### [173] [Decision models for UAVBench and the viability of edge deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28331)
*Matthew Michelson,Goran Muric,Adrien Bibal,Steven Minton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文利用UAVBench基准，研究小型结构化决策模型在无人机边缘部署中的适用性、Mac本地筛选可行性及部署软件与资源限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数无法反映模型能否在边缘计算机上实际有效运行，需探究小型模型在无人机决策任务中的真实部署表现。

Method: 在Mac上评估24种模型配置（20种生成模型尺寸/精度组合+4种开放权重结构化决策模型），对其中两种生成模型在Mac与Qualcomm RB3板上做匹配评估，并尝试在边缘设备部署决策模型。

Result: H2O Lightning 4B结构化决策模型在Mac上取得最高准确率77.65%；Mac与RB3板对LFM2 2.6B和Qwen2.5 1.5B的排名一致但准确率不完全相同；边缘设备部署时遇到转换和运行时限制。

Conclusion: 结构化决策模型适合此类任务，普通本地机器可用于候选模型初筛，但个体答案存在平台差异，实际硬件测试仍必不可少，且边缘部署受软件与资源限制约束。

Abstract: Small language models are often proposed for reasoning onboard a drone. However, a good benchmark score does not tell us whether a model will actually run, and run usefully, on an edge computer. In this paper we study three related questions using the UAV decision benchmark, UAVBench. First, are small structured decision models good candidates for these tasks? Second, can an ordinary local machine (a Mac) screen candidate models before we test them on physical devices? Third, what software and resource limits govern deployment? Our main Mac evaluation covers 24 model configurations on the same 2,000 validated multiple-choice questions from UAVBench. These include 20 size-and-precision configurations of 11 generative models, plus four open-weight structured decision models that received no additional UAVBench-specific training. A structured decision model, H2O Lightning 4B, achieved the highest accuracy we observed, scoring 77.65% on the Mac. This shows that these models are well suited to this kind of task. However, when we tried to deploy the decision models, we ran into conversion and runtime limits on the edge devices we tested, which we describe in detail. To test local screening, we ran matched evaluations of two generative models on both the Mac and a Qualcomm RB3 board. The accuracies did not match exactly, but the ranking did. LFM2 2.6B ranked above Qwen2.5 1.5B on both platforms. This supports using a local machine to shortlist candidates. That said, individual answers still differed between platforms, so testing on the actual hardware remained necessary.

</details>


### [174] [Particle Belief Representations for Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29884)
*Himanshu Gupta,Brendan Crowe,Christoffer Heckman,Bradley Hayes,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何将粒子滤波表示的多模态信念集合编码为强化学习策略可用的固定维输入，比较了统计摘要、累积量生成函数特征和置换不变集合编码器三类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子滤波能灵活表示复杂多模态信念，但其表示为加权无序集合，与传统固定维策略不兼容，因此需要研究粒子信念编码作为强化学习策略输入的效果。

Method: 在保持信念估计和策略优化固定的前提下，对比统计摘要、CGF特征和置换不变集合编码器；可训练编码器采用端到端学习或通过信念重建、任务特定预测进行预训练，并在八个任务上评估。

Result: 基于池化的集合编码器和梯度CGF特征在端到端设置下表现可靠；Set Transformer在适当预训练下性能最高；任务特定预训练在其目标捕捉决策相关信念结构时有益，而重建预训练和潜在对齐收益更具选择性。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中选择与训练粒子信念编码器时，能够良好重建信念的编码未必是控制任务的好输入。

Abstract: Particle filters flexibly represent complex, multimodal beliefs but expose them as weighted, unordered sets incompatible with conventional fixed-dimensional policies. We study particle-belief encodings as inputs to reinforcement-learning policies while holding belief estimation and policy optimization fixed. We compare statistical summaries, cumulant-generating-function (CGF) features, and permutation-invariant set encoders. Trainable encoders are learned end-to-end or pretrained through belief reconstruction or task-specific prediction. Across eight tasks, pooling-based set encoders and gradient CGF features are reliable end-to-end, while the Set Transformer achieves the highest performance with appropriate pretraining. Task-specific pretraining helps when its target captures decision-relevant belief structure, whereas reconstruction pretraining and latent alignment provide more selective benefits. For selecting and training particle-belief encoders in partially observable Markov decision processes, our results show that an encoding that reconstructs the belief well is not necessarily a good input for control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Operator-Valued Q-Learning: Compositional Bellman Recursions for Order-Sensitive Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30255)
*Swagatam Das,Swagato Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出算子Q学习（OpQ），用非对易算子表示动作对隐藏物理状态的作用，将价值函数推广为算子值函数，实现脉冲序列、航天器姿态控制和手中重定向等任务的高效规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子脉冲序列、航天器姿态控制和手中重定向等场景中，动作作为非对易算子作用于隐藏物理状态，结果依赖于动作顺序。经典标量价值函数无法捕捉这种顺序依赖性，需要新的价值函数表示。

Method: 将Q(s,a)定义为N×N算子，Bellman备份应用动作的对偶映射：Q(s,a)=K_a^*(R(s,a)+γE V(s'))。沿规划展开时构建动作算子的有序乘积，任意潜在状态的价值通过线性迹读出。证明备份对酉和正交动力学是收缩的，并设计异步算子TD方案。针对自伴算子无最大值的问题，提出算子log-sum-exp给出每个状态-动作对的单个算子，其读出上界所有策略在所有潜在状态的价值。

Result: 实验表明，学习策略在SO(3)指向任务上匹配1000次模拟树搜索，时域达20，决策成本低约10^4倍；可零样本迁移到未见初始状态；初始状态隐藏时精确作用于信念均值，而DQN直接使用均值则失败。短时域下同模型知识的规划器样本效率更高；给定算子的DQN匹配或超过OpQ。

Conclusion: 算子值价值函数能够精确处理非对易动作的顺序效应，OpQ在保持理论保证的同时显著降低决策成本并提升泛化能力，优于经典DQN和规划方法。

Abstract: In quantum pulse sequences, spacecraft attitude control and in-hand reorientation, actions act on a hidden physical state as non-commuting operators, so the outcome of a plan depends on the order of its actions. We let the value function carry this structure: each Q(s,a) is an N x N operator, and the Bellman backup applies the action's dual map to the continuation value, Q(s,a) = K_a^*(R(s,a) + gamma E V(s')). Unrolled along a plan, the recursion builds the ordered product of the actions' operators, and the value at any latent state is a linear trace readout. It is exact for policies that do not observe the latent, the backup contracts for unitary and orthogonal dynamics, and an asynchronous operator TD scheme learns it with almost-sure convergence. Classical values live in a lattice, so a maximum over actions exists; self-adjoint operators form an anti-lattice (Kadison) and have none. An operator log-sum-exp still gives one operator per state-action pair whose readout upper-bounds the value of every policy at every latent state. The sampled update converges to it for a finite horizon, and it reduces to soft Q-learning and the QMDP bound when actions commute. We prove that scalar values on observations that ignore action order must confuse swapped histories, that exact history tables grow exponentially for free action groups but polynomially for nilpotent ones, and give a Hankel-rank lower bound on the operator dimension. In experiments the learned policy matches 1000-simulation tree search on SO(3) pointing for horizons up to 20 at ~10^4 times lower decision cost, transfers zero-shot to unseen initial states, and acts exactly on its belief mean when the initial state is hidden, where plugging the mean into a DQN does not. A planner with the same model knowledge is more sample-efficient at short horizons, and a DQN given the operators matches or exceeds OpQ.

</details>


### [176] [What Should World Models Plan Over? Learning Geometry-Aligned Representations of Body Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29759)
*Renhao Zhang,Abhranil Chandra,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotionPort通过表示身体轨迹而非动作片段，并按运动相似性组织搜索空间，解决了预测精度与规划成功之间的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划器通常在为预测学习的动作表示上搜索，但预测精度本身不能保证优化能发现成功轨迹。

Method: MotionPort学习身体运动的低维潜在表示，表示身体轨迹而非动作块，并按运动相似性组织搜索空间，无需微调状态编码器。

Result: 在四个域、七个设置、三个骨干网络上，目标达成成功率从37.9%提升到87.8%；在AntMaze-OGBench任务上达到72%成功率，最强基线仅4%。

Conclusion: 按运动相似性表示和搜索身体轨迹能使预测改进转化为成功执行，显著提升长时规划性能。

Abstract: We introduce MotionPort, which learns low-dimensional latent representations of body motion for accurate dynamics prediction and effective long-horizon planning. Existing world-model planners often search over action representations learned for prediction, yet we show that predictive accuracy alone does not ensure that optimization can discover successful trajectories. MotionPort addresses this mismatch by representing body trajectories rather than action chunks and organizing the search space by motion similarity, helping predicted improvements translate into successful execution. Without fine-tuning state encoders, MotionPort raises average goal-reaching success from 37.9% to 87.8% across three backbones and seven settings in four domains. On the evaluated AntMaze-OGBench tasks, it achieves 72% success, compared with 4% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [177] [Mirrored Min-of-K Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29008)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Hyunsoo Yun,Yupeng Zhou,Bingyao Du,Juntong Peng,Zichong Yang,Can Cui,Chuheng Wei,Ruqi Zhang,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了Min-of-K (MoK)训练中的分布偏差，并提出镜像MoK (MMoK)方法，通过额外采样和减去候选间距离来改善分布匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoK训练虽然能产生多样输出，但生成的分布通常与数据分布不匹配，且其偏差随K值变化存在不同失败模式。

Method: 分析MoK偏差随K的变化，证明仅基于目标-候选距离的损失无法恢复某些数据分布；提出MMoK，在MoK基础上多采一个样本，应用最近候选选择规则并减去该距离，以提供候选间相对位置信息。

Result: 在五个场景（合成混合、行人车辆轨迹预测、热带气旋预报、机器人策略学习、模糊医学图像分割）上实验验证，MMoK在分布学习和任务性能上均有效且通用。

Conclusion: MMoK理论上能恢复MoK无法恢复的数据分布，实验证实其有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Min-of-K (MoK) training is widely used to train models that produce diverse outputs from random noise. For each target, it draws K outputs from the model and backpropagates only through the one closest to the target. However, MoK generally does not make the generated distribution match the data distribution. In this paper, we analyze the MoK bias across all values of K and show that tuning K shifts between failure modes, from mode dropping at small K to distribution flattening at large K. We further prove that every nontrivial loss based only on target-candidate distances fails to recover some data distribution, since these distances do not reveal how the candidates are placed relative to each other. Based on these analyses, we propose mirrored MoK (MMoK), a drop-in modification of MoK training that draws one extra sample from the model, applies the closest-candidate selection rule against it, and subtracts the resulting distance. Since this distance is measured between samples of the model, it provides additional information about how the candidates are placed relative to each other. We prove that MMoK can in principle recover data distributions that MoK cannot. We empirically validate MMoK on five scenarios, namely synthetic mixtures, pedestrian and vehicle trajectory prediction, tropical cyclone forecasting, robotic policy learning, and ambiguous medical image segmentation. The results confirm the effectiveness and generality of MMoK in both distribution learning and task performance.

</details>


### [178] [Toward Adversarially Robust World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28345)
*Ethan Rathbun,Jonathan Nöther,Christopher Amato,Alina Oprea,Goran Radanović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出潜在动态世界模型在具身AI中作用关键但存在集中式失效风险，提出两种新攻击（状态幻觉与动态偏移）并建立理论分析框架，发现鲁棒潜在空间编码器是鲁棒世界模型的核心，并在四个模型家族上通过对抗编码器训练验证了鲁棒性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在动态世界模型同时支撑在线动作规划、安全策略评估与可扩展策略学习，但其集中化特性使其一旦被攻破即可同时影响规划、评估与训练，因此在广泛部署前必须理解其安全与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出两种针对下游系统的新型攻击——状态幻觉攻击与动态偏移攻击，并提出一种新的理论视角来分析潜在动态世界模型的鲁棒性；通过分析识别出鲁棒潜在空间编码器是关键组件，并在Dino World Model、Le World Model、r2Dreamer和Cosmos 3四个模型家族上进行实证验证，采用对抗编码器模型训练。

Result: 分析表明鲁棒潜在空间编码器是鲁棒世界模型的关键组成部分；在四个潜在动态世界模型家族上的实验显示，通过对抗编码器模型训练可显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 世界模型的安全与鲁棒性应在普遍部署前被充分理解；鲁棒潜在空间编码器是提升潜在动态世界模型鲁棒性的核心，对抗编码器训练是有效的改进手段。

Abstract: Latent dynamic world models have proven to be highly versatile tools for embodied artificial intelligence systems, enabling online action planning, safe policy evaluation, and scalable policy learning. However, this versatility makes world models a vulnerable and centralized point of failure as a compromised model can impact planning, evaluation, and training at once. Therefore it is critical that the security and robustness properties of world models are properly understood before they are universally deployed in embodied AI systems. In this work we take a unifying approach towards this understanding, developing two novel attacks against world models, named state hallucination and dynamic shift attacks, aimed directly at impacting downstream systems, and proposing a new theoretical lens through which to study the robustness of latent dynamic world models. Through our analysis we identify robust latent space encoders as the key component of robust world models. We empirically validate this finding on four separate latent dynamic world model families, studying Dino World Model, Le World Model, r2Dreamer, and Cosmos 3, demonstrating significant improvements in robustness through adversarial encoder model training.

</details>


### [179] [Scalable Beckmann Maps Improve One-step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28286)
*Ulrik Unneberg,Shiyi Wang,Lee Cheuk-Kit,Felix Berg,Boris Kozinsky,Yilun Du,Michael S. Albergo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Scalable Beckmann Maps（SBM），将无分类器引导（CFG）和可扩展训练配方引入Beckmann Transport Models（BTM），实现单步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BTM能从数据端到端学习一步生成映射，无需教师、蒸馏或预训练特征，但缺乏现代多步生成器所依赖的CFG和可扩展训练配方。

Method: 基于BTM流是自主动去噪场的正倍数这一观察，将CFG应用于该去噪场；实践中用映射自身的条件预测和空预测替代去噪器，无需额外网络，并刻画引入的偏差；证明引导更新与引导守恒方程一致，且引导可移动盆地边界。

Result: 在表示自编码器潜空间中从零训练，SBM在ImageNet 256x256上达到单步FID 1.6（无教师、无特征损失），FLOPs比Improved-MeanFlow基线少3.2倍；文本到图像生成中单次网络评估GenEval得分0.86；作为世界模型（无引导训练）在两项机器人操作基准的全部五项指标上优于DriftWorld，训练计算量少1.9-2.8倍且无需预训练辅助特征编码器。

Conclusion: SBM成功将CFG与可扩展训练引入BTM，在保持单步采样的同时显著提升生成质量与效率，并展现出作为世界模型的潜力。

Abstract: Beckmann Transport Models (BTM) learn one-step generative maps end-to-end from data, without a teacher, a distillation stage, or pretrained features. We introduce Scalable Beckmann Maps (SBM), which bring to BTM the two ingredients behind the quality of modern multistep generators: classifier-free guidance (CFG) and a training recipe that scales, while keeping sampling at a single network evaluation. CFG combines conditional and unconditional velocity or score predictions, which a Beckmann map does not have. Our guidance rests on one observation: the BTM current is a positive multiple of an autonomous denoising field, to which CFG applies as it does to a velocity. With exact denoisers this gives guided BTM. In practice we substitute the map's own conditional and null predictions for the denoisers, which requires no additional network and introduces a bias that we characterize. We show that the guided update is consistent with a guided conservation equation, and that guidance can move the boundaries between basins, sending more noise to the modes a condition favors. Trained from scratch in the latent of a representation autoencoder, SBM reaches a one-step FID of 1.6 on ImageNet 256x256 without a teacher or a feature loss, outperforming the Improved-MeanFlow baseline with 3.2x fewer FLOPs. In text-to-image generation it reaches a GenEval score of 0.86 with one network evaluation.
