As a world model, where we train without guidance, the recipe alone outperforms DriftWorld on all five metrics of two robot-manipulation benchmarks, with 1.9-2.8x less training compute and no pretrained auxiliary feature encoder.

</details>


### [180] [SGPC: Safety-Guiding Policy Constraint for Stable Off-Policy Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30265)
*Yifan Liu,Nan Zhang,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出安全引导策略约束(SGPC)，在演员更新时若预测越界则引导至低成本参考策略，进入安全集后约束自动失效，可即插即用地集成到离策略安全RL算法中，实验显示显著降低约束违反与成本振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始-对偶方法中累积奖励与约束惩罚的耦合优化通过自适应拉格朗日乘子进行，会在安全边界附近引发策略振荡，并放大安全集外不可靠奖励估计的影响，导致任务最优性与约束可行性难以兼顾。

Method: 引入安全引导策略(SGP)与安全引导策略约束(SGPC)构造新的约束优化问题(COP)，在演员更新时用学习到的成本评论家评估当前演员，若预测位于安全集外则由SGP提供邻近的低成本训练参考并由SGPC引导优化，进入安全集后该约束失效并退化为原COP；对新COP应用原始-对偶优化导出SGPC算法，并实现为SAC-SGPC与TD3-SGPC。

Result: 在多个Safety-Gymnasium基准上，SGPC大幅减少约束违反与成本振荡，同时保持有竞争力或更优的任务回报，实现更好的最优性-安全性权衡。

Conclusion: SGPC作为一种可插拔的演员训练约束，能有效缓解安全边界附近的振荡与不可靠估计问题，在离策略安全强化学习中实现更优的安全性与任务性能平衡。

Abstract: Off-policy safe reinforcement learning (RL) faces a challenging trade-off between task optimality and constraint feasibility. In primal-dual methods, the coupled optimization of cumulative reward and constraint penalties through adaptive Lagrange multipliers may induce oscillatory policy updates near the safety boundary and amplify the effect of unreliable reward estimates outside the safe set. To address these issues, we formulate a new constrained optimization problem (COP) for safe RL by introducing a Safety-Guiding Policy (SGP) and a corresponding Safety-Guiding Policy Constraint (SGPC). The proposed COP augments the standard COP in safe RL with a conditional safety-guidance constraint. During actor updates, the current actor is evaluated using the learned cost critic. If it is predicted to be outside the safe set, SGP provides a nearby lower-cost training reference, and SGPC guides actor optimization toward this reference. Once the policy enters the safe set, the added constraint becomes inactive, and the formulation reduces to the original COP. This formulation leads to a plug-in actor-training constraint that can be integrated into off-policy safe RL algorithms. By applying primal-dual optimization to the auxiliary COP, we finally derive an SGPC-based off-policy safe RL algorithm and implement it in two representative off-policy algorithms, yielding SAC-SGPC and TD3-SGPC. Experiments on multiple Safety-Gymnasium benchmarks demonstrate that SGPC achieves a better optimality-safety trade-off by substantially reducing constraint violations and cost oscillations while maintaining competitive or better task returns.

</details>


### [181] [How Latent Transition Geometry Matters for Planning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30064)
*Xuanya Zhu,Da Li,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究潜空间转移几何如何影响世界模型的多步规划能力，通过曲率正则化作为干预手段，发现适度正则化改善规划而过度则有害，并揭示其机制主要源于非仿射分量对动作敏感性的调节以及预测未来分布的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步预测精度无法刻画多步规划效用，而规划效用依赖于区分候选未来；因此需要理解潜空间转移几何如何塑造规划行为。

Method: 采用动作条件转移场视角，以曲率正则化为受控干预，在四个连续控制任务上评估；将转移场分解为仿射与非仿射分量；在冻结模型中直接操纵非仿射动作响应；并在LeWM和DINO-WM上使用五种采样与梯度求解器进行分析，辅以注意力可视化。

Result: 适度曲率正则化改善规划，过度正则化有害；正则化主要通过非仿射分量降低动作敏感性；直接操纵非仿射动作响应会影响候选选择与闭环成功率；曲率通常使预测未来更接近，但保留沿目标方向的差异；规划趋势在五种求解器上 broadly 一致，DINO-WM上呈现类似的rollout几何趋势，注意力可视化显示向任务相关视觉结构偏移。

Conclusion: 潜空间转移几何通过非仿射动作响应和预测未来分布结构影响多步规划，曲率正则化可作为调节规划效用的有效干预，且该效应在不同求解器和模型间具有一定一致性。

Abstract: World models support planning by predicting how the environment evolves under candidate actions. However, one-step predictive accuracy alone does not characterize multi-step planning utility, which depends on distinguishing candidate futures. This raises a natural question: How does latent transition geometry shape planning? We investigate this through an action-conditioned transition-field view, using curvature regularization as a controlled intervention. Across four continuous-control tasks, appropriately tuned regularization improves planning, while excessive regularization can be detrimental. By decomposing the transition field into affine and non-affine components, we find that regularization reduces action sensitivity mainly through the non-affine component. To assess its role in planning, we directly manipulate the non-affine action response in frozen models and show that it affects candidate selection and closed-loop success. Further analysis shows that curvature generally brings predicted futures closer together, while preserving more of the differences along the goal direction. This helps explain why the planning effects vary across tasks. We observe broadly consistent planning trends across five sampling- and gradient-based solvers on LeWM and similar rollout-geometry trends on DINO-WM. Qualitative attention visualizations reveal accompanying shifts toward task-relevant visual structure. Together, these findings clarify how latent transition geometry influences multi-step planning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Pruning Autonomous Driving VLAs: What to Preserve for Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31240)
*Seongchan Kim,Soowon Son,Sangoh Kim,Hyunah Ko,Eunbeen Hong,Hyunkoo Lee,Sanghyun Lee,WonJun Moon,Kihong Kim,Dongyeon Son,Woongchan Nam,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoOP结构化剪枝方法，通过融合推理与轨迹目标的重要性秩剪枝VLM，并聚合去噪步重要性选择固定专家MLP通道，在无微调下实现显著参数压缩与加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLA模型依赖CoT推理导致内存与计算开销大，需在保留场景理解与轨迹预测能力的前提下进行结构化剪枝。

Method: 分析Alpamayo-1.5-10B的Taylor重要性，发现推理与轨迹目标对VLM结构重要性不同、专家MLP通道跨去噪步排序更稳定且少数通道贡献主要重要性；据此提出MoOP，融合双目标重要性秩用于VLM剪枝，并聚合逐步重要性选取固定专家MLP通道子集。

Result: 24.0%参数削减下VLM剪枝性能与最强基线相当或略优，保留94.0% LingoQA分数；30.0%削减下退化最小且推理未崩溃；移除93.75%专家MLP通道使总削减达39.4%，开环误差近乎不变、闭环具竞争力；端到端推理加速1.37倍，峰值内存降低36.6%，无需剪枝后微调。

Conclusion: MoOP能有效压缩推理型VLA模型，在保持推理与驾驶性能的同时显著降低内存与计算成本。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving use chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to guide action generation, but incur substantial memory and computational costs. We study structured pruning for these models, focusing on preserving both scene understanding and trajectory prediction. Our analysis of Taylor importance in Alpamayo-1.5-10B shows that reasoning and trajectory objectives assign different importance to the same vision-language model (VLM) structures. In the action expert, MLP channels exhibit more stable importance rankings across denoising steps than query heads, and a small fraction of channels accounts for most of the measured importance. Based on these findings, we propose MoOP, which combines importance ranks from both objectives for VLM pruning and aggregates stepwise importance to select a fixed subset of expert MLP channels. At 24.0% total parameter reduction, VLM-only pruning with MoOP performs comparably to or slightly better than the strongest baselines in driving, while retaining 94.0% of the dense model's LingoQA score, far above all other methods. At a higher reduction of 30.0%, MoOP degrades much less than every other method, as it is the only one whose reasoning does not collapse. Removing 93.75% of expert MLP channels increases total parameter reduction to 39.4%, with nearly unchanged open-loop errors relative to VLM-only pruning and competitive closed-loop performance. The resulting model achieves a 1.37x end-to-end inference speedup and 36.6% lower peak memory than the dense model, without post-pruning fine-tuning.

</details>


### [183] [Lyris: Layout-Guided Long Video Generation with Persistent 3D Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31239)
*Chen Wang,Jiahui Huang,Riccardo de Lutio,Alex Trevithick,Bryan Chu,Jiawei Ren,Laura Leal-Taixé,Shengyu Huang,Lingjie Liu,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lyris框架通过结合持久点云记忆与VLM生成的3D布局锚点，实现了从单张照片生成长达三分钟、跨越50米的相机控制长视频漫游，并在返回一致性和相机精度上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的视频生成方法在已观测区域外缺乏锚点，导致相机漂移且文本提示无法精确放置物体；布局条件方法虽提供精确控制，但依赖人工编写布局。两者局限均源于“未捕获区域无锚点”这一根本问题。

Method: 提出Lyris，一个永不脱离锚点的长视频生成框架：在已观测或已生成区域使用持久点云记忆，在未观测区域由视觉语言模型（VLM）生成3D布局。关键洞察是两模型间的接口可以非常粗糙——每个物体一个带标签的3D边界框，既简单到VLM可生成、用户可编辑，又足以让视频模型沿相机路径渲染时遵循。

Result: 从单张照片生成了超过三分钟、50米的漫游视频，可穿过门进入从未展示的房间。在四个数据集上进行一分钟往返评测，相机误差降低3.8倍，返回一致性平均提升22%，大幅优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 利用粗粒度3D布局作为VLM与视频模型之间的锚点接口，能有效解决长视频生成中未观测区域的锚定问题，实现高精度相机控制与返回一致性，验证了该框架的优越性。

Abstract: Exploring a scene from as little as a single photo requires generating a long, camera-controlled walkthrough that extends far beyond what was captured, yet remains consistent upon return. Memory-based video generation methods preserve what they have already seen, but have no anchor beyond it, where the camera drifts from its path and a text prompt can name objects but not place them. Layout-conditioned methods provide such an anchor and precise control over what appears where, but rely on layouts that are typically authored by hand. Both limitations arise because, beyond what was captured, nothing anchors the video model unless a human authors one. To overcome both, we propose Lyris, a long video generation framework that never leaves the video model without an anchor: a persistent point-cloud memory where the scene has been observed or generated, and a 3D layout that a vision-language model authors where it has not. Our key insight is that the interface between the two models can be surprisingly coarse. A layout of one labeled 3D box per object is simple enough for a vision-language model to author (and for a user to edit), yet expressive enough for a video model to follow once rendered along the camera path. We demonstrate walkthroughs of over three minutes and 50 meters from a single photo, passing through doorways into rooms it never shows. When evaluated on minute-long round trips across four datasets, Lyris outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a wide margin, reducing camera error by 3.8x and improving consistency upon return by 22% on average.

</details>


### [184] [Go Far, Look Less: Active Multi-Room Reconstruction with Persistent Goals and Selective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30802)
*Jiawei Jiang,Haonan Zhou,Wendi Hu,Jiabei Hu,Yina Xiang,Kaiyue Zhou,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROAM-GS 是一种免训练主动多房间三维重建框架，通过持久目标与选择性视角耦合，在极少视角下实现高覆盖高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动三维重建在多房间场景中表现急剧下降：短视的下一最佳视角评分不为远处房间的移动过程赋值，且过度优化无法消除的近处残差；而稠密建图需流式处理数千帧才能到达远处房间。

Method: 提出 ROAM-GS：每个决策选择一个持久目标及其可变视界前缀，仅在端点采集；利用表面重建记忆通过投影更新后缺陷与观察方向对位姿评分；持久目标以剩余价值为移动过程赋值，当到达不再显著改善目标时释放。

Result: 在 HSSD 上平均覆盖率从 74% 提升至 93%，在 HM3D 上从 47% 提升至 96%（PSNR 提升 11.7 dB），保持单房间 Replica 保真度，并以少 173 倍的视角匹配 90 分钟稠密建图。

Conclusion: ROAM-GS 无需逐场景调参即可实现最先进的多房间重建，显著提升覆盖率与渲染质量并大幅降低视角预算。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction aims to allocate a sparse view budget to recover complete geometry and high-fidelity renderings. However, existing methods degrade sharply in multi-room homes: myopic next-best-view scoring gives transit no credit for distant rooms and over-polishes irreducible nearby residuals, whereas dense mappers reach far rooms only by streaming thousands of frames. We propose ROAM-GS, a training-free active multi-room reconstruction framework that couples persistent goals with selective views: each decision selects a goal and a variable-horizon prefix toward it, capturing only at its endpoint. A Surface Reconstruction Memory scores poses by projecting post-update defects and viewing directions. Persistent goals credit transit with their remaining value and are released once arrivals stop measurably improving them. Without per-scene tuning, ROAM-GS achieves state-of-the-art multi-room reconstruction while preserving single-room Replica fidelity, raising mean coverage from 74% to 93% on HSSD and from 47% to 96% (+11.7 dB PSNR) on HM3D, and matching 90-minute dense mapping using $173\times$ fewer views.

</details>


### [185] [DriveDreamer-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of Driving Perception and Agentic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30671)
*Dingyi Yao,Tingdong Yu,Guosheng Zhao,Chaojun Ni,Xiaofeng Wang,Jiming Mao,Guan Huang,Lihui Peng,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveDreamer-RSI通过感知学习与数据构建的反馈闭环实现递归自我改进，在nuScenes验证集上以约一半合成帧超越数据回放基线4.09 mAP和4.13 NDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控生成可扩展驾驶感知训练数据，但仅视觉真实感不足以保证学习收益；有效生成需随感知模型演化同时适应生成什么数据和如何构建数据。

Method: 提出DriveDreamer-RSI框架：外环实施Gap-Guided Target Adaptation，根据当前感知差距生成带标签视频并在训练后更新目标；内环使用视觉-语言智能体通过观察与执行反馈迭代编辑场景；跨轮次通过Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement从执行经验和下游学习结果中提炼可复用构建建议。

Result: 在nuScenes验证集上，使用约一半合成帧，DriveDreamer-RSI较数据集回放基线提升4.09 mAP和4.13 NDS点。

Conclusion: 感知学习与数据构建之间的双向反馈闭环能有效提升合成数据的学习增益，实现驾驶感知的递归自我改进。

Abstract: Controllable generation can expand training data for driving perception, but visual realism alone does not ensure learning gains. Effective generation must adapt both what data to generate and how to construct them as the perception model evolves. In this paper, we present DriveDreamer-RSI, a framework for recursive self-improvement of driving perception and agentic data generation through feedback between perception learning and data construction. Its outer loop implements Gap-Guided Target Adaptation, generating labeled videos from current perception gaps and updating targets after training. Its inner loop uses a vision-language agent to iteratively edit scenes through observation and execution feedback. Across rounds, Learning-Feedback-Driven Strategy Refinement derives reusable construction advice from execution experience and downstream learning outcomes. On the nuScenes validation set, DriveDreamer-RSI surpasses the dataset-replay baseline by 4.09 mAP and 4.13 NDS points using approximately half the synthetic frames.

</details>


### [186] [Zhengrong: World Models with Pole-Based Action-Response Factorization for Direct Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30666)
*Yu Zhang,Jingrui Li,Congyu Wang,Yuhang Cheng,Wenbo Chen,Xiaobo Zhang,Jing Zou,Yuxi Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zhengrong-PoleWM将潜在轨迹分解为初始锚点、自由响应和延迟响应，用共享稳定共轭极点建模时间结构，在四个视觉目标控制基准上取得90.83%宏平均成功率，并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉世界模型虽无需递归潜在展开即可预测动作序列效果，但未明确刻画动作效果的时间结构，缺乏对初始状态锚定、因果性及有界响应的保证。

Method: 提出Zhengrong-PoleWM，将潜在轨迹分解为初始条件锚点、自由响应和动作创新的延迟响应；非线性网络将视觉历史与因果动作前缀映射为响应系数，共享稳定共轭极点库决定各响应随时间演化；推导精确重锚定恒等式以识别表示平移轨迹尾部所需的额外基方向。

Result: 在Cube、TwoRoom、PushT、Reacher四个离线图像目标控制基准上，epoch 20时两个训练种子的宏平均成功率为90.83%±0.47%，比最强基线QQWorld高6.08个百分点；按验证损失选检查点达91.21%±1.00%；首轮Cube无重启正则化对照中，神经与极点响应头分别比通用直接预测器高20.67和23.34个百分点；A100上30次迭代交叉熵方法搜索中位延迟74–87 ms，均值77.5 ms，较Fast-LeWM降低15.8%，较自回归LeWM加速7.1倍。

Conclusion: 该分解在保持动作间非线性交互、精确锚定初始状态、防止未来动作影响早期预测及保证有限控制计划有界响应的同时，实现了更高成功率与更低推理延迟。

Abstract: Direct visual world models predict the effects of candidate action sequences without recursive latent rollout, yet they leave the temporal structure of action effects unspecified. We introduce Zhengrong (Zhengrong-PoleWM), a world model that decomposes a latent trajectory into an initial-condition anchor, a free response, and delayed responses to action innovations. Nonlinear networks map visual history and causal action prefixes to response coefficients. A shared bank of stable conjugate poles determines how each response evolves with elapsed time. This factorization preserves nonlinear interactions across actions, anchors predictions exactly at the initial state, prevents future actions from affecting earlier predictions, and yields bounded responses to finite control plans. We derive an exact re-anchoring identity that identifies the additional basis direction $\Psi_\kappa(s)$ needed to represent shifted trajectory tails. Across four offline image-goal control benchmarks (Cube, TwoRoom, PushT, and Reacher), Zhengrong-PoleWM achieves $90.83\% \pm 0.47\%$ macro-average success over two training seeds at epoch 20, exceeding the strongest baseline (QQWorld) by 6.08 percentage points. Selecting checkpoints by validation loss yields $91.21\% \pm 1.00\%$. In a controlled first-epoch Cube study without restart regularization, neural and pole response heads outperform a generic direct predictor by 20.67 and 23.34 percentage points, respectively. On an NVIDIA A100 GPU, median latency for a full 30-iteration cross-entropy method search ranges from 74 to 87 ms across the four tasks. The mean of these medians is 77.5 ms, a 15.8% latency reduction relative to Fast-LeWM and a $7.1\times$ speedup over autoregressive LeWM.

</details>


### [187] [Part-wise Certified State Estimation of Articulated Objects from Passive Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30646)
*Orion Desbonnet,Kei Higuchi,Alex Sykes,Ayush Deshmukh,Victor Leve,Joao Moura,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于被动双目视觉的铰接物体状态估计方法，通过检测CAD模型定义的角点并进行部件级几何认证，在无需深度、初始估计和掩码的情况下达到与最佳模型拟合方法相当的精度，并利用认证估计生成标签以提升仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业中机器人常操作薄型铰接物体（如折叠纸盒），需估计其铰接状态、位姿和半变形部件形状；学习型估计器泛化性好但处理薄部件精度低，模型拟合精度高但依赖精确深度、接近的初始估计和干净掩码。

Method: 从双目图像中检测CAD模型定义的角点，将铰接模型拟合到这些角点；将几何认证从刚性物体扩展到铰接物体，判断每个部件是否可信（因拟合对隐藏部件也会返回状态）；将未标注视频上的认证估计作为标签，用于改进仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 在七个仿真物体中的五个上，认证部件的定位精度比最强学习基线（给定深度和真实掩码）高3至5倍；在真实折叠纸盒上，端面板中位角点误差为纸盒尺寸的2.1%，比所有学习基线至少低40%，也低于在真实状态附近初始化的基于深度的拟合。

Conclusion: 方法通过部件级几何认证实现了高精度的铰接物体状态估计，在无需深度、初始估计和掩码的情况下优于学习基线，并可用于生成训练标签。

Abstract: Robots in manufacturing often handle thin, hinged objects known from CAD models that change shape during the task, like folding cartons. Monitoring such tasks requires estimating the object's articulation state, its pose and the shape of any semi-deformable part. Learning-based estimators generalise across objects but lose precision while handling thin parts. Model-based fitting is precise but often needs accurate depth, a close initial estimate and a clean object mask. We present part-wise certification of an articulated object's state, estimated from passive stereo. Our estimator rivals the precision of the best model-based fitting method without using depth, a close initial estimate or a mask. We detect the corners that the CAD model defines in both stereo images and fit the articulated model to them. Geometric certificates, which we extend from rigid to articulated objects, then tell whether to trust each part, because the fit returns a state even for hidden parts. Certified estimates on unlabelled video become labels that improve sim-to-real transfer. On five of seven simulated objects, we locate the parts we certify three to five times more precisely than the strongest learned baseline on the same readings, given depth and true masks. On a real folding carton, our median corner error on the end panels is 2.1% of the carton's size, at least 40% lower than every learned baseline and also lower than a depth-based fit initialised near the true state.

</details>


### [188] [Tri-DNO: Trilateral Diffusion Noise Optimization for 3D Human Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30607)
*Paul Hinzer,Niklas Gard,Anna Hilsmann,Peter Eisert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tri-DNO提出一种扩散噪声优化策略，通过对预训练多模态HOI扩散模型的初始噪声进行迭代优化，在不修改或重训练生成模型的前提下提升手-物体接触的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式HOI生成方法难以保证手部抓握的物理真实性，即使是微小的几何不一致（如接触不足或相互穿透）也会显著破坏真实感，而修改或重训练生成模型成本高。

Method: 该方法不修改生成模型，而是迭代优化扩散生成器的初始噪声，从而生成牢固的手-物体接触，同时减轻穿透并保持手部位置合理。

Result: 在GRAB数据集上的实验表明，相比基线方法，抓握质量得到提升，且无需额外模型训练，计算成本与现有方法相比也未增加。

Conclusion: Tri-DNO可有效改善预训练HOI扩散模型的手-物体接触质量，并具备应用于实际媒体制作场景的可行性。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D human object interactions (HOI) is essential for applications, such as augmented or virtual reality (AR/VR) or character animation of interactive entertainment. A particular challenge lies in the faithful generation of hand grasps in human object interaction scenes, where even minor geometric inconsistencies, such as insufficient contact or interpenetration, can significantly compromise realism. While diffusion-based generative models have shown promising results in HOI synthesis, ensuring physically plausible hand-object interactions remains challenging. We present Tri-DNO, a diffusion noise optimization strategy to sample from a pretrained multimodal HOI diffusion model, improving hand-object contact. Rather than modifying or retraining the generative model, our approach iteratively optimizes the initial noise of the diffusion generator, thereby generating firm hand object contact, while mitigating penetration and keeping the hand position realistic. Experiments on the GRAB dataset demonstrate improvements over the baseline in grasp quality without additional model training or increased computational cost compared to existing methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of our approach to practical media production scenarios.

</details>


### [189] [ViDiHand2: Unifying Video Diffusion Priors and Hand Geometry for 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30597)
*Yuxi Wang,Yufei Liu,Chengkai Jin,Qingyue Chen,Wenqi Ouyang,Tianyi Wei,Kerui Ren,Siyuan Huang,Zhiqi Shen,Hongsheng Li,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViDiHand2，将基于视频扩散模型的单手深度估计从直接回归改为几何约束求解，在七个数据集上显著优于18个基线，野外关节误差降低42%，检测失败减少8.82倍，运行速度57 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心人类视频中学习机器人操作需要准确的米制3D手部运动，而现有基于预训练视频扩散模型的方法依赖外观深度回归，对未见相机泛化性差。

Method: 提出几何驱动的ViDiHand2框架：引入细粒度几何提升与片段级射线拟合，通过像素对齐2D关节与MANO手部米制结构之间的几何一致性恢复米制深度；开发置信门控融合，按定位置信度自适应结合几何提升关节与MANO预测以提升遮挡和截断鲁棒性；并增强VDM骨干以更好捕捉时空表示，输出每帧2D关节和MANO预测。

Result: 在七个公开数据集上一致优于18个基线，野外绝对关节误差较最佳基线降低42%，检测失败减少8.82倍，运行速度57 FPS。

Conclusion: 将VDM手部深度估计重构为细粒度几何约束求解可显著提升泛化性和鲁棒性，为从自我中心视频学习机器人操作提供更准确的米制3D手部运动估计。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from egocentric human videos requires accurate metric 3D hand motion. Recent methods leverage pretrained video diffusion model (VDM) priors to recover occluded hands directly from full-frame videos, but their appearance-based depth regression generalizes poorly to unseen cameras. In this work, we propose ViDiHand2, a geometry-driven framework that reformulates VDM-based hand depth estimation from direct regression into fine-grained geometric constraint solving. Specifically, we introduce Fine-Grained Geometric Lifting and Clip-Level Ray Fitting, which recover metric depth by enforcing geometric consistency between pixel-aligned 2D joints and the metric structure of MANO hands, while leveraging temporal observations across the clip. Since the lifting relies on these 2D joints, to improve robustness under occlusion and image truncation, we further develop Confidence-Gated Fusion to adaptively combine geometrically lifted joints with MANO predictions according to localization confidence. Finally, we enhance the VDM backbone to better capture spatial-temporal representations and produce the per-frame 2D joint and MANO predictions that both steps rely on. Extensive experiments on seven public datasets demonstrate that ViDiHand2 consistently outperforms 18 baselines, reducing in-the-wild absolute joint error by 42% and detection failures by 8.82x compared with the best baseline on each metric, while running at 57 FPS. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [190] [Write, Evolve, Read: Streaming Cameras into a Persistent World State for 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30573)
*ZiAn Wang,Tianyi Wang,Zhihao Chen,Manni Cui,Junkai Chen,ZhiJin Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Junfei Wu,Huanyu Zhang,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersistWorld 提出了一种持久化 3D 世界状态机制，将每帧相机图像一次性写入锚定在场景中的状态而非重建，从而显著提升 4D 占据预测的 IoU 与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的世界模型在预测未来时，对场景中“不变空间”的保持能力不足，容易丢失已观测信息，而短时间内场景主要变化很小、前方大部分空间已被看到。

Method: 将相机帧流式写入一个锚定于场景的持久化 3D 世界状态，每帧仅写入一次而非从历史帧重建；该状态与预测端到端联合学习，并由一个 evolver 在自车轨迹上任意查询时刻预测该状态。

Result: 在 Occ3D-nuScenes 上，1-3 秒时最佳相机世界模型的 IoU 提升 19 点，至 8 秒提升 18 至 24 点，同时运行速度提升两倍以上。

Conclusion: 将已见空间作为模型状态并持久化维护，是提升相机 4D 占据预测精度与效率的有效途径。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting predicts the 3D semantic occupancy of a driving scene seconds ahead. Over a few seconds, the scene changes little while the vehicle moves, so most of the space ahead has already been seen. Sorting the future into unchanged, changed, and unseen space, we find that current camera-based world models fall short on the unchanged part: they fail to hold on to what they have already seen, rather than to forecast what will change. We therefore make what the model has seen its state. PersistWorld streams camera frames into a persistent 3D world state anchored in the scene, writing each frame once instead of rebuilding the state from past frames. The state is learned end to end with the forecast, and an evolver predicts it at any queried time along the ego trajectory. On Occ3D-nuScenes, PersistWorld raises the IoU of the best camera-based world model by 19 points at 1-3 s and by 18 to 24 points up to 8 s, while running more than twice as fast. Code and models: this https URL . Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [191] [Inclusion of obstacles in ground point classification in ALS data for terrain models used for analysis of vehicle traversability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30471)
*Mikael Lundbäck,Erik Wallin,Sima Mohtasami,Martin Servin,Mattias Nyström,Henrik Persson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了不同ALS点云地面分类算法在包含障碍物的森林场景中的性能，发现CSF算法获得最低地形模型RMSE（平均0.08米），但性能高度依赖参数设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境中ALS点云分类因地形、传感器、飞行参数和点密度差异而具有挑战性；近年点密度显著增加，为低密度点云设计的现有算法可能失效，且大多数算法无法在分类巨石等障碍物的同时区分灌木等非障碍物。

Method: 在将障碍物纳入地面类别的条件下，使用点云直接指标（精确率、召回率、F1分数）和地形模型指标（RMSE及差异图）评估多种ALS地面点分类算法。

Result: 多种算法表现良好，但性能受参数影响很大；CSF算法在地形模型RMSE上表现最佳，平均RMSE为0.08米。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，现有算法可经调整以将障碍物纳入地面类别，从而生成适用于越野机械路径规划与导航的地形模型。

Abstract: Classification of ALS point clouds has been studied for decades, but it remains challenging in forest contexts due to the great variety of terrain types, sensor equipment, flight parameters, and resulting point densities. Point cloud density has increased significantly in recent years, and existing algorithms, developed for lower density point clouds, may no longer be effective. It is beyond the scope of most existing algorithms to classify obstacles, such as boulders, while also distinguishing non-obstacles, such as bushes and undergrowth. We evaluate the performance of different algorithms for ground point classification in ALS data in a context where obstacles are included in the ground class, resulting in terrain models useful for offroad machinery route planning and navigation. The evaluation is performed using metrics directly on the point clouds (Precision, Recall, $F_1$-score) and on resulting terrain models (RMSE and maps of differences). The results show that there are several algorithms that perform well, but performance is highly affected by the parameters used. The best results in terms of terrain model RMSE are obtained with the CSF-algorithm, with an average RMSE of 0.08 meters. The results indicate that existing algorithms can be adapted to include obstacles in the ground class under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [192] [ColonoWorld: Camera-Controlled Full-Procedure Colonoscopy Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30372)
*Jihwan Hong,Sunghwan Steve Cho,Ping Shu Ho,Joo Seong Kim,Bokyung Kim,Nam-Joon Kim,Simon See,Ji Won Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ColonoWorld 是一个结肠镜世界模型，根据相机轨迹和结肠三维管道生成整个结肠镜操作视频，可控制病变与器械事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜导航系统正从辅助医生转向自主操控内镜，错误运动可能伤害患者，因此需要能遵循指令运动、保持解剖一致并呈现病变与操作事件的结肠镜世界模型。现有通用视频生成器在运动下丢失结肠结构，内镜专用生成器无相机控制。

Method: 提出 ColonoWorld，以相机轨迹和粗略结肠三维管道为条件，基于活体视频训练；可从全结肠管道生成盲肠到直肠的完整操作视频，并在指定位置和时间放置息肉、器械与冲洗。

Result: 相比最先进视频生成器，相机轨迹误差降低 32%，FVD 降低 29%；可泛化到未见过的结肠形状。

Conclusion: ColonoWorld 为结肠镜导航系统的开发与评估提供了可控、解剖一致的世界模型，代码与标注将开源。

Abstract: Navigation systems in colonoscopy are advancing from guiding the endoscopist to steering the scope autonomously. Since an incorrect motion can injure the patient, developing and evaluating these systems calls for a world model of colonoscopy. Such a model must follow a commanded scope motion, keep the colon anatomy consistent, and show lesions and procedural events. Yet camera-controlled generators trained on general scenes lose the colon's structure under motion, and endoscopic generators offer no camera control. We present ColonoWorld, a colonoscopy world model conditioned on a camera trajectory and a coarse 3D tube of the colon, trained on in vivo videos. From a whole-colon tube, it generates a full cecum-to-rectum procedure, even for unseen colon shapes. It also places polyps, instruments, and irrigation at specified locations and times. ColonoWorld outperforms state-of-the-art video generators, reducing camera trajectory error by 32% and FVD by 29%. Our code and annotations will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [193] [AdvMTO: Multimodal and Temporal Adversarial Object Attacks via 2D Gaussian Splatting for BEV-Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30283)
*Zhiyang Cui,Hao Tan,Ruonan Li,Shibin Wu,Junjian Zhang,Hong'an Lin,Zhaoquan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdvMTO框架，利用2D高斯泼溅统一建模当前与历史观测，生成相机图像和激光雷达回波，以抑制BEV 3D检测器对持续性对抗目标的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗目标攻击通常针对单一模态、仅在当前时间戳协调相机与激光雷达观测，或仅在相机流中建模时间持续性，缺乏对当前与历史观测的统一构建，导致检测器累积证据未被完全抑制，攻击效果受限。

Method: AdvMTO采用2D高斯泼溅框架，用有向面元在同一物体状态中编码外观、几何和透明度，并据此生成相机图像和扫描线激光雷达回波；通过检测器原生处理构建当前与历史输入，对融合检测器在每个原生扫描中重新生成激光雷达回波；针对各检测器优化有界外观扰动，并通过历史BEV记忆传播梯度以攻击时序相机检测器；对融合检测器沿局部法线进行有界几何细化以同时扰动两种传感模态。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效抑制三种BEV检测器对交通锥的检测；时序消融实验进一步表明，检测器原生历史建模对攻击构建和优化均至关重要。

Conclusion: 统一构建当前与历史观测并采用检测器原生历史建模，可显著提升对BEV 3D目标检测器的持续性对抗攻击效果。

Abstract: Adversarial object attacks pose a threat to BEV-based 3D object detectors in autonomous driving. Existing attacks typically target a single modality, coordinate camera and LiDAR observations at the current timestamp, or model temporal persistence mainly in camera streams. Without a unified construction of current and historical observations, attacks can leave part of the detector's accumulated evidence unsuppressed, limiting their effectiveness. In this paper, we propose AdvMTO, a 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) framework for suppressing detection of persistent adversarial objects. Its oriented surfels encode appearance, geometry, and opacity in one object state, from which camera images and scanline LiDAR returns are generated. AdvMTO then constructs current and historical inputs through detector-native processing, regenerating LiDAR returns in each native source sweep for fusion detectors. We optimize bounded appearance perturbations for each detector, propagating gradients through historical BEV memory for temporal camera detectors. For fusion detectors, bounded geometry refinement along local normals further perturbs both sensing modalities. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate effective suppression of traffic-cone detection across three BEV detectors. Temporal ablations further highlight the importance of detector-native history modeling for both attack construction and optimization.

</details>


### [194] [RigidAnchor: Cross-View Geo-Localization for UAV via Iterative Planar Rigid Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30278)
*Bo Wang,Dekang Zhu,Jian Li,Zhipeng Meng,Bo Wang,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RigidAnchor，将无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位重新表述为求解两幅图像间的平面刚体变换（平移+旋转），通过迭代相关金字塔同时估计绝对位置和航向，突破网格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有U-S CVGL方法将任务视为图像检索，输出只能落在预定义网格上，精度受瓦片尺寸限制且无法给出航向；单次回归姿态虽去除网格但无法后续校正。作者观察到卫星瓦片按固定间距、尺度和方向排列，相邻瓦片构成连续卫星块，无人机下视图像与该块仅相差平面刚体变换。

Method: RigidAnchor迭代求解该变换：网络编码两幅图像并构建多级相关金字塔；每次迭代用当前估计索引金字塔，将全部空中单元的描述子池化为一个隐状态，由两个解耦头分别预测平移增量和旋转增量，旋转增量采用复合而非相加。

Result: 在Bearing-UAV-90K上，相比Bearing-UAV，跨视角平均位置误差降低78.6%，航向误差降低69.7%，HSR@15提升19.06个点。

Conclusion: 将位置与航向统一为一个几何量并迭代求解平面刚体变换，可摆脱瓦片网格对精度的限制，同时输出绝对位置和航向，显著优于检索式基线。

Abstract: A UAV that flies without GNSS has to estimate its absolute position and heading without any external signal. Most existing methods for UAV-to-satellite cross-view geo-localization (U-S CVGL) treat the task as image retrieval: they compress the UAV image and each satellite tile into separate descriptors, rank the tiles by their similarity, and return the center of the best tile. The output can therefore only lie on a pre-defined grid, its accuracy is limited by the tile size, and no heading is produced. Regressing the pose in a single pass removes the grid, but it leaves no chance to correct the estimate afterwards. We observe that satellite tiles are not independent samples. They are laid on the map at a fixed spacing, scale and orientation, so neighboring tiles join into one continuous satellite block with a consistent metric, and a nadir UAV view of that block differs from it by a planar rigid transform only, i.e. a 2D translation plus an in-plane rotation. We therefore reformulate U-S CVGL as solving for this transform between two images of different sizes. Position and heading then become parts of one geometric quantity, and the accuracy is no longer limited by the tile grid. We propose RigidAnchor, which solves the transform iteratively. The network encodes the two images and builds a multi-level correlation pyramid between them. At each iteration, it indexes the pyramid with every aerial cell carried through the current estimate, pools the descriptors of all cells into one hidden state, and lets two decoupled heads predict a translation increment and a rotation increment, the latter being composed rather than added. On Bearing-UAV-90K, the cross-view mean errors are 78.6% lower in position and 69.7% lower in heading than Bearing-UAV, and HSR@15 increases by 19.06 points. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [195] [RoomTokens: Masked Diffusion over Structured Tokens for Indoor Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30219)
*Xiaohao Sun,Angel X. Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoomTokens结构化token表示与MDIRT掩码离散扩散模型，用于生成尊重房间几何的3D室内场景布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将场景建模为对象向量序列，隐式学习空间约束，碰撞和边界违规只能靠损失函数或后处理解决；语义地图方法显式表达占用但丢失实例与方向结构。

Method: RoomTokens将每个token编码为语义类别、实例-颜色身份与朝向场，以离散网格形式表示场景；MDIRT为掩码离散扩散模型，通过共享跨切片主干联合去噪语义、实例-颜色与朝向通道，并将建筑token钳制为显式平面图约束。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集上，RoomTokens结合MDIRT在布局保真度和物理一致性上优于基于序列/向量和基于地图的方法。

Conclusion: 结构化空间token是离散3D场景合成的实用选择，能在尊重房间几何的同时保留实例与对象属性结构。

Abstract: Generating 3D indoor scenes requires a representation that captures object semantics, instance identity, height structure, and orientation while respecting room geometry. Methods that model scenes as sequences of object vectors learn spatial constraints implicitly, leaving collisions and boundary violations to losses or post-processing; semantic-map methods make occupancy explicit, but collapse instance and directional structure. We introduce RoomTokens, a sliced structured-token representation in which each token stores semantic class, instance-color identity, and facing field. This representation allows a scene to be generated as a discrete grid while preserving cues for instance grouping, orientation estimation, and 3D extent prediction. To learn the RoomToken distribution, we propose MDIRT (Masked Diffusion over RoomTokens), a masked discrete diffusion model that denoises semantic/instance-color/facing channels jointly with a shared cross-slice backbone. Architectural tokens are clamped as explicit floor- plan constraints, so the model samples furniture layouts that respect room geometry while retaining instance and object-attribute structure. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that RoomTokens with MDIRT improve layout fidelity and physical consistency over sequence/vector- and map-based methods, suggesting structured spatial tokens as a practical choice for discrete 3D scene synthesis.

</details>


### [196] [Quantization for Action, Execution for Deployment: Joint Precision and Execution Selection for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30195)
*Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Le Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PTQ4WAM 是一个面向世界动作模型的训练后量化框架，在显存、延迟与任务评测预算约束下自动选择逐层位宽分配与执行路径；在 15 个负载上任务得分中位数为 FP32 的 99.2%，固定 GPU 回放中提速中位数 2.36 倍、峰值显存下降中位数 71.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需在动作前比较大量候选未来并反复读取必须装入设备内存的权重；训练后量化虽减少权重位宽，但小模型未必可部署——临时张量可能超出 GPU 显存，且微小预测误差即可改变所选动作。因此存在两个耦合难题：找到保持任务性能的位宽分配，以及在耗尽昂贵任务试验前判定哪些分配满足真实执行约束。

Method: 提出 PTQ4WAM 框架：用校准测量引导候选位宽方案（可选引入语言模型智能体）；先在 GPU 上对全模型执行路径做性能剖析，再对可行分配投入任务试验；通过融合分组重缩放与原地读取视觉历史，使原本不可行的分配变得可部署；在内存、延迟与任务评测预算约束下联合选择逐层位宽与执行路径。

Result: 在来自四个模型族的 15 个工作负载上，任务得分中位数为对应 FP32 得分的 99.2%；固定 GPU 回放中相对 FP32 提速中位数 2.36 倍，峰值分配内存下降中位数 71.5%。

Conclusion: PTQ4WAM 表明，将位宽分配搜索与执行路径剖析、系统级优化相结合，可在严格显存与延迟约束下保持世界动作模型的任务性能，实现接近 FP32 的精度与显著的速度和内存收益；代码即将发布。

Abstract: World action models guide robots by predicting the effects of actions or generating commands from observations. A model may compare hundreds of candidate futures before acting, repeatedly reading weights that must fit in device memory. Post-training quantization reduces the number of bits used to store pretrained weights, but a smaller model is not necessarily a deployable one: temporary tensors can exceed graphics processing unit (GPU) memory limits, while even small prediction errors can change the selected action. This creates two coupled challenges: identifying bit assignments that preserve task performance and determining which of them meet real execution constraints, without exhausting costly task trials. We present PTQ4WAM, a framework for choosing layer-wise bit assignments and execution routes under memory, latency and task-evaluation budgets. Calibration measurements guide candidate proposals, optionally with a language model agent. We profile full-model routes on the GPU before spending task trials on feasible assignments. Fusing group rescaling and reading visual history in place can make an otherwise infeasible assignment deployable. Across 15 workloads from four model families, the median task score is 99.2% of the corresponding 32-bit floating-point (FP32) score. On fixed GPU replays, the median speedup over FP32 is 2.36 times and the median reduction in peak allocated memory is 71.5%. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [197] [AxE: Benchmarking Agent-Environment Generalization of Video World Models via Grounded Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30120)
*Haiyu Xu,Yiyang Chen,Jiayi Zhou,Jiaming Ji,Juntao Dai,Yaodong Yang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AxE Bench基准，沿智能体与环境8个因素组织条件变化并配对受控变体，结合视觉语言推理与计算机视觉证据评估生成未来视频的事件保真度与稳定性，20个模型均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统部署中会频繁遇到训练数据未覆盖的条件组合，通用物理智能需要能复用先验知识、预测后果并少样本适应的世界模型，但现有条件视频生成评估侧重视觉质量、提示对齐或孤立物理合理性，缺乏系统泛化能力的可测量评估。

Method: 构建AxE Bench，沿行动智能体与环境的八个因素组织条件变化，每个原始条件配对一个单侧或双侧变化的受控变体；将同一结构应用于WBench形成WBench-plus，通过世界设定或相机行为改变环境侧、通过交互指令改变智能体侧；开发评估工具，融合视觉语言推理与针对性计算机视觉证据，将视频转化为时间局部化事件并与参考事件在时间和语义上比较；用事件保真度按原始-变体对稳定性加权得到AxE Score。

Result: 在20个模型上，生成事件在时间上匹配参考事件远多于在内容上匹配；最高保真度不超过13.01，最高稳定性为0.55，最高AxE Score为7.10。

Conclusion: 这些组件将世界模型泛化从非正式愿望转变为针对真实与模拟交互的可证伪评估方案。

Abstract: Edge cases are not the long tail; they are the norm. An embodied system deployed across changing environments, bodies, objects, and tasks will routinely encounter combinations that were absent from its training data. General-purpose physical intelligence therefore requires world models that reuse prior knowledge, predict consequences, and adapt with little or no additional training. Yet current evaluations of conditional video generation emphasize visual quality, prompt alignment, or isolated physical plausibility, leaving systematic generalization difficult to measure. We introduce AxE Bench, a benchmark that organizes condition changes along eight factors of the acting agent and of its environment, and pairs every original condition with controlled variants that change one side or both. WBench-plus applies the same structure to an existing multi-turn benchmark, pairing each WBench scenario with variants on the environment side, through its world setting or camera behavior, or on the agent side, through its interaction instructions. Additionally, to score generated futures, we develop an evaluation harness that combines vision-language reasoning with targeted computer-vision evidence, converting each video into temporally localized events and comparing generated events with reference events in both time and semantics. We summarize each model with the AxE Score, event Fidelity weighted by its Stability across original-variant pairs. Across twenty models, generated events match reference events far more often in timing than in content: no model exceeds a Fidelity of 13.01, the highest Stability is 0.55, and the highest AxE Score is 7.10. Together, these components turn world-model generalization from an informal aspiration into a falsifiable evaluation program for real and simulated interactions. Our project website can be found at: this http URL

</details>


### [198] [Thermal-Guided Low-Light UAV Image Enhancement: A Reliability-Aware Guidance Network and a Unified Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30114)
*Zhicheng Zhao,Yu Wang,Jiangong Xu,Lixiang Xu,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RMGNet，用于弱光下无人机热引导低光图像增强，通过可靠性感知引导校准和多策略增强路由解决热引导空间错位与局部适用性问题，并构建UWOT数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热引导低光图像增强在夜间无人机感知中有潜力，但低光可见图像同时作为对齐参考和增强目标，导致热引导在空间对应和局部效用上不确定；错位或不合适的热线索会引入伪影，空间变化的退化需要区域特定恢复而非统一增强。

Method: 提出可靠性感知多策略引导网络RMGNet，包含可靠性感知引导校准模块RAGCM（由粗到细位移估计并联合评估对齐热引导可靠性和退化可见证据可用性）和多策略增强路由模块MSERM（基于校准引导、退化和可靠性线索生成细节保留、高光保护重光照和热补偿候选并空间路由其贡献）；构建UWOT数据集，含5000个三元组。

Result: 大量实验表明，RMGNet在增强质量上优于代表性单模态和多模态最先进方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率，并对实际跨模态错位具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RMGNet能有效应对弱光无人机场景下热引导不确定性和空间变化退化，实现区域特定增强，并公开数据集与代码。

Abstract: Thermal-guided low-light image enhancement (LLIE) is promising for nighttime unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) perception because thermal imagery remains informative under severe visible degradation. However, the degraded visible image serves as both the alignment reference and the enhancement target, making thermal guidance uncertain in its spatial correspondence and local utility under low illumination, sensor differences, and UAV motion. Misaligned or unsuitable thermal cues may introduce artifacts, while spatially varying degradation requires region-specific restoration rather than uniform enhancement. To address these challenges, we propose a Reliability-Aware Multi-Strategy Guidance Network (RMGNet) for thermal-guided UAV LLIE under weak optical-thermal alignment. Its Reliability-Aware Guidance Calibration Module (RAGCM) performs coarse-to-fine displacement estimation and jointly evaluates the reliability of aligned thermal guidance and the usability of degraded visible evidence. Based on calibrated guidance, degradation, and reliability cues, the Multi-Strategy Enhancement Routing Module (MSERM) generates candidates for detail preservation, highlight-protected relighting, and thermal compensation, then spatially routes their contributions. We further construct the UAV weakly aligned Optical-Thermal dataset (UWOT), the largest UAV-oriented benchmark to date for weakly aligned optical-thermal low-light enhancement, containing 5,000 triplets. Extensive experiments show that RMGNet outperforms representative single-modal and multimodal state-of-the-art methods in enhancement quality while retaining competitive computational efficiency and greater robustness to practical cross-modal misalignment. The dataset and code will be publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [199] [QSRadar: Query-Conditioned Sparse Radar-Camera Fusion for Efficient 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30023)
*Chenhao Yan,Hongyu Liu,Xiang Cai,Yifei Sun,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QSRadar：查询条件稀疏雷达融合框架，在Sparse4Dv3式检测器上按查询检索原始雷达点，用于高效长距离3D检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶卡车需相机语义与4D雷达几何/运动信息；全局雷达BEV计算与内存随感知范围增长，稀疏查询式检测器虽高效但全局特征构建会掩盖点级雷达几何与运动信息。

Method: 基于Sparse4Dv3式视觉-时序检测器，围绕每个演化中的目标假设检索少量原始雷达点；点表示在锚点对齐坐标系中，增强运动残差、雷达散射截面与扫描时序，由查询条件集合编码器聚合；每次锚点细化后重新检索雷达邻域，使证据选择与目标估计耦合。

Result: 在MAN TruckScenes官方验证协议下达到38.09 mAP和52.20 NDS，优于复现的Sparse4D-Radar全局Radar-BEV基线的37.08 mAP和50.78 NDS；RTX 4090 D匹配测试中延迟降低45%、峰值内存降低49%、GFLOPs降低43%。

Conclusion: 查询条件稀疏雷达融合可在保持点级雷达几何与运动信息的同时显著提升效率，并在长距离3D检测精度上超越全局Radar-BEV基线。

Abstract: Reliable 3D object detection for autonomous trucks requires both the semantic detail provided by cameras and the geometric and motion cues measured by 4D radar. Existing radar-camera detectors commonly construct global radar bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations, whose computational and memory costs grow with the perception range. Sparse query-based detectors offer greater efficiency, but global feature construction can obscure point-level radar geometry and motion information. We present QSRadar, a query-conditioned sparse radar fusion framework for efficient long-range 3D detection. Built on a Sparse4Dv3-style visual-temporal detector, QSRadar retrieves a compact set of raw radar points around each evolving object hypothesis. The selected points are represented in an anchor-aligned coordinate frame, augmented with motion residuals, radar cross section, and sweep timing, and aggregated by a query-conditioned set encoder. Radar neighborhoods are retrieved again after each anchor refinement, coupling evidence selection with object estimation. Under the official MAN TruckScenes validation protocol, QSRadar achieves 38.09 mAP and 52.20 NDS, compared with 37.08 mAP and 50.78 NDS for our reproduced Sparse4D-Radar global Radar-BEV baseline. In matched tests on an RTX 4090 D, QSRadar reduces latency by 45%, peak memory usage by 49%, and GFLOPs by 43%.

</details>


### [200] [Ego2Wrist: Bootstrapping Wrist-View Synthesis from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29850)
*Ziyao Zeng,Xiatao Sun,Hao Wang,Ziyu Guo,Youheng Yao,Jingcheng Ni,Shenyuan Gao,Fengyu Yang,Renrui Zhang,Bangya Liu,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego2Wrist从人类头戴式自我中心视频和任务描述中合成同步的手腕相机视图，并借助无真值评估指标HCC实现递归自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手腕相机视角对机器人操作至关重要，但大规模采集人类手腕相机视频困难且易干扰自然手部运动；头戴式自我中心视频更易扩展，因此需要从后者合成前者。

Method: 提出Ego2Wrist，基于约940小时配对头戴-手腕相机数据训练，联合生成三视图；提出以手为中心的跨视图一致性（HCC）指标，用于无真值评估生成手腕视图与输入头视图中手部区域的一致性。

Result: 在四个数据集上，Ego2Wrist优于微调的WorldWander，LPIPS降低28%，人类偏好率76%；HCC与基于真值的LPIPS排序相当，人类评估者在77%的回复中偏好HCC选出的生成结果；HCC支持在野外自我中心视频上进行三轮递归自我提升，生成质量逐步提高。

Conclusion: Ego2Wrist提供了一种可扩展的方法，可生成真实世界的手腕视角人类操作视频，用于机器人策略训练。

Abstract: Wrist cameras provide an action-centric view that is directly relevant to robot manipulation. However, collecting human wrist-camera videos at scale is challenging due to cumbersome setups that can interfere with natural hand movements. In contrast, head-mounted human egocentric videos are much easier to scale. We introduce Ego2Wrist, which synthesizes synchronized wrist-camera views from human egocentric videos and task descriptions. Trained on approximately 940 hours of paired head- and wrist-camera recordings, Ego2Wrist jointly generates three views. To evaluate generated wrist views without ground truth, we propose hand-centric cross-view consistency (HCC), which measures consistency between generated wrist views and corresponding hand regions in the input head view. HCC ranks model checkpoints comparably to ground-truth-based LPIPS, and human evaluators prefer HCC-selected generations in 77% of responses. Evaluated on four datasets, Ego2Wrist outperforms fine-tuned WorldWander, reducing LPIPS by 28% and receiving human preference in 76% of responses. More importantly, HCC facilitates recursive self-improvement on in-the-wild egocentric videos by selecting generated wrist views for further training. Generation quality progressively improves over three rounds. These results demonstrate a scalable approach to generating real-world wrist-view human manipulation videos for robot policy training.

</details>


### [201] [DREAM: Dual-Memory Reasoning for Anticipatory 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29753)
*Sriram Mandalika,Athira Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DREAM提出双记忆推理框架，将LiDAR语义分割重构为经验驱动过程，通过在线维护潜在记忆和错误记忆提升动态场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR语义分割方法以帧为中心，逐帧独立处理并丢弃历史上下文，导致动态场景变化下鲁棒性不足，难以应对遮挡、稀疏观测和重复失败模式。

Method: DREAM在严格在线设置下维护两个互补记忆库：潜在记忆存储先前场景的紧凑语义抽象，错误记忆记录不确定预测的表示；每步通过余弦相似度检索相关记忆条目，经轻量调制机制融入特征空间，强化一致语义模式并抑制重复失败；骨干网络冻结且不重放历史扫描，保证计算效率与记忆有界增长。

Result: 在多个大规模LiDAR基准上实验表明，DREAM达到最先进性能，在动态和小尺度物体上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 持续且错误感知的记忆机制对鲁棒的长时程感知有效，验证了将感知视为经验驱动与预判性过程的框架价值。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must not only interpret the current scene but also anticipate perceptual challenges such as occlusions, sparse observations, and recurring failure patterns. However, most existing LiDAR semantic segmentation methods operate in a frame-centric manner, processing each scan independently and discarding past contextual experience, which limits robustness under dynamic scene changes. We propose DREAM, a Dual-memory REAsoning framework for continual LiDAR seMantic segmentation that reframes perception as an experience-driven and anticipatory process. DREAM maintains two complementary memory banks in a strictly online setting: a latent memory that stores compact semantic abstractions of previously observed scenes, and an error memory that records representations associated with uncertain predictions. At each timestep, relevant memory entries are retrieved via cosine similarity and integrated into the feature space through a lightweight modulation mechanism, enabling the model to reinforce consistent semantic patterns while suppressing recurring failure modes. The backbone remains frozen and no past scans are replayed, ensuring computational efficiency and bounded memory growth. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale LiDAR benchmarks demonstrate that DREAM achieves state-of-the-art performance, with consistent improvements on dynamic and small-scale objects, highlighting the effectiveness of persistent and error-aware memory for robust long-horizon perception.

</details>


### [202] [ClothCast: Generative Cloth World Modeling under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29369)
*Yushen Hu,Yilong Song,Zhen Zhou,Apurv Kushwaha,Harshvardhan Chandirasekar,Zhu-Tian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClothCast是一个用于机器人布料操作的世界模型，通过将夹爪运动作为刚性先验并学习残差运动，从单次RGB-D观测预测布料变形，同时发布了大规模仿真数据集ClothCastBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料变形复杂且存在自遮挡，现有世界模型难以准确预测布料在规划动作下的演化；夹爪夹持的布料随夹爪运动，这一运动先验可被利用来简化预测问题。

Method: ClothCast输入单帧RGB-D、相机位姿、机器人模型和规划夹爪轨迹，无需布料网格或材质参数；将观测提升为含假设遮挡层的分层点集，用基于夹爪位姿的混合刚性先验带动点运动，并用视频扩散点流模型预测残差运动；点渲染为深度后由几何条件补全模型合成RGB，释放后采用手工设计的沉降步骤；同时发布含10,000个双臂布料操作仿真回合的ClothCastBench数据集。

Result: 在492个留出回合上，ClothCast的序列平均可见表面Chamfer距离为14.0 mm，比最强几何基线Flex-π（35.6 mm）低2.5倍；布料区域PSNR比最强布料PSNR基线Cosmos-Predict2.5高3.7 dB；在50回合消融子集上，仅用刚性先验配合学习到的初始几何和相同后处理已优于两个具备几何能力的基线，学习残差进一步将误差降低约三分之一。

Conclusion: 利用夹爪运动先验并学习残差运动的点流世界模型能有效预测布料变形，显著优于现有基线，且刚性先验本身贡献显著。

Abstract: World models support robotic manipulation by predicting how the scene evolves under a planned action. For garments this prediction is notoriously difficult: deformations are complex, and self-occlusion leaves much of the cloth unobserved. Planned gripper trajectories provide a useful motion prior because cloth held by a gripper moves with it, while the remaining deformation must be predicted. We introduce ClothCast, a world model that applies this prior to the cloth's points and learns the residual motion. ClothCast takes one RGB-D observation, the camera pose, the robot's own model and the planned gripper trajectories, with no cloth mesh or material parameters. It lifts the observation to a layered point set that includes hypothesized occluded layers, carries the points with a blended rigid prior derived from the gripper poses, and predicts the residual motion with a video-diffusion point-flow model. Over entire 4- to 11-second episodes at 10 Hz, the moved points are rendered into depth, from which a geometry-conditioned completion model synthesizes RGB; after release, a hand-designed settling step replaces the learned motion. We further release ClothCastBench, 10,000 simulated dual-arm cloth-manipulation episodes with the ground-truth cloth mesh, including occluded layers, at every frame. On 492 held-out episodes, ClothCast achieves 2.5x lower sequence-averaged visible-surface Chamfer distance than Flex-{\pi}, the strongest baseline in geometry (14.0 mm vs. 35.6 mm), and 3.7 dB higher cloth-region PSNR than Cosmos-Predict2.5, the strongest baseline in cloth PSNR. On a 50-episode ablation subset, the rigid prior alone, applied to ClothCast's learned initial geometry with the same post-processing, already outperforms both geometry-capable baselines, and the learned residual lowers its error by about a third.

</details>


### [203] [ContinuLoc: Continuous Pose Inference over Neural Fields for UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29276)
*Weikun Zhou,Jinliang Lin,Yu Zang,Lin Xie,Haiqing Fu,Sheng Ao,Shangshu Yu,Zhiming Luo,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将地图表示与位姿推断解耦，把地理参考地图编码为连续可查询的语义神经场，并在连续4DoF位姿空间中进行显式优化，从而突破离散候选库的采样分辨率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流跨视角无人机地理定位范式将地图组织为离散的2DoF候选库，使地图表示与位姿推断耦合，导致空间定位精度受候选采样分辨率限制。

Method: 将地理参考地图编码为连续可查询的语义神经场；将定位建模为在由2D地面位置、平面内朝向和视场尺度参数化的连续4DoF位姿空间上的显式优化；在共享语义特征空间中使用全局描述子按需评估位姿假设；借助ContinuScenes基准提供的完整4DoF监督学习对4DoF位姿敏感的视觉表示；推理采用从广覆盖到自适应聚焦高兼容区域的分层策略。

Result: 在逐步严格的4DoF精度标准下，实验表明该方法相较代表性定位范式取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 将地图表示与位姿推断解耦并在连续4DoF位姿空间中进行显式优化，可有效克服检索式采样的精度瓶颈，实现更高精度的跨视角无人机地理定位。

Abstract: Cross-view UAV geo-localization involves three design choices: how to represent the UAV observation, how to represent the geo-referenced map, and how to infer pose from their interaction. The dominant paradigm organizes the map as a discretized 2DoF candidate library, coupling map representation to pose inference and thus bounding spatial localization precision by candidate sampling resolution. We decouple map representation from pose inference by encoding the geo-referenced map as a continuously queryable semantic neural field, enabling localization to be formulated as explicit optimization over a continuous 4DoF pose space parameterized by 2D ground position, in-plane heading, and viewing scale. Pose hypotheses are evaluated on demand using global descriptors in a shared semantic feature space, bypassing the sampling bottleneck of retrieval. We learn 4DoF pose-sensitive visual representations under full 4DoF supervision provided by ContinuScenes, a multi-city benchmark with dense near-nadir UAV imagery and complete pose annotations. Inference proceeds hierarchically, progressing from broad pose-space coverage to adaptive concentration on high-compatibility regions. Experiments demonstrate substantial gains over representative localization paradigms under progressively stricter 4DoF accuracy criteria. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [204] [SilentWAM: Planning in the Masked State of a Diffusion World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29125)
*Ziyu Zhao,Casey Sun,Zeyu Wang,Shicong Ma,Emily T Zhang,Cheng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种规划器，直接读取被掩码的未来token的隐藏状态，无需解码未来帧即可生成轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型依赖未来预测，但测试时直接访问未来表示需要昂贵的帧生成：先生成未来帧再规划会引入额外前向传播，而未来协同训练方法在推理时又省略了显式未来预测。

Method: 使用帧级掩码世界建模在驾驶视频上预训练MMaDA-Parallel-M，预测未来帧的被掩码后缀，并显式包含所有未来帧均被掩码的输入；学习到的规划查询关注这些掩码位置和观测上下文，连续头在同一次前向传播中预测轨迹。

Result: 仅使用单前视相机，在NAVSIM v1上达到92.0 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.3 EPDMS，端到端延迟为60.3毫秒。

Conclusion: 该方法在不生成未来帧的情况下利用掩码未来token的隐藏状态进行规划，兼顾高性能与低延迟。

Abstract: World-action models learn from future prediction, but accessing their future representations directly at test time can require costly frame generation. Methods that generate future frames before planning incur additional forward passes, while future co-training methods omit explicit future predictions at inference. We introduce \method, a planner that reads the hidden states of masked future tokens without decoding future frames. We pretrain MMaDA-Parallel-M on driving video using frame-level masked world modeling, which predicts a masked suffix of future frames and explicitly includes inputs with all future frames masked. Learned planning queries attend to these masked positions and the observed context, and a continuous head predicts the trajectory in the same forward pass. With a single front camera, \method reaches 92.0 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, with an end-to-end latency of 60.3\,ms.

</details>


### [205] [RAGA: Refreshable Animatable Gaussian Avatars from a Single Photo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29026)
*Sadia Mubashshira,John Quarles,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAGA提出一种可从单张照片创建全身高斯化身的方法，约1秒完成，并能在获得新图像时无需重训练或梯度优化地刷新化身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单照片化身一旦生成即固定，照片未覆盖区域（如衣物背面）只能靠幻觉填充且无法修正；作者希望化身能随新观测持续更新。

Method: 将每张新观测映射到规范UV空间，以可见性和角度加权累积为运行和，复用单图像训练的解码器重新解码融合图；即使解码器仅见过单图像，也能利用融合证据。

Result: 在5个数据集163段视频上，单照片创建的RAGA优于LHM、LHM++和IDOL（含3个未训练数据集）；后续帧刷新整体提升0.49 dB PSNR，在偏离照片≥135°视角提升0.68 dB，SSIM和LPIPS也改善；相同16张图下LHM++提升0.15 dB而RAGA提升0.48 dB；创建耗时1.08 s，动画每帧48.1 ms（RTX 3080笔记本GPU，含拟合、抠图和姿态估计）。

Conclusion: RAGA实现了快速、可刷新且免重训练的单图像全身高斯化身，刷新机制能有效利用新视角证据并显著提升新视角合成质量。

Abstract: Avatars created from a single photo are fixed once made: regions the photo never showed, such as the back of a garment, are hallucinated and remain that way. We present Refreshable Animatable Gaussian Avatars (RAGA), a full-body Gaussian avatar that is created from a single image in about one second and can be refreshed whenever new images of the same person become available, without retraining or gradient-based optimization. RAGA maps each new observation into canonical UV space, accumulates it as visibility- and angle-weighted running sums, and re-decodes the fused map with the same decoder used for the photo. Although this decoder is trained only on single images, it makes use of the fused evidence. On 163 clips from five datasets, RAGA created from one photo outperforms LHM, LHM++ and IDOL, including on three datasets never used in training. Refreshing from later frames adds 0.49 dB PSNR overall and 0.68 dB on views turned at least 135° from the photo, where SSIM and LPIPS also improve. Given the same 16 images on all clips, LHM++ gains 0.15 dB, against 0.48 dB for RAGA. Creating an avatar takes 1.08 s and animating it 48.1 ms per frame on a laptop RTX 3080 GPU, with body fitting and matting included in creation and pose estimation included in animation. Code will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [206] [AlignHand: Multi-View Hand Reconstruction from a Frozen Monocular Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28979)
*Zhilu Yang,Kun Yang,Shiyan Wang,Anqi Xu,Xinran Huang,Yizheng Jiao,Huan Yang,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignHand是一个校准的多视图框架，基于冻结的单目手部重建先验，通过跨视图对齐和选择性融合来提升3D手部网格估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立预测的单目手部网格在尺度、位置和关节连接上不一致，限制了多视图互补信息的有效整合。

Method: 提出AlignHand框架：精化跨视图关节观测并估计其可靠性以建立共享度量参考进行对齐；在共同坐标系下按解剖区域选择性融合互补表面证据；最后通过像素对齐细化，从局部图像特征预测有界残差位移，实现全局到局部的集成。

Result: 在DexYCB-MV、HanCo和HO3D-Eval-MV上的实验表明，AlignHand在各自评估协议下取得最低的MPVPE和MPJPE，相比匹配的几何基线将MPVPE分别降低30.1%、29.1%和8.0%。

Conclusion: AlignHand能够在保留强单目先验的同时有效利用多视图互补观测，显著提升多视图3D手部重建精度。

Abstract: Modern monocular hand estimators recover detailed 3D meshes from individual images, yet effectively leveraging their predictions across multiple views remains challenging. Independently predicted meshes can disagree in scale, placement, and articulation, limiting the integration of complementary multi-view information. Hence, we present AlignHand, a calibrated multi-view framework that builds on a frozen monocular hand reconstruction prior to align and enhance its predictions. Specifically, AlignHand refines cross-view joint observations and estimates their reliability to establish a shared metric reference for alignment. Once the meshes are brought into a common frame, their complementary surface evidence is selectively fused across anatomical regions, followed by pixel-aligned refinement that predicts bounded residual displacements from local image features. This global-to-local integration enables AlignHand to exploit complementary observations while preserving the strong monocular prior. Extensive experiments on DexYCB-MV, HanCo, and HO3D-Eval-MV demonstrate that AlignHand achieves the lowest MPVPE and MPJPE among the compared methods under the respective evaluation protocols, reducing MPVPE by 30.1%, 29.1%, and 8.0%, respectively, compared with a matched geometry baseline.

</details>


### [207] [ViTrust: Learning 3D Observation Trust for Deformable Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28815)
*Zhihao Guo,Zhihao Li,Xu Ge,Lijian Zhong,Yu Lu,Haitao Wu,Longqing Zhao,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTrust通过学习3D观测信任权重，让每个跟踪点对物理参数辨识的影响随时间变化，从而提升可变形物理世界模型的未来预测泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠精确重建不能保证准确的未来预测，因为视觉可靠性本身无法决定一个观测对物理动力学辨识的信息量；需要学习观测应如何影响物理辨识。

Method: 提出ViTrust学习3D观测信任，核心是视觉-接触信任适配器，结合逐点时空证据与持久接触图上的交互历史来预测时变观测权重；采用单阶段共享前向目标，通过加权几何与对应损失联合优化权重和物理参数，并加入约束防止观测权重坍塌；仅用观测前缀学习，拟合后的物理参数在给定控制下固定用于未来预测。

Result: 在真实世界序列上，未来预测优于所评估的基线，并定性展示了新控制滚动和跨物体迁移。

Conclusion: 学习时变的3D观测信任能有效改善物理辨识，使可变形世界模型在未来预测和新控制条件下具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning deformable physical world models requires dynamics that generalize beyond observed motion. Accurate reconstruction alone does not ensure accurate future prediction, motivating us to learn how observations should influence physical identification. However, visual reliability alone does not determine how informative an observation is about physical dynamics. We propose ViTrust to learn 3D observation trust, determining how strongly each tracked point influences physical identification. Its key design is a visual-contact trust adapter that combines pointwise spatiotemporal evidence with interaction history on a persistent contact graph to predict time-varying observation weights. A single-stage, shared-forward objective jointly optimizes these weights and physical parameters through weighted geometry and correspondence losses, with constraints to prevent observation-weight collapse. All learning uses only the observed prefix. The fitted physics remains fixed for future prediction under supplied controls. Experiments on real-world sequences demonstrate improved future prediction over the evaluated baselines, alongside qualitative demonstrations of novel-control rollouts and cross-object transfer.

</details>


### [208] [NavDecision: Parallel Multi-Step Action Prediction with Persistent Self-Referential Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28796)
*Kai Wu,Yufan Hu,Quan Fang,Boning Zhang,Junyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NavDecision，一种高效多步视觉语言导航决策模型，通过并行多步动作预测与记忆增强自我指涉策略优化，在保持导航性能的同时实现最高22倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLN模型逐token生成动作描述，推理开销大；决策模型虽高效但难以兼顾短期动作连贯性与长期目标对齐。

Method: 引入并行多步动作预测（PMAP）从共享视觉语言状态预测多个动作分布，以及记忆增强自我指涉策略优化（M-SRPO）以成功轨迹为参考从失败轨迹中学习，并利用跨迭代成功记忆积累历史成功轨迹提供训练反馈。

Result: 基于4B主干在R2R-CE上成功率达64.60%，在RxR-CE上达64.57%；相比现有自回归VLN方法推理加速最高达22.28倍和18.96倍，且导航性能相当。

Conclusion: NavDecision能有效提升VLN推理效率并保持导航性能，验证了多步决策与记忆增强策略优化的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires timely decisions based on instructions and visual observations. However, autoregressive VLN models generate action descriptions token by token, introducing substantial inference overhead. Decision models offer an efficient alternative but face challenges in maintaining short-horizon action coherence and long-horizon goal alignment. To address these challenges, we propose NavDecision, an efficient multi-step VLN decision model. We introduce parallel multi-step action prediction (PMAP) to predict multiple action distributions from a shared vision-language state, and memory-augmented self-referential policy optimization (M-SRPO) to learn from unsuccessful trajectories using successful trajectories as references. A cross-iteration success memory further accumulates historical successful trajectories to provide informative training feedback. NavDecision achieves success rates of 64.60% on R2R-CE and 64.57% on RxR-CE with a 4B backbone. Compared with existing autoregressive VLN methods, it delivers significant inference speedups of up to $22.28\times$ and $18.96\times$ while maintaining comparable navigation performance.

</details>


### [209] [ArgsWorld: Unifying 3DGS Scene Reconstruction, Generation and Extrapolation with Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28792)
*Weiming Chen,Shuaiyan Liu,Xinyu Shao,Zhewen Chen,Tian Guan,Yonghong He,Xitong Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArgsWorld将自回归扩散世界模型与显式3D高斯泼溅表示统一，可直接生成可用于下游任务的三维场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散世界模型仅输出RGB帧，缺乏显式3DGS场景表示，导致学到的视觉世界动态与机器人、仿真、渲染所需的三维资产之间存在断层。

Method: 提出ArgsWorld自回归扩散模型：每个生成块中用扩散Transformer联合处理干净的历史图像潜变量与新视角的噪声潜变量，固定历史潜变量并刷新场景特征以随新区域出现修订高斯表示；采用3D自强制蒸馏，用单视图与多视图混合数据在自身生成历史上训练；通过分布匹配同时监督直接图像潜变量与高斯渲染在生成相机及额外相机上的潜变量。

Result: 实验表明ArgsWorld在多个3D场景建模任务上达到最先进性能，并统一了由位姿图像重建3D场景、由图像或文本提示生成场景、以及跨相机段（端点不连通）的场景外推三类能力。

Conclusion: ArgsWorld弥合了视频世界模型与可用3D资产之间的差距，将显式3DGS场景生成与自回归扩散世界建模统一在一个框架中。

Abstract: Autoregressive diffusion world models deliver strong video generation performance, yet they typically output only RGB frames rather than explicit 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scene representations, creating a gap between learned visual world dynamics and usable 3D assets for downstream robotics, simulation and rendering. To bridge this gap, we introduce ArgsWorld, an autoregressive diffusion model that also produces an explicit 3DGS scene. For each generation chunk, the diffusion transformer jointly processes clean historical image latents and noisy latents for new views. It keeps the historical image latents fixed and refreshes the scene features so that the Gaussian representation can be revised as new regions come into view. Our 3D Self-Forcing Distillation trains the model on its own generated histories using a mixture of single-view and multi-view data. Distribution matching supervises direct image latents and latents of Gaussian renderings at both generation and additional cameras. ArgsWorld unifies 3D scene reconstruction from posed images, scene generation from image or text prompts, and scene extrapolation across camera segments with disconnected endpoints. Experimental results demonstrate that ArgsWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple 3D scene modeling tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [210] [PSC-Gen: Physical Sequence Conditioning for Video Generation via Agentic Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28756)
*Wonsik Shin,Jinju Jang,Junhoo Lee,Yeonjin Chang,Jimyeong Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSC-Gen是一个无需训练的视频生成框架，通过智能体规划物理状态并低频注入物理序列，生成物理上合理的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型虽能生成高保真视频，但难以理解物理规律并生成物理上合理的动态；现有物理条件方法未能融入详细物理指导，导致运动不合理或视觉伪影。

Method: 提出PSC-Gen，一个无需训练的智能体框架：Physics Agent迭代规划物理状态并验证生成的关键帧，构建视觉与物理一致的关键帧序列；插值成连续物理序列后，仅注入其低频分量以引导视频模型保留物理动态。

Result: 在物理信息与机器人操作基准上的定性和定量评估表明，PSC-Gen忠实遵循指令并生成物理上合理的视频。

Conclusion: PSC-Gen通过智能体规划与低频注入有效提升视频生成的物理合理性，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have enabled the synthesis of high-fidelity, visually compelling videos. However, despite being trained on internet-scale video data, understanding underlying physical laws and generating physically plausible dynamics remain challenging. To mitigate this, conditioning video generators on explicit physical reasoning has emerged as a promising direction. Nevertheless, existing conditioning approaches often fail to incorporate detailed physical guidance into generation, resulting in physically implausible motion or visual artifacts. We propose PSC-Gen, a training-free framework that employs an agentic approach to synthesize visual conditions and inject their core dynamics via low-frequency filtering. By iteratively planning physical states and verifying generated keyframes, our Physics Agent constructs visually and physically consistent keyframe sequences. We then interpolate these keyframes into a continuous physical sequence and inject only its low-frequency components, steering the video model to preserve physical dynamics while synthesizing fine visual details. Qualitative and quantitative evaluations across physics-informed and robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that PSC-Gen faithfully follows instructions and generates physically plausible videos.

</details>


### [211] [CoopOcc: Cooperative 3D Semantic Occupancy Perception via Multi-Agent and Cross-Modal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28710)
*Zengle Zhu,Zhen Li,Tianyi Huai,Xinhu Zheng,Liuqing Yang,Rongqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoopOcc，通过多智能体与跨模态融合进行协同3D语义占用感知，并构建仿真数据集SemanticVVSim及蒸馏方法CHORD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义占用感知因遮挡和传感器覆盖存在信息缺失，多智能体合作可缓解但不同智能体信息空间效用不均，且需可靠特征表示与跨智能体3D对应及模态结构保持。

Method: CoopOcc使用自车查询跨智能体注意力（EQCA）选择性聚合对齐特征，采用基于锚点的双向特征交互（ABFI）在匹配坐标进行跨模态交换；构建SemanticVVSim仿真数据集与统一基准，并提出置信度感知层次占用与关系蒸馏（CHORD）用于仿真到真实迁移。

Result: 在SemanticVVSim上十种方法中CoopOcc总体表现最佳；在Occ3D-nuScenes上CHORD利用SemanticVVSim知识提升性能。

Conclusion: CoopOcc有效实现多智能体与跨模态协同3D语义占用感知，CHORD支持仿真到真实的知识迁移。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy perception has emerged as a promising paradigm for jointly modeling 3D scene geometry and semantic information. However, this unified representation cannot compensate for missing information caused by occlusion and sensor coverage. Multi-agent cooperation alleviates this limitation, but information from different agents is not equally useful across spatial regions. Meanwhile, feature representations from each agent form the basis of reliable multi-agent fusion. Cross?modal fusion constructs such representations by integrating complementary semantic and geometric information. Despite this complementarity, jointly establishing reliable 3D correspondence across agents and preserving modality-specific structures remains challenging. To address these limitations, we propose CoopOcc, which performs cooperative 3D semantic occupancy perception through multi-agent and cross-modal fusion. CoopOcc uses ego?query cross-agent attention (EQCA) to selectively aggregate aligned features across agents, and anchor-based bidirectional feature interaction (ABFI) for cross-modal exchange at matched coordinates. We further construct SemanticVVSim, a simulation dataset, and establish a unified benchmark. For simulation-to-real transfer, we propose confidence-aware hierarchical occupancy and relational distillation (CHORD). CoopOcc performs best overall among ten evaluated methods on SemanticVVSim. On Occ3D-nuScenes, CHORD improves performance using knowledge from SemanticVVSim. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [212] [RecallWorld: Episodic Memory for Persistent Embodied Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28666)
*Sarosij Bose,Gitika Karumuri,Chenqi Li,Allen Tu,Srikumar Sastry,Mellon Zhang,Avinash Ravichandran,Alexander Schwing,Konstantinos Karydis,Amit K. Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecallWorld是一种具有持久记忆的具身视频世界模型，通过将短多视角片段重建为Scenelet并保持片段间几何独立，解决场景重访时几何与外观保持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型缺乏持久记忆，智能体重访场景时无法保持几何与外观；独立重建未利用近邻视角的几何约束，反复重建增长的历史会把后期预测误差传播到早期几何。

Method: 从单张源图出发，用相机感知的射线提升将透视与原生鱼眼图像放入共同世界坐标系；将短多视角片段联合重建为Scenelet且片段间几何独立；基于覆盖率的检索从完整历史中选择互补Scenelet；在融合外观前用几何方法解决目标视角可见性，为后续生成提供一致记忆条件。

Result: 在四个数据集上，RecallWorld在部分重访时PSNR比最强基线提升2.6 dB，在完整循环时提升3.9 dB。

Conclusion: RecallWorld在探索与返回过程中显著更强地保持了场景身份一致性。

Abstract: Video world models generate plausible novel views but often lack persistent memory, failing to preserve a place's geometry and appearance when an agent revisits a scene. A central challenge is how previous views should be effectively used for creating memory. Reconstructing them independently underutilizes geometric constraints between nearby views, while repeatedly reconstructing a growing history can propagate later prediction errors into earlier geometry. We introduce RecallWorld, a persistent embodied video world model that jointly reconstructs short multi-view episodes into Scenelets while keeping geometry independent across episodes. Starting from a single source image, camera-aware ray lifting places perspective and native fisheye images in a common world frame, retaining wide-field content for perspective generation. Each generated segment extends the Scenelet memory and conditions subsequent generation. Coverage-based retrieval selects complementary Scenelets from the full history, while target-view visibility is resolved geometrically before appearance fusion, providing consistent memory conditioning for guiding subsequent generation. Across four datasets, RecallWorld improves revisit PSNR over the strongest evaluated baseline by 2.6 dB on partial revisits and 3.9 dB on full cycles, demonstrating substantially stronger preservation of scene identity across exploration and return.

</details>


### [213] [Dual-Stream Event-to-Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28656)
*Lei Sun,Zhendong Li,Chenxin Shao,Weilun Li,Ruibo Ming,Kaiwei Wang,Jinjin Gu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvDualDiff双流框架利用时间映射事件提供亮度参考，结合运动事件线索，通过视频扩散模型自回归重建长序列视频，减少漂移并提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机记录亮度变化但缺乏绝对强度信息，导致事件流到视频的转换本质不适定，需要亮度参考来补充事件记录的变化。

Method: 受人类视觉小细胞和大细胞通路互补处理启发，提出双流框架：时间映射事件提供亮度参考，运动事件提供运动线索；以二者为条件，视频扩散模型从单一时间映射自回归重建长序列，并用光度约束和自生成回放微调减少漂移；硬件上仅需事件相机镜头上的最小光调制机制，并构建包含时间映射事件、运动事件和同步RGB帧的EvDual数据集。

Result: 在公开基准和自建EvDual数据集上，EvDualDiff在保真度、感知质量和视频级真实感方面优于现有事件到视频重建方法。

Conclusion: 双流互补设计有效解决事件到视频的亮度歧义，实现高质量长序列重建，代码和数据集将公开。

Abstract: Event cameras capture brightness changes with high temporal resolution and dynamic range, but converting event streams into videos remains fundamentally ill-posed due to the lack of absolute intensity information. Resolving this ambiguity calls for a brightness reference that complements the intensity changes recorded by events. Inspired by the complementary spatial and temporal processing of the parvocellular and magnocellular pathways in human vision, we propose EvDualDiff, a dual-stream framework that combines a brightness reference from temporal-mapping events with motion-event cues for video reconstruction. Conditioned on both streams, our video diffusion model autoregressively reconstructs long sequences from a single temporal map. Photometric constraints and finetuning on self-generated rollouts reduce accumulated drift. At the hardware level, our approach requires only a minimal light-modulation mechanism on the lens of an event camera. We build this setup and collect a dataset comprising temporal-mapping events, motion events and synchronised ground-truth RGB frames. Experiments on a public benchmark and on our collected EvDual dataset demonstrate that EvDualDiff outperforms existing event-to-video reconstruction methods in fidelity, perceptual quality and video-level realism. The code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [214] [Seeing 3D Occupancy Through Scene-Adaptive Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28629)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Zhengxue Wang,Guangfa Wang,Jun Li,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAP场景自适应投影框架，利用深度几何先验引导2D到3D视图变换，在Occ3D上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反向投影方法将均匀分布的3D参考点投影到图像平面，采样模式与场景无关，忽视几何非均匀分布，在固定采样预算下将大量参考点分配到空白空间，导致采样效率低。

Method: 先由冻结深度模型预测度量深度图并反投影到3D空间获得几何先验；设计包含两部分的场景自适应投影编码器：先验引导参考生成在先验支持的高度区域内分配参考点、无有效支持时保留全范围采样；径向结构特征聚合通过学习的径向偏移探索多个候选位置，聚合可能位移的特征证据以应对深度误差引起的空间错位。

Result: 在Occ3D上的大量实验表明SAP达到最先进性能，并在多种占据预测框架中持续提升结果。

Conclusion: 利用深度几何先验的场景自适应投影能有效提高视图变换采样效率，并可作为通用改进稳定提升现有占据预测框架的表现。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction infers voxel-wise geometry and semantics from multi-view images, where 2D-to-3D view transformation is a crucial step. Many existing methods adopt a backward-projection paradigm, projecting uniformly distributed 3D reference points onto image planes to aggregate multi-view features. However, their scene-agnostic sampling patterns overlook the non-uniform distribution of scene geometry, allocating many references to empty space and reducing sampling efficiency under a fixed sampling budget. To address this challenge, we propose SAP, a scene-adaptive projection framework that leverages depth-based geometric priors to guide view transformation. Specifically, we back-project metric depth maps predicted by a frozen depth model into 3D space to obtain geometric priors. To leverage these priors, we design a Scene-Adaptive Projection Encoder comprising two complementary components. First, Prior-Guided Reference Generation constructs a hybrid reference set for feature sampling, allocating references within prior-supported height regions and retaining full-range sampling where valid support is unavailable. Furthermore, to account for potential spatial misalignment caused by depth estimation errors, Radial-Structured Feature Aggregation explores multiple candidate locations through learned radial offsets to aggregate potentially displaced feature evidence. Extensive experiments on Occ3D demonstrate that SAP achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently improves results across diverse occupancy frameworks.

</details>


### [215] [When Fog Becomes a Weapon: Atmospheric Attacks on Visible-Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28592)
*Chengyin Hu,Dehe Zhao,Dingyi Lu,Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Banglong Nong,Xu Kang,Jiujiang Guo,Kalibinuer Tiliwalidi,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出雾操作攻击（FMA），通过优化共享3D雾状态同时破坏可见光与红外视觉语言模型的语义性能，无需人工伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击依赖显眼非自然伪影缺乏物理隐蔽性，且先前天气攻击忽视同一大气对异构传感器的同时影响。

Method: FMA为零梯度攻击，将深度和分层感知的大气传播与波段特定成像物理（可见光气溶胶散射与热路径辐射）相结合，优化一个共同3D雾状态。

Result: FMA使同时失败率比保真度匹配的随机雾提高一倍以上，可迁移至图像描述和VQA任务，并消除跨模态补偿——恢复干净伴随视图可恢复64%–74%的双输入VQA答案。

Conclusion: 纯自然共享雾环境能够同时破坏可见光与红外互补线索，表明物理隐蔽的天气攻击对多模态感知构成实质威胁。

Abstract: Existing adversarial attacks on vision-language models (VLMs) and cross-spectral perception primarily rely on conspicuous, unnatural artifacts, such as vivid adversarial patches or active light beams, that lack physical stealthiness, whereas prior weather attacks largely overlook how one atmosphere simultaneously affects heterogeneous sensors. This raises a key scientific question: can a purely natural, shared fog environment simultaneously disrupt complementary visible and thermal infrared cues without artificial surface modifications? We introduce Fog Manipulation Attack (FMA), a zero-gradient attack that optimizes a common 3D fog state to induce simultaneous semantic failures in visible-infrared VLMs. FMA links shared, depth- and layer-aware atmospheric propagation along scene paths with band-specific imaging physics---visible airlight scattering and thermal path radiance---allowing one realistic fog state to erode both modalities' distinct cues. Experiments show that FMA more than doubles simultaneous failure rates over fidelity-matched random fog, transfers to captioning and VQA, and removes cross-modal compensation, where restoring a clean companion view recovers 64%--74% of lost dual-input VQA answers.

</details>


### [216] [WorldWeave: Rethinking Embodied Navigation through Cross-Task World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28553)
*Xuan Yao,Jiahao Zhang,Rongjunchen Zhang,Kaixuan Huang,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeave通过逆-前向动力学从状态变化中学习潜在动作，建立跨任务共享转移表示，并将未来状态预测自适应融入任务特定表示，在VLN和EVT基准上均优于专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动具身智能体的状态演化由自身运动与外部动态共同塑造，不同任务（如VLN中导航至静止目标、EVT中跟踪移动目标）捕捉互补的演化模式，但跨此类交互联合学习世界动力学仍研究不足。

Method: 提出跨任务世界模型WorldWeave，将共享预测动力学与任务特定适配相耦合；不依赖异构物理动作作为条件，而是通过逆-前向动力学从观测到的状态变化中学习潜在动作，从而建立跨任务的共享转移表示；并将生成的状态预测自适应集成到任务特定表示中用于下游决策。

Result: 在多个VLN和EVT基准上的实验表明，WorldWeave相较各任务的专用基线均取得一致提升。
