Conclusion: 以潜在动作统一跨任务状态转移、并结合任务特定适配的共享世界模型，可有效联合学习不同移动具身任务的世界动力学并提升下游决策性能。

Abstract: Mobile embodied agents continuously alter their observations through their own motion, while external dynamics further shape future interactions. Different mobile embodied tasks therefore capture complementary patterns of state evolution, yet jointly learning world dynamics across such interactions remains less explored. We investigate this problem through Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) and Embodied Visual Tracking (EVT), which respectively involve navigation toward a stationary goal and interaction with a moving target. We propose WorldWeave, a cross-task world model that couples shared predictive dynamics with task-specific adaptation. Instead of conditioning state transitions on heterogeneous physical actions, WorldWeave learns latent actions from observed state changes via inverse-forward dynamics, establishing a shared transition representation across tasks. The resulting future-state predictions are adaptively integrated into task-specific representations for downstream decision making. Experiments across multiple VLN and EVT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over specialized baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Reconstruction-Guided Dual-Branch Learning for LiDAR Anomalous Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28545)
*Guanyu Jiang,Junjie Cao,Jinshan Pan,Zhixun Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构化残差推理(SRR)与重建引导双分支学习(RGDB)，利用重建与语义判别的互补异常证据提升LiDAR异常目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR异常检测器多依赖语义判别，主要捕获类别判别特征，可能忽略正常场景中更广泛特征关系的偏差；而逐点重建误差难以区分复杂室外点云中的异常与正常变异。

Method: 提出SRR，从特征通道、时间序列和几何空间的残差结构中提取额外异常证据；并建立语义判别与正常模式重建的互补性；进一步提出RGDB，用SRR导出的正常模式一致性线索在训练中引导语义条件异常解码器。

Result: 在匹配背景误报率下，SRR比语义分支找回更多被完全漏检的目标，且在纯正常场景中误报更少；独立SRR无需目标场景训练数据的语义标签即建立强重建基线，RGDB在STU验证集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 语义判别与正常模式重建提供根本不同且互补的异常证据，二者联合可显著提升LiDAR异常目标检测的召回与精度。

Abstract: Detecting unexpected objects is essential for autonomous driving safety. Most existing LiDAR anomaly detectors rely on semantic discrimination, which primarily captures category-discriminative features but may overlook deviations from broader feature relationships in normal scenes. Reconstruction offers a complementary perspective by assessing consistency with learned normal patterns. However, pointwise reconstruction errors alone struggle to distinguish anomalies from normal variability in complex outdoor point clouds. We propose Structured Residual Reasoning (SRR) to extract additional anomaly evidence from residual structure across feature channels, time sequences, and geometric space. We further establish that semantic discrimination and normal-pattern reconstruction capture fundamentally different yet complementary anomaly evidence: category compatibility and consistency with learned normal patterns, respectively. At matched background false-positive rates, SRR recovers more objects missed entirely by the semantic branch while producing fewer false positives in normal-only scenes. Building on this finding, we propose Reconstruction-Guided Dual-Branch (RGDB) learning, in which SRR-derived normal-pattern consistency cues guide a semantic-conditioned anomaly decoder to complement reconstruction-based predictions during training. At inference, the two branches jointly exploit their complementary anomaly evidence. Experiments show that standalone SRR establishes a strong reconstruction-based baseline without semantic labels from the target-scene training data, while RGDB achieves state-of-the-art performance on the STU validation set.

</details>


### [218] [BReD: BEV Residual Diffusion for Feed-Forward Road Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28191)
*Baizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BReD是一种前馈框架，仅用相机输入即可从六相机序列重建路面高程、外观和语义，在四个nuScenes场景上达到7.34 cm高程RMSE和90.92%语义mIoU，优于需要逐场景优化的RoGS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路面重建方法难以兼顾重建质量与跨场景推理能力：基于优化的方法需要昂贵的逐场景优化，而前馈方法缺乏道路特定约束，常产生较大高程误差。

Method: BReD先通过LiDAR监督的深度学习和BEV提升估计粗高程（推理时仅用相机）；再用轻量级BEV残差扩散模型，以相机推导的深度-高程差异和感知高程的图像特征为条件进行高程精化，训练时用扩散索引扰动模拟不同差异损坏程度，推理时从干净差异做一次确定性精化；精化后的高程经时间聚合锚定道路对齐高斯，其属性由几何先验引导的解码器预测，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在四个留出的nuScenes场景上，BReD达到7.34 cm高程RMSE和90.92%语义mIoU；对比RoGS无测试时LiDAR为14.43 cm，其测试时LiDAR辅助变体为8.88 cm，且两者均需逐场景优化。

Conclusion: BReD以纯相机前馈推理实现了高精度路面高程、外观与语义重建，在精度上超越需逐场景优化的RoGS方法，兼顾了重建质量与跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate road surface reconstruction is essential for autonomous-driving mapping and simulation, but existing methods trade reconstruction quality for cross-scene inference. Optimization-based methods recover detailed surfaces through costly per-scene optimization, while feed-forward methods lack road-specific constraints and often produce large elevation errors. We propose \textbf{BReD}, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing road elevation, appearance, and semantics from calibrated six-camera sequences. BReD first estimates coarse elevation through LiDAR-supervised depth learning and BEV lifting, using only cameras at inference. A lightweight BEV residual diffusion model then conditions elevation refinement on a camera-derived depth--elevation discrepancy and elevation-aware image features. Diffusion-indexed perturbations expose the model to different discrepancy corruption levels during training, while inference performs one deterministic refinement from the clean discrepancy. The refined elevations are temporally aggregated to anchor road-aligned Gaussians, whose attributes are predicted by a geometry-prior-guided decoder without test-time optimization. On four held-out nuScenes scenes, BReD achieves 7.34 cm elevation RMSE and 90.92\% semantic mIoU, compared with 14.43 cm for RoGS without test-time LiDAR and 8.88 cm for its test-time-LiDAR-assisted variant; both RoGS variants require per-scene optimization.

</details>


### [219] [RALC: Risk-Controlled Local Adoption of Segmentation Updates with Few Calibration Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28122)
*Shize Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RALC提出一种在少量正常图像校准下选择性采纳模型局部更新的风险控制方法，在恶劣场景性能提升的同时控制正常场景误差增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为雨天和夜间驾驶微调道路场景分割模型会在正常白天图像上引入误差，而可用于校准的正常图像标注很少；整图风险检查可能拒绝本可在局部安全采纳的更新。

Method: RALC从固定模型更新中选取局部变化，利用置信度差异和图像得分构成嵌套候选；在每张正常校准图上统计每个候选相对原模型的额外误差数，误差不增或下降时损失记为0；测试时每个替换最多增加一个误差，因此替换数量可无标签地约束净增量；结合候选相关界与校准损失，选择通过风险检查的最大集合。

Result: 在BDD100K上，每次校准抽取10张正常图像、固定预算下，RALC的恶劣场景mIoU比整图采纳高1.89--3.19个百分点，但实测平均正常损失也更高；事后在每图1000像素处匹配该损失后，RALC仍领先1.135--2.428个百分点；以8×8像素单元选择变化可恢复93--98%的优势；使用250张校准图像时，两种规则均完整采纳全部三个更新。

Conclusion: RALC能在有限正常图像校准下安全地局部采纳更新，在控制正常场景风险的同时更充分地提升恶劣场景性能；细粒度选择可保留大部分收益。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a road-scene segmenter for rain and nighttime driving can introduce errors on normal daytime images. With few labeled normal images for calibration, a whole-image risk check may reject changes that would pass if adopted locally. Risk-Controlled Adoption of Label Changes (RALC) selects local changes from a fixed model update, using confidence differences and an image score to form nested candidates. On each normal calibration image, RALC measures how many more errors each candidate makes than the original model, assigning zero loss when errors stay the same or fall. At test time, each replacement can add at most one error, so the replacement count bounds the net increase without test labels. RALC combines this candidate-dependent bound with recorded calibration losses to select the largest set that passes the risk check. If the models and adoption rules are fixed before calibration and normal calibration and test images are exchangeable, the expected per-image loss stays within budget. On held-out BDD100K, we evaluated three weight-interpolated updates with 10 additional normal images per calibration draw. At a common fixed budget, adverse-scene mIoU was 1.89--3.19 percentage points higher with RALC than with whole-image adoption, while measured mean normal loss was also higher. After matching this loss post hoc at 1,000 pixels per image, RALC still led by 1.135--2.428 points. Selecting changes in $8\times8$-pixel cells recovered 93--98% of this advantage. With 250 calibration images at the original budget, both rules adopted all three updates in full.

</details>


### [220] [Audio-Visual Rearrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30171)
*Ruohao Guo,Jiatong Ye,Chenwei Dong,Jinxing Zhou,Dantong Niu,Guangyao Li,Zhenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出音视觉重排任务AVR，构建含三类查询八种子类型的数据集，并设计融合运动感知空间音频模块的音视觉大模型框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体难以同时理解语音请求、声学定位说话人并完成物体递送，缺乏支持该任务的基准与有效方法。

Method: 定义AVR任务：听语音请求、取合适物体并送达请求者或指定目的地；构建含显式目标、说话人相对空间指代、隐式意图三类查询的数据集；在音视觉大模型中集成运动感知空间音频模块编码空间线索并随移动更新，并通过多任务指令微调加入语音理解、空间音频感知与视觉定位的辅助监督。

Result: 在可见与不可见环境中建立基线性能。

Conclusion: AVR任务与基线为具身智能和人机交互的后续研究奠定基础，代码将公开。

Abstract: We introduce Audio-Visual Rearrangement (AVR), a novel task in which an agent hears a spoken request, retrieves a suitable object, and delivers it to the requesting user or an instruction-specified destination. AVR requires the agent to understand the spoken request and acoustically localize the speaker for delivery. To support this task, we construct a dataset covering three query types: explicit target specification, speaker-relative spatial references, and implicit user intent, spanning eight subtypes with diverse object selection and delivery requirements. Moreover, we propose a framework that integrates a motion-aware spatial audio module into an audio-visual large language model to encode spatial cues from the spoken request and update them as the agent moves. We further incorporate auxiliary supervision for speech understanding, spatial audio perception, and visual grounding through multitask instruction tuning to improve action prediction. Experiments establish baseline performance in both seen and unseen environments, laying the groundwork for future research on embodied intelligence and human-robot interaction. The code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [221] [Bridging Blind Spots With Words: Decentralized Multi-View Spatial Reasoning via Language-Constrained Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29112)
*Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化并基准测试了去中心化多视图空间推理问题，发现自然语言预算限制的惩罚远小于先前认为，但仍有提升空间，强化学习可缩小与集中式基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM作为协作伙伴时，空间推理常分散于部分环境可见性的多个智能体，需集成；但既有语言中介空间集成研究仅限于简化的双智能体场景。

Method: 形式化N个智能体各观察不同视角、交换预算受限自然语言消息并协作回答空间查询的去中心化多视图空间推理问题；引入新多视图空间基准隔离需非平凡多视图集成的查询；进行广泛实证研究；采用带信用分配的强化学习。

Result: 自然语言有限预算对去中心化空间推理的惩罚远小于先前双智能体文献所示；当前集中式性能与oracle best-of-K上限间存在显著差距，表明多智能体空间推理仍有大量提升空间；带信用分配的强化学习显著增强去中心化模型，有效缩小与集中式基线的差距。

Conclusion: 去中心化多视图空间推理中自然语言预算限制影响有限，但多智能体空间推理仍有巨大提升空间，带信用分配的强化学习是缩小与集中式基线差距的有效方法。

Abstract: As multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) get increasingly used as collaborative partners across embodied robotics, AR/VR, and multi-user systems, spatial reasoning is now often distributed across agents with partial environment visibility, necessitating spatial reasoning integration. Prior work in language-mediated spatial integration is however strictly limited to simplified two-agent scenarios. To address this gap, we first formalize the general problem of decentralized multi-view spatial reasoning, where N agents observe distinct viewpoints, exchange budget-constrained natural language messages, and collaborate to enable a listener to answer spatial queries. We then introduce a novel multi-view spatial benchmark to isolate queries that demand non-trivial multi-view integration. Through an extensive empirical study, we discover that the penalty of restricting decentralized spatial reasoning to natural language with limited budget is far smaller than suggested by prior two-agent literature. Nevertheless, we observe that substantial headroom still remains for multi-agent spatial reasoning in this setting, as evidenced by a significant gap between current centralized performance and an oracle best-of-K ceiling. Moreover, we show that reinforcement learning with credit assignment substantially bolsters the decentralized model, effectively closing the gap with the centralized baseline.

</details>


### [222] [KineState-SWAM: A Surgical World Action Model with Explicit Kinematics and Causal Interaction Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28871)
*Rulin Zhou,Yujie Ma,Rui Tang,Qiujie Song,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Liangjing Shao,An Wang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KineState-SWAM手术世界-动作模型，通过持久交互状态耦合动作预测与视频生成，分离运动学确定的器械运动与学习到的场景演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术世界模型未明确器械运动如何驱动局部组织响应，以及交互历史如何影响后续动作。

Method: 用流匹配策略在交互状态、机器人配置和任务指令条件下预测关节轨迹；Kinematic Geometry Conditioning将轨迹转为表面运动token和空间对齐的器械渲染；Causal Interaction Dynamics通过几何引导注意力和运动对齐特征传输循环更新状态；State-Geometry Adapter结合状态相关空间调制与局部几何残差引导视频生成；引入交互感知监督；构建dVRK-Chole5.2k数据集（5255个视频，17项任务）。

Result: 在匹配动作条件设置下，动作精度和视频保真度优于评估基线；消融实验支持保留和传输交互状态对长时程预测的作用。

Conclusion: 显式建模并传递交互状态可有效提升手术动作预测与视频生成的长时程性能。

Abstract: Existing surgical world models often leave implicit how instrument motion drives localized tissue responses and how interaction history informs subsequent actions. We introduce KineState-SWAM, a surgical world-action model coupling action prediction and video generation through a persistent interaction state, separating kinematically determined instrument motion from learned scene evolution. A flow-matching policy predicts joint trajectories conditioned on interaction state, robot configuration, and task instruction. Kinematic Geometry Conditioning converts these trajectories into surface-motion tokens and spatially aligned instrument renderings using calibrated URDF kinematics. Causal Interaction Dynamics recurrently updates the state through geometry-guided attention and motion-aligned feature transport, carrying interaction history into subsequent action predictions without intermediate video synthesis. The State-Geometry Adapter combines state-dependent spatial modulation with localized geometric residuals to guide video generation. Interaction-aware supervision emphasizes tissue responses near instrument working regions. We introduce dVRK-Chole5.2k, comprising 5,255 videos spanning 17 surgical tasks. Experiments demonstrate improved action accuracy and video fidelity over evaluated baselines within matched action-conditioning settings. Ablations support preserving and transporting interaction states for long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [223] [AERIN: Aerial Instance Navigation with a Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28723)
*Zhenzhen Jiao,Rui Tian,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AERIN视觉世界模型，通过动作条件预测引导无人机逼近目标并调整视角以获取高质量观测，实现高效目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机从高空搜索并导航至参考图像指定的目标实例时，高空观测覆盖广但目标像素少，导致实例验证与动作选择困难；同时密集预测视觉表示计算开销大。

Method: 构建紧凑的固定大小、目标条件潜在状态，分别表示目标细节和与可见性相关的场景上下文，用于高效动作评估；采用动作条件预测引导无人机在逼近目标的同时调整视角。

Result: 实验表明AERIN在目标逼近和观测质量上优于所评估的基线，且决策时间显著少于类似世界模型。

Conclusion: AERIN通过紧凑潜在状态与动作条件预测，有效解决了高空目标导航中精度与效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Searching from high altitude and navigating to a target instance specified by a reference image is a common application for UAVs. In such tasks, high-altitude observations provide broad ground coverage for efficient search, but targets occupy fewer pixels, making instance verification and action selection more difficult. We present AERIN, a visual world model that uses action-conditioned prediction to guide the UAV toward the target while adjusting its viewpoint to obtain higherquality observations for subsequent instance verification. Highaltitude navigation requires fine-grained target details and scene context relevant to visibility, but repeatedly predicting dense visual representations of this information is computationally expensive. AERIN addresses this challenge with a compact, fixedsize, target-conditioned latent state that separately represents both for efficient action evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that AERIN outperforms the evaluated baselines in target approach and observation quality, while requiring substantially less decision time than similar world models.

</details>


### [224] [Two Actions from One State: Fork Futures Teach JEPA World Models to Compare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28350)
*Yihao Wu,Runjia Zeng,Tongyan Wang,Renzhong Huang,Chongzhou Fang,Qifan Wang,Fan Heng,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JEPA世界模型仅用行为数据训练时，动作响应在策略未变化方向不可识别；通过在同一状态下执行替代动作的fork futures可估计动作响应，Fork-JEPA在视觉控制任务上提升规划成功率最多14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA世界模型用记录转移训练却用于从单一状态比较反事实动作，但行为数据每个状态仅有一个动作，局部线性近似下动作响应在策略未变化方向不可识别，因此需要补充同一状态下替代动作的测量结果。

Method: 提出Fork-JEPA：用fork futures从同一恢复状态执行两个动作，其结局差抵消状态项，在采样跨度上识别动作响应；训练时加入行为预测锚定记录后继，并增加lookback head保持前驱可恢复，规划过程不变。

Result: 在三个视觉控制任务上，仅行为训练的动作效应预测不优于常数猜测；Fork-JEPA使动作效应可读，规划成功率较基座LeWM最高提升14%，动作响应跨隐藏区域变化时跨区成功率近乎翻倍，1024对fork优于120万额外行为步。

Conclusion: 补充同一状态下的替代动作测量能识别JEPA世界模型的动作响应，Fork-JEPA以少量fork数据显著提升反事实动作比较与规划性能，且不改变规划流程。

Abstract: JEPA world models are trained to predict logged transitions but used to compare counterfactual actions from one state. The logged behavior data show one action per state, and under a local linear approximation we prove the action response is unidentified along any direction the policy never varies within a state. We therefore supplement behavior data with measured outcomes of alternative actions from the same state. Our fork futures execute two actions from one restored state, and their outcome difference cancels the state term, identifying the action response on the sampled span. The resulting model, Fork-JEPA, matches predicted to measured differences, anchors the logged successor with behavior prediction, and adds a lookback head that keeps the predecessor recoverable, all at training time, leaving planning unchanged. On three visual control tasks, behavior-only training leaves the predicted action effect no better than a constant guess. Fork-JEPA makes it readable and raises planning success by up to 14\% over LeWM, its base model. Where the action response changes across hidden zones, it nearly doubles cross-zone success, and 1,024 fork pairs beat 1.2M extra behavior steps. Our project is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [225] [Seeing Beyond Pixels: Radar Feature Generation from Images and Sparse Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28119)
*Hongyu Zhou,Yubo Peng,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VRTG方法，利用摄像头图像引导稀疏雷达点生成任务相关的雷达令牌，在仅用每帧4个雷达点时接近全雷达模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-雷达融合通常依赖完整雷达表示，计算开销大，而视觉信息能在多大程度上补偿有限雷达测量尚未充分探索。

Method: VRTG先用图像中的目标线索选择少量信息量大的雷达点，建立视觉目标与雷达回波的一对一标定关联，用匹配测量细化目标距离（未匹配目标保留视觉估计），最后将关联的目标特征和视觉上下文转换为与现有融合网络兼容的雷达令牌。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上，每帧仅用4个雷达点即达到90.76%的车辆检测F1和86.68%的自由空间mIoU，接近全雷达模型性能。

Conclusion: 视觉信息可有效补偿稀疏雷达测量，VRTG能以极低雷达开销实现接近完整雷达表示的融合性能。

Abstract: Camera-radar fusion benefits from complementary visual and geometric information but often relies on complete radar representations, leading to substantial computing overhead. However, the extent to which visual information can compensate for limited radar measurements remains insufficiently explored. Therefore, we propose Vision-informed Radar Token Generation (VRTG), which exploits cross-modal information to generate task-relevant radar features from camera images and sparse radar measurements. Specifically, VRTG first leverages image-derived object cues to select a limited number of informative radar points. It then establishes calibrated one-to-one associations between visual objects and radar returns, using matched measurements to refine object ranges while retaining visual estimates for unmatched objects. Finally, the associated object features and visual context are transformed into radar tokens compatible with existing camera-radar fusion networks. Experiments on RADIal demonstrate that VRTG achieves 90.76\% vehicle-detection F1 and 86.68\% free-space mIoU using only four radar points per frame, approaching the performance of the full-radar model.

</details>


### [226] [GlanceNav: Efficient Vision-and-Language Navigation with Streaming Memory and Counterfactual Rewind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30900)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Danh Vinh Le,Tuyen Pham Le,Quan Nguyen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlanceNav，一种基于混合MLLM的流式导航器，每帧只编码一次，用恒定内存实现长历史记忆，并配合反事实回放强化学习提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM视觉语言导航方法在每步重复编码历史帧且训练于孤立决策，导致计算冗余、可用历史短；同时仅用回合级标量奖励学习错误，效率低。

Method: GlanceNav采用混合MLLM：Gated DeltaNet层携带循环状态贯穿整个回合，注意力层保留近期观测窗口和由状态变化量选取的关键帧库，形成恒定大小内存；并引入Counterfactual Rewind强化学习，将漂移轨迹回退至最接近目标处，由专家纠正少量动作，按结果变化分配决策信用。

Result: 仅用R2R-CE和RxR-CE训练集及1000条指令做强化学习，4B模型在R2R-CE和RxR-CE val-unseen成功率分别达71.9%和73.6%；R2R-CE上比无外部数据训练的最强单RGB方法高9.1点，每步耗时104毫秒。

Conclusion: 导航器可在每步恒定成本下仅编码每帧一次，仍有效记忆关键信息并从错误中高效学习，验证了流式记忆与反事实回放结合的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become strong policies for vision-and-language navigation (VLN). However, most of them encode a sample of past frames again at every decision and are trained on isolated decisions, so computation is redundant and the usable history is short. They also learn from their own mistakes through outcome rewards that give one scalar to a whole episode. We ask whether a navigator can encode each frame only once, at a constant cost per step, and still remember what matters and learn efficiently from its mistakes. We present GlanceNav, a streaming navigator built on a hybrid MLLM. Its Gated DeltaNet layers carry a recurrent state through the whole episode, while its attention layers keep a short window of recent observations and a small bank of keyframes, selected by how much each frame changes the recurrent state. This constant-size memory keeps the cost per step fixed and allows training on whole episodes with the same memory as at inference. Because the entire memory can also be copied in constant time, we further propose Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe), a reinforcement learning stage that rewinds a drifting rollout to its closest approach to the goal, lets an expert correct a few actions, and credits decisions by the resulting change in outcome. Trained only on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE training splits, with 1,000 instructions for reinforcement learning, a 4B GlanceNav reaches success rates of 71.9% and 73.6% on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen. On R2R-CE, it outperforms the strongest previous single-RGB method trained without external data by 9.1 points, while running at 104 ms per step.

</details>


### [227] [World Models as Native Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30898)
*Qinyue Li,Weijie Qiao,Qingyi Tao,Zerui Gong,Zhe Yang,Quanzhou Li,Taolin Zhang,Jinglin He,Zhonghua Wu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 原生潜在动作模型（Native LAM）将预训练视频扩散Transformer同时用作逆向和正向动力学模型，仅通过学习到的动力学寄存器作为因果桥梁，并在仅运动不同的反事实时间对上训练，从而将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑的潜在动作；基于此提出PAWlicy视觉-语言-动作策略预训练范式，在多个基准和真实机器人上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构视频源（如第一人称人类视频和机器人录像）蕴含丰富动力学，但缺乏动作标注或共享动作空间；现有潜在动作模型从头训练IDM和FDM，需在像素重建与潜在容量/精度间权衡，而预训练视频世界模型虽能生成高保真动力学，却以密集、跨层纠缠的方式表示。

Method: Native LAM以单个预训练视频扩散Transformer同时充当IDM和FDM，仅用可学习的动力学寄存器作为两者间唯一的因果桥梁，并在仅运动不同的反事实时间对上训练，使寄存器将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑的潜在动作；进一步提出PAWlicy预训练范式，让Native LAM作为大规模视频上的动力学教师，且从不生成视频。

Result: Native潜在动作在LARY基准的动作分类和控制回归上均超越现有LAM，并能在新机器人、新场景及从人手到机器人之间重放相同运动，且训练中未使用迁移目标；3.8B模型PAWlicy在域随机化演示训练的RoboTwin 2.0上达到93.6%平均成功率，为VLA中最高；在RoboDojo上各评估维度均具竞争力，其Precision与排行榜领先条目相当；在真实机器人上两个任务均优于pi_0.5基线。

Conclusion: 将预训练视频世界模型视为LAM并仅做最小改动即可提取其动力学，所得紧凑潜在动作具备强泛化与跨具身迁移能力；PAWlicy为VLA策略提供了一种仅依赖公开数据和公开世界模型的高效预训练范式，在仿真与真实机器人上均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large and diverse video sources, such as egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot recordings, carry rich dynamics but come either without actions or without a shared action space. Latent action models (LAMs) can recover these dynamics, but they train their inverse- and forward-dynamics models (IDM, FDM) from scratch, trading off pixel reconstruction against latent capacity and accuracy. Pretrained video world models natively generate high-fidelity dynamics, yet represent them densely, entangled across all layers. Viewing the world model as a LAM, we show that these dynamics can be extracted with minimal changes to the model. Native LAM operates one pretrained video diffusion transformer as both IDM and FDM, with learnable dynamics registers as the only causal bridge between the two, and trains on counterfactual temporal pairs that differ in motion alone, so that the registers distill a video's dynamics into a compact native latent action. We further propose a pretraining paradigm for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, Pretraining Actions from World models (PAWlicy), in which Native LAM serves as a dynamics teacher on large-scale video; unlike world action models, it never generates video. Native latent actions outperform existing LAMs on the LARY benchmark in both action classification and control regression, and replay the same motion on new robots, in new scenes, and from human hands to robots, although no transfer objective is used in training. With a 3.8B model, PAWlicy reaches a 93.6% average success rate on RoboTwin 2.0 trained with domain-randomized demonstrations, the highest among VLAs. On RoboDojo, it is competitive on every dimension we evaluate, and its Precision, evaluated by us, is on par with the leading leaderboard entries. On real robots, it improves over the pi_0.5 baseline on both tasks. Both stages use only public data and a publicly released world model.

</details>


### [228] [Can Multimodal Fusion MakeSense for Embodied Vision in Degraded Sensing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30836)
*Cheng-Ru Chou,Shih-Yu Lai,Pei-Lin Chiang,Shou-Po Tsai,Jing-Zhe Huang,Fukuharu Tanaka,Hirozumi Yamaguchi,Bing-Yu Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Hsun-Ping Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MakeSense 是一种多模态融合框架，通过私有流保留各传感器证据并用共享瓶颈令牌融合，训练时配对完整与子集视图以抑制负融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉系统需互补传感器适应条件变化，但即使接受任意传感器子集的模型也会出现负融合：增加传感器反而增大预测误差。

Method: 保留传感器私有流，通过共享瓶颈令牌组合信息；训练时对同一退化观测的完整视图和子集视图配对，对传感器更多却误差更高的情况施加惩罚；在几何允许时，距离读出仅用测距测量，其余预测使用所有可用输入。

Result: 五个数据集上的配对测试揭示了有益和有害的传感器添加；MakeSense 在 MM-Fi 上减少负融合；借助轻量适配器和逐数据集训练，同一设计在 MMAUD 无人机定位、V2X-Real 协同 3D 检测、RELI11D 少样本姿态迁移和 Humanoid-Everyday 命令预测上均有提升，其中基于冻结核心输出的世界模型在更长测试时域上比线性基线更准确地预测未来融合状态。

Conclusion: MakeSense 支持人类活动、空中系统、机器人和自动驾驶等具身感知场景。

Abstract: Embodied vision systems rely on complementary sensors to perceive and act as conditions change. Even models that accept arbitrary sensor subsets can exhibit negative fusion: adding a sensor increases prediction error. We propose MakeSense, a multimodal fusion framework that preserves sensor-specific evidence in private streams and combines information through shared bottleneck tokens. Training pairs full and subset views of the same degraded observations, penalizing higher error with more sensors. Where geometry permits, the distance readout uses range measurements alone, while the rest of the prediction draws on all available inputs. Paired tests across five datasets reveal helpful and harmful sensor additions. MakeSense reduces negative fusion on MM-Fi. With lightweight adapters and per-dataset training, the same design improves UAV localization on MMAUD, cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Real, few-shot pose transfer to RELI11D, and command prediction on Humanoid-Everyday, where a world model trained on the frozen core's outputs forecasts future fused states more accurately than a linear baseline at longer tested horizons. MakeSense supports embodied sensing across human activity, aerial systems, robotics, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [229] [EgoDepth-Bench: Benchmarking Metric Depth for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30734)
*Yuan Gao,Dongchen Wei,Xinyang Lin,Shuhua Cao,Xueliang Guo,Zhuohan Li,Yihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoDepth-Bench基准，包含94万余个同步样本，用于第一人称操作场景下深度估计的监督与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作场景缺乏兼具度量深度与大范围环境泛化的深度评测基准，难以严格评估深度模型在未见过环境下的准确性与覆盖度。

Method: 采集37个物理环境的941,233个同步样本，每个样本含六个标定RGB视图与原生鱼眼视图的度量深度（由五个主动红外立体模块重建）；以标定球杆验证毫米级几何精度，并提供环境留出划分以及单目、双目、多视图模型评测。

Result: 标定球在已知球间距上的中位误差为1.27 mm；MapAnything在EgoDepth-Bench上微调后，在未见环境上度量AbsRel由0.708降至0.091，尺度对齐AbsRel由0.327降至0.072。

Conclusion: EgoDepth-Bench能提供有效监督，并支持对第一人称操作中的度量与相对深度估计进行严格评测。

Abstract: We introduce EgoDepth-Bench, comprising 941,233 synchronized samples collected across 37 distinct physical environments. Each sample combines six calibrated RGB views with metric depth in the native fisheye view, reconstructed from five active-infrared stereo modules. A calibrated ball-bar confirms millimeter-level geometric accuracy, with a median error of 1.27 mm on a known sphere spacing. Environment-held-out splits enable evaluation of generalization to unseen environments. Across monocular, stereo, and multi-view models, the benchmark measures both depth accuracy and prediction coverage, including on hands and objects. Fine-tuning MapAnything on EgoDepth-Bench reduces metric AbsRel from 0.708 to 0.091 and scale-aligned AbsRel from 0.327 to 0.072 on unseen environments. These results demonstrate that EgoDepth-Bench provides effective supervision and enables rigorous evaluation of metric and relative depth estimation in egocentric manipulation. The project page provides the evaluation code and interactive RGB-depth sequences.

</details>


### [230] [GeLiFiSH: Recursive LiDAR Keypoint Estimation with Ground and Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30402)
*Nikolaos Stathoulopoulos,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeLiFiSH是一种无需学习的序贯假设检验滤波器，在扫描频率下递归估计持久关键点，提升LiDAR配准与回环检测中关键点的可重复性与跨会话配准召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR配准与回环检测依赖稀疏关键点，但关键点需跨会话、视角和传感器保持可重复；基于累积子图检测器有延迟且不融合重复观测，基于高度的检测器依赖估计地面导致不一致传播，动态物体产生鬼影被误认为静态结构。

Method: 提出GeLiFiSH，一种无学习滤波器：在滚动体素地图上运行高度-结构检测器，检测初始化轨迹（位置、协方差、存在概率），通过序贯概率比检验接受或拒绝；地面分割提供传感器无关的高度参考，运动物体分割在插入地图前移除动态点。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI、HeLiMOS和HeLiPR（涵盖五种LiDAR和多次会话）上，GeLiFiSH在比较的检测器中获得最高平均关键点可重复性，仅用关键点实现第二高跨会话配准召回率（仅次于提取四倍关键点的ISS）；用一套参数且无传感器高度先验，地面和运动物体分割模块平均优于基线。

Conclusion: GeLiFiSH通过序贯假设检验递归估计持久关键点，无需学习且参数统一，在多种LiDAR和会话下有效提升关键点可重复性与配准召回率，并改善地面与动态物体分割。

Abstract: LiDAR registration and loop closure often rely on sparse keypoints, which must remain repeatable across sessions, viewpoints, and sensors. To handle differing scan patterns, detectors typically operate on accumulated submaps, which introduces latency and processes each submap independently, so repeated observations are never fused. Height-based detectors also reference structure to an estimated ground, so its inconsistency across sessions propagates into the keypoints. Moreover, dynamic objects leave ghost trails that can be mistaken for static structure. We present GeLiFiSH (Geometric LiDAR Filtering with Sequential Hypothesis testing), a learning-free filter that recursively estimates persistent keypoints at scan rate. A height-structure detector runs on a rolling voxel map, and detections initialize tracks with a position, covariance, and existence probability, accepted or rejected by a sequential probability ratio test. Ground segmentation provides a sensor-agnostic height reference, and moving object segmentation removes dynamic points before map insertion. On SemanticKITTI, HeLiMOS, and HeLiPR, spanning five LiDARs and multiple sessions, GeLiFiSH attains the highest average keypoint repeatability among the compared detectors and the second-highest cross-session registration recall from keypoints alone, behind ISS which extracts four times as many keypoints. With one parameter set and no sensor-height prior, both ground and moving object segmentation modules outperform their baselines on average.

</details>


### [231] [RMR-VLA: Recurrent Memory Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30145)
*Yiheng Du,Jingqi Tian,Xu Jiang,Jiexuan Zhang,Shuyu Wang,Zecheng Tang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RMR在VLA模型中引入显式循环记忆推理步骤，无需外部推理模型，在RoboMME和LIBERO-Mem上取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、依赖历史的机器人操作需要依据已消失的视觉线索行动，关键挑战是在场景变化中保持过去证据对下一步动作的意义。

Method: 提出循环记忆推理（RMR），在VLA内部将记忆与动作连接：每次更新先由视觉证据推断事件，再修正任务状态并在当前场景中定位子目标以指导动作生成；状态与子目标跨更新传递；联合训练使同一VLA既能推理又能行动，并利用记忆决策的文本token精确似然，通过GRPO从轨迹奖励中优化而不使动作生成随机化。

Result: RMR-VLA在RoboMME上总体成功率66.46%（Reference类别79.83%），在LIBERO-Mem上达90.08%，均为对比方法中最佳；相比无循环的相同模型，RoboMME成功率翻倍。

Conclusion: 显式循环记忆推理能有效连接记忆与动作，提升长时程历史依赖的机器人操作性能，且无需外部推理模型。

Abstract: Long-horizon, history-dependent robotic manipulation often requires acting on visual cues that have disappeared, such as an object indicated earlier or a target hidden under a moving cup. The key challenge is to maintain what past evidence means for the next action as the scene changes. We introduce Recurrent Memory Reasoning (RMR), an explicit reasoning step that connects memory to action inside a vision-language-action model (VLA), with no external reasoning model. At each update, the VLA first infers an event from visual evidence, then revises the task state and grounds a subgoal in the current scene to guide action generation. Carrying the state and subgoal across updates lets remembered identities and task progress determine the current target, doubling RoboMME success over an otherwise identical model without recurrence. Joint training lets one VLA both reason and act; because its memory decisions are text tokens with exact likelihoods, GRPO refines them from rollout rewards without making action generation stochastic. Among the compared methods, RMR-VLA achieves the best results on RoboMME (66.46% overall, 79.83% on the Reference category) and LIBERO-Mem (90.08%).

</details>


### [232] [RadarOSV: Unified 3D Scene and Motion Perception from mmWave Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30078)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadarOSV是一个仅使用雷达的框架，从原始雷达数据统一预测占据、动态物体语义和地相对速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达占据方法主要建模几何，语义与运动联合建模不足；级联成像雷达空间细节好但无模糊速度范围有限，单芯片雷达速度范围宽但空间分辨率粗。

Method: 提出Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry骨干，通过固定与可变形采样将极坐标成像雷达特征映射到笛卡尔体素并引导跨雷达融合；单芯片检测提供类别证据，原始多普勒谱提供运动特征与径向速度候选，速度读出利用融合特征选择候选以解混叠并结合学习运动；使用雷达估计速度进行自运动补偿，无需外部里程计。

Result: 在K-Radar上单帧PCG在12.8米内达到40.7% mIoU（RadarOcc报告34.3%）；在BRIDGE-Liv上相比预测相同输出并使用外部自速度的级联雷达基线，四类动态物体标注区域内宏平均准确率从63.1%提升至75.5%，物体径向速度MAE从1.03降至0.51 m/s，同时保持几何占据mIoU。

Conclusion: RadarOSV验证了仅用雷达统一体素联合预测占据、动态语义与地相对速度的可行性，并利用双雷达互补测量提升性能且无需外部传感器。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar offers weather-resilient sensing for 3D occupancy prediction. Existing radar occupancy methods mainly model geometry, with limited joint modeling of semantics and motion. Cascaded imaging radar provides fine spatial detail but has a limited unambiguous velocity range, whereas single-chip radar offers a wider range but coarser spatial resolution. We propose RadarOSV, to our knowledge the first radar-only framework that jointly predicts occupancy, dynamic object semantics, and ground-relative velocity from raw radar data in unified voxels, by exploiting complementary measurements from both radars. Its Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry (PCG) backbone maps polar imaging-radar features into Cartesian voxels through fixed and deformable sampling, guiding cross-radar fusion for geometry and attribute prediction. Single-chip detection outputs provide class evidence, while raw Doppler spectra supply motion features and radial-velocity candidates. The velocity readout selects from these candidates using fused features to resolve aliasing, then combines the selected radial estimate with learned motion. Ego-motion compensation uses radar-estimated velocity, so inference requires no external odometry or other sensors. On K-Radar, single-frame PCG achieves 40.7% mIoU within 12.8 m, compared with RadarOcc's reported 34.3%. On the BRIDGE-Liv dataset, compared with a cascaded-radar baseline predicting the same outputs with external ego velocity, RadarOSV improves macro-averaged accuracy across four dynamic object classes within annotated object regions from 63.1% to 75.5% and reduces object radial-velocity MAE against manual Doppler annotations from 1.03 to 0.51 m/s, while maintaining geometric occupancy mIoU.

</details>


### [233] [MOVER: Planning Beyond Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30022)
*Xiangyu Li,Yu Jiang,Tianyi Wang,Liang He,Qixing Huang,Zhaomiao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOVER提出一种互补事件路由机制，通过训练时教师识别依赖历史但残差证据弱的事件（如等待行人、静止车辆），学生从无扰动历史预测优先级，在不增加推理成本下提升驾驶场景规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差世界模型通过时间变化预测驾驶场景，导致时间上恒定的特征组件从残差通路中消失；作者探究弱时间变化证据（如等待行人或静止车辆）能否更有效地用于规划。

Method: 引入MOVER互补事件路由机制：训练时教师通过移除过去帧测量空间事件查询的敏感性，并与事件池化时间残差对比，优先选择依赖历史但残差证据弱的事件；学生从无扰动历史预测优先级，教师不增加推理成本；在固定总路由质量下，一个分布缩放低秩未来鸟瞰图更新并偏置轨迹条件事件读取。

Result: 在nuScenes上，MOVER将ResWorld的UniAD风格平均L2从0.65m降至0.55m，碰撞率从0.23%降至0.12%（无直接规划器自车状态时；有则为0.50m和0.09%），每帧增加4.1ms；匹配对照表明互补教师优于单一信号，且分配主要在训练时起作用；适配到BridgeDrive-LEAD后，在220条路线的Bench2Drive闭环基准上三个种子达到96.52±0.65驾驶分数，每个配对种子均超过匹配对照，差距在种子变异范围内。

Conclusion: MOVER通过互补事件路由有效利用弱时间变化证据，在不增加推理成本的情况下显著提升驾驶场景规划的准确性和安全性，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Residual world models forecast a driving scene through its temporal change, so feature components that stay constant over time vanish from the residual pathway. We ask whether evidence with weak temporal change, such as a waiting pedestrian or a stopped vehicle, can be used more effectively for planning, and introduce MOVER, a complementary event-routing mechanism. A training-time teacher measures how each spatially grounded event query responds when a past frame is removed from shared history and contrasts that sensitivity with the event's pooled temporal residual, prioritizing events that depend on history but receive weak residual evidence. A student predicts the resulting priorities from unperturbed history, so the teacher adds no inference cost. Under fixed total routing mass, one distribution scales a low-rank future bird's-eye-view update and biases trajectory-conditioned event reading. On nuScenes, MOVER reduces ResWorld's UniAD-style average L2 from 0.65 to 0.55 m and its collision rate from 0.23% to 0.12% without direct planner ego status (0.50 m and 0.09% with it), adding 4.1 ms per frame. Matched controls show that the complementary teacher outperforms either signal alone and that the allocation acts mainly during training. Adapted to BridgeDrive-LEAD, MOVER reaches a 96.52 +/- 0.65 driving score over three seeds on the 220-route Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, exceeding matched controls in each paired seed by a margin within seed variation.

</details>


### [234] [Dynamics Forcing: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Causal Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29839)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dynamics Forcing训练方案，通过扰动历史（随机加噪、自重采样、联合去噪）并将历史引导和首帧锚定引导从推理移入训练，解决因果视频世界模型误差累积和复制上一帧的捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果视频世界模型逐块生成并依赖自身历史输出，早期小误差会反馈累积导致场景崩溃；教师强制使用干净真值历史，模型从未见过实际推理时的历史，且学会复制上一帧而非预测变化。

Method: 仅以真实视频为监督，通过三种方式扰动历史：随机加噪、模型自重采样、联合去噪（预测下一块时最近块仍部分带噪）；同时将历史引导和首帧锚定引导从推理阶段移入训练，迫使模型利用历史和参考帧；无需教师模型、蒸馏或推理额外模型。

Result: 在单GPU上对5B视频模型用LoRA在77小时操作视频上训练，更好匹配未见真实延续并减少误差累积。

Conclusion: Dynamics Forcing有效缓解因果视频世界模型的误差累积和捷径学习，提升对真实延续的匹配能力，且训练和推理开销低。

Abstract: Causal video world models generate block by block from their own previous outputs. Small errors in early blocks are fed back as history and grow until the scene and the robot fall apart. Teacher forcing trains on clean ground-truth history, so the model never sees the history it will actually condition on, and it also finds a shortcut: copying the previous frame instead of predicting how the scene changes. We present Dynamics Forcing, a training scheme that keeps real video as the only supervision and reshapes how history is presented. History is perturbed in three ways: random noising, self-resampling by the model itself, and joint denoising, in which the most recent blocks are still partly noisy when the next block is predicted. On top of this we move history guidance and first-frame anchor guidance from inference into training, so that the model is pushed to use its history and its reference frame rather than ignore them. Dynamics Forcing needs no teacher model, no distillation, and no additional model at inference. Applied to a 5B video model with LoRA on 77 hours of manipulation video on a single GPU, it better matches unseen real continuations and reduces error accumulation.

</details>


### [235] [Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head for Tiny Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29813)
*Aditya Makineni,Amitesh Sahoo,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层推理动作头HR-AH，用快慢两个潜在状态在紧凑头内迭代推理，在不增大骨干的前提下提升轻量VLA模型的决策能力并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量VLA模型骨干容量小、推理时缺乏深思能力，而思维链解码、双系统架构和更大骨干会重新引入延迟与内存开销；现有循环动作头只精炼单一未分化状态，难以同时编码缓慢变化的任务上下文和快速变化的运动指令。

Method: HR-AH维护两个不同频率更新的潜在状态：快速更新的低层状态以动作token和本体感知为依据，缓慢更新的高层状态以视觉理解和由指令引导的低层状态为依据，二者通过嵌套推理循环交换信息；全部迭代在单次骨干前向后的紧凑头内完成。

Result: 在相同骨干下，22.5M参数的头比RD-VLA和VLA-Adapter小3-4.3倍，在LIBERO上达到97.0%成功率，在CALVIN ABC->D上平均链长4.40，且为对比方法中延迟最低；分层结构优于参数匹配的单状态头，将不同模态路由到不同层级可进一步提升精度并降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 在紧凑动作头内引入快慢分层潜在推理，可在不增加骨干规模的情况下为轻量VLA模型恢复深思能力，同时保持低延迟和低内存开销。

Abstract: Real-time onboard robot control calls for a lightweight vision-language-action (VLA) model, yet shrinking the backbone leaves less capacity for deliberation at inference time. Chain-of-thought decoding, dual-system architectures, and larger backbones can restore deliberation but reintroduce the latency and memory costs that motivated going small. Recurrent action heads offer a lighter alternative by iterating in latent space before committing to an action, but existing designs refine a single undifferentiated state that must simultaneously encode slowly evolving task context and rapidly changing motor commands. We propose the Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head (HR-AH), which maintains two latent states updated at different frequencies: a low-level state that updates fast, grounded in action tokens and proprioception, and a high-level state that updates slowly, grounded in visual understanding and the instruction-informed low-level state. The two states exchange information through nested reasoning cycles. Because all iteration occurs in the compact head after a single backbone pass, HR-AH adds hierarchical reasoning without increasing the backbone size. With a 22.5M-parameter head, 3-4.3x smaller than those of RD-VLA and VLA-Adapter on the same backbone, HR-AH reaches 97.0% success rate on LIBERO and a 4.40 average chain length on CALVIN ABC->D, at the lowest latency among compared methods. We show that this hierarchy improves performance over a parameter-matched single-state head, and that routing distinct modalities to each level yields further gains in both accuracy and inference cost.

</details>


### [236] [A colour-marker tracker for pose estimation of slender surgical instruments: vision-language-model-guided adaptive colour calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29421)
*Jaesung Hong,Dong Won Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自适应颜色校准方法，解决彩色条纹标记在图像中颜色漂移导致的姿态估计难题，并在合成图像、实验室视频与手术机器人视频上验证其优于两种基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长手术器械需要小而窄的标记用于基于相机的位姿估计，彩色条纹标记满足该尺寸要求，但其图像颜色会因光照、打印和相机设置而偏离设计值；由于颜色只能在标记被找到之后才能校正，依赖设计颜色的方法面临循环问题。

Method: 提出VLM引导的自适应颜色校准，用微调后的VLM在不依赖设计颜色的情况下寻找标记并从其像素构建颜色轮廓；与两种规则方法对比：初始颜色更新（跟踪开始后一次性重测颜色）和颜色适应（跟踪过程中持续重测颜色）。

Result: 召回率/F1（%）：在672对合成图像上，初始颜色更新、颜色适应和VLM引导分别为92.0/95.7、87.8/93.3和98.2/99.1；在8段退化实验室视频上分别为51.3/67.2、48.8/65.0和83.8/87.0；在颜色强烈偏移的da Vinci手术机器人视频上分别为38.9/55.2、19.7/29.7和100/100，其中规则方法从未找到紫/青色标记，而VLM引导找到全部234个标记且无误报；仿真中曝光增益0.5-2.0时，两条纹间色相边界移动小于0.1像素，而固定亮度阈值法移动2.9-4.0像素。

Conclusion: 退化轻微时，无需训练和GPU的规则方法已足够；颜色变化剧烈时，收益来自不依赖设计颜色的VLM引导候选搜索。将彩色条纹标记与自适应颜色校准结合，可实现使用小标记的细长手术器械跟踪。

Abstract: Slender surgical instruments need small, narrow markers for camera-based pose estimation. A colour-stripe marker fits this requirement, but its colours in the image drift from the design values with lighting, printing and camera settings. Because the colours can only be corrected after the marker has been found, methods that rely on the design colours face a circular problem. We propose vision-language-model (VLM)-based adaptive colour calibration and compare it with two rule-based methods, which re-measure the colours from detections made with the design colours: Initial colour update does so once after tracking starts, and Colour adaptation continuously during tracking. VLM guidance uses a fine-tuned VLM to find the markers without relying on the design colours and builds the colour profile from their pixels. Recall/F1 (%) for Initial colour update, Colour adaptation and VLM guidance were 92.0/95.7, 87.8/93.3 and 98.2/99.1 on 672 synthetic image pairs; 51.3/67.2, 48.8/65.0 and 83.8/87.0 on eight degraded laboratory videos; and 38.9/55.2, 19.7/29.7 and 100/100 on a surgical-robot (da Vinci) video with strongly shifted colours. In that video the rule-based methods never found the purple/cyan marker, whereas VLM guidance found all 234 markers with no false positives. In a simulation, the hue boundary between the two stripes moved by less than 0.1 pixel for exposure gains of 0.5-2.0, compared with 2.9-4.0 pixels for a boundary found with a fixed brightness threshold. When the degradation is mild, the rule-based methods, which need no training and no GPU, are sufficient. When the colours change greatly, the gain comes from a VLM-guided candidate search that does not rely on the design colours. Combining colour-stripe markers with adaptive colour calibration enables tracking of slender surgical instruments with small markers.

</details>


### [237] [DriveOddsBench: Are Driving Video World Models Aligned with Real-World Odds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29275)
*Jiaxi Liu,Keshu Wu,Bin Ran,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveOddsBench预注册基准，测试视频世界模型采样分布是否与真实世界概率对齐；在200个Argoverse 2历史、11个模型、35,200个生成未来上，所有模型均未对齐且低估不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的采样未来被用于规划与仿真等替代真实场景的任务，这隐含假设采样频率与真实概率一致，但现有评估只衡量单样本逼真度，未检验分布对齐性；直接用真实驾驶数据检验存在三大难点：真实未来唯一导致真实概率未知、从视频读取物理量存在误差、分析选择易产生选择性报告。

Method: 构建预注册基准DriveOddsBench：利用真实结果在采样中的秩在每段历史下应服从均匀分布，从而无需概率标签即可用合并秩检验对齐性；使用单一经验证仪器从生成未来与经世界模型自编码器处理的真实未来中读取相同物理量（如前车间距与自车运动），保证读取一致；所有确证性分析在任何测试片段测量前冻结。在Argoverse 2的200个留出历史、5类场景族上测试11个通用与驾驶世界模型，共35,200个生成未来。

Result: 没有模型实现对齐，且所有模型低估自身不确定性：真实结果落在全部16个样本范围之外的比例为28%–61%，远高于真实分布下预期的11.8%；5个模型显著偏斜且方向相反；自车运动误差最大；9个模型在未对齐情况下优于气候学基线，且在这些模型中模型类型或规模均不能预测谁最接近对齐。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型的采样未来不能按真实世界概率出现，其不确定性被系统性低估，需以分布对齐指标而非仅样本逼真度进行评估，且模型类型与规模无法指示对齐优劣。

Abstract: Video world models predict how a scene will evolve, and their sampled futures are increasingly used in place of the real one, from planning to simulation. These uses assume that the samples are aligned with real-world odds, with each future appearing among them as often as it would follow the same history in reality, yet current evaluations only score how real each sample looks. Testing this assumption on real driving data is hard for three reasons. Each recorded history has only one real future, so the true probabilities are unknown. Physical quantities must be read from video, where reading errors can look like model errors. Finally, the many possible analyses invite selective reporting. We introduce DriveOddsBench, a pre-registered benchmark that addresses all three. First, if the samples follow the real distribution, the rank of the real outcome among them is uniform in every history, so pooled ranks test them without probability labels. Second, one validated instrument reads the same quantities, such as the lead-vehicle gap and the ego motion, from the generated futures and from the real future, which first passes through the world model's autoencoder so that both are read alike. Third, all confirmatory analyses were frozen before any test clip was measured. We test eleven general and driving world models on 200 held-out Argoverse 2 histories in five scenario families, with 35,200 generated futures. None is aligned, and every model underestimates its uncertainty. The real outcome falls outside the range of all 16 samples in 28-61% of cases instead of the 11.8% expected under the real distribution. Five models are significantly skewed, in opposite directions, and the largest errors are in the ego motion. Nine models beat climatology without being aligned, and among these models neither type nor scale predicts which comes closest.

</details>


### [238] [SpatialDuet: Bayesian task routing between a metric expert and a geometry expert for spatial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29168)
*Duy Tran Thanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialDuet 将度量专家与几何专家通过贝叶斯任务路由器结合，无需训练模型权重，在多个空间基准上超越现有小视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小空间视觉语言模型从训练中获得不同能力：基于大规模空间问题集训练的模型擅长估计物体和房间尺寸，而带3D几何编码器的模型更擅长相对方向和绝对距离。与其训练单一模型处理所有空间任务，不如构建系统让每个冻结模型专注于其最擅长的任务。

Method: 提出 SpatialDuet，在贝叶斯任务路由器下配对度量专家和几何专家。度量专家是数据驱动的空间VLM，采用三个免训练模块：训练对齐采样、度量感知最小贝叶斯风险解码和按类型度量校准。几何专家是带3D几何编码器的VLM。路由器根据标注的开发问题估计每个专家在每种问题类型上表现更好的概率，并以该概率为权重在log空间（数值答案）或选项分布（多选答案）上池化专家答案。

Result: 在涵盖视频和单图像的四项空间基准上广泛实验验证了 SpatialDuet；在VSI-Bench上比最佳已发表小VLM高3.17%，在CV-Bench上（排除一个在CV-Bench风格数据上训练的模型）高1.44%，且未训练任何模型权重。

Conclusion: SpatialDuet 通过路由和池化多个冻结专家，有效利用各自优势，在无需训练的情况下提升了小VLM的空间推理性能。

Abstract: Embodied assistants and wearable devices need 3D spatial reasoning, and their limited hardware restricts them to small vision-language models (VLMs). Small spatial VLMs acquire different abilities from their training: a model trained on large spatial question sets estimates object and room sizes well, whereas a model with a 3D geometry encoder is better at relative directions and absolute distances. Instead of training a single model for all spatial tasks, as published methods do, we build one system that uses each frozen model for the tasks it handles best. We propose SpatialDuet, which pairs a metric expert with a geometry expert under a Bayesian task router. The metric expert is a data-driven spatial VLM run with three training-free modules: training-aligned sampling, metric-aware minimum Bayes risk decoding and per-type metric calibration. The geometry expert is a VLM with a 3D geometry encoder. For each question type, the router estimates from labelled development questions how likely each expert is to be better, and pools their answers with that probability as weight, in log space for numeric answers and over the option distributions for multiple-choice answers. Extensive experiments on four spatial benchmarks covering video and single images validate SpatialDuet; it outperforms the best published small VLMs by 3.17% on VSI-Bench and, excluding a model trained on CV-Bench-style data, by 1.44% on CV-Bench, without training any model weight.

</details>


### [239] [Div3DAfford: Diverse 3D affordance grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28917)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多样3D可供性定位新任务，并设计Div3DAffordNet模型与自动数据合成流程，实现多掩码SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性定位方法仅预测单一掩码，忽略了人-物交互的固有多样性。

Method: 定义多样3D可供性定位任务；提出基于Transformer的集合预测模型Div3DAffordNet生成多样区域；利用2D生成模型与3D重建的自动流程合成数据并经轻量人工验证（标注时间减少72倍），构建Div3DAfford基准。

Result: 在多掩码设置下达到SOTA，GED-SIM从0.745降至0.492；在标准基准单掩码设置下保持19.0 mIoU的竞争力。

Conclusion: Div3DAffordNet能有效捕捉多掩码可供性区域，验证了多样3D可供性定位任务与数据构建方法的有效性。

Abstract: 3D affordance grounding aims to localize 3D regions specified by an interaction query. Existing methods predict a single affordance mask, overlooking the inherent diversity of human-object interactions. We introduce diverse 3D affordance grounding, a new task that requires predicting multiple plausible regions for a given object and affordance query. We then propose Div3DAffordNet, a transformer-based set prediction model capable of generating diverse affordance regions. To address the scarcity of diverse 3D affordance data, we develop an automated pipeline leveraging 2D generative models and 3D reconstruction techniques to synthesize 3D affordances, followed by lightweight human verification (reducing the annotation time by 72X), yielding the Div3DAfford benchmark. Extensive experiments show Div3DAffordNet effectively captures multi-mask affordance regions. Our method achieves the SOTA affordance grounding performance in the multi-mask setting, reducing GED-SIM from 0.745 to 0.492, while maintaining competitive single-mask performance on the standard benchmark of 19.0 mIoU.

</details>


### [240] [Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28839)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-LL通过预测以当前场景为参考的可操作近未来表征，并将该表征融入动作专家，提升视觉-语言-动作模型的精细操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型语义强但缺乏对物体位置、空间关系及场景演化的精确估计；几何感知方法仅显式化当前场景，预测方法未将未来信息锚定在控制相关空间状态或直接用于指导动作生成。

Method: 先用冻结的空间教师将几何先验蒸馏到策略连接的空间token；训练当前条件瓶颈学习相对当前场景的紧凑未来潜在表示；该潜在表示由当前策略上下文和初始动作专家特征预测，不访问未来观测或空间教师；最后用零初始化残差融合将其并入动作专家以细化动作生成。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN及三个真实机器人操作任务上，仿真与真实机器人设置均优于基线。

Conclusion: 以空间锚定的当前场景为参考预测近未来可操作表征并融合到动作生成，可有效提升VLA模型的精细操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from strong semantic representations that support object recognition and instruction understanding. However, precise manipulation also depends on resolving object locations and workspace relations, and on selecting actions with an estimate of how the scene will evolve. Existing geometry-aware methods make the current scene more explicit, while predictive methods model future observations or representations. Yet future information is often neither grounded in a control-relevant spatial state nor directly used to guide action generation. We introduce Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead (SG-LL), which uses the grounded present as the reference for predicting an actionable near-future representation. A frozen spatial teacher first distills geometric priors into policy-connected spatial tokens. A current-conditioned bottleneck then learns a compact latent that captures future information relative to the present scene. This latent is predicted from the current policy context and initial Action Expert features, without access to future observations or the spatial teacher. Finally, zero-initialized residual fusion incorporates the predicted latent into the Action Expert to refine action generation. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and three real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improvements over in both simulation and real-robot settings.

</details>


### [241] [Imagine Smarter, Drive Faster: Efficient World Modeling for Decision-Aware Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28779)
*Feiyang Jia,Lin Liu,Zhicheng Bao,Caiyan Jia,Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lina Han,Yan Zhao,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastWorld是一个原生轻量级世界模型框架，通过紧凑场景表示、短时域动作条件预测和选择性候选验证实现高效自动驾驶规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在自动驾驶规划中计算成本高昂，而知识蒸馏和领域预训练等方法将负担转移给教师模型或训练流程，轻量级世界建模仍是开放挑战。

Method: 提出FastWorld框架，采用紧凑场景表示、短时域动作条件预测、选择性候选验证与分数精炼，无需教师蒸馏或额外领域预训练。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1上单张4090 GPU达到92.2 PDMS和132 FPS；NAVSIMv2上89.4 EPDMS；nuScenes上3秒平均L2为0.40，17 FPS。

Conclusion: FastWorld实现了高效轻量的未来感知规划，在多个基准上兼顾高精度与实时性，无需依赖蒸馏或预训练。

Abstract: World models hold great promise for autonomous driving planning, yet their ability to imagine the future often comes at a formidable computational cost. Efficiency efforts, such as knowledge distillation and domain-specific pre-training, often shift this burden to costly teachers or training pipelines, leaving lightweight world modeling an open challenge. We introduce FastWorld, a natively lightweight world-model framework for efficient autonomous driving planning. FastWorld reduces computation through compact scene representations, short-horizon action-conditioned prediction, and selective candidate verification and score refinement. These designs support efficient future-informed planning without teacher distillation or additional domain-specific pre-training. On NAVSIMv1, FastWorld achieves 92.2 PDMS at 132 FPS on a single 4090 GPU. It also achieves 89.4 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2 and an average L2 of 0.40 over 3 seconds at 17 FPS on nuScenes. Code and models can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [242] [Don't Test Where You Trained: Geographic Overlap Inflates End-to-End Driving Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28177)
*Willi Poh,Kevin Rösch,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVSIMv1测试场景中89.6%与训练场景地理重叠，导致评分虚高；提出地理不相交划分并同场景对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准端到端驾驶基准NAVSIMv1的测试集与训练集存在严重地理重叠，可能使模型通过记忆位置而非泛化能力获得高分。

Method: 构建NAVSIMv1的地理不相交划分，并在双方均未见过的新场景上对比原始划分与地理划分训练的模型；测试DiffusionDrive、Latent TransFuser、DrivoR及无传感器基线，并在nuScenes上验证SparseDrive。

Result: 基于传感器的模型在未见场景上损失3.4至6.1 PDMS点（主要因驶出道路和接近物体），无传感器基线仅损失1.5点；损失集中在原始划分中训练轨迹经过的地点；移除辅助地图损失后差距仍存在；nuScenes上地理划分使SparseDrive建图精度下降59%并降低规划性能。

Conclusion: 日志场景上的驾驶分数部分衡量了模型对测试地点的记忆能力，而非泛化能力；发布地理划分以评估对新地点的泛化。

Abstract: In NAVSIMv1, the standard benchmark for end-to-end driving, $89.6\%$ of the test scenes lie within 4m of a training scene. We show that this geographic overlap inflates the reported scores. We propose a geographically disjoint split of NAVSIMv1 and an evaluation that compares models trained on either split on the same scenes, which neither has seen in training. All sensor-based models we test, DiffusionDrive, Latent TransFuser, and DrivoR, lose 3.4 to 6.1 PDMS points on these scenes, mainly by leaving the road and coming close to other objects, against 1.5 points for a baseline without sensors. The loss concentrates where a training drive of the original split passed the same place. At all other places, models trained on the original split are no better than those trained on the geographic split. Models trained on the original split predict road geometry that no camera can see. The gap persists when the auxiliary map loss is removed, so the models memorize locations even without being supervised to. On nuScenes, the geographic split cuts SparseDrive's mapping accuracy by $59\%$ and also degrades its planning. Driving scores on logged scenes thus partly measure how well a model remembers the test locations. We release the split to evaluate generalization to new places.

</details>


### [243] [Urban State Representation for Autonomous Mobility: A Survey of Multimodal Perception, Architectures, Datasets, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28129)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述提出城市状态表示（USR）作为统一框架，整合多平台感知数据，并系统梳理相关技术、数据集与应用，提出评估协议USR-5和六个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前城市感知的学习表示大多针对单一平台、单一城市和单一任务，导致表示碎片化，缺乏跨平台共享与跨任务复用的统一状态表示。

Method: 形式化USR及其五项期望属性，将其与BEV特征、占据栅格、矢量化地图、神经场、3D高斯、场景图、路网图、语言token和世界模型潜变量关联；构建覆盖传感器到决策的统一分类法；汇编26个城市数据集与模拟器并提供指标表、跨基准结果快照、趋势分析和平台覆盖差距分析；综述应用并提出USR-5评估协议与六个开放问题。

Result: 建立了USR的形式化定义和五项期望属性，给出了跨平台、跨视图、融合算子、3D几何基础模型、端到端与视觉-语言-动作模型、生成式世界模型和数据引擎的统一分类；整理了26个数据集与模拟器的多维度对比及跨基准结果快照；提出USR-5五测试协议及聚合评分。

Conclusion: USR为计算机视觉、自动驾驶、交通和城市计算研究者提供了统一入口，有望推动跨城市与跨传感器迁移、不确定性、效率、仿真到现实评估及治理等方向的研究。

Abstract: Cities are sensed by many platforms at once: on-board cameras, LiDAR and radar on autonomous vehicles, roadside units at intersections, drones and satellites overhead, and the GNSS traces of millions of trips. Yet most learned representations are built for one platform, one city, and one task. We survey this fragmented landscape through a single lens, the Urban State Representation (USR): a georeferenced, layered, time-varying, uncertainty-aware state that any sensing platform can write to and any downstream task can read from. We (i) formalize USR and five desiderata for it, and relate it to bird's-eye-view (BEV) features, occupancy, vectorized maps, neural fields and 3D Gaussians, scene graphs, road-network graphs, language tokens, and world-model latents; (ii) organize sensing platforms, cross-view (ground-aerial) matching, fusion operators, 3D geometric foundation models, end-to-end and vision-language-action models, and generative world models and data engines in one taxonomy that spans sensors to decisions; (iii) compile 26 urban datasets and simulators across ego, cooperative (V2X), aerial, trajectory, and language settings, with a metric glossary, a cross-benchmark results snapshot, a trend analysis, and a platform-coverage gap analysis; and (iv) review applications and propose, as an outlook, USR-5, a five-test protocol with explicit references and one aggregate score for measuring whether a state is shared, together with a research agenda of six open problems covering cross-city and cross-sensor transfer, uncertainty, efficiency, sim-to-real evaluation, and governance. This survey is an entry point for computer vision, autonomous driving, transportation, and urban computing researchers; the paper list, datasets, and USR-5 code are at this https URL .

</details>


### [244] [Robust Trajectory Prediction Under Sensing and V2X Noise: Evaluation and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28128)
*Shahab Salehi,Luca Lusvarghi,Miguel Sepulcre,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Transformer交互感知轨迹预测模型对实际噪声对象状态估计的敏感性，发现噪声导致预测精度最多下降6倍，并证明在噪声增强数据集上重新训练比使用输入去噪模块更有效，可将精度下降限制在1.5倍以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的Transformer轨迹预测模型通常在大规模高度精选数据集上训练和评估，这些数据集通过离线后处理过滤了检测和跟踪噪声；但实际部署中对象状态（位置、朝向、速度）估计受传感和定位不确定性影响，V2X通信场景下不确定性更高，因此需要评估模型对现实噪声的敏感性并寻找缓解策略。

Method: 首先研究五个最先进的Transformer交互感知轨迹预测模型对现实噪声对象状态估计的敏感性；然后比较在噪声增强数据集上重新训练与在输入端使用去噪模块两种策略对缓解噪声影响的效果；并公开释放噪声增强数据集。

Result: 数值结果表明，随着噪声水平增加，预测精度最多下降6倍，突显模型对训练与部署噪声条件不匹配的敏感性；重新训练模型可将预测精度下降限制在1.5倍以内，效果优于输入去噪模块。

Conclusion: 在更现实的噪声增强数据集上重新训练是缓解噪声对象状态估计对轨迹预测精度影响的有效策略，优于输入去噪模块，且公开的数据集可支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Anticipating the future behavior of surrounding objects is key for the safe and efficient navigation of autonomous vehicles. State-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models are typically trained and evaluated on large-scale, highly curated datasets obtained by filtering any object detection and tracking noise through extensive offline post-processing. However, in real-world deployments, the detected objects' state (position, heading, and speed) estimates are inherently affected by sensing and localization uncertainties. These uncertainties can be even higher for connected and automated vehicles receiving information from other road users through Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications. In this paper, we first investigate the sensitivity of five state-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models to realistic noisy object state estimates. Numerical results show that prediction accuracy degrades by up to a 6x factor as the noise level increases, highlighting the models' sensitivity to the mismatch between the noise conditions encountered during training and those experienced at deployment. We then show that retraining models on a more realistic noise-augmented dataset is a more effective strategy to mitigate the impact of noisy object state estimates on trajectory prediction accuracy than using denoising modules at the input. Retrained models limit the prediction accuracy degradation below a 1.5x factor. The noise-augmented dataset is openly released to support further research.

</details>


### [245] [Vision-Language Truck Driving Advisories Grounded in Provincial Rules and Forecast Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28079)
*Xintong Jiang,Shucheng Huang,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Jincheng Xu,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出数据生成与蒸馏框架，结合场景理解、风险预测和交规检索生成卡车驾驶建议，并用8B教师模型蒸馏3B学生模型实现车载部署，在加拿大四省数据集上验证一致性、风险预警和交规符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型生成的驾驶建议可能与道路条件或交通规则冲突，且通常只在危险可见后才响应；长途卡车制动距离长、跨辖区法规不同，需要提前预警和合规建议。

Method: 构建集成结构化场景理解、风险预测和交通规则检索的数据生成与蒸馏框架，每条记录经一致性检查；由8B教师模型生成记录，蒸馏3B学生模型用于车载；并发布覆盖加拿大四省的行车记录仪数据集（11,142个标注车道线帧和9,494个驾驶片段）。

Result: 在四省留出驾驶数据上，学生模型94.6%的片段通过一致性检查（未调优8B教师为20.2%）；提前10至30秒预警26.2%的风险；97.7%的生成建议符合省级交通规则与指引。

Conclusion: 该框架可为紧凑型驾驶建议模型生成符合规则的训练数据。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate context-aware driving advisories, but their outputs may conflict with road conditions or traffic rules, and they typically address a hazard only once it is visible. This matters for long-haul trucking, where heavy trucks need long stopping distances and a single trip may cross several jurisdictions with different traffic regulations. This paper proposes a data generation and distillation framework that integrates structured scene understanding, risk forecasting, and traffic-rule retrieval to generate truck driving decisions and natural-language advisories, with a consistency check on every record. The records, written by an 8B teacher, are then used to distill a 3B-parameter student model for onboard deployment. We also introduce a dashcam dataset covering four Canadian provinces, comprising 11,142 annotated lane-marking frames and 9,494 driving segments. On held-out driving data across all four provinces, the student passes the consistency check in 94.6% of the segments, against 20.2% for the untuned 8B teacher, and announces 26.2% of the risks in the next 10 to 30 seconds before they appear. Of the generated advisories, 97.7% conform to the provincial traffic rules and guidance. These findings suggest that the framework can produce rule-conforming training data for compact driving advisory models.

</details>
